===== SOURCE FILE modules/cpu/cpu_gate_up.hpp =====
#pragma once

#include "ggml.h"
#include "ggml-cpu.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>

namespace llama_exp {

// Strata native_gu_rows groups gate/up and activation. Use GGML dots and layout here.
inline void cpu_gate_up_clamp(ggml_tensor * dst, int ith, int nth, void * userdata) {
    const auto * x = dst->src[0];
    const auto * ids = dst->src[1];
    const auto * gate = dst->src[2];
    const auto * up = dst->src[3];
    const auto * gt = ggml_get_type_traits_cpu(gate->type);
    const auto * ut = ggml_get_type_traits_cpu(up->type);
    const float limit = *static_cast<const float *>(userdata);
    const int64_t rows = dst->ne[0];
    int64_t active = 0;
    for (int64_t k = 0; k < ids->ne[0]; ++k) {
        const int32_t e = *reinterpret_cast<const int32_t *>(static_cast<const char *>(ids->data) + k*ids->nb[0]);
        if (e >= 0) ++active;
    }
    const int64_t first = active*rows*ith/nth;
    const int64_t last = active*rows*(ith + 1)/nth;
    int64_t offset = 0;
    for (int64_t k = 0; k < ids->ne[0]; ++k) {
        const int32_t e = *reinterpret_cast<const int32_t *>(static_cast<const char *>(ids->data) + k*ids->nb[0]);
        float * out = reinterpret_cast<float *>(static_cast<char *>(dst->data) + k*dst->nb[1]);
        if (e == -1) {
            if (k%nth == ith) std::fill_n(out, rows, 0.0f);
            continue;
        }
        GGML_ASSERT(e >= 0 && e < gate->ne[2]);
        const void * q = static_cast<const char *>(x->data) + (k%x->ne[1])*x->nb[1];
        const int64_t begin = std::max(int64_t(0), first - offset);
        const int64_t end = std::min(rows, last - offset);
        offset += rows;
        for (int64_t row = begin; row < end; ++row) {
            float g, u;
            gt->vec_dot(gate->ne[0], &g, 0, static_cast<const char *>(gate->data) + e*gate->nb[2] + row*gate->nb[1], 0, q, 0, 1);
            ut->vec_dot(up->ne[0], &u, 0, static_cast<const char *>(up->data) + e*up->nb[2] + row*up->nb[1], 0, q, 0, 1);
            g = std::min(g, limit);
            u = std::clamp(u, -limit, limit);
            out[row] = g/(1.0f + expf(-g))*u;
        }
    }
}

inline ggml_tensor * build_cpu_gate_up_clamp(ggml_context * ctx, ggml_tensor * x, ggml_tensor * ids,
                                            ggml_tensor * gate, ggml_tensor * up, const float * limit) {
    if (!gate || !up || !limit || !(*limit > 1e-6f) || !std::isfinite(*limit) ||
        x->type != GGML_TYPE_F32 || ids->type != GGML_TYPE_I32 ||
        x->ne[2] != 1 || x->ne[3] != 1 || ids->ne[1] != 1 || ids->ne[2] != 1 || ids->ne[3] != 1 ||
        !ggml_is_contiguous(x) || !ggml_is_quantized(gate->type) || !ggml_is_quantized(up->type) ||
        gate->type == GGML_TYPE_NVFP4 || up->type == GGML_TYPE_NVFP4 ||
        gate->ne[0] != x->ne[0] || up->ne[0] != x->ne[0] || gate->ne[1] != up->ne[1] ||
        gate->ne[2] != up->ne[2] || gate->ne[3] != 1 || up->ne[3] != 1 ||
        !ggml_is_contiguous(gate) || !ggml_is_contiguous(up)) return nullptr;
    const auto * gt = ggml_get_type_traits_cpu(gate->type);
    const auto * ut = ggml_get_type_traits_cpu(up->type);
    if (!gt->vec_dot || !ut->vec_dot || gt->vec_dot_type != ut->vec_dot_type ||
        !ggml_get_type_traits_cpu(gt->vec_dot_type)->from_float) return nullptr;
    auto * quant = ggml_cast(ctx, x, gt->vec_dot_type);
    ggml_set_name(quant, "cpu_moe.shared_quant");
    ggml_tensor * args[] = { quant, ids, gate, up };
    return ggml_custom_4d(ctx, GGML_TYPE_F32, gate->ne[1], ids->ne[0], 1, 1, args, 4,
                          cpu_gate_up_clamp, GGML_N_TASKS_MAX, const_cast<float *>(limit));
}

} // namespace llama_exp

===== SOURCE FILE modules/cuda/mmq_select_probe.hpp =====
#pragma once

// P15C: opt-in HOST profiling of the MMQ tile selector, NOT a GPU timer.
// The observed region covers only the J / thread-count selection in mmq.cu.
// No CUDA calls, memory allocations on the hot path, or changes to GGML math.
// Each host thread emits bounded JSONL buckets on stderr at most once per
// 4096 observed calls, plus a final partial chunk when the thread exits.

#include <array>
#include <cstddef>
#include <atomic>
#include <chrono>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <limits>

namespace llama_exp {

class mmq_select_probe final {
public:
    static constexpr std::size_t max_buckets = 512;
    static constexpr std::uint64_t report_every = 4096;

    struct sample {
        int type = -1;
        int ncols_bucket = -1;
        bool moe = false;
        std::uint64_t calls = 0;
        std::uint64_t cache_hits = 0;
        std::uint64_t select_ns = 0;
        std::uint64_t max_select_ns = 0;
    };

    static bool enabled() noexcept {
        // Exact opt-in. As with existing GGML_CUDA_MMQ_PREC, changing the env
        // inside a running process is intentionally not supported.
        static const bool value = [] {
            const char * v = std::getenv("LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_CUDA_MMQ_PROFILE");
            return v && v[0] == '1' && v[1] == '\0';
        }();
        return value;
    }

    static std::uint64_t clock_ns() noexcept {
        using namespace std::chrono;
        return static_cast<std::uint64_t>(duration_cast<nanoseconds>(steady_clock::now().time_since_epoch()).count());
    }

    static mmq_select_probe & local() noexcept {
        static thread_local mmq_select_probe instance;
        return instance;
    }

    void record(int type, bool moe, std::int64_t ncols, bool hit, std::uint64_t select_ns) noexcept {
        const int bucket = ncols <= 0 ? 0 : ncols == 1 ? 1 : ncols <= 8 ? 2 : ncols <= 64 ? 3 : ncols <= 256 ? 4 : 5;
        // Fixed open-addressing hash map: no heap, predictable bounded work,
        // no linear scan of all existing format/shape categories per call.
        sample * s = nullptr;
        const std::uint64_t h = std::uint64_t(std::uint32_t(type))*UINT64_C(0x9e3779b97f4a7c15)
                ^ std::uint64_t(bucket)*UINT64_C(0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9)
                ^ std::uint64_t(moe)*UINT64_C(0x94d049bb133111eb);
        const std::size_t first = std::size_t(h ^ (h >> 32)) & (max_buckets - 1);
        for (std::size_t attempt = 0; attempt < max_buckets; ++attempt) {
            auto & entry = samples_[(first + attempt) & (max_buckets - 1)];
            if (!entry.calls) {
                entry.type = type;
                entry.moe = moe;
                entry.ncols_bucket = bucket;
                s = &entry;
                break;
            }
            if (entry.type == type && entry.moe == moe && entry.ncols_bucket == bucket) {
                s = &entry;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (s) {
            s->calls = sat_add(s->calls, 1);
            s->cache_hits = sat_add(s->cache_hits, hit ? 1 : 0);
            s->select_ns = sat_add(s->select_ns, select_ns);
            if (s->max_select_ns < select_ns) {
                s->max_select_ns = select_ns;
            }
        } else {
            dropped_ = sat_add(dropped_, 1);
        }
        count_ += 1;
        if (count_ == report_every) {
            flush();
        }
    }

    void flush() noexcept {
        if (!count_) {
            return;
        }
        // One fprintf per JSONL record is atomically locked by the C stdio
        // implementation; no global profiler mutex on the hot path.
        for (const auto & s : samples_) {
            if (!s.calls) {
                continue;
            }
            std::fprintf(stderr,
                    "P15C_MMQ_SELECT_JSON {\"thread\":%" PRIu64 ",\"type\":%d,\"moe\":%s,"
                    "\"ncols_bucket\":%d,\"calls\":%" PRIu64 ",\"cache_hits\":%" PRIu64 ","
                    "\"select_ns\":%" PRIu64 ",\"max_select_ns\":%" PRIu64 "}\n",
                    thread_id_, s.type, s.moe ? "true" : "false", s.ncols_bucket,
                    s.calls, s.cache_hits, s.select_ns, s.max_select_ns);
        }
        if (dropped_) {
            std::fprintf(stderr,
                    "P15C_MMQ_SELECT_JSON {\"thread\":%" PRIu64 ",\"overflow\":%" PRIu64 "}\n",
                    thread_id_, dropped_);
        }
        samples_ = {};
        dropped_ = 0;
        count_ = 0;
    }

    ~mmq_select_probe() noexcept { flush(); }

    mmq_select_probe(const mmq_select_probe &) = delete;
    mmq_select_probe & operator=(const mmq_select_probe &) = delete;

private:
    mmq_select_probe() noexcept : thread_id_(next_thread_id()) {}

    static std::uint64_t next_thread_id() noexcept {
        static std::atomic<std::uint64_t> id{0};
        return id.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed) + 1;
    }

    static std::uint64_t sat_add(std::uint64_t left, std::uint64_t right) noexcept {
        const auto max = std::numeric_limits<std::uint64_t>::max();
        return left > max - right ? max : left + right;
    }

    std::array<sample, max_buckets> samples_{};
    std::uint64_t count_ = 0;
    std::uint64_t dropped_ = 0;
    const std::uint64_t thread_id_;
};

}  // namespace llama_exp

===== SOURCE FILE modules/cuda/mmq_tile_cache.hpp =====
#pragma once

// P15B: pure HOST-SIDE memoization of GGML-CUDA MMQ MoE tile selection.
// The key contains every argument used by the original J/threads solver.
// Collisions cause recomputation, never an approximate result.
// No CUDA device state or GGUF weights are stored in this cache.

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>

namespace llama_exp {

struct mmq_tile_key {
    int32_t type = 0;
    int64_t ncols = 0;
    bool fallback = false;
    int32_t cc = 0;
    uint64_t shared_bytes = 0;
    int32_t precision = 0;

    bool operator==(const mmq_tile_key & rhs) const noexcept {
        return type == rhs.type && ncols == rhs.ncols && fallback == rhs.fallback &&
               cc == rhs.cc && shared_bytes == rhs.shared_bytes && precision == rhs.precision;
    }
};

struct mmq_tile_result {
    int J = 0;
    int nthreads = 0;
};

template <size_t Slots = 32>
class mmq_tile_cache {
    static_assert(Slots > 0 && (Slots & (Slots - 1)) == 0,
                  "MMQ cache Slots must be a nonzero power of two");

    struct entry {
        mmq_tile_key key{};
        mmq_tile_result result{};
        bool valid = false;
    };
    entry entries_[Slots] = {};

    static uint64_t mix(uint64_t x) noexcept {
        // Fixed-width unsigned operations, independent of signed overflow.
        x ^= x >> 30;
        x *= UINT64_C(0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9);
        x ^= x >> 27;
        x *= UINT64_C(0x94d049bb133111eb);
        return x ^ (x >> 31);
    }
    static size_t index(const mmq_tile_key & k) noexcept {
        uint64_t h = UINT64_C(0x9e3779b97f4a7c15);
        h = mix(h ^ uint64_t(uint32_t(k.type)));
        h = mix(h ^ uint64_t(k.ncols));
        h = mix(h ^ uint64_t(k.fallback));
        h = mix(h ^ uint64_t(uint32_t(k.cc)));
        h = mix(h ^ k.shared_bytes);
        h = mix(h ^ uint64_t(uint32_t(k.precision)));
        return size_t(h) & (Slots - 1);
    }

public:
    template <typename Compute>
    mmq_tile_result get_or_compute(const mmq_tile_key & key, Compute && compute) {
        entry & e = entries_[index(key)];
        if (e.valid && e.key == key) {
            return e.result;
        }
        // Never install a partially calculated entry if compute throws.
        const mmq_tile_result result = compute();
        e.key = key;
        e.result = result;
        e.valid = true;
        return result;
    }
};

} // namespace llama_exp

===== SOURCE FILE modules/engram/graph_stage.hpp =====
#pragma once

// P08: exact-raw-row adapter for an opt-in GGML_GET_ROWS staging input.
// The caller owns the immutable mapped GGUF tensor. Do not outlive it.
// P13 optionally shares a model-owned source and async worker between graphs.
// The model must close the cache *before* releasing the tensor mapping.
// This header has no dependency on ggml; the patch to deepseek41.cpp provides
// the GGML input tensors and preserves their original quantized element type.

#include "model_cache.hpp"

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <limits>
#include <memory>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <vector>

namespace llama_exp {

class engram_graph_stage final {
public:
    static constexpr size_t max_stage_bytes = size_t(64) << 20;
    static constexpr size_t default_cache_bytes = size_t(16) << 20;

    static bool enabled() noexcept {
        // Graph reuse requires the switch to be fixed for the process lifetime.
        static const bool active = [] {
            const char * value = std::getenv("LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_ENGRAM_STAGE");
            return value && value[0] == '1' && value[1] == '\0';
        }();
        return active;
    }

    // Size gate is checked before any GGML tensors are replaced. Failure means
    // the original, directly mmap-backed GET_ROWS path is selected instead.
    static bool eligible(uint64_t table_rows, size_t raw_row_bytes, size_t stride,
                         size_t mapped_bytes, uint64_t rows_per_layer,
                         uint64_t number_of_layers) noexcept {
        if (!table_rows || !raw_row_bytes || !stride || raw_row_bytes > stride ||
                raw_row_bytes > (size_t(1) << 20) || mapped_bytes < raw_row_bytes ||
                rows_per_layer == 0 || number_of_layers == 0 ||
                rows_per_layer > uint64_t(INT32_MAX) ||
                table_rows - 1 > (mapped_bytes - raw_row_bytes)/stride) {
            return false;
        }
        const size_t max_rows = max_stage_bytes / raw_row_bytes;
        // Total bytes of all graph input tensors, not merely a per-layer limit.
        return rows_per_layer <= max_rows && number_of_layers <= max_rows/rows_per_layer;
    }

    // Legacy P08 constructor remains synchronous, self-contained and exact.
    engram_graph_stage(mapped_rows view, uint64_t total_rows, size_t data_budget = default_cache_bytes)
        : engram_graph_stage(std::make_shared<engram_model_cache>(view, total_rows, data_budget, 0)) {}

    // P13: the model (not the graph) owns the shared cache and worker.
    explicit engram_graph_stage(std::shared_ptr<engram_model_cache> model_cache)
        : model_cache_(std::move(model_cache)),
          total_rows_(model_cache_ ? model_cache_->total_rows() : 0),
          row_bytes_(model_cache_ ? model_cache_->row_bytes() : 0) {
        if (!model_cache_ || !row_bytes_ || !total_rows_) {
            throw std::invalid_argument("Engram graph stage needs a live model cache");
        }
    }

    engram_graph_stage(const engram_graph_stage &) = delete;
    engram_graph_stage & operator=(const engram_graph_stage &) = delete;

    // Stage raw GGUF rows, in the original index order. The GGML graph uses
    // identity indices [0, count) into a compact tensor of the SAME GGML type.
    // No conversion, dequantization, approximation, or dropped row occurs here.
    // The caller must not publish data()/bytes() if this method returns false.
    // On failure, the whole request must fail closed; switching graph sources
    // after build would be unsafe. Disable the flag for the baseline fallback.
    bool stage(const int32_t * indices, size_t count) {
        if (!indices || count == 0 || count > size_t(INT32_MAX) ||
                count > max_stage_bytes/row_bytes_) return false;
        // Validate all indices before potentially modifying the staging buffer.
        for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
            if (indices[i] < 0 || uint64_t(indices[i]) >= total_rows_) return false;
        }
        scratch_.resize(count*row_bytes_);
        return model_cache_->get_many(indices, count, scratch_.data(), scratch_.size());
    }

    const uint8_t * data() const noexcept { return scratch_.data(); }
    size_t bytes() const noexcept { return scratch_.size(); }
    size_t row_bytes() const noexcept { return row_bytes_; }
    engram_row_cache::counters counters() const { return model_cache_->counters(); }
    size_t prefetch(const int32_t * indices, size_t count) {
        return model_cache_->prefetch(indices, count);
    }

private:
    const std::shared_ptr<engram_model_cache> model_cache_;
    const uint64_t total_rows_;
    const size_t row_bytes_;
    std::vector<uint8_t> scratch_;
};

} // namespace llama_exp

===== SOURCE FILE modules/engram/mapped_rows.hpp =====
#pragma once

// Exact byte reader for an immutable mmap-backed, possibly strided GGUF tensor.
// The mapping is borrowed, NEVER owned. close() the row_cache before the
// mapping is released. Stride is a byte offset between rows, not row bytes.

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstring>
#include <stdexcept>

namespace llama_exp {
struct mapped_rows final {
    const uint8_t * base;
    size_t mapped_bytes;
    size_t stride;
    size_t payload_bytes;

    mapped_rows(const void * base, size_t mapped_bytes, size_t stride, size_t payload_bytes)
            : base(static_cast<const uint8_t *>(base)), mapped_bytes(mapped_bytes),
              stride(stride), payload_bytes(payload_bytes) {
        if (!base || !payload_bytes || !stride || payload_bytes > stride || payload_bytes > mapped_bytes) {
            throw std::invalid_argument("mapped rows have invalid bounds/stride");
        }
    }

    bool operator()(uint64_t id, uint8_t * dest, size_t bytes) const noexcept {
        if (!dest || bytes != payload_bytes) return false;
        // Avoid id*stride overflow before checking the mapped extent.
        if (id > (mapped_bytes - payload_bytes)/stride) return false;
        std::memcpy(dest, base + size_t(id)*stride, payload_bytes);
        return true;
    }
};
}

===== SOURCE FILE modules/engram/model_cache.hpp =====
#pragma once

// P13: model-owned, exact-raw-row Engram cache. The source is a *borrowed*
// immutable GGUF host mapping. The owning llama_model_deepseek41 must call
// close() in its derived destructor, before the base releases tensor buffers.
// Graph-stage instances may share this cache but own their staging scratch.
// No second disk format, speculative hash, lossy conversion or global worker.

#include "mapped_rows.hpp"
#include "row_cache.hpp"

#include <atomic>
#include <chrono>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <limits>
#include <stdexcept>

namespace llama_exp {

class engram_model_cache final {
public:
    static constexpr size_t default_data_budget = size_t(16) << 20;
    static constexpr size_t async_queue_limit = 128;

    engram_model_cache(mapped_rows view, uint64_t total_rows,
                       size_t budget = default_data_budget,
                       size_t pending_limit = 0)
        : view_(view), total_rows_(total_rows), trace_(trace_enabled()),
          cache_([this](uint64_t id, uint8_t * dest, size_t bytes) {
              return view_(id, dest, bytes);
          }, total_rows, view.payload_bytes, budget, pending_limit) {
        if (!total_rows_ || total_rows_ - 1 >
                (view_.mapped_bytes - view_.payload_bytes) / view_.stride) {
            throw std::invalid_argument("Engram source extent is smaller than tensor rows");
        }
    }

    engram_model_cache(const engram_model_cache &) = delete;
    engram_model_cache & operator=(const engram_model_cache &) = delete;
    ~engram_model_cache() {
        close();
        if (trace_) {
            const auto stats = cache_.snapshot();
            // One record per cache object after draining. Reads count mapping
            // accesses; Linux page-cache hits are possible on every read.
            std::fprintf(stderr, "R20_ENGRAM_JSON {\"table_rows\":%" PRIu64
                    ",\"row_bytes\":%zu,\"stage_calls\":%" PRIu64 ",\"rows_completed\":%" PRIu64
                    ",\"stage_host_ns_sum\":%" PRIu64 ",\"cache_hits\":%" PRIu64
                    ",\"foreground_reads\":%" PRIu64 ",\"worker_reads\":%" PRIu64
                    ",\"already_filled\":%" PRIu64 ",\"queue_dropped\":%" PRIu64
                    ",\"wait_hits\":%" PRIu64 ",\"read_failures\":%" PRIu64 "}\n",
                    total_rows_, view_.payload_bytes, calls_.load(), rows_done_.load(),
                    host_ns_.load(), stats.hit, stats.synchronous_reads, stats.background_reads,
                    stats.already_filled, stats.queue_dropped, stats.wait_hits, stats.read_failures);
        }
    }

    bool matches(mapped_rows view, uint64_t rows) const noexcept {
        return view_.base == view.base && view_.mapped_bytes == view.mapped_bytes &&
               view_.stride == view.stride && view_.payload_bytes == view.payload_bytes &&
               total_rows_ == rows;
    }

    // Enqueue only validated row IDs, copying every ID into a bounded queue.
    // A queue-full or disabled async worker is never an inference failure:
    // foreground stage() still reads the exact source bytes.
    size_t prefetch(const int32_t * ids, size_t count) {
        return cache_.prefetch_i32(ids, count);
    }

    engram_row_cache::read_result get(uint64_t id, uint8_t * dest, size_t n) {
        return cache_.get(id, dest, n, std::chrono::milliseconds(1));
    }

    bool get_many(const int32_t * ids, size_t count, uint8_t * dest, size_t bytes) {
        if (!trace_) return cache_.get_many_i32(ids, count, dest, bytes, std::chrono::milliseconds(1));
        const auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
        const bool ok = cache_.get_many_i32(ids, count, dest, bytes, std::chrono::milliseconds(1));
        const auto elapsed = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - start;
        calls_.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        if (ok) rows_done_.fetch_add(count, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        host_ns_.fetch_add(std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::nanoseconds>(elapsed).count(),
                           std::memory_order_relaxed);
        return ok;
    }

    size_t row_bytes() const noexcept { return view_.payload_bytes; }
    uint64_t total_rows() const noexcept { return total_rows_; }
    engram_row_cache::counters counters() const { return cache_.snapshot(); }

    // Idempotent. Must complete BEFORE tensor buffers and mmap are released.
    // A blocked page fault cannot be force-cancelled: close() waits for it.
    void close() noexcept { cache_.close(); }

private:
    static bool trace_enabled() noexcept {
        const char * value = std::getenv("LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_ENGRAM_TRACE");
        return value && value[0] == '1' && value[1] == '\0';
    }
    const mapped_rows view_;
    const uint64_t total_rows_;
    const bool trace_;
    std::atomic<uint64_t> calls_{0}, rows_done_{0}, host_ns_{0};
    engram_row_cache cache_;
};

} // namespace llama_exp

===== SOURCE FILE modules/engram/row_cache.hpp =====
#pragma once

// Source-only experimental building block for exact GGUF Engram/PLE row bytes.
// Used by the optional DeepSeek 4.1 graph stage. The caller must close()
// the cache before unmapping it. No GGUF conversion, decoded-row cache, or
// extra tensor representation is involved. C++17, no dependencies on ggml.

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <condition_variable>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstring>
#include <deque>
#include <functional>
#include <list>
#include <mutex>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <thread>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

namespace llama_exp {

class engram_row_cache final {
public:
    using row_id = uint64_t;
    // Reader is called with *exactly* row_bytes() of writable storage.
    // Reader can return false or throw on an I/O fault; no partial row is cached.
    // Calls from the worker and foreground reader are serialized.
    using reader_type = std::function<bool(row_id, uint8_t *, size_t)>;

    enum class read_result { cache_hit, source_read, io_error, closed, invalid_row, invalid_output };

    struct counters {
        uint64_t hit = 0;
        uint64_t synchronous_reads = 0;
        uint64_t background_reads = 0;
        uint64_t read_failures = 0;
        uint64_t queued = 0;
        uint64_t duplicate_prefetch = 0;
        uint64_t already_filled = 0;
        uint64_t queue_dropped = 0;
        uint64_t evicted = 0;
        uint64_t wait_hits = 0;
        size_t resident_rows = 0;
        size_t queued_or_inflight = 0;
        size_t resident_data_bytes = 0;
        size_t row_capacity = 0;
        bool stopped = false;
    };

    // max_data_bytes is a hard limit for retained payload, not an accounting of
    // std::unordered_map/list/allocator metadata. queue_capacity bounds pending
    // row IDs; 0 disables the worker and the queue but permits sync caching.
    explicit engram_row_cache(reader_type reader, row_id total_rows,
                              size_t row_bytes, size_t max_data_bytes,
                              size_t queue_capacity)
            : reader_(std::move(reader)), total_rows_(total_rows), row_bytes_(row_bytes),
              row_capacity_(row_bytes ? max_data_bytes / row_bytes : 0),
              queue_capacity_(queue_capacity) {
        if (!reader_ || total_rows_ == 0 || row_bytes_ == 0 || row_capacity_ == 0 ||
                row_bytes_ > (size_t(1) << 20) || max_data_bytes > (size_t(64) << 20) ||
                queue_capacity_ > (size_t(1) << 16)) {
            throw std::invalid_argument("invalid Engram row cache geometry/budget");
        }
        if (queue_capacity_) {
            worker_ = std::thread(&engram_row_cache::run, this);
        }
    }

    engram_row_cache(const engram_row_cache &) = delete;
    engram_row_cache & operator=(const engram_row_cache &) = delete;
    engram_row_cache(engram_row_cache &&) = delete;
    engram_row_cache & operator=(engram_row_cache &&) = delete;

    ~engram_row_cache() { close(); }

    size_t row_bytes() const noexcept { return row_bytes_; }
    size_t row_capacity() const noexcept { return row_capacity_; }

    // Nonblocking. The queue never holds pointers supplied by the caller.
    // Full queue requests are dropped, NOT delayed, preserving decode latency.
    size_t prefetch(const row_id * ids, size_t n) {
        if (!ids || n == 0 || queue_capacity_ == 0) return 0;
        std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(mu_);
        if (stopping_) return 0;
        size_t submitted = 0;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            const row_id id = ids[i];
            if (id >= total_rows_) continue;
            if (entries_.count(id) || pending_.count(id)) {
                ++stats_.duplicate_prefetch;
                continue;
            }
            if (pending_.size() >= queue_capacity_) {
                ++stats_.queue_dropped;
                continue;
            }
            pending_.insert(id);
            try {
                queue_.push_back(id);
            } catch (...) {
                pending_.erase(id);
                throw;
            }
            ++submitted;
        }
        stats_.queued += submitted;
        if (submitted) cv_work_.notify_one();
        return submitted;
    }

    // P13: no temporary std::vector for per-graph int32_t hashed row IDs.
    // No source pointer survives this call. Duplicates/negative IDs are ignored.
    size_t prefetch_i32(const int32_t * ids, size_t n) {
        if (!ids || n == 0 || queue_capacity_ == 0) return 0;
        std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(mu_);
        if (stopping_) return 0;
        size_t submitted = 0;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            if (ids[i] < 0) continue;
            const row_id id = uint64_t(ids[i]);
            if (id >= total_rows_) continue;
            if (entries_.count(id) || pending_.count(id)) {
                ++stats_.duplicate_prefetch;
                continue;
            }
            if (pending_.size() >= queue_capacity_) {
                ++stats_.queue_dropped;
                continue;
            }
            pending_.insert(id);
            try {
                queue_.push_back(id);
            } catch (...) {
                pending_.erase(id);
                throw;
            }
            ++submitted;
        }
        stats_.queued += submitted;
        if (submitted) cv_work_.notify_one();
        return submitted;
    }

    // Foreground read provides an exact fallback independent of prefetch state.
    // A zero wait never blocks on a pending prefetch condition (but may need to
    // wait for the shared source reader mutex). A bounded wait may use a newly
    // completed prefetch, otherwise it falls through to a source read.
    read_result get(row_id id, uint8_t * dest, size_t dest_size,
                    std::chrono::milliseconds max_wait = std::chrono::milliseconds(0)) {
        if (!dest || dest_size < row_bytes_) return read_result::invalid_output;
        if (id >= total_rows_) return read_result::invalid_row;
        std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(mu_);
        return get_locked(lock, id, dest, max_wait);
    }

    // Called for already hashed GGML rows in logical gather order. Keep the
    // metadata lock over a short run of hits. get_locked releases it before
    // all waits and source reads; the chunk bound also lets close/other graphs
    // make progress on all-hit prefill batches.
    bool get_many_i32(const int32_t * ids, size_t n, uint8_t * dest, size_t bytes,
                      std::chrono::milliseconds max_wait) {
        if (!ids || !dest || n == 0 || n > bytes / row_bytes_) return false;
        const size_t chunk = std::min<size_t>(32, std::max<size_t>(1, 65536 / row_bytes_));
        for (size_t first = 0; first < n; ) {
            const size_t count = std::min(chunk, n - first);
            std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(mu_);
            for (size_t i = first; i < first + count; ++i) {
                if (ids[i] < 0 || row_id(ids[i]) >= total_rows_) return false;
                const auto result = get_locked(lock, row_id(ids[i]), dest + i * row_bytes_, max_wait);
                if (result != read_result::cache_hit && result != read_result::source_read) return false;
            }
            first += count;
        }
        return true;
    }

    // Close is idempotent and waits for worker plus in-flight get() readers.
    // This must precede unmapping/releasing the source tensor. Blocking source
    // readers cannot be force-cancelled; the owner must bound their I/O time.
    void close() noexcept {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> shutdown(shutdown_mu_);
        {
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mu_);
            if (!stopping_) {
                stopping_ = true;
                queue_.clear();
                cv_work_.notify_all();
                cv_ready_.notify_all();
            }
        }
        if (worker_.joinable()) worker_.join();
        {
            std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(mu_);
            cv_ready_.wait(lock, [&] { return active_reads_ == 0; });
            entries_.clear();
            lru_.clear();
            pending_.clear();
        }
    }

    counters snapshot() const {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mu_);
        counters result = stats_;
        result.resident_rows = entries_.size();
        result.queued_or_inflight = pending_.size();
        result.resident_data_bytes = entries_.size() * row_bytes_;
        result.row_capacity = row_capacity_;
        result.stopped = stopping_;
        return result;
    }

private:
    read_result get_locked(std::unique_lock<std::mutex> &lock, row_id id, uint8_t *dest,
                           std::chrono::milliseconds max_wait) {
        if (stopping_) return read_result::closed;
        if (fetch_hit(id, dest)) return read_result::cache_hit;

        // A waiter releases mu_ in wait_until; it is already an in-flight
        // get(). Keep it registered until it has released reader_mu_ as well.
        ++active_reads_;
        struct active_read {
            engram_row_cache & cache;
            std::unique_lock<std::mutex> & lock;
            ~active_read() {
                if (!lock.owns_lock()) lock.lock();
                if (--cache.active_reads_ == 0 && cache.stopping_) cache.cv_ready_.notify_all();
            }
        } registered{*this, lock};
        if (max_wait.count() > 0 && pending_.count(id)) {
            const auto end = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + max_wait;
            cv_ready_.wait_until(lock, end, [&] {
                return stopping_ || !pending_.count(id) || entries_.count(id);
            });
            if (stopping_) return read_result::closed;
            if (fetch_hit(id, dest)) {
                ++stats_.wait_hits;
                return read_result::cache_hit;
            }
        }

        lock.unlock();
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> source_lock(reader_mu_);
        lock.lock();
        if (stopping_) return read_result::closed;
        if (fetch_hit(id, dest)) return read_result::cache_hit;

        lock.unlock();
        bool ok = false;
        try { ok = reader_(id, dest, row_bytes_); } catch (...) { ok = false; }
        lock.lock();
        if (stopping_) return read_result::closed;
        if (!ok) {
            ++stats_.read_failures;
            return read_result::io_error;
        }
        ++stats_.synchronous_reads;
        insert(id, dest);
        cv_ready_.notify_all();
        return read_result::source_read;
    }

    struct entry {
        std::vector<uint8_t> raw;
        std::list<row_id>::iterator lru;
    };

    bool fetch_hit(row_id id, uint8_t * dest) {
        const auto it = entries_.find(id);
        if (it == entries_.end()) return false;
        std::memcpy(dest, it->second.raw.data(), row_bytes_);
        lru_.splice(lru_.begin(), lru_, it->second.lru);
        ++stats_.hit;
        return true;
    }

    void insert(row_id id, const uint8_t * data) {
        if (entries_.find(id) != entries_.end()) return;
        while (entries_.size() >= row_capacity_) {
            const row_id old = lru_.back();
            entries_.erase(old);
            lru_.pop_back();
            ++stats_.evicted;
        }
        std::vector<uint8_t> raw(data, data + row_bytes_);
        lru_.push_front(id);
        try {
            entries_.emplace(id, entry{std::move(raw), lru_.begin()});
        } catch (...) {
            lru_.pop_front();
            throw;
        }
    }

    void run() noexcept {
        std::vector<uint8_t> raw; // one scratch row per worker lifetime
        for (;;) {
            row_id id = 0;
            {
                std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(mu_);
                cv_work_.wait(lock, [&] { return stopping_ || !queue_.empty(); });
                if (stopping_) return;
                id = queue_.front();
                queue_.pop_front();
            }
            // Serialize callback and publication with foreground readers.
            // A queued id can become resident while this worker waits for the
            // source. Recheck after taking the same lock used by get_locked.
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> source_lock(reader_mu_);
            {
                std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mu_);
                if (stopping_) return;
                if (entries_.count(id)) {
                    pending_.erase(id);
                    ++stats_.already_filled;
                    cv_ready_.notify_all();
                    continue;
                }
            }
            bool ok = false;
            try {
                raw.resize(row_bytes_);
                ok = reader_(id, raw.data(), raw.size());
            } catch (...) { ok = false; }
            {
                std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mu_);
                pending_.erase(id);
                if (!stopping_) {
                    if (ok) {
                        ++stats_.background_reads;
                        try { insert(id, raw.data()); }
                        catch (...) { ++stats_.read_failures; }
                    } else {
                        ++stats_.read_failures;
                    }
                }
            }
            cv_ready_.notify_all();
        }
    }

    const reader_type reader_;
    const row_id total_rows_;
    const size_t row_bytes_;
    const size_t row_capacity_;
    const size_t queue_capacity_;
    mutable std::mutex mu_;
    std::mutex reader_mu_;
    std::mutex shutdown_mu_;
    std::condition_variable cv_work_;
    std::condition_variable cv_ready_;
    std::thread worker_;
    std::deque<row_id> queue_;
    std::unordered_set<row_id> pending_;
    std::unordered_map<row_id, entry> entries_;
    std::list<row_id> lru_;
    counters stats_;
    size_t active_reads_ = 0;
    bool stopping_ = false;
};

} // namespace llama_exp

===== SOURCE FILE modules/moe/upload_completion.hpp =====
#pragma once

// P14B: host-side completion/ownership contract for staged MoE uploads.
// Uses ONLY the existing backend's command queue; does not create a CUDA
// stream, poll device memory, change routing or time GPU kernels.
//
// An event, when available, is recorded AFTER the final slot-map upload.
// The next readback's existing backend synchronize retires the epoch. If an
// error interrupts the submission, or the owning context closes the cache
// with an epoch pending, the guard waits for all enqueued reads of borrowed
// host memory before that memory can be freed.
//
// BACKEND LIFETIME: the llama_context owner MUST call close() BEFORE freeing
// the backend. This guard deliberately never dereferences a backend in its
// destructor; an unclosed event guard terminates rather than risking UAF.

#include <cstdlib>
#include <exception>
#include <stdexcept>

namespace llama_exp {

template<class Adapter>
class moe_upload_completion final {
public:
    using backend_type = typename Adapter::backend_type;
    using event_type   = typename Adapter::event_type;

    static bool enabled() {
        static const bool on = [] {
            const char * v = std::getenv("LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_EVENT_FENCE");
            return v && v[0] == '1' && v[1] == '\0';
        }();
        return on;
    }

    explicit moe_upload_completion(backend_type backend) : backend_(backend) {
        if (!backend_) throw std::invalid_argument("MoE event fence: null backend");
        // nullptr means the device has no events; fall back to full backend sync
        // only for abort/close, not for every successful token.
        event_ = Adapter::create(backend_);
    }

    moe_upload_completion(const moe_upload_completion &) = delete;
    moe_upload_completion & operator=(const moe_upload_completion &) = delete;
    moe_upload_completion(moe_upload_completion &&) = delete;
    moe_upload_completion & operator=(moe_upload_completion &&) = delete;

    ~moe_upload_completion() noexcept {
        // A forgotten owner close is a programming error. We cannot safely
        // call any backend API from here: backend pointers may be dead.
        if (!closed_) std::terminate();
    }

    bool event_supported() const noexcept { return event_ != event_type{}; }
    bool pending()         const noexcept { return pending_; }
    bool closed()          const noexcept { return closed_; }

    // Must follow a backend sync (the original routing-ID readback, without
    // reordering). Can retire even if no new event has been recorded yet.
    bool retired_after_backend_sync() {
        if (closed_) throw std::logic_error("MoE event fence: retired after close");
        const bool was_pending = pending_;
        pending_  = false;
        recorded_ = false;
        return was_pending;
    }

    // Called BEFORE the first async upload, on the normal scheduler thread.
    // Once called, any exceptional exit must drain() on the same backend.
    void begin_submission() {
        if (closed_ || pending_)
            throw std::logic_error("MoE event fence: overlapping or closed epoch");
        pending_ = true;
        recorded_ = false;
    }

    // Called AFTER every bank transfer and the slot-map write have been
    // enqueued, on the SAME backend/stream (never from the CPU producer).
    void record_after_slot_map() {
        if (closed_ || !pending_ || recorded_)
            throw std::logic_error("MoE event fence: invalid record transition");
        if (event_supported()) {
            Adapter::record(event_, backend_);
            recorded_ = true;
        }
        // An eventless backend retains pending until the readback sync.
    }

    void drain() {
        if (closed_) return;
        if (!pending_) return;
        if (recorded_ && event_supported()) Adapter::synchronize_event(event_);
        else Adapter::synchronize_backend(backend_);
        pending_  = false;
        recorded_ = false;
    }

    void drain_noexcept() noexcept {
        try { drain(); } catch (...) { std::terminate(); }
    }

    // Must be invoked explicitly by llama_context before backend teardown.
    // Reentrant: close() twice is harmless; event free runs exactly once.
    void close() {
        if (closed_) return;
        drain();
        if (event_supported()) {
            Adapter::destroy(event_);
            event_ = event_type{};
        }
        closed_ = true;
    }

private:
    backend_type backend_;
    event_type event_{};
    bool pending_ = false;
    bool recorded_ = false;
    bool closed_ = false;
};

// Scope whose destructor drains on ANY exception or early exit after begin().
// The caller explicitly commit()s only after recording the last slot-map
// transfer. An abort never attempts to continue with half-uploaded banks.
template<class Completion>
class moe_upload_abort_scope final {
public:
    explicit moe_upload_abort_scope(Completion * completion) : completion_(completion) {}
    moe_upload_abort_scope(const moe_upload_abort_scope &) = delete;
    moe_upload_abort_scope & operator=(const moe_upload_abort_scope &) = delete;
    ~moe_upload_abort_scope() noexcept {
        if (completion_ && !committed_) completion_->drain_noexcept();
    }
    void commit() noexcept { committed_ = true; }
private:
    Completion * completion_;
    bool committed_ = false;
};

} // namespace llama_exp

===== SOURCE FILE modules/moe/upload_pipeline.hpp =====
#pragma once

// P09: opt-in host-side MoE upload producer, C++17, no ggml dependency.
// Dependencies: the caller has already completed GPU routing ID readback and
// SLRU planning, and owns an immutable list of selected exact expert IDs.
// This class never calls a backend from its producer thread. It only copies
// CPU source bytes into disjoint slices of the caller-owned staging buffer.
// The owner MUST keep that buffer and all weight sources alive until the next
// backend synchronization, including when an upload is asynchronous.

#include "upload_types.hpp"
#include "upload_worker.hpp"

#include <condition_variable>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <exception>
#include <memory>
#include <mutex>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <system_error>
#include <thread>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

namespace llama_exp {

class moe_upload_pipeline final {
public:
    static bool enabled() {
        // Exact opt-in; no interpretation of arbitrary nonzero strings.
        static const bool value = [] {
            const char * env = std::getenv("LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_PIPELINE");
            return env && env[0] == '1' && env[1] == '\0';
        }();
        return value;
    }

    // A true return means every bank was prepared and handed to submit in
    // original bank order. A false return means the caller must use its
    // existing serial path; no preparation or submission has taken place.
    // Failures after work has started propagate and must abort the inference
    // operation (never silently continue with incomplete GPU bank contents).
    //
    // prepare(bank, slice_base, slice_bytes) is called concurrently for bank 0
    // (caller) and banks 1..n-1 (one producer). It must neither mutate the
    // SLRU/fills nor issue GPU calls. A completed batch owns descriptors only;
    // all source/staging memory stays under the caller's lifetime contract.
    // submit(bank, batch) is called ONLY on the caller thread, in bank order.
    // On a failure after submit begins, abort_fence() must synchronously drain
    // queued device reads before this function propagates the exception.
    template <class Prepare, class Submit, class AbortFence>
    static bool execute(
            uint8_t * staging, size_t capacity, size_t banks, size_t minimum_slice,
            Prepare prepare, Submit submit, AbortFence abort_fence, bool prepare_inline = false) {
        constexpr size_t alignment = 256;
        constexpr size_t max_banks = 8;
        if (!staging || banks < 2 || banks > max_banks || minimum_slice == 0 ||
                capacity < banks || capacity / banks < alignment) {
            return false;
        }
        const size_t slice_bytes = (capacity / banks / alignment) * alignment;
        if (slice_bytes < minimum_slice) {
            return false;
        }

        // No bank can pack bytes for this exact fill geometry. Preserve the
        // same slices, callbacks, bank order and abort fence, without a worker.
        if (prepare_inline) {
            bool submission_started = false;
            try {
                for (size_t i = 0; i < banks; ++i) {
                    const auto batch = prepare(i, staging + i*slice_bytes, slice_bytes);
                    submission_started = true;
                    submit(i, batch);
                }
            } catch (...) {
                if (submission_started) {
                    try { abort_fence(); } catch (...) { std::terminate(); }
                }
                throw;
            }
            return true;
        }

        // P10: reuse one producer per invoking scheduler thread. A thread-local
        // worker never holds model/weight pointers between execute() calls and
        // is safe when a scheduler processes multiple GPU backends serially.
        // Failure to create the worker falls back to the P09B per-call thread.
        if (moe_upload_worker::enabled()) {
            static thread_local std::unique_ptr<moe_upload_worker> persistent;
            static thread_local bool unavailable = false;
            if (!persistent && !unavailable) {
                try {
                    persistent = std::make_unique<moe_upload_worker>();
                } catch (const std::system_error &) {
                    unavailable = true;
                }
            }
            if (persistent) {
                return persistent->execute(staging, capacity, banks, minimum_slice, prepare, submit, abort_fence);
            }
        }
        std::vector<moe_upload_batch> batches(banks);
        std::vector<bool> ready(banks, false);
        std::mutex lock;
        std::condition_variable wake;
        std::exception_ptr producer_error;

        std::thread producer;
        try {
            producer = std::thread([&] {
                for (size_t i = 1; i < banks; ++i) {
                    try {
                        batches[i] = prepare(i, staging + i*slice_bytes, slice_bytes);
                    } catch (...) {
                        {
                            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> guard(lock);
                            producer_error = std::current_exception();
                        }
                        wake.notify_all();
                        return;
                    }
                    {
                        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> guard(lock);
                        ready[i] = true;
                    }
                    wake.notify_all();
                }
            });
        } catch (const std::system_error &) {
            return false;
        }

        // Always join before leaving this scope, including exceptions from
        // prepare(0) or submit. No detached work can access mappings/buffers.
        struct producer_join {
            std::thread & thread;
            ~producer_join() { if (thread.joinable()) thread.join(); }
        } join{producer};

        bool submission_started = false;
        try {
            batches[0] = prepare(0, staging, slice_bytes);
            submission_started = true;
            submit(0, batches[0]);
            for (size_t i = 1; i < banks; ++i) {
                {
                    std::unique_lock<std::mutex> guard(lock);
                    wake.wait(guard, [&] { return ready[i] || producer_error; });
                    if (producer_error) {
                        std::rethrow_exception(producer_error);
                    }
                }
                submit(i, batches[i]);
            }
        } catch (...) {
            if (submission_started) {
                // A backend may already be reading staging asynchronously.
                // Its owner provides the backend-specific synchronization.
                // A failed fence cannot safely continue unwinding/freeing.
                try { abort_fence(); } catch (...) { std::terminate(); }
            }
            throw;
        }
        return true;
    }
};

} // namespace llama_exp

===== SOURCE FILE modules/moe/upload_plan.hpp =====
#pragma once

// P12: one immutable layout of exact MoE fill runs per cache update.
// The same plan can be read concurrently by P09B producer and caller threads,
// and reused by every expert tensor bank with its own expert_size/staging budget.
// No speculative routing, truncation, tensor/quantization changes or backend calls.
// The caller owns source bytes, staging, and the plan for the entire execute().
// C++17, no llama.cpp / GGML dependencies. Opt-in and disabled by default.

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <vector>

namespace llama_exp {

struct moe_fill_plan_stats {
    size_t bytes = 0;
    size_t staged_bytes = 0;
    size_t calls = 0;
    size_t calls_saved = 0;
};

class moe_fill_plan final {
public:
    struct run {
        size_t expert;
        size_t slot;
        size_t count;
    };

    static bool enabled() {
        static const bool value = [] {
            const char * env = std::getenv("LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_SHARED_PLAN");
            return env && env[0] == '1' && env[1] == '\0';
        }();
        return value;
    }

    template <class Fill>
    explicit moe_fill_plan(const std::vector<Fill> & fills) {
        runs_.reserve(fills.size());
        for (size_t i = 0; i < fills.size();) {
            // The SLRU fill planner emits nonnegative 32-bit IDs and slots.
            // Reject malformed input before any copies are submitted.
            if (fills[i].expert < 0 || fills[i].slot < 0) {
                throw std::invalid_argument("MoE shared fill plan: negative expert or slot");
            }
            const size_t expert = static_cast<size_t>(fills[i].expert);
            const size_t slot   = static_cast<size_t>(fills[i].slot);
            size_t n = 1;
            while (i + n < fills.size() &&
                   static_cast<int64_t>(fills[i + n].expert) == static_cast<int64_t>(fills[i].expert) + static_cast<int64_t>(n) &&
                   static_cast<int64_t>(fills[i + n].slot)   == static_cast<int64_t>(fills[i].slot) + static_cast<int64_t>(n)) {
                ++n;
            }
            runs_.push_back({ expert, slot, n });
            i += n;
        }
    }

    size_t run_count() const noexcept { return runs_.size(); }

    // Exact P04 semantics: start with the full direct run (even if it cannot
    // fit staging), then append whole next runs only when slots are adjacent
    // and the entire run fits. Never split a direct run to fill a slice.
    // Every bank has its own width and own cursor: the same immutable plan is
    // safe for concurrent prepares with disjoint output slices.
    template <class Upload>
    moe_fill_plan_stats upload(const uint8_t * source, size_t expert_size,
                               uint8_t * staging, size_t capacity, size_t & used,
                               Upload upload) const {
        if (!source || expert_size == 0 || used > capacity) {
            throw std::invalid_argument("MoE shared fill plan: invalid upload parameters");
        }
        moe_fill_plan_stats stats;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < runs_.size();) {
            const auto & start = runs_[i];
            const size_t fits = staging ? (capacity - used)/expert_size : 0;
            size_t packed_n = start.count;
            size_t run_end = i + 1;
            while (packed_n < fits && run_end < runs_.size()) {
                const auto & next = runs_[run_end];
                if (start.slot > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() - packed_n ||
                    next.slot != start.slot + packed_n ||
                    next.count > fits - packed_n) {
                    break;
                }
                packed_n += next.count;
                ++run_end;
            }
            if (run_end > i + 1) {
                // All multiplications are bounded by fits; the stage cursor
                // cannot wrap or overrun its slice.
                for (size_t k = i, offset = 0; k < run_end; ++k) {
                    const auto & r = runs_[k];
                    copy_to_stage(staging + used + offset*expert_size,
                                  source, r.expert, r.count, expert_size);
                    offset += r.count;
                }
                const size_t bytes = packed_n*expert_size;
                upload(staging + used, checked_offset(start.slot, expert_size), bytes);
                used += bytes;
                stats.bytes        += bytes;
                stats.staged_bytes += bytes;
                ++stats.calls;
                stats.calls_saved += run_end - i - 1;
                i = run_end;
            } else {
                const size_t bytes = checked_offset(start.count, expert_size);
                upload(checked_source(source, start.expert, expert_size),
                       checked_offset(start.slot, expert_size), bytes);
                stats.bytes += bytes;
                ++stats.calls;
                ++i;
            }
        }
        return stats;
    }

private:
    static size_t checked_offset(size_t value, size_t width) {
        if (value > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()/width) {
            throw std::overflow_error("MoE shared fill plan: offset overflow");
        }
        return value*width;
    }

    static const uint8_t * checked_source(const uint8_t * source, size_t expert, size_t width) {
        return source + checked_offset(expert, width);
    }

    static void copy_to_stage(uint8_t * dst, const uint8_t * source,
                              size_t expert, size_t count, size_t width) {
        std::memcpy(dst, checked_source(source, expert, width), checked_offset(count, width));
    }

    std::vector<run> runs_;
};

} // namespace llama_exp

===== SOURCE FILE modules/moe/upload_scheduler.hpp =====
#pragma once

// P14A: admission control for the existing P09B host CPU/GPU upload pipeline.
// This is a SOURCE-ONLY, CPU-side scheduler: it estimates the EXACT P04
// staging/transfer layout before starting a producer. It neither calls a
// backend, predicts future experts, creates CUDA streams nor changes GGUF/KV.
// All selected expert IDs have already been read back and SLRU-planned.
// A rejected candidate must use the caller's existing serial/P04 path.
//
// Opt-in: LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_SCHEDULER=1 (also requires the existing
// PIPELINE=1 and pinned staging flags at the call site). Default is OFF.
// The admission thresholds are deliberately conservative and must be tuned
// against real single-token decode before making any production default.

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <limits>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <vector>

namespace llama_exp {

enum class moe_schedule_reason : uint8_t {
    admitted,
    bank_count,
    empty_fills,
    insufficient_staging,
    too_small,
    no_coalescing,
    too_little_packing,
};

struct moe_schedule_decision {
    bool admitted = false;
    moe_schedule_reason reason = moe_schedule_reason::bank_count;
    size_t bank_count = 0;
    size_t slice_bytes = 0;
    size_t bytes = 0;             // exact H2D payload (no transfer reduction claim)
    size_t staged_bytes = 0;      // host-to-host packing estimate
    size_t upload_calls = 0;      // exact predicted P04 upload invocation count
    size_t calls_saved = 0;       // direct runs coalesced into packed transfers
    size_t packing_banks = 0;
};

class moe_upload_scheduler final {
public:
    static constexpr size_t max_banks = 8;
    static constexpr size_t alignment = 256;
    static constexpr size_t min_payload_bytes = 256 * 1024;
    static constexpr size_t min_pack_bytes = 128 * 1024;

    static bool enabled() {
        static const bool flag = [] {
            const char * s = std::getenv("LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_SCHEDULER");
            return s && s[0] == '1' && s[1] == '\0';
        }();
        return flag;
    }

    // WidthAt(bank) yields the exact bytes in one expert of this bank.
    // No copying and no allocation. O(n_banks * n_fills), up to 8 banks.
    // Invalid/overflowing source geometry is an exception BEFORE any backend
    // upload, never a silent fallback that could conceal a partial update.
    template <class Fill, class WidthAt>
    static moe_schedule_decision assess(
            const std::vector<Fill> & fills, size_t banks, WidthAt width_at,
            size_t staging_capacity) {
        moe_schedule_decision d;
        d.bank_count = banks;
        if (banks < 2 || banks > max_banks) return d;
        if (fills.empty()) {
            d.reason = moe_schedule_reason::empty_fills;
            return d;
        }
        // The P09 producer requires a 256-aligned slice per bank and at least
        // one full expert in each slice. Mirror its exact size calculation.
        if (staging_capacity / banks < alignment) {
            d.reason = moe_schedule_reason::insufficient_staging;
            return d;
        }
        d.slice_bytes = (staging_capacity / banks / alignment) * alignment;
        if (fills.size() > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int32_t>::max())) {
            throw std::length_error("MoE P14 scheduler: implausibly many fills");
        }
        for (const auto & f : fills) {
            if (f.expert < 0 || f.slot < 0) {
                throw std::invalid_argument("MoE P14 scheduler: negative expert or slot");
            }
        }

        // Count exact P04 direct runs once: whether expert AND slot advance by
        // one controls a direct H2D transfer. Only an adjacent SLOT allows us
        // to pack several otherwise discontiguous expert runs into one upload.
        const auto direct_run = [&](size_t first) {
            size_t n = 1;
            while (first + n < fills.size() &&
                    static_cast<int64_t>(fills[first+n].expert) ==
                        static_cast<int64_t>(fills[first].expert) + static_cast<int64_t>(n) &&
                    static_cast<int64_t>(fills[first+n].slot) ==
                        static_cast<int64_t>(fills[first].slot) + static_cast<int64_t>(n)) {
                ++n;
            }
            return n;
        };

        for (size_t ip = 0; ip < banks; ++ip) {
            const size_t width = width_at(ip);
            if (width == 0) {
                throw std::invalid_argument("MoE P14 scheduler: zero expert width");
            }
            if (width > d.slice_bytes) {
                d.reason = moe_schedule_reason::insufficient_staging;
                return d;
            }
            const size_t payload = mul_checked(fills.size(), width);
            d.bytes = add_checked(d.bytes, payload);
            size_t used = 0;
            bool packed_bank = false;
            for (size_t i = 0; i < fills.size();) {
                const size_t first_count = direct_run(i);
                size_t packed_count = first_count;
                size_t fragments = 1;
                const size_t fits = (d.slice_bytes - used) / width;
                // Exactly match P04's "whole run or no packing" rule.
                while (packed_count < fits && i + packed_count < fills.size() &&
                        static_cast<int64_t>(fills[i + packed_count].slot) ==
                            static_cast<int64_t>(fills[i].slot) + static_cast<int64_t>(packed_count)) {
                    const size_t count = direct_run(i + packed_count);
                    if (count > fits - packed_count) break;
                    packed_count += count;
                    ++fragments;
                }
                if (packed_count > first_count) {
                    const size_t copied = mul_checked(packed_count, width);
                    used = add_checked(used, copied);
                    d.staged_bytes = add_checked(d.staged_bytes, copied);
                    d.calls_saved = add_checked(d.calls_saved, fragments - 1);
                    i += packed_count;
                    packed_bank = true;
                } else {
                    i += first_count;
                }
                d.upload_calls = add_checked(d.upload_calls, 1);
            }
            if (packed_bank) ++d.packing_banks;
        }
        if (d.bytes < min_payload_bytes) {
            d.reason = moe_schedule_reason::too_small;
        } else if (d.calls_saved == 0) {
            d.reason = moe_schedule_reason::no_coalescing;
        } else if (d.staged_bytes < min_pack_bytes) {
            d.reason = moe_schedule_reason::too_little_packing;
        } else {
            d.reason = moe_schedule_reason::admitted;
            d.admitted = true;
        }
        return d;
    }

private:
    static size_t mul_checked(size_t a, size_t b) {
        if (a && b > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()/a) {
            throw std::overflow_error("MoE P14 scheduler: payload multiplication overflow");
        }
        return a*b;
    }

    static size_t add_checked(size_t a, size_t b) {
        if (b > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()-a) {
            throw std::overflow_error("MoE P14 scheduler: accumulated bytes overflow");
        }
        return a+b;
    }
};

} // namespace llama_exp

===== SOURCE FILE modules/moe/upload_types.hpp =====
#pragma once

// Common zero-copy upload descriptors for P09B and P10. Source or staging
// memory is borrowed from the caller; no backend work is done here.
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <vector>

namespace llama_exp {

struct moe_upload_span {
    const uint8_t * data = nullptr;
    size_t dst_offset = 0;
    size_t size = 0;
};

struct moe_upload_batch {
    std::vector<moe_upload_span> spans;
    size_t bytes = 0;
    size_t staged_bytes = 0;
    size_t calls_saved = 0;
};

} // namespace llama_exp

===== SOURCE FILE modules/moe/upload_worker.hpp =====
#pragma once

// P11: persistent MoE CPU producer with fixed-capacity control metadata.
// The P10 job previously allocated a std::function + two std::vectors for
// every token/cache update. This version uses an 8-element array and a
// non-owning, type-erased function pointer to the caller's stack job frame.
// The worker MUST finish before execute() returns; backend operations stay
// on the calling thread. Only the caller's prepare() may allocate spans.
// No GGML/CUDA dependencies. C++17.

#include "upload_types.hpp"

#include <array>
#include <condition_variable>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <exception>
#include <mutex>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <system_error>
#include <thread>
#include <utility>

namespace llama_exp {

class moe_upload_worker final {
public:
    static bool enabled() {
        static const bool value = [] {
            const char * env = std::getenv("LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_PERSISTENT");
            return env && env[0] == '1' && env[1] == '\0';
        }();
        return value;
    }

    moe_upload_worker() : thread_([this] { run(); }) {}
    moe_upload_worker(const moe_upload_worker &) = delete;
    moe_upload_worker & operator=(const moe_upload_worker &) = delete;

    // The owning scheduler must not destroy the worker while execute() runs.
    ~moe_upload_worker() {
        {
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> guard(mutex_);
            stop_ = true;
        }
        cv_.notify_all();
        if (thread_.joinable()) thread_.join();
    }

    template <class Prepare, class Submit, class AbortFence>
    bool execute(uint8_t * staging, size_t capacity, size_t banks, size_t minimum_slice,
                 Prepare prepare, Submit submit, AbortFence abort_fence) {
        constexpr size_t alignment = 256;
        if (!staging || banks < 2 || banks > max_banks || minimum_slice == 0 ||
                capacity < banks || capacity / banks < alignment) {
            return false;
        }
        const size_t slice_bytes = (capacity / banks / alignment)*alignment;
        if (slice_bytes < minimum_slice) return false;

        // The frame is borrowed by the worker for THIS invocation only.
        // await_done() is compulsory on both success and exception paths.
        struct job_frame {
            Prepare * callback;
            uint8_t * buffer;
            size_t slice_bytes;
        } frame{&prepare, staging, slice_bytes};
        const auto invoke = +[](void * opaque, size_t bank) -> moe_upload_batch {
            auto & f = *static_cast<job_frame *>(opaque);
            return (*f.callback)(bank, f.buffer + bank*f.slice_bytes, f.slice_bytes);
        };
        {
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> guard(mutex_);
            if (active_ || stop_) {
                throw std::logic_error("MoE worker is active or stopping");
            }
            ready_.fill(false);
            error_ = nullptr;
            total_banks_ = banks;
            job_ctx_ = &frame;
            job_fn_ = invoke;
            cancel_ = false;
            done_ = false;
            active_ = true;
            job_ready_ = true;
        }
        cv_.notify_all();

        bool submission_attempted = false;
        try {
            // Bank zero runs on the calling scheduler thread. The worker
            // handles banks [1..N) using separate, non-overlapping slices.
            const auto first = invoke(&frame, 0);
            submission_attempted = true;  // submit() may enqueue and throw
            submit(0, first);
            for (size_t bank = 1; bank < banks; ++bank) {
                std::unique_lock<std::mutex> guard(mutex_);
                cv_.wait(guard, [&] { return ready_[bank] || error_ || done_; });
                if (error_) std::rethrow_exception(error_);
                if (!ready_[bank]) throw std::runtime_error("MoE worker incomplete upload");
                // No worker writes this bank again before execute returns.
                const moe_upload_batch * prepared = &batches_[bank];
                guard.unlock();
                submission_attempted = true;
                submit(bank, *prepared);
            }
            await_done();
        } catch (...) {
            {
                std::lock_guard<std::mutex> guard(mutex_);
                cancel_ = true;
            }
            cv_.notify_all();
            await_done();
            if (submission_attempted) {
                try { abort_fence(); } catch (...) { std::terminate(); }
            }
            clear_job();
            throw;  // never fall back to serial after a partial submission
        }
        clear_job();
        return true;
    }

private:
    static constexpr size_t max_banks = 8;
    using job_fn = moe_upload_batch (*)(void *, size_t);

    void await_done() {
        std::unique_lock<std::mutex> guard(mutex_);
        cv_.wait(guard, [&] { return done_; });
    }

    void clear_job() {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> guard(mutex_);
        // The worker is finished. Release payload descriptors and callbacks
        // without retaining any source/staging pointers across invocations.
        for (size_t i = 1; i < total_banks_; ++i) {
            batches_[i].spans.clear();
            batches_[i].bytes = 0;
            batches_[i].staged_bytes = 0;
            batches_[i].calls_saved = 0;
        }
        job_fn_ = nullptr;
        job_ctx_ = nullptr;
        total_banks_ = 0;
        active_ = false;
    }

    void run() {
        for (;;) {
            std::unique_lock<std::mutex> guard(mutex_);
            cv_.wait(guard, [&] { return job_ready_ || stop_; });
            if (stop_) return;
            job_ready_ = false;
            const size_t banks = total_banks_;
            const job_fn callback = job_fn_;
            void * const context = job_ctx_;
            // The borrower cannot clear the frame until done_ is true.
            guard.unlock();

            for (size_t bank = 1; bank < banks; ++bank) {
                {
                    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> state(mutex_);
                    if (cancel_) break;
                }
                try {
                    auto prepared = callback(context, bank);
                    {
                        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> state(mutex_);
                        batches_[bank] = std::move(prepared);
                        ready_[bank] = true;
                    }
                    cv_.notify_all();
                } catch (...) {
                    {
                        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> state(mutex_);
                        error_ = std::current_exception();
                    }
                    cv_.notify_all();
                    break;
                }
            }
            {
                std::lock_guard<std::mutex> state(mutex_);
                done_ = true;
            }
            cv_.notify_all();
        }
    }

    std::mutex mutex_;
    std::condition_variable cv_;
    std::array<moe_upload_batch, max_banks> batches_;
    std::array<bool, max_banks> ready_{};
    job_fn job_fn_ = nullptr;
    void * job_ctx_ = nullptr;
    std::exception_ptr error_;
    size_t total_banks_ = 0;
    bool active_ = false;
    bool job_ready_ = false;
    bool done_ = false;
    bool cancel_ = false;
    bool stop_ = false;
    // run() may observe every member as soon as the thread constructor starts.
    std::thread thread_;
};

} // namespace llama_exp

===== SOURCE FILE modules/speculative/mtp_rewind_guard.hpp =====
#pragma once

// P16B: per-sequence, bounded host snapshot of MTP's pending hidden row.
// Does NOT store or roll back KV/recurrent/Engram tensors, draft RNG, or GPU buffers.
// The upstream server restores target + draft checkpoints BEFORE calling rewind().

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <vector>

namespace llama_exp {

inline bool mtp_rewind_guard_enabled() {
    const char * p = std::getenv("LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MTP_REWIND_GUARD");
    return p != nullptr && p[0] == '1' && p[1] == '\0';
}

class mtp_rewind_guard {
public:
    // A new draft must snapshot the hidden boundary *before* draft() can
    // advance its context. The bounded storage is precisely one hidden row.
    void checkpoint(const std::vector<float> & pending_h) {
        if (pending_h.empty()) {
            throw std::invalid_argument("MTP checkpoint needs a nonempty hidden row");
        }
        checkpoint_h_ = pending_h;
        has_checkpoint_ = true;
        clear_verification();
    }

    // Used before speculative process() touches any target verification rows.
    void clear_verification() noexcept {
        verified_rows_ = 0;
        can_accept_ = false;
    }

    // Called only after source embeddings were fully copied for this sequence.
    void verified(std::size_t count) {
        if (count == 0) {
            throw std::invalid_argument("MTP verification has no token rows");
        }
        verified_rows_ = count;
        can_accept_ = true;
    }

    // Validate BEFORE modifying the live boundary. Accepting beyond the real
    // verified rows or accepting twice must fail rather than reuse stale data.
    void validate_accept(std::uint16_t count) const {
        if (!can_accept_ || verified_rows_ == 0) {
            throw std::logic_error("MTP accept without fresh verification");
        }
        if (static_cast<std::size_t>(count) >= verified_rows_) {
            throw std::out_of_range("MTP accepted more tokens than verified");
        }
    }

    void commit_accept(std::uint16_t count) {
        validate_accept(count);
        clear_verification();
        checkpoint_h_.clear();
        has_checkpoint_ = false;
    }

    // Restore only the MTP host boundary AFTER upstream restores both model
    // context snapshots. Keep the checkpoint for replay until acceptance.
    void rewind(std::vector<float> & pending_h) {
        if (!has_checkpoint_ || checkpoint_h_.empty() || checkpoint_h_.size() != pending_h.size()) {
            throw std::logic_error("MTP rewind has no matching checkpoint");
        }
        pending_h = checkpoint_h_;
        clear_verification();
    }

    // Called on request release/cancellation. Do not erase the current hidden
    // state: upstream may retain prompt KV across requests; next process()
    // is responsible for reconstructing the correct boundary.
    void discard() noexcept {
        clear_verification();
        checkpoint_h_.clear();
        has_checkpoint_ = false;
    }

    std::size_t checkpoint_bytes() const noexcept { return checkpoint_h_.size() * sizeof(float); }
    bool has_checkpoint() const noexcept { return has_checkpoint_; }
    bool can_accept() const noexcept { return can_accept_; }

private:
    std::vector<float> checkpoint_h_;
    std::size_t verified_rows_ = 0;
    bool has_checkpoint_ = false;
    bool can_accept_ = false;
};

} // namespace llama_exp

===== SOURCE FILE modules/speculative/mtp_verify_window.hpp =====
#pragma once

// P16A: exact, bounded host snapshot for a single MTP speculator.
// This is deliberately independent of ggml / CUDA and does NOT roll back KV,
// recurrent state, samplers, or the target/draft contexts.

#include <algorithm>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <vector>

namespace llama_exp {

inline bool mtp_verify_window_enabled() {
    const char * value = std::getenv("LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MTP_VERIFY_WINDOW");
    return value && value[0] == '1' && value[1] == '\0';
}

// The normal server batch keeps each sequence contiguous. Custom callers can
// interleave sequences; only that case needs explicit per-token indices.
class mtp_batch_rows {
public:
    template <typename Token>
    bool index(const std::vector<Token> & tokens, std::vector<int32_t> & first,
               std::vector<int32_t> & last) {
        rows_.clear();
        previous_.clear();
        if (first.size() != last.size()) {
            return false;
        }
        std::fill(first.begin(), first.end(), -1);
        std::fill(last.begin(), last.end(), -1);
        bool interleaved = false;
        for (int32_t i = 0; i < static_cast<int32_t>(tokens.size()); ++i) {
            const auto seq = tokens[i].seq_id;
            if (seq < 0 || static_cast<std::size_t>(seq) >= first.size()) {
                return false;
            }
            if (first[seq] < 0) {
                first[seq] = i;
            } else if (last[seq] != i - 1) {
                interleaved = true;
            }
            last[seq] = i;
        }
        if (interleaved) {
            rows_.resize(first.size());
            previous_.resize(tokens.size());
            for (int32_t i = 0; i < static_cast<int32_t>(tokens.size()); ++i) {
                auto & rows = rows_[tokens[i].seq_id];
                previous_[i] = rows.empty() ? -1 : rows.back();
                rows.push_back(i);
            }
        }
        return true;
    }

    int32_t previous(int32_t row) const {
        return previous_.empty() ? row - 1 : previous_[row];
    }

    int32_t count(int32_t seq, int32_t first, int32_t last) const {
        return rows_.empty() ? last - first + 1 : static_cast<int32_t>(rows_[seq].size());
    }

    int32_t at(int32_t seq, int32_t first, int32_t index) const {
        return rows_.empty() ? first + index : rows_[seq][index];
    }

private:
    std::vector<std::vector<int32_t>> rows_;
    std::vector<int32_t> previous_;
};

struct mtp_verify_window {
    std::size_t total_rows = 0;     // target verification rows, prior to compaction
    std::size_t retained_rows = 0;  // row 0..retained_rows-1 are retained

    mtp_verify_window(std::size_t n_rows, int n_max, bool single_mtp_enabled)
            : total_rows(n_rows), retained_rows(n_rows) {
        // An unknown/negative draft cap cannot safely shrink the snapshot.
        // The pinned MTP driver checks n_max after emitting its first token,
        // so even an explicit zero can produce one accepted draft token.
        if (single_mtp_enabled && n_max >= 0 && n_rows > 0) {
            const std::size_t cap = static_cast<std::size_t>(std::max(1, n_max)) + 1;
            retained_rows = std::min(n_rows, cap);
        }
    }

    std::size_t accepted_row(std::uint16_t n_accepted) const {
        if (total_rows == 0) {
            throw std::out_of_range("MTP acceptance without verified rows");
        }
        // Exact upstream selection: min(accepted, n_rows - 1).
        const std::size_t row = std::min<std::size_t>(n_accepted, total_rows - 1);
        if (row >= retained_rows) {
            // Refuse an invalid acceptance count rather than silently use a
            // wrong hidden-state row. Disable the experiment for this driver.
            throw std::out_of_range("MTP acceptance exceeds retained verify window");
        }
        return row;
    }
};

} // namespace llama_exp

===== SOURCE FILE tests_cpp/engram_graph_stage_fixture.cpp =====
#include "../modules/engram/graph_stage.hpp"

#include <array>
#include <cassert>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <limits>
#include <random>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <vector>

namespace {
constexpr size_t row_bytes = 210; // DeepSeek V4.1 Engram Q6_K/256 row bytes
constexpr size_t stride = 224;    // Exercise padding in a mapped GGUF-like region
constexpr uint64_t n_rows = 512;

void require(bool v, const char * msg) { if (!v) throw std::runtime_error(msg); }
uint8_t value(size_t r, size_t k) { return uint8_t((r*19 + k*41 + (k ^ r)) & 0xff); }

std::vector<uint8_t> make_source() {
    std::vector<uint8_t> src(n_rows*stride, 0xcc);
    for (size_t row = 0; row < n_rows; ++row) {
        for (size_t k = 0; k < row_bytes; ++k) src[row*stride+k] = value(row,k);
    }
    return src;
}
void check_stage(const llama_exp::engram_graph_stage & stage,
                 const std::vector<int32_t> & ids) {
    require(stage.bytes() == ids.size()*row_bytes, "staged length mismatch");
    for (size_t i=0; i<ids.size(); ++i)
        for (size_t k=0; k<row_bytes; ++k)
            require(stage.data()[i*row_bytes+k] == value(ids[i],k), "wrong row bytes/order");
}

void exact_raw_stage_and_cache() {
    const auto raw = make_source();
    const auto original = raw;
    llama_exp::mapped_rows view(raw.data(),raw.size(),stride,row_bytes);
    llama_exp::engram_graph_stage stage(view,n_rows,row_bytes*8);
    const std::vector<int32_t> ids{10, 10, 11, 511, 0, 255, 11, 10};
    require(stage.stage(ids.data(),ids.size()),"initial stage failed");
    check_stage(stage,ids);
    require(stage.counters().hit>=3, "repeat rows should hit cache");
    require(stage.counters().resident_data_bytes<=row_bytes*8, "budget exceeded");
    for (int round=0; round<128; ++round) {
        std::vector<int32_t> next;
