        for(int j=0;j<40;++j) next.push_back(int32_t((round*73+j*11)%n_rows));
        require(stage.stage(next.data(),next.size()),"repeated stage failed");
        check_stage(stage,next);
        require(stage.counters().resident_data_bytes<=row_bytes*8,"cache budget exceeded");
    }
    require(stage.counters().evicted>0, "missing bounded LRU eviction");
    require(raw==original,"mmap source was changed");
}
void reject_invalid_without_dirtying_previous_buffer() {
    auto raw=make_source();
    llama_exp::engram_graph_stage stage(llama_exp::mapped_rows(raw.data(),raw.size(),stride,row_bytes),n_rows);
    std::vector<int32_t> good{1,2,3};
    require(stage.stage(good.data(),good.size()),"good stage failed");
    const std::vector<uint8_t> saved(stage.data(),stage.data()+stage.bytes());
    const int32_t negative[] = {0,-1,5};
    const int32_t overrun[] = {0,512,5};
    require(!stage.stage(negative,3),"negative index accepted");
    require(!stage.stage(overrun,3),"oversized index accepted");
    require(!stage.stage(nullptr,3),"null pointer accepted");
    require(!stage.stage(good.data(),0),"empty stage accepted");
    require(std::vector<uint8_t>(stage.data(),stage.data()+stage.bytes()) == saved,
            "failed validation changed previous input tensor data");
}
void eligibility_and_overflow() {
    using S=llama_exp::engram_graph_stage;
    require(S::eligible(n_rows,row_bytes,stride,n_rows*stride,64,3),"valid staging geometry refused");
    require(!S::eligible(n_rows,row_bytes,stride,n_rows*stride,0,3),"empty stage accepted");
    require(!S::eligible(n_rows,row_bytes,stride,n_rows*stride,64,0),"empty modules accepted");
    require(!S::eligible(n_rows,row_bytes,stride,row_bytes,64,1),"truncated mmap accepted");
    require(!S::eligible(n_rows,0,stride,n_rows*stride,64,1),"zero bytes accepted");
    require(!S::eligible(n_rows,row_bytes,100,n_rows*stride,64,1),"short stride accepted");
    require(!S::eligible(n_rows,row_bytes,stride,n_rows*stride,uint64_t(INT32_MAX)+1,1),"int32 overflow accepted");
    require(!S::eligible(n_rows,row_bytes,stride,n_rows*stride,200000,10000),"budget product overflow accepted");
    require(!S::eligible(std::numeric_limits<uint64_t>::max(),row_bytes,stride,n_rows*stride,1,1),
            "mmap range overflow accepted");
}
void random_row_byte_parity() {
    auto raw=make_source();
    llama_exp::engram_graph_stage stage(llama_exp::mapped_rows(raw.data(),raw.size(),stride,row_bytes),n_rows);
    std::mt19937 rng(0xC0FFEE);
    for (int trial=0;trial<180;++trial) {
        std::vector<int32_t> ids;
        for (int i=0;i<1+trial%160;++i) ids.push_back(int32_t(rng()%n_rows));
        require(stage.stage(ids.data(),ids.size()),"random stage failure");
        check_stage(stage,ids);
    }
}
}

int main() {
    try {
        exact_raw_stage_and_cache();
        reject_invalid_without_dirtying_previous_buffer();
        eligibility_and_overflow();
        random_row_byte_parity();
        std::cout << "{\"status\":\"PASS\",\"scenarios\":4,\"gpu\":false,\"model\":false}\n";
        return 0;
    } catch (const std::exception & e) {
        std::cerr << "FAIL " << e.what() << "\n";
        return 1;
    }
}

===== SOURCE FILE tests_cpp/engram_model_cache_fixture.cpp =====
#include "../modules/engram/graph_stage.hpp"

#include <array>
#include <atomic>
#include <chrono>
#include <condition_variable>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <memory>
#include <mutex>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <thread>
#include <vector>

namespace {
constexpr size_t rows = 2048;
constexpr size_t payload = 210;
constexpr size_t stride = 224;
using llama_exp::engram_graph_stage;
using llama_exp::engram_model_cache;
using llama_exp::mapped_rows;
using llama_exp::engram_row_cache;

void ck(bool v, const char * why) { if (!v) throw std::runtime_error(why); }
uint8_t byte_for(int32_t row, size_t offset) {
    return uint8_t((uint64_t(row)*31 + offset*13 + ((row ^ int(offset))*7)) & 255);
}
std::vector<uint8_t> mapping() {
    std::vector<uint8_t> data(rows * stride, 0xCA);
    for (size_t id = 0; id < rows; ++id) {
        for (size_t b = 0; b < payload; ++b) data[id*stride+b] = byte_for(int32_t(id),b);
    }
    return data;
}
void parity(const engram_graph_stage & stage, const std::vector<int32_t> & ids) {
    ck(stage.bytes() == ids.size()*payload, "wrong raw stage length");
    for (size_t i=0; i<ids.size(); ++i) {
        for(size_t b=0;b<payload;++b) {
            ck(stage.data()[i*payload+b]==byte_for(ids[i],b), "raw byte mismatch");
        }
    }
}
void reuse_between_graphs() {
    auto raw = mapping();
    const auto saved = raw;
    mapped_rows view(raw.data(), raw.size(), stride, payload);
    auto model = std::make_shared<engram_model_cache>(view, rows, 12*payload, 16);
    ck(model->matches(view, rows), "same source rejected");
    ck(!model->matches(view, rows-1), "different row count accepted");
    auto first = std::make_unique<engram_graph_stage>(model);
    auto second = std::make_unique<engram_graph_stage>(model);
    const std::vector<int32_t> ids{12,4,12,2000,9,4,17,19};
    ck(first->stage(ids.data(),ids.size()), "first graph failed");
    parity(*first, ids);
    const auto first_count = model->counters();
    ck(second->stage(ids.data(),ids.size()), "second graph failed");
    parity(*second, ids);
    const auto second_count = model->counters();
    ck(second_count.hit >= first_count.hit + ids.size(), "graphs did not share cache");
    ck(second_count.resident_data_bytes <= 12*payload, "cache exceeded budget");
    ck(raw == saved, "borrowed source mutated");
    first.reset();
    second.reset();
    ck(!model->counters().stopped, "graph destructor incorrectly closed model cache");
    model->close();
    ck(model->counters().stopped && model->counters().resident_rows == 0, "model close did not release payload");
}
void async_and_duplicates() {
    auto raw = mapping();
    auto model = std::make_shared<engram_model_cache>(mapped_rows(raw.data(), raw.size(), stride, payload),
                                                     rows, 64*payload, 32);
    engram_graph_stage stage(model);
    std::vector<int32_t> ids(512);
    for(int i=0;i<512;++i) ids[i] = (i/2)%rows;
    ids[4] = -1;
    ids[6] = INT32_MAX;
    const size_t queued = stage.prefetch(ids.data(),ids.size());
    ck(queued > 0 && queued <= 512, "async queue not bounded");
    std::vector<int32_t> valid{4,5,6,7,8,9,10,11,12};
    ck(stage.stage(valid.data(),valid.size()), "foreground failed under async contention");
    parity(stage,valid);
    const auto snap = stage.counters();
    ck(snap.queued == queued, "prefetch accounting inaccurate");
    ck(snap.resident_data_bytes<=64*payload, "async limit exceeded");
    ck(snap.queued_or_inflight<=33, "async queue overflowed");
    const std::vector<int32_t> invalid{12,-1,13};
    ck(!stage.stage(invalid.data(),invalid.size()), "stage accepted invalid row ID");
    model->close();
    ck(!stage.stage(valid.data(), valid.size()), "closed cache served stale model rows");
    ck(stage.prefetch(valid.data(),valid.size())==0, "prefetch allowed after close");
}
void close_while_worker_reads() {
    // Explicit mapping lifetime contract: never unmap while a callback is active.
    auto raw=mapping();
    std::mutex mu;
    std::condition_variable cv;
    bool started=false, release=false;
    std::atomic<int> callbacks{0};
    // The row cache is the I/O owner used by the model cache, with controllable
    // blocked source read for deterministic model-unload drain verification.
    engram_row_cache cache([&](uint64_t id, uint8_t * dest, size_t n) {
        callbacks.fetch_add(1);
        {
            std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lk(mu);
            started=true; cv.notify_all();
            cv.wait(lk,[&] {return release;});
        }
        std::memcpy(dest,raw.data()+id*stride,n);
        return true;
    },rows,payload,4*payload,4);
    const uint64_t targets[3] = {12,13,14};
    ck(cache.prefetch(targets,3)==3, "worker did not accept task");
    {
        std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lk(mu);
        cv.wait(lk,[&] {return started;});
    }
    std::atomic<bool> closed{false};
    std::thread unload([&] {cache.close();closed=true;});
    bool saw_stop=false;
    for(int t=0;t<2000;++t) {
        if(cache.snapshot().stopped) {saw_stop=true;break;}
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(1));
    }
    ck(saw_stop, "model close not signaled");
    ck(!closed, "model closed before mapped reader finished");
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(mu); release=true;
    }
    cv.notify_all(); unload.join();
    ck(closed && callbacks == 1, "unload failed to drain worker");
    raw.clear(); raw.shrink_to_fit(); // only after worker has terminated
    ck(cache.snapshot().stopped && cache.snapshot().resident_data_bytes==0, "cache retained payload after close");
}
void multiple_simultaneous_graphs() {
    auto raw=mapping();
    auto model=std::make_shared<engram_model_cache>(mapped_rows(raw.data(),raw.size(),stride,payload),
                                                    rows,128*payload,48);
    std::atomic<int> failures{0};
    std::vector<std::thread> threads;
    for(int t=0;t<6;++t) threads.emplace_back([&,t] {
        engram_graph_stage graph(model);
        std::vector<int32_t> ids(17);
        for(int r=0;r<140;++r) {
            for(int j=0;j<17;++j) ids[j]=int32_t((t*37+r*71+j*13)%rows);
            graph.prefetch(ids.data(),ids.size());
            if(!graph.stage(ids.data(),ids.size())){++failures;continue;}
            for(size_t i=0;i<ids.size();++i) for(size_t b=0;b<payload;++b)
                if(graph.data()[i*payload+b]!=byte_for(ids[i],b)) ++failures;
        }
    });
    for(auto &t:threads)t.join();
    ck(failures==0, "concurrent graph output corrupted");
    ck(model->counters().resident_data_bytes<=128*payload,"concurrent cache payload overflowed");
    model->close();
}
} // namespace
int main() {
    try {
        reuse_between_graphs();
        async_and_duplicates();
        close_while_worker_reads();
        multiple_simultaneous_graphs();
        std::cout << "{\"status\":\"PASS\",\"scenarios\":4,\"gpu\":false,\"model\":false}\n";
        return 0;
    } catch(const std::exception & e) {
        std::cerr << "FAIL " << e.what() << "\n";
        return 1;
    }
}

===== SOURCE FILE tests_cpp/engram_row_cache_fixture.cpp =====
#include "../modules/engram/row_cache.hpp"
#include "../modules/engram/mapped_rows.hpp"
#include <atomic>
#include <array>
#include <cassert>
#include <condition_variable>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <mutex>
#include <random>
#include <chrono>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <thread>
#include <vector>
using llama_exp::engram_row_cache;

static void check(bool v, const char * reason) {
    if (!v) throw std::runtime_error(reason);
}
static constexpr size_t N = 8192;
static constexpr size_t W = 256;
static uint8_t correct(engram_row_cache::row_id row, size_t b) {
    return uint8_t((row*31 + b*17 + b*b) & 255);
}
static std::vector<uint8_t> reference() {
    std::vector<uint8_t> x(N*W);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) for (size_t j = 0; j < W; ++j) x[i*W+j] = correct(i,j);
    return x;
}
static void check_row(const uint8_t * data, size_t row) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < W; ++i) check(data[i] == correct(row,i), "incorrect quantized row bytes");
}
static void geometry_and_sync() {
    const auto source = reference();
    auto reader = [&](uint64_t id, uint8_t * dst, size_t n) {
        check(n == W && id < N, "bad reader invocation");
        std::memcpy(dst, source.data() + id*W, W);
        return true;
    };
    for (const auto budget : {size_t(0),size_t(W-1),size_t(65)*1024*1024}) {
        bool rejected = false;
        try { engram_row_cache cache(reader,N,W,budget,0); } catch (const std::invalid_argument &) { rejected = true; }
        check(rejected, "budget should be rejected");
    }
    engram_row_cache cache(reader,N,W,8*W,0);
    std::array<uint8_t,W> dest{};
    check(cache.get(3,dest.data(),W) == engram_row_cache::read_result::source_read, "first miss");
    check_row(dest.data(),3);
    check(cache.get(3,dest.data(),W) == engram_row_cache::read_result::cache_hit, "cached row hit");
    check(cache.get(3,nullptr,W) == engram_row_cache::read_result::invalid_output, "null destination accepted");
    check(cache.get(N,dest.data(),W) == engram_row_cache::read_result::invalid_row, "bad row accepted");
    check(cache.get(2,dest.data(),W-1) == engram_row_cache::read_result::invalid_output, "short destination accepted");
    for (uint64_t i = 0; i < 512; ++i) {
        check(cache.get(i,dest.data(),W) != engram_row_cache::read_result::io_error, "sync I/O error");
        check_row(dest.data(),i);
        check(cache.snapshot().resident_data_bytes <= 8*W, "cache budget overflow");
    }
    check(cache.snapshot().evicted > 0, "no evictions");
    cache.close(); cache.close();
    check(cache.snapshot().resident_rows == 0 && cache.snapshot().stopped, "close did not free payload");
    check(cache.get(3,dest.data(),W) == engram_row_cache::read_result::closed, "read after close");
    check(cache.prefetch(nullptr,0) == 0, "unexpected queue fill");
}
static void async_and_eviction() {
    const auto source = reference();
    std::atomic<size_t> reads{0};
    auto reader = [&](uint64_t id, uint8_t * dst, size_t n) {
        check(n == W && id < N, "bad reader input");
        std::memcpy(dst, source.data()+id*W, W);
        ++reads;
        return true;
    };
    engram_row_cache cache(reader,N,W,16*W,64);
    for (uint64_t round = 0; round < 36; ++round) {
        std::vector<uint64_t> ids;
        for (uint64_t i=0;i<12;++i) ids.push_back((round*73+i*3)%N);
        cache.prefetch(ids.data(),ids.size());
        std::array<uint8_t,W> buf{};
        for (auto id : ids) {
            const auto status = cache.get(id,buf.data(),W,std::chrono::milliseconds(10));
            check(status == engram_row_cache::read_result::cache_hit ||
                  status == engram_row_cache::read_result::source_read, "row unavailable");
            check_row(buf.data(),id);
        }
        check(cache.snapshot().resident_data_bytes<=16*W, "async cache byte limit exceeded");
    }
    const auto before = cache.snapshot();
    check(before.background_reads>0, "worker never executed");
    check(before.synchronous_reads + before.hit > 0, "foreground path unused");
    cache.close();
    const auto after = cache.snapshot();
    check(after.resident_rows == 0 && after.queued_or_inflight == 0, "close leaked rows");
    check(reads.load() == after.background_reads+after.synchronous_reads,
          "unsynchronized callback read counters");
}
static void failure_isolation() {
    const auto source = reference();
    std::atomic<int> fail{1};
    auto reader = [&](uint64_t id, uint8_t * dst, size_t) {
        if (id == 37 && fail.exchange(0) == 1) throw std::runtime_error("fake EIO");
        std::memcpy(dst,source.data()+id*W,W);
        return true;
    };
    engram_row_cache cache(reader,N,W,W*4,0);
    std::array<uint8_t,W> dest{};
    check(cache.get(37,dest.data(),W) == engram_row_cache::read_result::io_error, "EIO not propagated");
    check(cache.snapshot().resident_rows == 0, "partial row cached");
    check(cache.get(37,dest.data(),W) == engram_row_cache::read_result::source_read, "retry failed");
    check_row(dest.data(),37);
    cache.close();
}
static void close_during_blocked_io() {
    std::mutex m;
    std::condition_variable cv;
    bool started = false, release = false;
    std::atomic<int> calls{0};
    auto reader = [&](uint64_t id, uint8_t * dst, size_t len) {
        check(len == W, "wrong byte count");
        ++calls;
        {
            std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(m);
            started = true;
            cv.notify_all();
            cv.wait(lock,[&] { return release; });
        }
        for(size_t i=0;i<len;++i) dst[i] = correct(id,i);
        return true;
    };
    engram_row_cache cache(reader,N,W,4*W,4);
    std::array<uint64_t,5> ids{3,3,4,5,6};
    check(cache.prefetch(ids.data(),ids.size()) == 4, "deduplication/queue bound failed");
    {
        std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(m);
        cv.wait(lock, [&] { return started; });
    }
    // These readers can wait on the prefetch CV while the source callback is
    // blocked. close() must wake them and make each return closed.
    constexpr size_t waiter_count = 8;
    std::atomic<size_t> entering{0}, cancelled{0};
    std::vector<std::thread> waiters;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < waiter_count; ++i) {
        waiters.emplace_back([&] {
            std::array<uint8_t, W> dest{};
            ++entering;
            if (cache.get(ids[0], dest.data(), W, std::chrono::seconds(30)) ==
                    engram_row_cache::read_result::closed) ++cancelled;
        });
    }
    while (entering.load() != waiter_count) std::this_thread::yield();
    std::atomic<bool> closed{false};
    std::thread closer([&] { cache.close(); closed.store(true); });
    // Wait until the close thread has published stopping, then unblock the
    // already running callback. The queued IDs must not start new reads.
    bool saw_stopping = false;
    for (int spin=0; spin<50000; ++spin) {
        if (cache.snapshot().stopped) { saw_stopping = true; break; }
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::microseconds(20));
    }
    // The callback is ALWAYS released before failing a test, otherwise stack
    // unwinding would hang while close() correctly waits for a blocked reader.
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(m);
        release = true;
    }
    cv.notify_all();
    closer.join();
    for (auto & waiter : waiters) waiter.join();
    check(cancelled.load() == waiter_count, "close failed to wake waiting reads");
    check(saw_stopping, "close did not begin");
    check(closed.load() && calls.load() == 1, "close failed to drain exactly one inflight callback");
    check(cache.snapshot().resident_rows == 0, "cache retained bytes after close");
}
static void mapped_strided_rows() {
    // Q6_K payload may be smaller than mmap's configured row stride; preserve
    // the exact payload, never read padding into the cache.
    constexpr size_t ROWS=64, PAYLOAD=210, STRIDE=256;
    std::vector<uint8_t> mapping(ROWS*STRIDE,0xCC);
    for(size_t row=0;row<ROWS;++row) {
        for(size_t b=0;b<PAYLOAD;++b) mapping[row*STRIDE+b]=uint8_t((row*37+b*13)&255);
    }
    llama_exp::mapped_rows reader(mapping.data(),mapping.size(),STRIDE,PAYLOAD);
    engram_row_cache cache(reader,ROWS,PAYLOAD,7*PAYLOAD,16);
    std::array<uint8_t,PAYLOAD> dst{};
    for(size_t row=0;row<ROWS;++row) {
        auto result=cache.get(row,dst.data(),dst.size());
        check(result==engram_row_cache::read_result::cache_hit ||
              result==engram_row_cache::read_result::source_read,"strided row unavailable");
        for(size_t b=0;b<PAYLOAD;++b) check(dst[b]==uint8_t((row*37+b*13)&255),"strided payload mismatch");
    }
    check(!reader(ROWS,dst.data(),PAYLOAD),"out-of-mapping read accepted");
    check(!reader(UINT64_MAX,dst.data(),PAYLOAD),"overflowed row accepted");
    check(!reader(1,dst.data(),PAYLOAD-1),"incorrect byte width accepted");
    cache.close();
    bool bad=false;
    try { llama_exp::mapped_rows invalid(mapping.data(),mapping.size(),16,210); }
    catch(const std::invalid_argument &) { bad=true; }
    check(bad,"invalid GGUF stride accepted");
}
static void multithreaded_gets() {
    const auto source=reference();
    std::atomic<int> inside{0}, simultaneous{0};
    auto reader=[&](uint64_t id,uint8_t * out,size_t n) {
        if (++inside>1) ++simultaneous;
        std::memcpy(out,source.data()+id*W,n);
        --inside;
        return true;
    };
    engram_row_cache cache(reader,N,W,24*W,128);
    std::vector<std::thread> workers;
    for(int thread=0;thread<6;++thread) workers.emplace_back([&,thread] {
        std::mt19937 rng(31337u+thread);
        std::array<uint8_t,W> dest{};
        for(int i=0;i<1200;++i) {
            const uint64_t id=uint64_t(rng()%256);
            const auto status=cache.get(id,dest.data(),W);
            check(status == engram_row_cache::read_result::cache_hit ||
                  status == engram_row_cache::read_result::source_read, "foreground failure");
            check_row(dest.data(),id);
        }
    });
    for(auto & t:workers)t.join();
    check(simultaneous.load()==0,"reader callback invoked concurrently");
    check(cache.snapshot().resident_data_bytes<=24*W,"concurrent cache overflow");
    cache.close();
}
int main() {
    try {
        geometry_and_sync();
        async_and_eviction();
        failure_isolation();
        close_during_blocked_io();
        multithreaded_gets();
        mapped_strided_rows();
        std::cout << "{\"status\":\"PASS\",\"tests\":6,\"gpu_executed\":false,\"model_executed\":false}\n";
        return 0;
    } catch(const std::exception & exc) {
        std::cerr<<"FAIL: "<<exc.what()<<'\n';
        return 1;
    }
}

#include "llama-experimental/graph_stage.hpp"

// Host-boundary fixtures only. The generated file inserts the actual baseline
// and candidate graph-input classes and methods, not a rewritten hash algorithm.
static bool count_allocations = false;
static size_t allocations = 0;
void * operator new(size_t n) {
    if (count_allocations) { ++allocations; }
    if (void * p = std::malloc(n ? n : 1)) { return p; }
    throw std::bad_alloc();
}
void operator delete(void * p) noexcept { std::free(p); }
void operator delete(void * p, size_t) noexcept { std::free(p); }
void * operator new[](size_t n) { return ::operator new(n); }
void operator delete[](void * p) noexcept { ::operator delete(p); }
void operator delete[](void * p, size_t) noexcept { ::operator delete(p); }

#define GGML_ASSERT(x) do { if (!(x)) { throw std::runtime_error(#x); } } while (0)
using llama_token = int32_t;
static constexpr llama_token LLAMA_TOKEN_NULL = -1;
static constexpr int64_t DSV41_ENGRAM_DEAD = -1;

struct ggml_tensor {
    int id = 0;
    void * buffer = reinterpret_cast<void *>(1);
    int64_t ne[4] = {1, 1, 1, 1};
};
struct write_event {
    int id;
    size_t offset;
    std::vector<uint8_t> bytes;
    bool operator==(const write_event & b) const {
        return id == b.id && offset == b.offset && bytes == b.bytes;
    }
};
struct observation {
    std::vector<write_event> writes;
    std::vector<std::vector<int32_t>> prefetches;
    bool operator==(const observation & b) const {
        return writes == b.writes && prefetches == b.prefetches;
    }
};
static observation captured;
static bool capture = true;
static volatile uint64_t consumed_bytes = 0;
static size_t ggml_nbytes(const ggml_tensor * t) {
    return size_t(t->ne[0])*size_t(t->ne[1])*sizeof(int32_t);
}
static size_t ggml_element_size(const ggml_tensor *) { return sizeof(int32_t); }
static void ggml_backend_tensor_set(ggml_tensor * t, const void * data, size_t off, size_t n) {
    if (capture) {
        write_event event{t->id, off, std::vector<uint8_t>(n)};
        if (n) { std::memcpy(event.bytes.data(), data, n); }
        captured.writes.push_back(std::move(event));
    } else if (n) {
        consumed_bytes += static_cast<const uint8_t *>(data)[n - 1] + n;
    }
}
static void llama_prefetch_rows(ggml_tensor *, const int32_t * rows, size_t n) {
    if (capture) { captured.prefetches.emplace_back(rows, rows + n); }
    else { consumed_bytes += n; }
}

struct llama_hparams {
    uint32_t ple_ngram_size = 2;
    uint32_t ple_heads_per_ngram = 1;
    uint32_t ple_n_heads = 1;
    uint32_t ple_image_token_id = 0;
    uint32_t ple_eos_token_id = 7;
    uint32_t engram_pad_id = 0;
    int64_t dsv41_cand_block_size = 4;
    std::array<uint64_t, 8> ple_layer_multipliers{};
    std::array<uint32_t, 64> ple_head_offsets{};
    std::array<uint32_t, 64> ple_head_vocab_sizes{};
};
struct llama_ubatch {
    uint32_t n_tokens = 0;
    llama_token * token = nullptr;
    float * embd = nullptr;
    uint8_t * type = nullptr;
    int32_t * n_seq_id = nullptr;
    int64_t * pos = nullptr;
    bool mixed = false;
    bool is_mixed() const { return mixed; }
};
struct llama_kv_cache_context {
    std::vector<llama_token> previous;
    void get_prev_tokens(const llama_ubatch & batch, uint32_t n, std::vector<llama_token> & out) const {
        GGML_ASSERT(previous.size() == size_t(batch.n_tokens)*n);
        out.assign(previous.begin(), previous.end());
    }
};
struct llama_memory_hybrid_idx_context {
    llama_kv_cache_context * attn;
    const llama_kv_cache_context * get_attn() const { return attn; }
};
struct llama_kv_cache_dsv4_context {
    struct comp_plan { std::vector<int64_t> n_visible; int64_t n_kv = 0; } plan;
    llama_kv_cache_context raw;
    const llama_kv_cache_context * get_raw() const { return &raw; }
    const comp_plan & get_csa_plan(const llama_ubatch &) const { return plan; }
    const comp_plan & get_hca_plan(const llama_ubatch &) const { return plan; }
};
struct llm_graph_params { const void * mctx; llama_ubatch ubatch; };
struct llm_graph_input_i {
    virtual ~llm_graph_input_i() = default;
    virtual void set_input(const llama_ubatch *) = 0;
    virtual bool can_reuse(const llm_graph_params &) = 0;
};
struct llama_model {
    llama_hparams hparams;
    ggml_tensor * per_layer_tok_embd = nullptr;
    std::set<ggml_tensor *> can_prefetch;
};
struct llama_model_deepseek41 : llama_model {
    std::vector<int64_t> engram_token_map;
    void engram_hash(int, const llama_ubatch &, const std::vector<llama_token> &, std::vector<int32_t> &) const;
};
