static std::mt19937_64 rng(0x6b38d941);
static size_t comparisons = 0;
static void require(bool value, const char * message) {
    if (!value) { throw std::runtime_error(message); }
}
static void metadata(llama_hparams & h, uint32_t ng, uint32_t per, uint32_t modules) {
    h.ple_ngram_size = ng; h.ple_heads_per_ngram = per; h.ple_n_heads = (ng - 1)*per;
    require(ng*modules <= h.ple_layer_multipliers.size(), "multiplier bound");
    require(h.ple_n_heads*modules <= h.ple_head_offsets.size(), "head bound");
    for (auto & x : h.ple_layer_multipliers) { x = rng(); }
    // Include overflow in uint64 multiplication, nonzero offsets, and moduli
    // near the metadata bounds. Their primality is irrelevant to equivalence.
    for (size_t i = 0; i < h.ple_head_vocab_sizes.size(); ++i) {
        h.ple_head_vocab_sizes[i] = i % 3 == 0 ? 2147483000u : uint32_t(2 + rng()%100003);
        h.ple_head_offsets[i] = uint32_t(rng()%100);
    }
    h.ple_image_token_id = rng()%2 ? 91 : 0;
}
struct input_data {
    std::vector<llama_token> tokens, previous;
    std::vector<uint8_t> types;
    std::vector<int32_t> seqs;
    std::vector<int64_t> positions;
    float embedding = 0;
    llama_ubatch batch;
    input_data(uint32_t n, uint32_t ng, unsigned mode) : tokens(n), previous(size_t(n)*(ng-1)),
        types(n), seqs(n, 1), positions(n) {
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            tokens[i] = int32_t(rng()%145)-5;
            types[i] = rng()%3 == 0;
            positions[i] = i < ng ? i : int64_t(rng()%800);
        }
        for (auto & x : previous) { x = int32_t(rng()%145)-5; }
        batch = {n, mode == 1 ? nullptr : tokens.data(), mode ? &embedding : nullptr,
                 types.data(), seqs.data(), positions.data(), mode == 2};
    }
};
template<class A, class B> static void compare(A & a, B & b, const llama_ubatch & batch) {
    captured = {}; a.set_input(&batch); observation expected = std::move(captured);
    captured = {}; b.set_input(&batch); observation actual = std::move(captured);
    require(expected == actual, "host output or prefetch ordering mismatch"); ++comparisons;
}
template<class T> static size_t count_run(T & input, const llama_ubatch & batch) {
    capture = false; input.set_input(&batch); // capacity warmup is not counted
    allocations = 0; count_allocations = true;
    for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i) { input.set_input(&batch); }
    count_allocations = false; capture = true; return allocations;
}
static void qwen_case(uint32_t ng, uint32_t per, uint32_t n, unsigned mode, bool prefetch, const char * flag) {
    if (flag) { setenv("LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_HASH_SCRATCH", flag, 1); }
    else { unsetenv("LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_HASH_SCRATCH"); }
    llama_model model; metadata(model.hparams, ng, per, 1);
    ggml_tensor table{90}, rows{1}; model.per_layer_tok_embd = &table;
    if (prefetch) { model.can_prefetch.insert(&table); }
    input_data data(n, ng, mode); llama_kv_cache_context ctx; ctx.previous = data.previous;
    rows.ne[0] = int64_t(model.hparams.ple_n_heads)*n;
    baseline::llm_graph_input_qwen4exp_ple a(model, &ctx);
    candidate::llm_graph_input_qwen4exp_ple b(model, &ctx); a.rows = b.rows = &rows;
    for (int round = 0; round < 3; ++round) {
        compare(a, b, data.batch);
        for (auto & x : ctx.previous) { x = int32_t(rng()%145)-5; }
    }
    llama_memory_hybrid_idx_context memory{&ctx}; llm_graph_params params{&memory, data.batch};
    require(a.can_reuse(params) == b.can_reuse(params), "Qwen graph reuse mismatch");
    if (flag && std::string(flag) == "1" && n == 128 && ng == 4 && per == 2 && mode == 0) {
        size_t old_count = count_run(a, data.batch), new_count = count_run(b, data.batch);
        require(new_count < old_count, "Qwen scratch did not remove allocations after warmup");
        std::cout << "Qwen allocation events / 16 calls: baseline=" << old_count << " candidate=" << new_count << '\n';
    }
}
static void deep_case(uint32_t ng, uint32_t per, uint32_t modules, uint32_t n, unsigned mode,
                      bool prefetch, const char * flag, bool omit_buffer = false) {
    if (flag) { setenv("LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_HASH_SCRATCH", flag, 1); }
    else { unsetenv("LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_HASH_SCRATCH"); }
    llama_model_deepseek41 model; metadata(model.hparams, ng, per, modules);
    model.engram_token_map.resize(128);
    for (auto & x : model.engram_token_map) { x = rng()%7 ? int64_t(rng()%200000) : -1; }
    model.engram_token_map[0] = 13;
    ggml_tensor table{90}, keep{80}, pin{81}; model.per_layer_tok_embd = &table;
    if (prefetch) { model.can_prefetch.insert(&table); }
    input_data data(n, ng, mode); llama_kv_cache_dsv4_context ctx; ctx.raw.previous = data.previous;
    ctx.plan.n_kv = 7; ctx.plan.n_visible.resize(n);
    for (auto & x : ctx.plan.n_visible) { x = int64_t(rng()%8); }
    keep.ne[2] = n; pin.ne[0] = 2; pin.ne[1] = n;
    std::vector<ggml_tensor> tensors(modules);
    baseline::llm_graph_input_dsv41 a(model, &ctx); candidate::llm_graph_input_dsv41 b(model, &ctx);
    for (uint32_t e = 0; e < modules; ++e) {
        tensors[e].id = int(e+1); tensors[e].ne[0] = int64_t(model.hparams.ple_n_heads)*n;
        if (omit_buffer && e % 2 == 0) { tensors[e].buffer = nullptr; }
        a.engram_ids.push_back(&tensors[e]); b.engram_ids.push_back(&tensors[e]);
    }
    a.engram_keep = b.engram_keep = mode ? &keep : nullptr;
    a.cand_pin = b.cand_pin = &pin; a.cand_csa = b.cand_csa = rng()%2;
    for (int round = 0; round < 3; ++round) {
        compare(a, b, data.batch);
        for (auto & x : ctx.raw.previous) { x = int32_t(rng()%145)-5; }
    }
    llm_graph_params params{&ctx, data.batch};
    require(a.can_reuse(params) == b.can_reuse(params), "DeepSeek graph reuse mismatch");
    if (flag && std::string(flag) == "1" && ng == 4 && per == 2 && modules == 2 && n == 128 && mode == 0) {
        size_t old_count = count_run(a, data.batch), new_count = count_run(b, data.batch);
        require(new_count < old_count, "DeepSeek scratch did not remove allocations after warmup");
        std::cout << "DeepSeek allocation events / 16 calls: baseline=" << old_count << " candidate=" << new_count << '\n';
    }
}
int main() {
    try {
        for (const char * flag : {static_cast<const char *>(nullptr), "0", "1", "01", "true", ""}) {
            for (unsigned mode = 0; mode < 3; ++mode) {
                for (bool prefetch : {false, true}) {
                    for (uint32_t n : {1u, 7u, 128u}) {
                        qwen_case(4, 2, n, mode, prefetch, flag);
                        deep_case(4, 2, 2, n, mode, prefetch, flag, n == 7);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        for (uint32_t ng = 2; ng <= 8; ++ng) {
            qwen_case(ng, 1, 29, 2, true, "1");
            qwen_case(ng, 8, 29, 1, false, "1");
            deep_case(ng, 1, 1, 29, 2, true, "1");
        }
        // Exercise the large-input fallback above the 256K-value retention cap.
        qwen_case(4, 2, 44000, 2, true, "1");
        deep_case(4, 2, 2, 22000, 2, true, "1");
        std::cout << "PASS exact host output comparisons=" << comparisons << '\n';
        std::cout << "Scope: real extracted hash/input methods with host boundary fixtures; no GPU, NVMe, or model inference.\n";
        return 0;
    } catch (const std::exception & e) {
        count_allocations = false;
        std::cerr << "FAIL " << e.what() << '\n'; return 1;
    }
}

===== SOURCE FILE tests_cpp/hc_post_reference_original.hpp =====
#pragma once
// Verbatim reference CUDA kernel from pinned upstream dde879bc4f954b809892fd0d660515f42536394a,
// ggml/src/ggml-cuda/dsv4-hc.cu (lines 148-203). Used only as host parity oracle.
template <bool has_comb, bool gated>
static __global__ void dsv4_hc_post_f32(
        const float * x,
        const float * residual,
        const float * post,
        const float * comb,
        float * dst,
        int64_t n_embd,
        int64_t hc,
        int64_t n_tokens,
        int64_t sx0,
        int64_t sx1,
        int64_t sr0,
        int64_t sr1,
        int64_t sr2,
        int64_t sp0,
        int64_t sp1,
        int64_t sc0,
        int64_t sc1,
        int64_t sc2,
        int64_t sd0,
        int64_t sd1,
        int64_t sd2,
        float gate_scale_in,
        float gate_scale_out) {
    ggml_cuda_pdl_lc();
    const int64_t ir = (int64_t) blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    const int64_t nr = n_embd * hc * n_tokens;

    if (ir >= nr) {
        return;
    }

    ggml_cuda_pdl_sync();

    const int64_t i0   = ir % n_embd;
    const int64_t idst = (ir / n_embd) % hc;
    const int64_t it   = ir / (n_embd * hc);

    float p = post[idst*sp0 + it*sp1];
    if constexpr (gated) {
        p = gate_scale_out * (1.0f / (1.0f + expf(-(gate_scale_in * p))));
    }

    float sum = x[i0*sx0 + it*sx1] * p;
    if constexpr (has_comb) {
        for (int64_t isrc = 0; isrc < hc; ++isrc) {
            sum += residual[i0*sr0 + isrc*sr1 + it*sr2] * comb[idst*sc0 + isrc*sc1 + it*sc2];
        }
    } else {
        sum += residual[i0*sr0 + idst*sr1 + it*sr2];
    }

    dst[i0*sd0 + idst*sd1 + it*sd2] = sum;
}

===== SOURCE FILE tests_cpp/hc_post_reuse_fixture.cpp =====
// P15A: compile upstream and alternative CUDA kernel bodies as host functions.
// This tests indexing/arithmetic/predicate, NOT NVCC, GPU scheduling or speed.
#include <algorithm>
#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <random>
#include <vector>

struct fake_dim3 { int64_t x = 0; };
static fake_dim3 blockIdx, threadIdx, blockDim;
#define __global__
#define ggml_cuda_pdl_lc() ((void) 0)
#define ggml_cuda_pdl_sync() ((void) 0)
#include "hc_post_reference_original.hpp"
#include "../modules/cuda/hc_post_reuse.cuh"

struct layout {
    int64_t ne, nt, sx0, sx1, sr0, sr1, sr2, sp0, sp1, sc0, sc1, sc2, sd0, sd1, sd2;
};

static void old_kernel(const layout & l, const float * x, const float * r, const float * p,
        const float * c, float * dst) {
    blockDim.x = 256;
    const int64_t nr = l.ne*4*l.nt;
    for (int64_t i=0; i<nr + 256; ++i) {
        blockIdx.x = i / 256;
        threadIdx.x = i % 256;
        dsv4_hc_post_f32<true, false>(x, r, p, c, dst,
                l.ne, 4, l.nt, l.sx0, l.sx1, l.sr0, l.sr1, l.sr2,
                l.sp0, l.sp1, l.sc0, l.sc1, l.sc2,
                l.sd0, l.sd1, l.sd2, 1.0f, 1.0f);
    }
}

static void new_kernel(const layout & l, const float * x, const float * r, const float * p,
        const float * c, float * dst) {
    blockDim.x = 256;
    const int64_t nr = l.ne*l.nt;
    for (int64_t i=0; i<nr + 256; ++i) {
        blockIdx.x = i / 256;
        threadIdx.x = i % 256;
        dsv4_hc_post_reuse_f32(x, r, p, c, dst,
                l.ne, l.nt, l.sx0, l.sx1, l.sr0, l.sr1, l.sr2,
                l.sp0, l.sp1, l.sc0, l.sc1, l.sc2,
                l.sd0, l.sd1, l.sd2);
    }
}

static void fill(std::vector<float> & v, std::mt19937 & rng) {
    std::uniform_real_distribution<float> distribution(-2.0f, 2.0f);
    for (float & f : v) f = distribution(rng);
}

static void check_case(int64_t ne, int64_t nt, bool strided, std::mt19937 & rng) {
    const int64_t a = strided ? 2 : 1;
    const int64_t b = strided ? 3 : 1;
    layout l{};
    l.ne=ne; l.nt=nt;
    l.sx0=a; l.sx1=ne*a + (strided ? 7 : 0);
    l.sr0=a; l.sr1=ne*a + (strided ? 3 : 0); l.sr2=4*l.sr1 + (strided ? 11 : 0);
    l.sp0=b; l.sp1=4*b + (strided ? 7 : 0);
    l.sc0=b; l.sc1=4*b + (strided ? 5 : 0); l.sc2=4*l.sc1 + (strided ? 9 : 0);
    l.sd0=a; l.sd1=ne*a + (strided ? 5 : 0); l.sd2=4*l.sd1 + (strided ? 13 : 0);
    std::vector<float> x((nt-1)*l.sx1 + (ne-1)*l.sx0 + 7);
    std::vector<float> r((nt-1)*l.sr2 + 3*l.sr1 + (ne-1)*l.sr0 + 7);
    std::vector<float> p((nt-1)*l.sp1 + 3*l.sp0 + 7);
    std::vector<float> c((nt-1)*l.sc2 + 3*l.sc1 + 3*l.sc0 + 7);
    const size_t out_size=(nt-1)*l.sd2 + 3*l.sd1 + (ne-1)*l.sd0 + 7;
    std::vector<float> baseline(out_size, 13.5f), candidate(out_size, 13.5f);
    fill(x,rng);fill(r,rng);fill(p,rng);fill(c,rng);
    old_kernel(l,x.data(),r.data(),p.data(),c.data(),baseline.data());
    new_kernel(l,x.data(),r.data(),p.data(),c.data(),candidate.data());
    if (std::memcmp(baseline.data(),candidate.data(),out_size*sizeof(float)) != 0) {
        for (size_t i=0;i<out_size;++i) if (baseline[i]!=candidate[i]) {
            fprintf(stderr,"P15 parity mismatch (ne=%lld,nt=%lld,stride=%d,index=%zu) legacy=%g candidate=%g\n",
                (long long)ne,(long long)nt,(int)strided,i,baseline[i],candidate[i]);
            std::abort();
        }
        std::abort();
    }
}

int main() {
    const auto admits = [](int64_t ne,int64_t hc,int64_t nt,bool comb,bool gate) {
        return llama_exp_dsv4_hc_post_reuse_eligible(ne,hc,nt,comb,gate);
    };
    assert(admits(1024,4,64,true,false));
    assert(admits(8192,4,8,true,false));
    assert(!admits(512,4,8,true,false));
    assert(!admits(8192,4,1,true,false));
    assert(!admits(1024,3,128,true,false));
    assert(!admits(1024,4,128,false,false));
    assert(!admits(1024,4,128,true,true));
    assert(!admits(0,4,128,true,false));
    assert(!admits(1024,4,-1,true,false));
    assert(!admits(std::numeric_limits<int64_t>::max(),4,8,true,false));
    std::mt19937 rng(0xD5A41u);
    int count=0;
    for (auto ne : {1,15,255,256,512,1024,2048}) {
        for (auto nt : {1,2,8,17,64}) {
            if (ne*nt>65536) continue;
            for (bool strided : {false,true}) {
                check_case(ne,nt,strided,rng);
                count++;
            }
        }
    }
    printf("P15A host arithmetic parity: %d shapes/layouts PASS\n",count);
}

===== SOURCE FILE tests_cpp/mmq_select_probe_fixture.cpp =====
// P15C host fixture. No nvcc/GPU; output is JSONL on stderr only if opt-in.
#include "mmq_select_probe.hpp"
#include <cassert>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <iostream>
#include <limits>
#include <thread>
#include <vector>

int main(int argc, char ** argv) {
    const bool profile = llama_exp::mmq_select_probe::enabled();
    if (!profile) {
        std::cout << "P15C host profile disabled\n";
        return 0;
    }
    const bool overflow = argc > 1 && argv[1][0] == 'o';
    const bool saturate = argc > 1 && argv[1][0] == 's';
    auto & probe = llama_exp::mmq_select_probe::local();
    if (saturate) {
        probe.record(42, true, 1, false, std::numeric_limits<std::uint64_t>::max());
        probe.record(42, true, 1, true, 30);
        probe.flush();
        return 0;
    }
    if (overflow) {
        for (int type = 0; type < 529; ++type) {
            probe.record(type, true, 1, false, 100);
        }
        probe.flush();
        return 0;
    }
    for (int i=0; i<4096; ++i) {
        probe.record(12, true, 1, i!=0, 100); // triggers periodic flush
    }
    for (int i=0; i<32; ++i) {
        probe.record(13, false, 32, false, 200);
    }
    std::vector<std::thread> workers;
    for (int t = 0; t < 4; ++t) {
        workers.emplace_back([t] {
            auto & p = llama_exp::mmq_select_probe::local();
            for (int i=0; i<64; ++i) {
                p.record(10+t, true, 128, i>0, 70);
            }
        });
    }
    for (auto & thread: workers) thread.join();
    probe.flush();
    std::cout << "P15C host profile enabled\n";
    return 0;
}

===== SOURCE FILE tests_cpp/moe_staging_fixture.cpp =====

#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <cassert>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <memory>
#include <numeric>
#include <random>
#include <vector>

#define GGML_ASSERT(x) assert(x)
#define GGML_ABORT(...) std::abort()

struct fake_backend;
struct fake_type { fake_backend * owner; bool host = true; size_t maximum = size_t(-1); };
struct fake_buffer {
    fake_backend * owner;
    fake_type * type;
    size_t size;
    bool host = true;
    bool null_base = false;
    uint8_t storage[8] = {}; // Allocation-control tests inspect metadata only.
};
struct fake_backend {
    bool async = true;
    bool host_buffer = true;
    bool missing_type = false;
    int alloc_mode = 0;
    int allocations = 0;
    int frees = 0;
    fake_type pinned {this};
    fake_type pageable {this};
};
using ggml_backend_t = fake_backend *;
using ggml_backend_dev_t = fake_backend *;
using ggml_backend_buffer_type_t = fake_type *;
using ggml_backend_buffer_t = fake_buffer *;
struct ggml_backend_dev_props { struct { bool async; bool host_buffer; } caps; };
static void ggml_backend_buffer_free(fake_buffer * p) {
    if (p) { p->owner->frees++; delete p; }
}
struct fake_deleter { void operator()(fake_buffer * p) const { ggml_backend_buffer_free(p); } };
using ggml_backend_buffer_ptr = std::unique_ptr<fake_buffer, fake_deleter>;
static ggml_backend_dev_t ggml_backend_get_device(ggml_backend_t p) { return p; }
static void ggml_backend_dev_get_props(ggml_backend_dev_t p, ggml_backend_dev_props * props) {
    props->caps.async = p->async; props->caps.host_buffer = p->host_buffer;
}
static fake_type * ggml_backend_dev_host_buffer_type(fake_backend * p) { return p->missing_type ? nullptr : &p->pinned; }
static bool ggml_backend_buft_is_host(fake_type * p) { return p->host; }
static size_t ggml_backend_buft_get_max_size(fake_type * p) { return p->maximum; }
static fake_buffer * ggml_backend_buft_alloc_buffer(fake_type * t, size_t size) {
    auto * p = t->owner;
    p->allocations++;
    if (p->alloc_mode == 1) { return nullptr; }
    auto * result = new fake_buffer {p, t, size};
    if (p->alloc_mode == 2) { result->type = &p->pageable; }
    if (p->alloc_mode == 3) { result->size = size - 1; }
    if (p->alloc_mode == 4) { result->size = size_t(64)*1024*1024 + 1; }
    if (p->alloc_mode == 5) { result->null_base = true; }
    if (p->alloc_mode == 6) { result->host = false; }
    return result;
}
static fake_type * ggml_backend_buffer_get_type(fake_buffer * p) { return p->type; }
static bool ggml_backend_buffer_is_host(fake_buffer * p) { return p->host; }
static size_t ggml_backend_buffer_get_size(fake_buffer * p) { return p->size; }
static void * ggml_backend_buffer_get_base(fake_buffer * p) { return p->null_base ? nullptr : p->storage; }

// @ACTUAL_STAGING_HELPERS@

static void check_env() {
    bool invalid = true;
    assert(llama_moe_cache_staging_limit(nullptr, invalid) == 0 && !invalid);
    for (const char * text : {"", "0", "00"}) {
        assert(llama_moe_cache_staging_limit(text, invalid) == 0 && !invalid);
    }
    for (const char * text : {"1x", "-1", "+1", " 1", "1 ", "65", "999999999999999999999999999999999999", "1.0"}) {
        assert(llama_moe_cache_staging_limit(text, invalid) == 0 && invalid);
    }
    assert(llama_moe_cache_staging_limit("1", invalid) == 1024*1024 && !invalid);
    assert(llama_moe_cache_staging_limit("032", invalid) == size_t(32)*1024*1024 && !invalid);
    assert(llama_moe_cache_staging_limit("64", invalid) == size_t(64)*1024*1024 && !invalid);
}

static void check_allocation() {
    constexpr size_t limit = 1024*1024;
    {
        fake_backend b;
        assert(!llama_moe_cache_alloc_staging(&b, limit, true));
        assert(!llama_moe_cache_alloc_staging(&b, 0, false));
        assert(b.allocations == 0);
    }
    for (int eligibility = 0; eligibility < 5; ++eligibility) {
        fake_backend b;
        if (eligibility == 0) b.async = false;
        if (eligibility == 1) b.host_buffer = false;
        if (eligibility == 2) b.missing_type = true;
        if (eligibility == 3) b.pinned.host = false;
        if (eligibility == 4) b.pinned.maximum = limit - 1;
        assert(!llama_moe_cache_alloc_staging(&b, limit, false));
        assert(b.allocations == 0);
    }
    for (int mode = 1; mode <= 6; ++mode) {
        fake_backend b;
        b.alloc_mode = mode;
        assert(!llama_moe_cache_alloc_staging(&b, limit, false));
        assert(b.allocations == 1);
        assert(b.frees == (mode == 1 ? 0 : 1));
    }
    fake_backend a, b;
    auto first = llama_moe_cache_alloc_staging(&a, limit, false);
    auto second = llama_moe_cache_alloc_staging(&b, limit, false);
    assert(first && second && first.get() != second.get());
    assert(ggml_backend_buffer_get_base(first.get()) != ggml_backend_buffer_get_base(second.get()));
    first.reset(); second.reset();
    assert(a.frees == 1 && b.frees == 1);
}

struct pending_upload { const uint8_t * data; size_t bank, offset, size; };
struct case_result { size_t calls = 0, saved = 0, staged = 0; };

static size_t direct_calls(const std::vector<moe_cache_slru::fill> & fills) {
    size_t calls = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < fills.size(); ++i) {
        calls += i == 0 || fills[i].expert != fills[i-1].expert + 1 || fills[i].slot != fills[i-1].slot + 1;
    }
    return calls;
}

static case_result check_bytes(const std::vector<moe_cache_slru::fill> & fills,
        const std::vector<size_t> & strides, size_t capacity, size_t initial_used = 0, bool null_staging = false) {
    constexpr size_t n_experts = 64, n_slots = 64, guard = 16;
    assert(initial_used <= capacity);
    std::vector<uint8_t> arena(capacity + 2*guard, 0xda);
    uint8_t * staging = arena.data() + guard;
    std::vector<std::vector<uint8_t>> sources(strides.size()), banks(strides.size()), expected(strides.size());
    std::vector<pending_upload> pending;
    size_t used = initial_used;
    case_result total;
    for (size_t bank = 0; bank < strides.size(); ++bank) {
        const size_t stride = strides[bank];
        sources[bank].resize(n_experts*stride);
        for (size_t i = 0; i < sources[bank].size(); ++i) {
            sources[bank][i] = uint8_t((i*17 + i/stride*37 + bank*71) % 251);
        }
        banks[bank].assign(n_slots*stride, 0xcc);
        expected[bank] = banks[bank];
        for (const auto & fill : fills) {
            std::memcpy(expected[bank].data() + size_t(fill.slot)*stride,
                        sources[bank].data() + size_t(fill.expert)*stride, stride);
        }
        const size_t before_used = used, before_calls = pending.size();
        const auto uploaded = llama_moe_cache_upload_experts(sources[bank].data(), stride, fills,
            null_staging ? nullptr : staging, capacity, used,
            [&](const uint8_t * p, size_t offset, size_t bytes) {
                assert(offset + bytes <= banks[bank].size());
                pending.push_back({p, bank, offset, bytes});
            });
        assert(used <= capacity && used >= before_used);
        assert(uploaded.bytes == fills.size()*stride);
        assert(uploaded.staged_bytes == used - before_used);
        assert(uploaded.calls == pending.size() - before_calls);
        assert(uploaded.calls + uploaded.calls_saved == direct_calls(fills));
        total.calls += uploaded.calls;
        total.saved += uploaded.calls_saved;
        total.staged += uploaded.staged_bytes;
    }
    // All banks have queued their transfers before ANY source pointer is read.
    // An incorrect per-bank cursor reset will overwrite an earlier bank here.
    size_t last_staged_end = initial_used;
    const auto begin = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(staging);
    for (const auto & upload : pending) {
        const auto address = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(upload.data);
        if (address >= begin && address < begin + capacity) {
            const size_t offset = size_t(address - begin);
            assert(offset >= last_staged_end && offset + upload.size <= used);
            last_staged_end = offset + upload.size;
        }
        std::memcpy(banks[upload.bank].data() + upload.offset, upload.data, upload.size);
    }
    assert(banks == expected);
    assert(std::all_of(arena.begin(), arena.begin() + guard + initial_used, [](uint8_t x) { return x == 0xda; }));
    assert(std::all_of(arena.begin() + guard + used, arena.end(), [](uint8_t x) { return x == 0xda; }));
    return total;
}

static void check_deferred_backends() {
    const std::vector<moe_cache_slru::fill> fills {{1, 0}, {4, 1}, {8, 2}};
    constexpr size_t stride = 17;
    std::array<std::vector<uint8_t>, 2> source, staging, bank;
    std::array<size_t, 2> used {{0, 0}};
    std::array<std::vector<pending_upload>, 2> pending;
    for (size_t dev = 0; dev < 2; ++dev) {
        source[dev].resize(10*stride);
        std::iota(source[dev].begin(), source[dev].end(), uint8_t(dev*71));
        staging[dev].resize(3*stride);
        bank[dev].resize(3*stride);
        const auto result = llama_moe_cache_upload_experts(source[dev].data(), stride, fills,
            staging[dev].data(), staging[dev].size(), used[dev],
            [&](const uint8_t * p, size_t offset, size_t bytes) { pending[dev].push_back({p, dev, offset, bytes}); });
        assert(result.calls == 1 && result.calls_saved == 2);
    }
    auto drain = [&](size_t dev) {
        for (const auto & p : pending[dev]) {
            std::memcpy(bank[dev].data() + p.offset, p.data, p.size);
        }
        pending[dev].clear();
        for (const auto & f : fills) {
            assert(std::memcmp(bank[dev].data() + size_t(f.slot)*stride,
                               source[dev].data() + size_t(f.expert)*stride, stride) == 0);
        }
    };
    drain(0); // This stands for the existing synchronization of backend zero.
    used[0] = 0;
    std::fill(source[0].begin(), source[0].end(), 0x5b);
    llama_moe_cache_upload_experts(source[0].data(), stride, fills,
        staging[0].data(), staging[0].size(), used[0],
        [&](const uint8_t * p, size_t offset, size_t bytes) { pending[0].push_back({p, 0, offset, bytes}); });
    drain(1); // Backend one's previous pointers were not overwritten by reuse of zero.
    drain(0);
}

int main() {
    check_env();
    check_allocation();
    const std::vector<moe_cache_slru::fill> gaps {{1, 0}, {4, 1}, {8, 2}};
    auto result = check_bytes(gaps, {144, 210, 272}, 3*(144 + 210 + 272));
    assert(result.calls == 3 && result.saved == 6 && result.staged == 3*(144 + 210 + 272));
    result = check_bytes(gaps, {17, 31, 97}, 3*17); // Exhausted after bank zero; no reuse before completion.
    assert(result.calls == 7 && result.saved == 2 && result.staged == 3*17);
    result = check_bytes(gaps, {17, 31}, 2*17); // A partial run may be packed; the remainder stays direct.
    assert(result.calls == 5 && result.saved == 1);
    result = check_bytes(gaps, {17}, 16); // Cannot fit even one expert.
    assert(result.calls == 3 && result.saved == 0 && result.staged == 0);
    result = check_bytes(gaps, {17}, 1024, 0, true);
    assert(result.calls == 3 && result.saved == 0 && result.staged == 0);
    result = check_bytes({{1, 0}, {2, 1}, {3, 2}}, {17, 144}, 4096);
    assert(result.calls == 2 && result.saved == 0 && result.staged == 0); // Already one direct run per bank.
    result = check_bytes({{1, 3}, {4, 0}, {8, 7}}, {17}, 4096);
    assert(result.calls == 3 && result.saved == 0); // Nonadjacent destination slots are never bridged.
    result = check_bytes({}, {17, 210}, 0);
    assert(result.calls == 0 && result.staged == 0);
    // The budget must not cut through the second original direct run. Doing
    // so would save no call yet copy bytes, and overcount calls_saved by one.
    result = check_bytes({{1, 0}, {4, 1}, {5, 2}, {6, 3}}, {17}, 3*17);
    assert(result.calls == 2 && result.saved == 0 && result.staged == 0);
    result = check_bytes({{1, 0}, {4, 1}, {5, 2}, {6, 3}, {8, 4}}, {17}, 4*17);
    assert(result.calls == 2 && result.saved == 1 && result.staged == 4*17);
    check_deferred_backends();

    std::mt19937 rng(0x50404);
    const std::array<size_t, 8> strides {{1, 3, 17, 144, 210, 256, 272, 1024}};
    size_t randomized_saved_calls = 0;
    for (size_t trial = 0; trial < 4000; ++trial) {
        std::vector<int32_t> experts(48), slots(48);
        std::iota(experts.begin(), experts.end(), 0);
        std::iota(slots.begin(), slots.end(), 0);
        std::shuffle(experts.begin(), experts.end(), rng);
        if (trial % 2) std::shuffle(slots.begin(), slots.end(), rng);
        const size_t count = rng() % 40;
        experts.resize(count);
        std::sort(experts.begin(), experts.end());
        std::vector<moe_cache_slru::fill> fills;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) fills.push_back({experts[i], slots[i]});
        std::vector<size_t> sizes {strides[rng() % strides.size()], strides[rng() % strides.size()], 13 + rng() % 101};
        const size_t required = count*std::accumulate(sizes.begin(), sizes.end(), size_t(0));
        const size_t capacity = rng() % (required + 31);
        const size_t initial = capacity == 0 ? 0 : rng() % (capacity + 1);
        randomized_saved_calls += check_bytes(fills, sizes, capacity, initial).saved;
    }
    assert(randomized_saved_calls > 0);
    std::printf("{\"status\":\"PASS\",\"randomized_byte_cases\":4000,\"deterministic_byte_cases\":10,"
                "\"deferred_backend_lifetime_cases\":1,\"allocation_control_cases\":14,\"env_cases\":15,"
                "\"randomized_upload_calls_saved\":%zu,\"gpu_or_inference_test\":false}\n", randomized_saved_calls);
}

===== SOURCE FILE tests_cpp/moe_upload_completion_fixture.cpp =====
#include "../modules/moe/upload_completion.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cassert>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace {
struct fake_backend {
    struct copy { uint8_t * dst; const uint8_t * src; size_t count; };
    std::vector<copy> pending;
    size_t completed = 0;
    size_t records = 0;
    size_t event_waits = 0;
    size_t backend_waits = 0;
    size_t destroyed = 0;
    bool events = true;
    bool fail_record = false;
    bool fail_submit = false;
    void enqueue(uint8_t * dst, const uint8_t * src, size_t count) {
        if (fail_submit) throw std::runtime_error("backend enqueue failure");
        pending.push_back({dst,src,count});
    }
    void flush_to(size_t last) {
        assert(last <= pending.size());
        for (; completed < last; ++completed) {
            const auto & c = pending.at(completed);
            std::memcpy(c.dst,c.src,c.count);
        }
    }
};
struct fake_event { fake_backend * backend; size_t marker = 0; bool recorded = false; };
struct adapter {
    using backend_type = fake_backend *;
    using event_type = fake_event *;
    static event_type create(backend_type b) { return b->events ? new fake_event{b,0,false} : nullptr; }
    static void record(event_type e, backend_type b) {
        assert(e && e->backend == b);
        if (b->fail_record) throw std::runtime_error("event record failure");
        e->marker = b->pending.size();
        e->recorded = true;
        ++b->records;
    }
    static void synchronize_event(event_type e) {
        assert(e && e->recorded);
        e->backend->flush_to(e->marker);
        ++e->backend->event_waits;
    }
    static void synchronize_backend(backend_type b) {
        b->flush_to(b->pending.size());
        ++b->backend_waits;
    }
    static void destroy(event_type e) {
        assert(e);
        ++e->backend->destroyed;
        delete e;
    }
};
using fence = llama_exp::moe_upload_completion<adapter>;
using abort_guard = llama_exp::moe_upload_abort_scope<fence>;

void normal_reuse() {
    fake_backend b;
    uint8_t src[8] = {1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8};
    uint8_t dst[8] = {};
    fence f(&b);
    assert(f.event_supported());
    for (int k=0; k<64; ++k) {
        f.begin_submission();
        abort_guard guard(&f);
        b.enqueue(dst,src,8);
        f.record_after_slot_map();
        guard.commit();
        // No added host-side synchronize on the ordinary success path.
        assert(b.completed == size_t(k));
        b.flush_to(b.pending.size()); // the original routing-ID readback sync
        assert(f.retired_after_backend_sync());
    }
    assert(std::memcmp(src,dst,8) == 0);
    assert(b.records == 64 && b.event_waits == 0 && b.backend_waits == 0);
    f.close(); f.close();
    assert(b.destroyed == 1 && f.closed());
}

void graceful_close_pending() {
    fake_backend b;
    uint8_t src[3] = {11,22,33};
    uint8_t dst[3] = {};
    fence f(&b);
    f.begin_submission();
    b.enqueue(dst,src,3);
    f.record_after_slot_map();
    assert(dst[0] == 0);
    f.close();
    assert(std::memcmp(src,dst,3) == 0);
    assert(b.event_waits == 1 && b.backend_waits == 0 && b.destroyed == 1);
}

void eventless_fallback() {
    fake_backend b; b.events = false;
    uint8_t src = 19, dst = 0;
    fence f(&b);
    assert(!f.event_supported());
    f.begin_submission();
    b.enqueue(&dst,&src,1);
    f.record_after_slot_map();
    f.close();
    assert(dst == 19 && b.backend_waits == 1 && b.event_waits == 0 && b.destroyed == 0);
}

void failure_after_enqueue() {
    fake_backend b;
    uint8_t src = 42, dst = 0;
    fence f(&b);
    try {
        f.begin_submission();
        abort_guard guard(&f);
        b.enqueue(&dst,&src,1);
        throw std::runtime_error("failure after one H2D");
    } catch (const std::runtime_error &) {}
    assert(dst == 42 && !f.pending());
    assert(b.backend_waits == 1); // no event was recorded yet
    f.close();
}

void failed_event_record() {
    fake_backend b; b.fail_record = true;
    uint8_t src = 88, dst = 0;
    fence f(&b);
    try {
        f.begin_submission();
        abort_guard guard(&f);
        b.enqueue(&dst,&src,1);
        f.record_after_slot_map();
        assert(false);
    } catch (const std::runtime_error &) {}
    assert(dst == 88 && b.backend_waits == 1 && !f.pending());
    f.close();
}

void rejected_double_begin() {
    fake_backend b;
    fence f(&b);
    f.begin_submission();
    bool threw=false;
    try { f.begin_submission(); } catch (const std::logic_error &) {threw=true;}
    assert(threw);
    f.drain();
    f.close();
    threw=false;
    try {f.begin_submission();} catch(const std::logic_error &){threw=true;}
    assert(threw);
}

void independent_backends() {
    fake_backend a,b; b.events=false;
    uint8_t src_a=7,src_b=9,dst_a=0,dst_b=0;
    fence fa(&a), fb(&b);
    fa.begin_submission(); fb.begin_submission();
    a.enqueue(&dst_a,&src_a,1); b.enqueue(&dst_b,&src_b,1);
    fa.record_after_slot_map();fb.record_after_slot_map();
    fa.close();
    assert(dst_a==7 && dst_b==0 && b.backend_waits==0);
    fb.close();
    assert(dst_b==9 && a.destroyed==1 && b.destroyed==0);
}

void no_false_retirement() {
    fake_backend b;
    fence f(&b);
    assert(!f.retired_after_backend_sync());
    f.begin_submission();
    assert(f.retired_after_backend_sync());
    assert(!f.pending());
    f.close();
}
}
int main(int argc,char **argv) {
    if(argc>1 && std::string(argv[1])=="--unclosed") {
        fake_backend b;
        fence bad(&b);
        return 0; // destructor MUST terminate: unclosed backend ownership
    }
    assert(fence::enabled() == (std::getenv("LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_EVENT_FENCE") &&
          std::string(std::getenv("LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_EVENT_FENCE"))=="1"));
    normal_reuse();
    graceful_close_pending();
    eventless_fallback();
    failure_after_enqueue();
    failed_event_record();
    rejected_double_begin();
    independent_backends();
    no_false_retirement();
    std::cout << "P14B event-completion tests PASS\n";
}

===== SOURCE FILE tests_cpp/moe_upload_pipeline_fixture.cpp =====
#include "moe/upload_pipeline.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <atomic>
#include <chrono>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstring>
#include <exception>
#include <iostream>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <thread>
#include <vector>

#define CHECK(x) do { if (!(x)) { std::cerr << "FAILED at " << __LINE__ << ": " #x "\n"; std::abort(); } } while (0)
using llama_exp::moe_upload_batch;
using llama_exp::moe_upload_pipeline;
using llama_exp::moe_upload_span;

static void test_exact_order_and_disjoint_slices() {
    constexpr size_t banks = 3;
    constexpr size_t nfill = 30;
    constexpr size_t width = 64;
    std::vector<uint8_t> source(banks*nfill*width);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < source.size(); ++i) source[i] = uint8_t((i * 97 + 23) % 251);
    std::vector<uint8_t> stage(12288, 0xf1);
    std::vector<uint8_t> reference(source.size());
    std::vector<uint8_t> device(source.size(), 0);
    std::atomic<int> active{0};
    std::atomic<bool> concurrent{false};
    std::atomic<bool> off_main{false};
    const auto main = std::this_thread::get_id();
    std::vector<size_t> order;
    bool used = moe_upload_pipeline::execute(stage.data(), stage.size(), banks, nfill*width,
        [&](size_t bank, uint8_t * dest, size_t cap) -> moe_upload_batch {
            CHECK(cap >= width*nfill);
            if (std::this_thread::get_id() != main) off_main = true;
            if (++active == 2) concurrent = true;
            // Indexed, non-consecutive expert positions; exact order matters.
            moe_upload_batch result;
            std::memcpy(dest, source.data()+bank*nfill*width, nfill*width);
            result.spans.push_back({dest, bank*nfill*width, nfill*width});
            result.bytes = result.staged_bytes = nfill*width;
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(3));
            --active;
            return result;
        },
        [&](size_t bank, const moe_upload_batch & data) {
            CHECK(std::this_thread::get_id() == main);
            CHECK(bank == order.size());
            order.push_back(bank);
            CHECK(data.spans.size() == 1);
            const auto & t = data.spans[0];
            std::memcpy(device.data()+t.dst_offset, t.data, t.size);
        }, [] {CHECK(false);});
    CHECK(used && concurrent && off_main && device == source);
    CHECK(order == (std::vector<size_t>{0,1,2}));
}

static void test_no_torn_async_reads() {
    // Simulate backend enqueue that does not read source until AFTER execute().
    // No staging slice may be reused within this invocation.
    std::vector<uint8_t> staging(8192, 0x4a);
    std::vector<uint8_t> weights(4*1024);
    for (size_t i=0; i<weights.size(); ++i) weights[i] = uint8_t(i*31 + 8);
    struct span { const uint8_t * data; size_t dest; size_t n; };
    std::vector<span> inflight;
    const auto success = moe_upload_pipeline::execute(staging.data(), staging.size(), 4, 512,
        [&](size_t bank, uint8_t * ptr, size_t capacity) {
            CHECK(capacity >= 1024);
            std::memcpy(ptr, weights.data()+bank*1024, 1024);
            moe_upload_batch b; b.spans.push_back({ptr,bank*1024,1024});return b;
        },
        [&](size_t, const moe_upload_batch & b) {
            for (const auto & v:b.spans) inflight.push_back({v.data,v.dst_offset,v.size});
        }, [] {CHECK(false);});
    CHECK(success && inflight.size() == 4);
    std::vector<uint8_t> device(weights.size());
    for (const auto & f:inflight) std::memcpy(device.data()+f.dest, f.data, f.n);
    CHECK(device == weights);
}

static void test_rejection_without_side_effects() {
    std::vector<uint8_t> stage(1024, 0);
    size_t calls = 0;
    auto prepare=[&](size_t, uint8_t *, size_t) {++calls;return moe_upload_batch{};};
    auto submit=[&](size_t, const moe_upload_batch &) {++calls;};
    auto fence = [] {CHECK(false);};
    CHECK(!moe_upload_pipeline::execute(nullptr, stage.size(), 3, 1, prepare, submit, fence));
    CHECK(!moe_upload_pipeline::execute(stage.data(), stage.size(), 1, 1, prepare, submit, fence));
    CHECK(!moe_upload_pipeline::execute(stage.data(), stage.size(), 9, 1, prepare, submit, fence));
    CHECK(!moe_upload_pipeline::execute(stage.data(), stage.size(), 3, 1025, prepare, submit, fence));
    CHECK(calls==0);
    CHECK(std::all_of(stage.begin(),stage.end(),[](uint8_t x){return x==0;}));
}

static void test_worker_exception_joins_and_aborts() {
    std::vector<uint8_t> stage(8192);
    std::atomic<int> active{0};
    bool caught=false;
    int fences=0;
    try {
        moe_upload_pipeline::execute(stage.data(),stage.size(),3,1024,
            [&](size_t bank,uint8_t * ptr,size_t cap) -> moe_upload_batch {
                CHECK(cap >= 1024);
                ++active;
                struct dec {std::atomic<int>& count; ~dec(){--count;}} guard{active};
                if(bank==1) throw std::runtime_error("planned failure");
                std::memset(ptr,0x17,1024);
                std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(3));
                return moe_upload_batch{};
            },
            [&](size_t,const moe_upload_batch &) {}, [&] {++fences;});
    } catch (const std::runtime_error &) {caught=true;}
    CHECK(caught && active==0 && fences==1);
}

static void test_caller_exception_joins_worker() {
    std::vector<uint8_t> stage(8192);
    std::atomic<int> active{0};
    bool caught=false;
    int fences=0;
    try {
        moe_upload_pipeline::execute(stage.data(),stage.size(),3,1024,
            [&](size_t bank,uint8_t * ptr,size_t cap) -> moe_upload_batch {
                CHECK(cap>=1024); ++active;
                struct dec {std::atomic<int>& count; ~dec(){--count;}} guard{active};
                std::memset(ptr,0x3c,1024);
                std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(3));
                if(bank==0) throw std::runtime_error("caller failure");
                return moe_upload_batch{};
            },
            [&](size_t,const moe_upload_batch &) {}, [&] {++fences;});
    } catch (const std::runtime_error &) {caught=true;}
    CHECK(caught && active==0 && fences==0);
}

static void test_submit_on_caller_only() {
    std::vector<uint8_t> stage(16*1024);
    const auto caller=std::this_thread::get_id();
    for (int repeat=0;repeat<40;++repeat) {
        size_t submitted=0;
        bool ran=moe_upload_pipeline::execute(stage.data(),stage.size(),3,128,
            [&](size_t bank,uint8_t * ptr,size_t) -> moe_upload_batch {
                std::memset(ptr,int(bank),128);
                moe_upload_batch result;
                result.spans.push_back({ptr,bank*128,128});
                return result;
            },
            [&](size_t ip,const moe_upload_batch & b) {
                CHECK(std::this_thread::get_id() == caller);
                CHECK(ip==submitted++);
                CHECK(b.spans.size()==1);
            }, [] {CHECK(false);});
        CHECK(ran && submitted==3);
    }
}

// An inline request can fail while preparing a later bank, or after a
// backend accepted an earlier async transfer. Both paths must fence.
static void test_inline_error_lifetime() {
    uint8_t stage[3*512] = {};
    const auto owner = std::this_thread::get_id();
    for (int fault = 0; fault < 3; ++fault) {
        bool pending = false;
        bool caught = false;
        int fences = 0;
        try {
            moe_upload_pipeline::execute(stage, sizeof(stage), 3, 256,
                [&](size_t i, uint8_t * p, size_t n) {
                    CHECK(owner == std::this_thread::get_id());
                    CHECK(p == stage + i*512 && n == 512);
                    if ((fault == 0 && i == 0) || (fault == 1 && i == 1)) {
                        throw std::runtime_error("prepare failed");
                    }
                    return moe_upload_batch{};
                },
                [&](size_t, const moe_upload_batch &) {
                    CHECK(owner == std::this_thread::get_id());
                    pending = true;
                    if (fault == 2) throw std::runtime_error("partial submit");
                },
                [&] { CHECK(pending); pending = false; ++fences; },
                true);
        } catch (const std::runtime_error &) {
            caught = true;
        }
        CHECK(caught && !pending && fences == (fault == 0 ? 0 : 1));
    }
}

int main(){
    test_exact_order_and_disjoint_slices();
    test_no_torn_async_reads();
    test_rejection_without_side_effects();
    test_worker_exception_joins_and_aborts();
    test_caller_exception_joins_worker();
    test_submit_on_caller_only();
    test_inline_error_lifetime();
    std::cout << "P09 host pipeline fixture: 6 PASS; R20 inline exception paths PASS\n";
}

===== SOURCE FILE tests_cpp/moe_upload_worker_fixture.cpp =====
#include "modules/moe/upload_worker.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <atomic>
#include <cassert>
#include <chrono>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <thread>
#include <vector>

using namespace llama_exp;

int main() {
    // Repeated cold starts exercise constructor/teardown before any job is submitted.
    for (size_t cycle = 0; cycle < 128; ++cycle) {
        moe_upload_worker idle;
    }
    constexpr size_t banks = 4;
    constexpr size_t row = 256;
    std::vector<uint8_t> src(banks * 2 * row);
    std::vector<uint8_t> staging(4096);
    std::vector<std::vector<uint8_t>> gpu(banks, std::vector<uint8_t>(2 * row));
    std::thread::id main_id = std::this_thread::get_id();
    std::thread::id worker_id;
    std::atomic<unsigned> background{0};
    std::vector<std::pair<size_t, moe_upload_span>> outstanding;
    int fences = 0;
    {
        moe_upload_worker worker;
        // Multiple updates must reuse exactly one producer thread.
        for (size_t generation = 1; generation < 50; ++generation) {
            for (size_t i = 0; i < src.size(); ++i) src[i] = uint8_t((i + generation * 19) & 255);
            assert(outstanding.empty());
            auto prepare = [&](size_t bank, uint8_t * dest, size_t bytes) -> moe_upload_batch {
                assert(bytes >= 512);
                if (bank > 0) {
                    ++background;
                    if (worker_id == std::thread::id{}) worker_id = std::this_thread::get_id();
                    assert(worker_id == std::this_thread::get_id());
                    assert(worker_id != main_id);
                } else { assert(std::this_thread::get_id() == main_id); }
                std::memcpy(dest, src.data() + bank * 2 * row, 2 * row);
                moe_upload_batch batch;
                batch.spans.push_back({dest, 0, 2 * row});
                batch.bytes = 2 * row;
                return batch;
            };
            auto submit = [&](size_t bank, const moe_upload_batch & batch) {
                assert(std::this_thread::get_id() == main_id);
                for (auto & span : batch.spans) outstanding.push_back({bank, span});
            };
            auto fence = [&] {
                fences++;
                for (auto & x : outstanding) {
                    assert(x.second.dst_offset + x.second.size <= gpu[x.first].size());
                    std::memcpy(gpu[x.first].data() + x.second.dst_offset, x.second.data, x.second.size);
                }
                outstanding.clear();
            };
            assert(worker.execute(staging.data(), staging.size(), banks, row, prepare, submit, fence));
            // Asynchronous device reads occur only after execute() returns.
            fence();
            for (size_t b = 0; b < banks; ++b)
                assert(std::equal(gpu[b].begin(), gpu[b].end(), src.begin() + b * 2 * row));
        }
        assert(background == 49 * (banks - 1));
        assert(fences == 49);
        // False means no job and no callback started.
        bool invoked = false;
        auto no_prepare = [&](size_t, uint8_t *, size_t) -> moe_upload_batch { invoked = true; return {}; };
        assert(!worker.execute(staging.data(), 255, 4, row, no_prepare, [](size_t, const moe_upload_batch &) {}, []{}));
        assert(!invoked);
        // Reentrant use on the same caller thread must fail closed. Running
        // the legacy serial path here would race the active staging owner.
        int rejected = 0;
        assert(worker.execute(staging.data(), staging.size(), banks, row,
            [&](size_t b, uint8_t * dest, size_t) -> moe_upload_batch {
                if (b == 0) {
                    try {
                        worker.execute(staging.data(), staging.size(), banks, row,
                            [](size_t, uint8_t *, size_t) -> moe_upload_batch { return {}; },
                            [](size_t, const moe_upload_batch &) {}, [] {});
                        assert(false);
                    } catch (const std::logic_error &) { ++rejected; }
                }
                std::memcpy(dest, src.data() + b * 2 * row, 512);
                moe_upload_batch x; x.spans.push_back({dest, 0, 512}); return x;
            }, [&](size_t b, const moe_upload_batch & x) { outstanding.push_back({b, x.spans[0]}); },
            [&] { assert(false); }));
        assert(rejected == 1);
        outstanding.clear();
        // Failure in background producer after first submit must synchronize.
        outstanding.clear();
        int before = fences;
        try {
            worker.execute(staging.data(), staging.size(), banks, row,
                [&](size_t bank, uint8_t * dest, size_t) -> moe_upload_batch {
                    if (bank == 2) throw std::runtime_error("injected prepare failure");
                    std::memcpy(dest, src.data() + bank * 2 * row, 2 * row);
                    moe_upload_batch x; x.spans.push_back({dest, 0, 512}); return x;
                }, [&](size_t bank, const moe_upload_batch & x) { outstanding.push_back({bank, x.spans[0]}); },
                [&] { fences++; outstanding.clear(); });
            assert(false);
        } catch (const std::runtime_error &) { assert(fences == before + 1); }
        // Worker survives producer failure and can accept a new update.
        outstanding.clear();
        assert(worker.execute(staging.data(), staging.size(), banks, row,
            [&](size_t bank, uint8_t * dest, size_t) -> moe_upload_batch {
                std::memcpy(dest, src.data() + bank * 2 * row, 512);
                moe_upload_batch x; x.spans.push_back({dest, 0, 512}); return x;
            }, [&](size_t b, const moe_upload_batch & x) { outstanding.push_back({b, x.spans[0]}); },
            [&] { assert(false); }));
        outstanding.clear();
        // Failure in first submit also fences (it may enqueue before throwing).
        int aborted = 0;
        try {
            worker.execute(staging.data(), staging.size(), banks, row,
                [&](size_t b, uint8_t * dest, size_t) -> moe_upload_batch {
                    std::memcpy(dest, src.data() + b * 2 * row, 512);
                    moe_upload_batch x; x.spans.push_back({dest, 0, 512}); return x;
                }, [&](size_t, const moe_upload_batch &) { throw std::runtime_error("submit failure"); },
                [&] { aborted++; });
            assert(false);
        } catch (const std::runtime_error &) { assert(aborted == 1); }
    }
    std::cout << "PASS: worker persists, 49 generations, exact deferred H2D bytes, "
                 "disabled no-op, producer failure, retry, submit abort/fence\n";
}

===== SOURCE FILE tests_cpp/moe_worker_metadata_fixture.cpp =====
// P11 host regression: the persistent CPU worker's per-job control metadata
// must be allocation-free after construction/warm-up. No GPU operations.
#include "modules/moe/upload_worker.hpp"

#include <array>
#include <atomic>
#include <cassert>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <iostream>
#include <new>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <thread>

static std::atomic<size_t> alloc_count{0};

void * operator new(std::size_t n) {
    ++alloc_count;
    if (void * p = std::malloc(n ? n : 1)) return p;
    throw std::bad_alloc();
}
void * operator new[](std::size_t n) {
    ++alloc_count;
    if (void * p = std::malloc(n ? n : 1)) return p;
    throw std::bad_alloc();
}
void operator delete(void * p) noexcept { std::free(p); }
void operator delete[](void * p) noexcept { std::free(p); }
void operator delete(void * p, std::size_t) noexcept { std::free(p); }
void operator delete[](void * p, std::size_t) noexcept { std::free(p); }

int main() {
    using llama_exp::moe_upload_batch;
    using llama_exp::moe_upload_worker;
    constexpr size_t banks = 4;
    constexpr size_t capacity = 32768;
    constexpr size_t slice = capacity / banks;
    alignas(256) std::array<uint8_t, capacity> staging{};
    std::array<uint8_t, 4096> capture{};
    capture.fill(31);
    moe_upload_worker worker;
    const auto thread = std::this_thread::get_id();
    size_t submit_count = 0;
    size_t prepared_off_main = 0;

    for (int i = 0; i < 250; ++i) {
        size_t next_submit = 0;
        // A deliberately large closure would not fit in std::function SBO.
        // The non-owning job frame should not heap-copy it.
        auto prepare = [&, large = capture, serial = i](size_t bank, uint8_t * buf, size_t cap) -> moe_upload_batch {
            assert(large[0] == 31);
            assert(cap == slice);
            if (bank) {
                assert(std::this_thread::get_id() != thread);
                ++prepared_off_main;
            } else {
                assert(std::this_thread::get_id() == thread);
            }
            buf[0] = uint8_t((bank + serial) & 255);
            return {};
        };
        const auto submit = [&](size_t bank, const moe_upload_batch &) {
            assert(std::this_thread::get_id() == thread);
            assert(bank == next_submit++);
            assert(staging[bank*slice] == uint8_t((bank + i) & 255));
            ++submit_count;
        };
        const auto fence = [] { assert(false && "no abort should occur"); };
        if (i == 10) {
            // Nothing measured until thread/standard-library initialization
            // has been warmed up. Count only C++ new/new[] after this point.
            alloc_count.store(0);
        }
        assert(worker.execute(staging.data(), staging.size(), banks, 512, prepare, submit, fence));
        assert(next_submit == banks);
    }
    assert(submit_count == 250*banks);
    assert(prepared_off_main == 250*(banks-1));
    // The count is read before printing (iostream itself may allocate).
    const size_t counted = alloc_count.load();
#ifndef P11_SKIP_ALLOC_ASSERT
    assert(counted == 0 && "P11 worker control path allocated after warm-up");
#endif
    std::cout << "PASS: P11 reusable worker; 250 updates, large callback, FIFO, "
              << "allocation_count=" << counted << "\n";
}

===== SOURCE FILE tests_cpp/mtp_rewind_guard_fixture.cpp =====
#include "../modules/speculative/mtp_rewind_guard.hpp"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cassert>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <string>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <vector>

static bool equal(const std::vector<float> & a, const std::vector<float> & b) {
    return a.size() == b.size() && std::memcmp(a.data(),b.data(),a.size()*sizeof(float)) == 0;
}

int main() {
    const char * env = std::getenv("LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MTP_REWIND_GUARD");
    const bool expected = env != nullptr && std::string(env) == "1";
    assert(llama_exp::mtp_rewind_guard_enabled() == expected);
    llama_exp::mtp_rewind_guard guards[4];
    int checks = 0;
    for (int width : {1,3,32,256,4096}) {
        for (int n_rows : {1,2,3,4,17,128,2048}) {
            for (int accepted=0; accepted<n_rows && accepted<=256; ++accepted) {
                for (int seq=0; seq<4; ++seq) {
                    std::vector<float> original(width), live(width);
                    for (int i=0;i<width;++i) original[i] = float(i + width*seq) * 0.125f;
                    live = original;
                    auto & g=guards[seq];
                    g.checkpoint(live);
                    assert(g.has_checkpoint() && g.checkpoint_bytes() == original.size()*sizeof(float));
                    g.verified(n_rows);
                    g.validate_accept(accepted);
                    for (auto & v:live) v += 41.0f;
                    // A checkpoint restoration must preserve the byte-exact boundary.
                    g.rewind(live);
                    assert(equal(live,original) && !g.can_accept());
                    bool blocked=false;
                    try {g.validate_accept(accepted);} catch(const std::logic_error &){blocked=true;}
                    assert(blocked);
                    g.verified(n_rows); // process(replay) now has fresh target rows
                    g.commit_accept(accepted);
                    assert(!g.has_checkpoint() && !g.can_accept());
                    blocked=false;
                    try {g.commit_accept(0);} catch(const std::logic_error &){blocked=true;}
                    assert(blocked);
                    ++checks;
                }
            }
            // All out-of-window acceptance is rejected before pending_h is touched.
            guards[0].checkpoint(std::vector<float>(width,0.0f));
            guards[0].verified(n_rows);
            bool rejected=false;
            try {guards[0].validate_accept(n_rows);} catch(const std::out_of_range &){rejected=true;}
            assert(rejected);
            guards[0].discard();
            ++checks;
        }
    }
    // Abort and missing snapshot cannot silently recover from an unrelated batch.
    guards[0].checkpoint(std::vector<float>(32,1.0f));
    guards[0].verified(3);
    guards[0].discard();
    bool blocked=false;
    std::vector<float> live(32,2.0f);
    try {guards[0].rewind(live);}catch(const std::logic_error &){blocked=true;}
    assert(blocked);
    assert(std::all_of(live.begin(),live.end(),[](float v){return v==2.0f;}));
    guards[1].checkpoint(std::vector<float>(32,1.0f));
    live.resize(33);
    blocked=false;
    try {guards[1].rewind(live);}catch(const std::logic_error &){blocked=true;}
    assert(blocked);
    guards[1].discard();
    assert(checks>8000);
    std::printf("P16B PASS %d accepted/replay/boundary cases\n", checks);
}

===== SOURCE FILE tests_cpp/mtp_verify_window_fixture.cpp =====
#include "../modules/speculative/mtp_verify_window.hpp"

#include <cassert>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <limits>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <vector>

struct test_seq {
    std::vector<float> full;
    std::vector<float> retained;
    std::vector<float> pending;
    llama_exp::mtp_verify_window window;
    std::size_t width;

    test_seq(std::size_t n, std::size_t dim, int cap, bool enabled)
          : window(n, cap, enabled), width(dim) {
        full.resize(n * dim);
        for (std::size_t i=0; i<n; ++i) {
            for (std::size_t j=0; j<dim; ++j) {
                // Exact binary float values; no rounding issue in comparisons.
                full[i*dim+j] = float((i*97 + j*7) % 8192);
            }
        }
        retained.assign(full.begin(), full.begin() + window.retained_rows*dim);
        pending.assign(dim, -1.0f);
        if (n) {
            // The full last row remains the pending boundary for the next
            // process() even when the verify prefix is compacted.
            std::memcpy(pending.data(),full.data()+(n-1)*dim,dim*sizeof(float));
        }
    }

    void accept(std::uint16_t a) {
        const auto i = window.accepted_row(a);
        std::memcpy(pending.data(), retained.data()+i*width, width*sizeof(float));
        assert(std::memcmp(pending.data(),full.data()+i*width,width*sizeof(float))==0);
    }
};

int main() {
    const char * env = std::getenv("LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MTP_VERIFY_WINDOW");
    const bool expected = env && std::strcmp(env, "1") == 0;
    assert(llama_exp::mtp_verify_window_enabled() == expected);
    std::size_t cases=0, compacted=0;
    for (std::size_t n : {std::size_t(1),std::size_t(2),std::size_t(3),std::size_t(4),std::size_t(8),std::size_t(9),std::size_t(16),std::size_t(17),std::size_t(33),std::size_t(64),std::size_t(256),std::size_t(1025)}) {
        for (std::size_t dim : {std::size_t(1),std::size_t(3),std::size_t(7),std::size_t(64)}) {
            for (int max : {-1,0,1,2,4,8,16,32}) {
                for (bool enabled : {false,true}) {
                    test_seq seq(n,dim,max,enabled);
                    assert(seq.window.total_rows==n);
                    assert(seq.window.retained_rows>0 && seq.window.retained_rows<=n);
                    const auto bound = std::size_t(std::max(1, max)) + 1;
                    if (enabled && max>=0 && n > bound) {
                        assert(seq.window.retained_rows==bound);
                        ++compacted;
                    } else {
                        assert(seq.window.retained_rows==n);
                    }
                    const auto snapshot=seq.pending;
                    assert(std::memcmp(snapshot.data(),seq.full.data()+(n-1)*dim,dim*sizeof(float))==0);
                    for(std::size_t k=0;k<n && k<45;++k) {
                        if(k >= seq.window.retained_rows) {
                            try {seq.accept(static_cast<std::uint16_t>(k));assert(false);} 
                            catch(const std::out_of_range &) {}
                        } else {
                            seq.accept(static_cast<std::uint16_t>(k));
                        }
                        ++cases;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    // n_max=0 still permits the first drafted token on this upstream.
    test_seq zero_cap(2, 4, 0, true);
    zero_cap.accept(1);
    assert(zero_cap.pending == std::vector<float>(zero_cap.full.begin()+4, zero_cap.full.end()));

    // Catch-up, acceptance, and last-row carryover for two client sequences.
    struct token { int32_t seq_id; };
    llama_exp::mtp_batch_rows order;
    std::vector<int32_t> first(3), last(3);
    assert(order.index(std::vector<token>{{0},{2},{2},{0},{2},{0}}, first, last));
    assert(first == std::vector<int32_t>({0,-1,1}));
    assert(last == std::vector<int32_t>({5,-1,4}));
    assert(order.count(0,first[0],last[0]) == 3);
    assert(order.count(2,first[2],last[2]) == 3);
    for (int32_t i=0; i<3; ++i) {
        const auto row0 = order.at(0,first[0],i);
        const auto row2 = order.at(2,first[2],i);
        assert(row0 == std::vector<int32_t>({0,3,5})[i]);
        assert(row2 == std::vector<int32_t>({1,2,4})[i]);
        if (i > 0) {
            assert(order.previous(row0) == order.at(0,first[0],i-1));
            assert(order.previous(row2) == order.at(2,first[2],i-1));
        }
    }
    assert(order.index(std::vector<token>{{2},{2},{0}}, first, last));
    assert(order.count(2,first[2],last[2]) == 2);
    assert(order.at(2,first[2],1) == 1 && order.previous(1) == 0);
    assert(!order.index(std::vector<token>{{-1}}, first, last));
    assert(!order.index(std::vector<token>{{3}}, first, last));

    // Special case: acceptance saturates to last row, including >n_rows.
    test_seq total(3,23,2,true);
    total.accept(std::numeric_limits<std::uint16_t>::max());
    bool threw=false;
    try {llama_exp::mtp_verify_window(0,4,true).accepted_row(0);} catch(const std::out_of_range &) {threw=true;}
    assert(threw);
    threw=false;
    try {test_seq insufficient(200,3,4,true);insufficient.accept(9);}catch(const std::out_of_range &) {threw=true;}
    assert(threw);
    // Separate streams remain isolated; no rows cross sequence boundaries.
    test_seq a(140,13,6,true),b(100,13,8,true);
    a.accept(6);b.accept(8);
    assert(std::memcmp(a.pending.data(), b.pending.data(), 13*sizeof(float))!=0);
    std::cout << "P16A mtp verify window: " << cases << " acceptance cases, "
              << compacted << " compact snapshots, parity OK\n";
}

===== SOURCE FILE tests_cpp/r20_ds41_fixture_decode.cpp =====
// Native CPU regression. Each invocation reads the same synthetic GGUF and
// writes ALL logit rows, including prefill. Store greedy IDs on stdout.
#include "ggml-backend.h"
#include "llama-cpp.h"
#include "llama.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <fstream>
#include <iostream>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <vector>

int main(int argc, char **argv) {
    if (argc != 4) return 2;
    const int tg = std::stoi(argv[3]);
    if (tg < 1 || tg > 400) return 2;
    ggml_backend_load_all();
    llama_backend_init();
    auto mp = llama_model_default_params();
    ggml_backend_dev_t cpu[] = {nullptr};
    mp.devices = cpu;
    mp.n_gpu_layers = 0;
    mp.lazy_mode = LLAMA_LAZY_MODE_ON;
    llama_model_ptr model(llama_model_load_from_file(argv[1], mp));
    if (!model) return 3;
    auto cp = llama_context_default_params();
    cp.n_ctx = 512;
    cp.n_batch = 64;
    cp.n_ubatch = 32;
    cp.n_threads = cp.n_threads_batch = 1;
    llama_context_ptr ctx(llama_init_from_model(model.get(), cp));
    if (!ctx) return 4;
    std::ofstream rows(argv[2], std::ios::binary);
    rows.exceptions(std::ios::failbit | std::ios::badbit);
    const int nv = llama_vocab_n_tokens(llama_model_get_vocab(model.get()));
    llama_batch_ext_ptr batch(llama_batch_ext_init(ctx.get()));
    int64_t pp_ns = 0, tg_ns = 0;
    for (int req = 0; req < 3; ++req) {
        llama_memory_clear(llama_get_memory(ctx.get()), true);
        uint32_t pos = 0;
        const int length = req == 1 ? 13 : 41;
        std::vector<llama_token> tokens;
        for (int i = 0; i < length; ++i) tokens.push_back(3 + ((i * 17 + req % 2) % (nv - 3)));
        for (int step = 0; step <= tg; ++step) {
            llama_batch_ext_clear(batch.get());
            for (const auto token : tokens) {
                const int i = llama_batch_ext_add_token(batch.get(), 0, token);
                llama_pos pp[4] = {(llama_pos) pos, (llama_pos) pos, (llama_pos) pos, 0};
                if (i < 0 || !llama_batch_ext_set_pos(batch.get(), i, pp) ||
                    !llama_batch_ext_set_output_logits(batch.get(), i, true)) return 5;
                ++pos;
            }
            const auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
            if (llama_process(ctx.get(), LLAMA_PROCESS_TYPE_DECODE, batch.get()) != 0) return 6;
            llama_synchronize(ctx.get());
            const auto elapsed = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - start;
            (step == 0 ? pp_ns : tg_ns) += std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::nanoseconds>(elapsed).count();
            llama_token next = 0;
            for (size_t i = 0; i < tokens.size(); ++i) {
                const float * logits = llama_get_logits_ith(ctx.get(), int(i));
                if (!logits || !std::all_of(logits, logits + nv, [](float x) { return std::isfinite(x); })) return 7;
                rows.write(reinterpret_cast<const char *>(logits), nv * sizeof(float));
                next = int(std::max_element(logits, logits + nv) - logits);
            }
            std::cout << next << (step == tg ? '\n' : ' ');
            tokens.assign(1, next);
        }
    }
    std::cerr << "R20_SYNTHETIC_TIMINGS {\"pp_ns\":" << pp_ns << ",\"tg_ns\":" << tg_ns
              << ",\"pp_tokens\":95,\"tg_tokens\":" << 3 * tg << "}\n";
    return 0;
}

===== SOURCE FILE tests_cpp/r20_ds41_fixture_make.cpp =====
// Reuse the fixture metadata and seeded tensor fillers from the pinned tree.
// The dedicated Q6_K table and 256-element rows activate the R19 graph guard.
#define main upstream_arch_tool_main
#include "test-llama-archs.cpp"
#undef main

static void fill_fixture_tensor(ggml_tensor *tensor, void *user) {
    if (tensor->type != GGML_TYPE_Q6_K && tensor->type != GGML_TYPE_IQ3_XXS) {
        set_tensor_data(tensor, user);
        return;
    }

    const auto &params = *static_cast<tensor_data_params *>(user);
    std::vector<float> floats(ggml_nelements(tensor));
    fill_normal(floats, tensor->name, params);
    std::vector<uint8_t> quantized(ggml_nbytes(tensor));
    const std::vector<float> importance(tensor->ne[0], 1.0f);
    const size_t written = ggml_quantize_chunk(tensor->type, floats.data(), quantized.data(), 0,
                                               ggml_nrows(tensor), tensor->ne[0], importance.data());
    GGML_ASSERT(written == quantized.size());
    ggml_backend_tensor_set(tensor, quantized.data(), 0, quantized.size());
}

int main(int argc, char **argv) {
    if (argc != 2) return 2;
    ggml_backend_load_all();
    llama_backend_init();
    auto meta = get_gguf_ctx(LLM_ARCH_DEEPSEEK41, true);
    kv_writer kv(LLM_ARCH_DEEPSEEK41, meta.get());
    kv.add_kv(LLM_KV_CONTEXT_LENGTH, uint32_t(512));
    kv.add_kv(LLM_KV_EMBEDDING_LENGTH_PER_LAYER, uint32_t(256));
    kv.add_kv(LLM_KV_EXPERT_COUNT, uint32_t(4));
    kv.add_kv(LLM_KV_EXPERT_USED_COUNT, uint32_t(2));
    kv.add_kv(LLM_KV_EMBEDDING_LENGTH, uint32_t(256));
    kv.add_kv(LLM_KV_EXPERT_FEED_FORWARD_LENGTH, uint32_t(256));

    ggml_tensor hint{};
    hint.type = GGML_TYPE_Q6_K;
    ggml_set_name(&hint, "per_layer_token_embd.weight");
    gguf_add_tensor(meta.get(), &hint);
    hint.type = GGML_TYPE_IQ3_XXS;
    for (int layer = 0; layer < 6; ++layer) {
        for (const char *kind : {"gate", "up", "down"}) {
            ggml_format_name(&hint, "blk.%d.ffn_%s_exps.weight", layer, kind);
            gguf_add_tensor(meta.get(), &hint);
        }
    }
    auto model_params = llama_model_default_params();
    ggml_backend_dev_t devices[] = {nullptr};
    model_params.devices = devices;
    tensor_data_params filler{123, .1f};
    init_normal_pool(filler.seed, filler.stdev);
    llama_model_ptr model(llama_model_init_from_user(meta.get(), fill_fixture_tensor, &filler, model_params));
    if (!model) return 3;
    llama_model_save_to_file(model.get(), argv[1]);
    return 0;
}

===== SOURCE FILE tests_cpp/r20_engram_measure.cpp =====
#include "engram/graph_stage.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <atomic>
#include <chrono>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <numeric>
#include <random>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <thread>
#include <vector>

using clock_type = std::chrono::steady_clock;
using cache_type = llama_exp::engram_row_cache;

static void require(bool ok, const char * message) {
    if (!ok) throw std::runtime_error(message);
}

// Exercise the interval after foreground miss lookup but before publication.
// The callback queues its own id, just as a different graph could during this read.
// Waiting for the queue to drain per iteration makes this interleaving reproducible.
static int duplicate() {
    constexpr size_t count = 512, width = 210;
    cache_type *cache_ptr = nullptr;
    const auto foreground = std::this_thread::get_id();
    std::atomic<size_t> reads{0};
    cache_type cache([&](uint64_t id, uint8_t * dest, size_t bytes) {
        ++reads;
        if (std::this_thread::get_id() == foreground)
            require(cache_ptr->prefetch(&id, 1) == 1, "enqueue");
        for (size_t j = 0; j < bytes; ++j) dest[j] = uint8_t(id + j * 15);
        return true;
    }, count, width, 8 * width, 8);
    cache_ptr = &cache;
    std::vector<uint8_t> out(width);
    const auto start = clock_type::now();
    for (size_t id = 0; id < count; ++id) {
        require(cache.get(id, out.data(), width) == cache_type::read_result::source_read, "miss");
        for (size_t j = 0; j < width; ++j) require(out[j] == uint8_t(id + j * 15), "row mismatch");
        while (cache.snapshot().queued_or_inflight) {
            require(clock_type::now() - start < std::chrono::seconds(15), "worker timeout");
            std::this_thread::yield();
        }
    }
    std::cout << "{\"rows\":" << count << ",\"reader_calls\":" << reads
              << ",\"worker_reads\":" << cache.snapshot().background_reads << "}\n";
    return 0;
}

static int stage(bool warm) {
    constexpr size_t width = 210, stride = 256, table_rows = 65536, per_layer = 32;
    const size_t groups = warm ? 50000 : 5000;
    std::vector<uint8_t> backing(table_rows * stride);
    std::mt19937 rng(754109);
    for (auto &byte : backing) byte = uint8_t(rng());
    llama_exp::mapped_rows source(backing.data(), backing.size(), stride, width);
    llama_exp::engram_graph_stage adapter(source, table_rows, 512 * width);
    std::vector<int32_t> ids(groups * per_layer);
    for (auto & id : ids) id = rng() % (warm ? 256 : table_rows);
    // Prime the 256-row hot set exactly; the cold scenario has the same setup.
    std::vector<int32_t> prime(256);
    std::iota(prime.begin(), prime.end(), 0);
    require(adapter.stage(prime.data(), prime.size()), "prime");
    const auto before = adapter.counters();
    const auto start = clock_type::now();
    uint64_t digest = 0;
    for (size_t group = 0; group < groups; ++group) {
        require(adapter.stage(ids.data() + group * per_layer, per_layer), "stage");
        digest += adapter.data()[(group * 17) % adapter.bytes()];
    }
    const auto ns = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::nanoseconds>(clock_type::now() - start).count();
    const auto after = adapter.counters();
    // Outside the timed region, compare every output byte from 256 mixed groups.
    for (size_t group = 0; group < 256; ++group) {
        auto * requested = ids.data() + group * per_layer;
        require(adapter.stage(requested, per_layer), "verify stage");
        for (size_t r = 0; r < per_layer; ++r)
            require(std::memcmp(adapter.data() + r * width, backing.data() + requested[r] * stride, width) == 0, "bytes");
    }
    std::cout << "{\"rows\":" << ids.size() << ",\"stage_calls\":" << groups
              << ",\"wall_ns\":" << ns << ",\"digest\":" << digest
              << ",\"hits\":" << after.hit - before.hit << ",\"source_reads\":"
              << after.synchronous_reads - before.synchronous_reads << "}\n";
    return 0;
}

int main(int argc, char ** argv) {
    if (argc == 2 && std::strcmp(argv[1], "duplicate") == 0) return duplicate();
    if (argc == 2 && std::strcmp(argv[1], "warm") == 0) return stage(true);
    if (argc == 2 && std::strcmp(argv[1], "cold") == 0) return stage(false);
    return 2;
}

===== SOURCE FILE tests_cpp/r20_iq3_dot.cpp =====
// Direct GGML CPU dot product, packed IQ3_XXS weights (little endian).
#include "ggml.h"
#include "ggml-cpu.h"
#include <chrono>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <fstream>
#include <iostream>
#include <vector>

static uint32_t rnd(uint32_t &s) {
    s ^= s << 13; s ^= s >> 17; s ^= s << 5;
    return s;
}

int main(int argc, char **argv) {
    if (argc != 5 && argc != 6) return 2;
    const int width = std::atoi(argv[1]), rows = std::atoi(argv[2]), passes = std::atoi(argv[3]);
    if (width <= 0 || width % 256 || rows <= 0 || passes <= 0) return 2;
    ggml_cpu_init();
    const size_t stride = ggml_row_size(GGML_TYPE_IQ3_XXS, width);
    const size_t ystride = ggml_row_size(GGML_TYPE_Q8_K, width);
    if (stride != size_t(width/256)*98 || ystride != size_t(width/256)*292) return 3;
    std::vector<uint8_t> x(rows*stride), y(ystride);
    std::vector<float> out(rows);
    uint32_t seed = 0x1badf00d;
    for (uint8_t &v : x) v = uint8_t(rnd(seed));
    for (size_t i=0; i<x.size(); i+=98) {
        const uint16_t fp16 = uint16_t(0x2800 + (rnd(seed) & 0xfff));
        std::memcpy(x.data()+i, &fp16, 2);
    }
    if (argc == 6) {
        std::ifstream packed(argv[5], std::ios::binary);
        if (!packed.read(reinterpret_cast<char *>(x.data()), x.size()) || packed.peek() != EOF) return 5;
    }
    for (uint8_t &v : y) v = uint8_t(rnd(seed));
    for (size_t i=0; i<y.size(); i+=292) {
        const float delta = float(1+(rnd(seed)%32))/128.f;
        std::memcpy(y.data()+i, &delta, 4);
        y[i+4] = 128;  // signed int8 minimum exercises wrapping negation
    }
    const auto dot = ggml_get_type_traits_cpu(GGML_TYPE_IQ3_XXS)->vec_dot;
    auto run = [&] {
        for (int r=0; r<rows; ++r)
            dot(width, &out[r], 0, x.data()+size_t(r)*stride, 0, y.data(), 0, 1);
    };
    run();
    const auto begin = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    for (int p=0; p<passes; ++p) run();
    const auto end = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    std::ofstream file(argv[4], std::ios::binary);
    file.write(reinterpret_cast<const char *>(out.data()), out.size()*sizeof(float));
    std::cout << std::chrono::duration<double,std::nano>(end-begin).count()/(rows*double(passes)) << "\n";
    return file.good() ? 0 : 4;
}

===== SOURCE FILE tests_cpp/r20_mapped_weight_probe.cpp =====
// Verify file-backed vs ordinary host allocation using the actual model loader.
#include "ggml-backend.h"
#include "llama-cpp.h"
#include "llama-model.h"
#include <iostream>
#include <string>
int main(int argc, char **argv) {
    if (argc != 4) return 2;
    const bool mapped = std::string(argv[2]) == "mmap", expected = std::string(argv[3]) == "eligible";
    ggml_backend_load_all();
    llama_backend_init();
    auto params = llama_model_default_params();
    ggml_backend_dev_t cpu[] = {nullptr};
    params.devices = cpu;
    params.n_gpu_layers = 0;
    params.load_mode = mapped ? LLAMA_LOAD_MODE_MMAP : LLAMA_LOAD_MODE_NONE;
    params.lazy_mode = LLAMA_LAZY_MODE_OFF;
    llama_model_ptr model(llama_model_load_from_file(argv[1], params));
    if (!model) return 3;
    int total = 0, eligible = 0;
    for (int il = 0; il < 6; ++il) {
        for (auto op : {"gate","up","down"}) {
            const auto name = "blk." + std::to_string(il) + ".ffn_" + op + "_exps.weight";
            const auto * w = model->get_tensor(name.c_str());
            if (!w || !w->buffer || !w->data || !ggml_backend_buffer_is_host(w->buffer)) return 4;
            ++total;
            eligible += model->moe_readahead_eligible(w);
        }
    }
    std::cout << "{\"mapped\":" << mapped << ",\"host_expert_tensors\":" << total << ",\"eligible\":" << eligible << "}\n";
    return eligible == (expected ? total : 0) ? 0 : 5;
}

static void fill(ggml_tensor * t,std::mt19937 & r) {

}
int main(){auto * cpu=ggml_backend_cpu_init();if(!cpu)return 2;
 for(int t:{1,4,16})for(auto ty:{GGML_TYPE_Q3_K,GGML_TYPE_Q4_K,GGML_TYPE_IQ3_XXS,GGML_TYPE_IQ3_S,GGML_TYPE_IQ2_S,GGML_TYPE_IQ2_XXS,GGML_TYPE_IQ2_XS,GGML_TYPE_MXFP4,GGML_TYPE_Q8_0})if(!test(cpu,ty,ty,ty,512,256,t,false))return 1;
 if(!test(cpu,GGML_TYPE_Q4_K,GGML_TYPE_Q3_K,GGML_TYPE_Q8_0,512,256,16,false))return 1;
 if(!test(cpu,GGML_TYPE_IQ3_XXS,GGML_TYPE_IQ3_XXS,GGML_TYPE_Q3_K,2304,5120,16,false))return 1;
 ggml_backend_free(cpu);puts("PASS exact gate-up activations and final FFN outputs; duplicate, empty and partitioned routes");}

===== SOURCE FILE tests_cpp/test_moe_plan.cpp =====
// Tests the literal moe_cache_slru extracted from the patched source file.
// Only the assertion/abort macros are supplied by this standalone CPU harness.
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <iostream>
#include <limits>
#include <numeric>
#include <random>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#define GGML_ASSERT(condition) do { if (!(condition)) { std::abort(); } } while (0)
#define GGML_ABORT(...) std::abort()
#include "planner_under_test.h"

static void require(bool value, const char * message) {
    if (!value) {
        throw std::runtime_error(message);
    }
}

struct state {
    moe_cache_slru cache;
    std::vector<std::vector<int32_t>> maps;
    std::vector<moe_cache_slru::fill> fills;

    state(int32_t nl, int32_t ne, int32_t ns) : maps(nl, std::vector<int32_t>(ne, -1)) {
        cache.init(nl, ne, ns);
        for (int32_t il = 0; il < nl; ++il) {
            cache.slot_map[il] = maps[il].data();
        }
    }
    state(const state &) = delete;
    state & operator=(const state &) = delete;
};

struct request {
    int32_t layer;
    std::vector<int32_t> ids;
};

static void invariants(const state & s) {
    const auto & c = s.cache;
    std::vector<bool> visited(c.n_slots, false);
    int protected_count = 0;
    for (int segment = 0; segment < 2; ++segment) {
        int32_t previous = -1;
        int count = 0;
        for (int32_t slot = c.head[segment]; slot >= 0; slot = c.next[slot]) {
            require(slot < c.n_slots && !visited[slot], "invalid/cyclic SLRU list");
            visited[slot] = true;
            require(c.prev[slot] == previous, "SLRU previous link mismatch");
