            require(c.is_protected[slot] == bool(segment), "SLRU segment mismatch");
            previous = slot;
            ++count;
        }
        require(previous == c.tail[segment], "SLRU tail mismatch");
        if (segment) {
            protected_count = count;
        }
    }
    require(std::all_of(visited.begin(), visited.end(), [](bool v) { return v; }), "unlinked slot");
    require(c.n_protected == protected_count && protected_count <= c.n_slots/2, "protected count mismatch");
    for (int32_t slot = 0; slot < c.n_slots; ++slot) {
        const int32_t key = c.key_of[slot];
        if (key >= 0) {
            require(size_t(key/c.n_expert) < s.maps.size(), "invalid key layer");
            require(s.maps[key/c.n_expert][key%c.n_expert] == slot, "key/map mismatch");
        }
    }
    for (size_t il = 0; il < s.maps.size(); ++il) {
        require(c.slot_map[il] == s.maps[il].data(), "slot map pointer changed");
        for (int32_t expert = 0; expert < c.n_expert; ++expert) {
            const int32_t slot = s.maps[il][expert];
            require(slot >= -1 && slot < c.n_slots, "invalid slot index");
            if (slot >= 0) {
                require(c.key_of[slot] == int32_t(il)*c.n_expert + expert, "map/key mismatch");
            }
        }
    }
}

static void equal_state(const state & a, const state & b) {
    const auto & x = a.cache;
    const auto & y = b.cache;
    require(x.n_expert == y.n_expert && x.n_slots == y.n_slots, "geometry differs");
    require(a.maps == b.maps && x.key_of == y.key_of, "slot assignments differ");
    require(x.prev == y.prev && x.next == y.next, "LRU links differ");
    require(x.head[0] == y.head[0] && x.head[1] == y.head[1] &&
            x.tail[0] == y.tail[0] && x.tail[1] == y.tail[1], "LRU endpoints differ");
    require(x.is_protected == y.is_protected && x.n_protected == y.n_protected, "SLRU segments differ");
    require(x.seen_gen == y.seen_gen && x.seen == y.seen, "dedup generations differ");
    require(a.fills.size() == b.fills.size(), "fill count differs");
    for (size_t i = 0; i < a.fills.size(); ++i) {
        require(a.fills[i].expert == b.fills[i].expert && a.fills[i].slot == b.fills[i].slot,
                "ordered fills differ");
    }
    // uniq is internal scratch. The new path retains only misses or skips sorting
    // for all-hit requests; all cache state and observable plan outputs are checked.
}

static uint64_t compared_updates = 0;
static void compare_update(state & legacy, state & reuse, const request & r) {
    size_t hits_old = size_t(-1), hits_new = size_t(-1);
    const bool old_ok = legacy.cache.plan(r.layer, r.ids.data(), r.ids.size(), legacy.fills, hits_old);
    const bool new_ok = reuse.cache.plan_reuse(r.layer, r.ids.data(), r.ids.size(), reuse.fills, hits_new);
    require(old_ok == new_ok && hits_old == hits_new, "return value or hits differ");
    equal_state(legacy, reuse);
    invariants(legacy);
    invariants(reuse);
    if (new_ok) {
        for (int32_t e : r.ids) {
            require(reuse.maps[r.layer][e] >= 0, "selected expert was evicted");
        }
    } else {
        require(hits_new == 0 && reuse.fills.empty(), "failed plan has hit/fill output");
    }
    ++compared_updates;
}

static std::vector<int32_t> sequence(int n) {
    std::vector<int32_t> result(n);
    std::iota(result.begin(), result.end(), 0);
    return result;
}

static void differential_tests() {
    const int geometries[][3] = {
        {1, 1, 1}, {1, 2, 1}, {1, 7, 3}, {1, 16, 8}, {1, 128, 64}, {1, 512, 128},
        {2, 7, 1}, {2, 7, 7}, {2, 16, 24}, {4, 16, 8}, {4, 63, 64},
        {8, 128, 128}, {8, 512, 256}, {17, 63, 1024},
    };
    std::mt19937_64 rng(0xd2a7ce195ULL);
    for (const auto & geometry : geometries) {
        const int nl = geometry[0], ne = geometry[1], ns = geometry[2];
        state legacy(nl, ne, ns), reuse(nl, ne, ns);
        for (int iteration = 0; iteration < 5000; ++iteration) {
            if (iteration == 100 || iteration == 3000) {
                legacy.cache.seen_gen = reuse.cache.seen_gen = std::numeric_limits<uint32_t>::max() - 1;
            }
            request r{int32_t(rng()%nl), {}};
            const int count = int(rng()%(std::min(ne*2, 192) + 1));
            const int mode = int(rng()%6);
            if (mode == 3) {
                for (int e = 0; e < ne; ++e) {
                    if (legacy.maps[r.layer][e] >= 0) { r.ids.push_back(e); }
                }
                std::shuffle(r.ids.begin(), r.ids.end(), rng);
            } else if (mode >= 4) {
                r.ids = sequence(ne);
                std::shuffle(r.ids.begin(), r.ids.end(), rng);
                r.ids.resize(mode == 4 ? size_t(rng()%(ne + 1)) : size_t(std::min(ne, ns)));
            } else {
                const int single = int(rng()%ne);
                for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
                    r.ids.push_back(mode == 1 ? single : int(rng()%(mode == 2 ? std::max(1, ne/8) : ne)));
                }
            }
            compare_update(legacy, reuse, r);
        }
    }
    // Exhaust probation, then continue into protected while each filled slot is
    // appended to probation. Repeated legacy scans must choose identical victims.
    for (int ns : {2, 4, 8, 64, 128, 256}) {
        state legacy(2, ns*2, ns), reuse(2, ns*2, ns);
        compare_update(legacy, reuse, {0, sequence(ns)});
        for (int cycle = 0; cycle < 256; ++cycle) {
            const int layer = cycle%2;
            auto hits = sequence(ns/2);
            std::shuffle(hits.begin(), hits.end(), rng);
            compare_update(legacy, reuse, {layer, hits});
            auto misses = sequence(ns);
            std::reverse(misses.begin(), misses.end());
            compare_update(legacy, reuse, {1-layer, misses});
        }
    }
    // The production fills vector is shared by groups. Vary its existing content
    // and capacity, including calls that fail before promotion or eviction.
    state a0(2, 7, 3), b0(2, 7, 3), a1(4, 79, 64), b1(4, 79, 64);
    for (int i = 0; i < 1000; ++i) {
        a0.fills.swap(a1.fills);
        b0.fills.swap(b1.fills);
        compare_update(a0, b0, {i%2, {int32_t(i%7), int32_t((i+1)%7), int32_t((i+2)%7)}});
        a0.fills.swap(a1.fills);
        b0.fills.swap(b1.fills);
        compare_update(a1, b1, {i%4, sequence(i%67)});
    }
    std::cout << "{\"type\":\"differential\",\"status\":\"PASS\",\"updates\":"
              << compared_updates << ",\"random_geometries\":14,\"generation_wrap_tested\":true}\n";
}

struct workload {
    const char * name;
    int nl, ne, ns;
    std::vector<request> warmup;
    std::vector<request> requests;
};

static std::vector<workload> workloads() {
    std::mt19937_64 rng(0xb34c4801ULL);
    std::vector<workload> result;
    for (const auto & shape : std::vector<std::pair<int, int>>{{32, 6}, {32, 10}, {128, 256}}) {
        workload w{shape.second == 6 ? "all_hit_top6" : shape.first == 32 ? "all_hit_short" : "all_hit_window",
                   1, 512, 256, {{0, sequence(shape.first)}}, {}};
        for (int k = 0; k < 128; ++k) {
            request r{0, {}};
            for (int i = 0; i < shape.second; ++i) { r.ids.push_back(int32_t(rng()%shape.first)); }
            w.requests.push_back(std::move(r));
        }
        result.push_back(std::move(w));
    }
    for (int count : {6, 10}) {
        workload w{count == 6 ? "mixed_top6" : "mixed_top10", 1, 512, 128, {{0, sequence(64)}}, {}};
        for (int k = 0; k < 512; ++k) {
            request r{0, {}};
            for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
                r.ids.push_back(int32_t(i < (count*2)/3 ? rng()%64 : 64 + rng()%448));
            }
            std::shuffle(r.ids.begin(), r.ids.end(), rng);
            w.requests.push_back(std::move(r));
        }
        result.push_back(std::move(w));
    }
    workload mixed{"mixed_hit_window", 1, 512, 128, {{0, sequence(64)}}, {}};
    for (int k = 0; k < 512; ++k) {
        request r{0, {}};
        for (int i = 0; i < 48; ++i) { r.ids.push_back(int32_t(i < 32 ? rng()%64 : 64 + rng()%448)); }
        std::shuffle(r.ids.begin(), r.ids.end(), rng);
        mixed.requests.push_back(std::move(r));
    }
    result.push_back(std::move(mixed));
    workload cold{"all_miss_stream", 2, 512, 128, {}, {}};
    for (int k = 0; k < 128; ++k) {
        auto ids = sequence(128);
        std::shuffle(ids.begin(), ids.end(), rng);
        cold.requests.push_back({k%2, std::move(ids)});
    }
    result.push_back(std::move(cold));
    workload tiny{"tiny_random", 1, 16, 4, {}, {}};
    for (int k = 0; k < 256; ++k) { tiny.requests.push_back({0, {int32_t(rng()%16)}}); }
    result.push_back(std::move(tiny));
    workload churn{"shared_layers", 8, 128, 64, {}, {}};
    for (int k = 0; k < 256; ++k) {
        request r{int32_t(rng()%8), {}};
        for (int i = 0; i < 24; ++i) { r.ids.push_back(int32_t(rng()%128)); }
        churn.requests.push_back(std::move(r));
    }
    result.push_back(std::move(churn));
    workload scan{"probation_exhaustion", 2, 256, 128, {{0, sequence(128)}}, {}};
    for (int layer : {0, 1}) {
        auto hits = sequence(64), misses = sequence(128);
        std::shuffle(hits.begin(), hits.end(), rng);
        std::shuffle(misses.begin(), misses.end(), rng);
        scan.requests.push_back({layer, std::move(hits)});
        scan.requests.push_back({1-layer, std::move(misses)});
    }
    result.push_back(std::move(scan));
    return result;
}

struct measurement { double ns_per_plan; uint64_t checksum, hits, misses; };
static volatile uint64_t benchmark_sink = 0;

template<bool reuse>
static measurement measure(const workload & w, int iterations) {
    state s(w.nl, w.ne, w.ns);
    auto apply = [&](const request & r, size_t & hit) {
        if constexpr (reuse) {
            return s.cache.plan_reuse(r.layer, r.ids.data(), r.ids.size(), s.fills, hit);
        } else {
            return s.cache.plan(r.layer, r.ids.data(), r.ids.size(), s.fills, hit);
        }
    };
    size_t hit = 0;
    for (const request & r : w.warmup) { require(apply(r, hit), "warmup capacity"); }
    for (const request & r : w.requests) { require(apply(r, hit), "stream warmup capacity"); }
    uint64_t hits = 0, misses = 0;
    const auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    for (int i = 0; i < iterations; ++i) {
        const request & r = w.requests[size_t(i)%w.requests.size()];
        require(apply(r, hit), "benchmark capacity");
        hits += hit;
        misses += s.fills.size();
    }
    const auto stop = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    uint64_t checksum = 1469598103934665603ULL;
    for (const auto & map : s.maps) {
        for (int32_t value : map) { checksum = (checksum ^ uint32_t(value))*1099511628211ULL; }
    }
    for (const auto * values : {&s.cache.key_of, &s.cache.prev, &s.cache.next}) {
        for (int32_t value : *values) { checksum = (checksum ^ uint32_t(value))*1099511628211ULL; }
    }
    benchmark_sink = checksum;
    return {std::chrono::duration<double, std::nano>(stop-start).count()/iterations, checksum, hits, misses};
}

static void failed_upload_recovery() {
    state s(2, 8, 4);
    const int32_t ids[] = {2, 5};
    size_t hits = 0;
    for (int layer : {0, 0, 1, 1}) {
        require(s.cache.plan(layer, ids, 2, s.fills, hits), "initial plan");
    }
    s.cache.invalidate_banks();
    invariants(s);
    for (int layer : {0, 1}) {
        require(s.cache.plan_reuse(layer, ids, 2, s.fills, hits), "retry plan");
        require(hits == 0 && s.fills.size() == 2, "partial bank reused on retry");
    }
}

int main(int argc, char ** argv) {
    try {
        failed_upload_recovery();
        differential_tests();
        if (argc > 1 && std::string(argv[1]) == "--bench") {
            const int iterations = argc > 2 ? std::stoi(argv[2]) : 100000;
            const int repeats = argc > 3 ? std::stoi(argv[3]) : 7;
            const std::string selected = argc > 4 ? std::string(",") + argv[4] + "," : "";
            int measured_cases = 0;
            require(iterations > 0 && repeats > 0, "positive benchmark dimensions required");
            for (const workload & w : workloads()) {
                if (!selected.empty() && selected.find(std::string(",") + w.name + ",") == std::string::npos) {
                    continue;
                }
                ++measured_cases;
                // Tiny plans need longer samples than window-sized plans to avoid
                // treating scheduler jitter in a 1-2 ms run as an optimization.
                const int case_iterations = std::max(iterations,
                    std::string(w.name) == "tiny_random" ? 5000000 :
                    (std::string(w.name) == "all_hit_short" || std::string(w.name) == "all_hit_top6" ||
                     std::string(w.name) == "mixed_top6" || std::string(w.name) == "mixed_top10") ? 1000000 : 1);
                for (int repeat = 0; repeat < repeats; ++repeat) {
                    measurement old, updated;
                    if (repeat%2) { updated = measure<true>(w, case_iterations); old = measure<false>(w, case_iterations); }
                    else { old = measure<false>(w, case_iterations); updated = measure<true>(w, case_iterations); }
                    require(old.checksum == updated.checksum && old.hits == updated.hits && old.misses == updated.misses,
                            "benchmark traces disagree");
                    std::cout << "{\"type\":\"benchmark\",\"case\":\"" << w.name << "\",\"sample\":" << repeat
                              << ",\"iterations\":" << case_iterations << ",\"baseline_ns_per_plan\":" << old.ns_per_plan
                              << ",\"reuse_ns_per_plan\":" << updated.ns_per_plan << ",\"speedup\":" << old.ns_per_plan/updated.ns_per_plan
                              << ",\"hits\":" << old.hits << ",\"misses\":" << old.misses << "}\n";
                }
            }
            require(measured_cases > 0, "no benchmark case matched");
        }
    } catch (const std::exception & error) {
        std::cerr << error.what() << '\n';
        return 1;
    }
}

===== SOURCE FILE tools/analyze_p15c_trace.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Analyze opt-in P15C CUDA MMQ *host selector* stderr JSONL.

No CUDA commands or subprocesses. Results are not GPU timings or tok/s.
Example: python3 tools/analyze_p15c_trace.py run.log
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys
from typing import Any

PREFIX = "P15C_MMQ_SELECT_JSON "
REQUIRED = {"thread", "type", "moe", "ncols_bucket", "calls", "cache_hits", "select_ns", "max_select_ns"}


def checked_int(value: Any, name: str, low: int = 0) -> int:
    if type(value) is not int or value < low:
        raise ValueError(f"{name}: expected integer >= {low}")
    return value


def accumulate(lines) -> dict:
    by_key: dict[tuple[int, bool, int], dict] = {}
    count, overflow, threads = 0, 0, set()
    for line_no, line in enumerate(lines, 1):
        if not line.startswith(PREFIX):
            continue
        if len(line) > 4096:
            raise ValueError(f"line {line_no}: oversized profile record")
        try:
            item = json.loads(line[len(PREFIX):])
        except json.JSONDecodeError as err:
            raise ValueError(f"line {line_no}: invalid profile JSON: {err}") from err
        if not isinstance(item, dict):
            raise ValueError(f"line {line_no}: expected a JSON object")
        thread = checked_int(item.get("thread"), "thread", 1)
        threads.add(thread)
        if "overflow" in item:
            if set(item) != {"thread", "overflow"}:
                raise ValueError(f"line {line_no}: malformed overflow record")
            overflow += checked_int(item["overflow"], "overflow", 1)
            continue
        if set(item) != REQUIRED or type(item["moe"]) is not bool:
            raise ValueError(f"line {line_no}: unsupported profile fields")
        type_id = checked_int(item["type"], "type")
        bucket = checked_int(item["ncols_bucket"], "ncols_bucket")
        if bucket > 5:
            raise ValueError(f"line {line_no}: unknown column bucket")
        calls = checked_int(item["calls"], "calls", 1)
        hits = checked_int(item["cache_hits"], "cache_hits")
        elapsed = checked_int(item["select_ns"], "select_ns")
        max_elapsed = checked_int(item["max_select_ns"], "max_select_ns")
        if hits > calls or max_elapsed > elapsed:
            raise ValueError(f"line {line_no}: internally inconsistent counters")
        key = (type_id, item["moe"], bucket)
        row = by_key.setdefault(key, {"type": type_id, "moe": item["moe"],
                       "ncols_bucket": bucket, "calls": 0, "cache_hits": 0,
                       "select_ns": 0, "max_select_ns": 0})
        row["calls"] += calls
        row["cache_hits"] += hits
        row["select_ns"] += elapsed
        row["max_select_ns"] = max(row["max_select_ns"], max_elapsed)
        count += 1
    if count == 0:
        raise ValueError("no valid P15C profile records found")
    groups = []
    for row in by_key.values():
        c = row["calls"]
        groups.append({**row, "avg_select_ns": round(row["select_ns"]/c, 2),
                       "cache_hit_rate": round(row["cache_hits"]/c, 6)})
    groups.sort(key=lambda x: (-x["select_ns"], -x["calls"], x["type"], x["ncols_bucket"]))
    total_calls = sum(x["calls"] for x in groups)
    total_ns = sum(x["select_ns"] for x in groups)
    hits = sum(x["cache_hits"] for x in groups)
    return {"kind":"HOST_TILE_SELECTOR_ONLY", "records":count,
            "threads":len(threads), "dropped_samples":overflow,
            "total_calls":total_calls, "total_cache_hits":hits,
            "host_selection_time_ms":round(total_ns/1e6, 6),
            "avg_select_ns_per_call":round(total_ns/total_calls, 2),
            "groups":groups,
            "warnings":(["Overflow occurred: rankings are incomplete"] if overflow else []) +
              ["This is CPU-side tile-selection instrumentation, not GPU kernel time or tok/s"]}


def read_logs(paths: list[Path]) -> dict:
    def source():
        for path in paths:
            with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:
                yield from f
    return accumulate(source())


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("logs", type=Path, nargs="+", help="Captured stderr log(s) from P15C profile")
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, help="Write summary JSON to a new file (no overwrite)")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    try:
        report = read_logs(args.logs)
        serialized = json.dumps(report, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n"
        if args.output:
            with args.output.open("x", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write(serialized)
        else:
            sys.stdout.write(serialized)
        return 0
    except (ValueError, OSError) as err:
        parser.error(str(err))
    return 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/auto_memory.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Startup memory planner using llama.cpp's own no-allocation estimator.

This does not resize an active context. Prompt files are raw text; a configurable
headroom accounts for chat formatting. An exact formatted token count can be supplied.
"""
import argparse, json, math, os, re, resource, subprocess
from pathlib import Path

def context_for(required, native, fallback):
    if required is None:
        return min(fallback, native)
    if required > native:
        raise ValueError('Input plus output/headroom exceeds the model context limit')
    if required < 1:
        raise ValueError('Required context must be positive')
    return min(native, max(4096, 1 << (required-1).bit_length()))

def memory_rows(text):
    rows = {}
    for line in text.splitlines():
        m = re.fullmatch(r'(CUDA\d+|Host)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)\s*', line)
        if m:
            if m[1] in rows:
                raise ValueError('Duplicate memory-estimator device')
            rows[m[1]] = dict(zip(['model_mib','context_mib','compute_mib'],map(int,m.groups()[1:])))
    if set(rows) != {'CUDA0','Host'}:
        raise ValueError('This startup adapter currently requires one CUDA device and one Host row')
    return rows

def choose_cache(cap, available, reserve, measure, step=128):
    if available <= reserve:
        raise ValueError('Insufficient free VRAM for the requested reserve')
    budget = available - reserve
    low, high, best = 0, cap//step, None
    while low <= high:
        mid=(low+high)//2
        size=mid*step
        used=measure(size)
        if not math.isfinite(used) or used < 0:
            raise ValueError('Invalid estimated memory size')
        if used <= budget:
            best=size;low=mid+1
        else:
            high=mid-1
    if best is None or best < step:
        raise ValueError('Requested context and useful expert cache do not fit; no context or precision was reduced')
    return best

def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('--binary',type=Path,required=True)
    p.add_argument('--model',type=Path,required=True)
    p.add_argument('--profile',type=Path,required=True)
    p.add_argument('--ctx',default='auto')
    inp=p.add_mutually_exclusive_group()
    inp.add_argument('--prompt-file',type=Path)
    inp.add_argument('--input-tokens',type=int,help='Exact formatted input token count')
    p.add_argument('--max-output',type=int,default=4096)
    p.add_argument('--template-headroom',type=int,default=1024)
    p.add_argument('--reserve-mib',type=int,default=2048)
    p.add_argument('--out',type=Path)
    p.add_argument('--run',action='store_true')
    p.add_argument('--port',type=int,default=8081)
    a=p.parse_args()
    if a.max_output<1 or a.template_headroom<0 or a.reserve_mib<512 or not 1<=a.port<=65535:
        p.error('Invalid output/headroom/reserve/port')
    binary=a.binary.resolve();model=a.model.resolve();fit=binary.with_name('llama-fit-params')
    if not all(x.is_file() for x in [binary,model,fit]):
        p.error('Binary, native memory estimator or model is missing; build the production tools')
    if a.out and a.out.exists():
        p.error('Output plan must be a new file')
    c=json.loads(a.profile.read_text())
    if c['parallel']!=1 or c['spec_type']!='none' or c['kv']!='f16':
        p.error('This adapter preserves the tested single-slot, F16, non-speculative profiles')
    if os.environ.get('CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES') not in [None,'','0']:
        p.error('GPU remapping needs an explicit device mapping; this adapter uses physical GPU 0')
    # Never compete with a live llama.cpp instance or restart a user service.
    for proc in Path('/proc').iterdir():
        if not proc.name.isdecimal():continue
        try:exe=(proc/'exe').resolve(strict=True)
        except (OSError,RuntimeError):continue
        if exe.name=='llama-server':
            p.error('An active llama-server exists; plan after it stops. No process was changed.')
    env={k:v for k,v in os.environ.items() if not k.startswith(('GGML_','LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_','LLAMA_ARG_'))}
    env.update(c['environment']);env['LD_LIBRARY_PATH']=str(binary.parent)
    def free_mib():
        text=subprocess.check_output(['nvidia-smi','--query-gpu=index,memory.free','--format=csv,noheader,nounits'],text=True,timeout=15)
        rows=[x.split(',') for x in text.strip().splitlines()]
        if len(rows)!=1 or rows[0][0].strip()!='0':
            raise ValueError('This startup adapter requires a single NVIDIA GPU')
        return int(rows[0][1])
    free_before=free_mib()
    if free_before<a.reserve_mib+512:
        p.error('Insufficient free VRAM for safe planning')
    measurements=[]
    def estimate(ctx,cache):
        cmd=[str(fit),'-m',str(model),'--fit-print','on','--fit','off','-lv','5',
          '-c',str(ctx),'-np','1','-ngl',str(c['ngl']),'-b',str(c['batch']),'-ub',str(c['ubatch']),
          '-t',str(c['threads']),'--cpu-mask',c['cpu_mask'],'--cpu-strict','1',
          '--cache-type-k','f16','--cache-type-v','f16','--cpu-moe','--moe-cache-mib',str(cache),'--offline']
        q=subprocess.run(cmd,env=env,capture_output=True,text=True,errors="replace",timeout=120)
        if q.returncode:
            raise RuntimeError('Native memory estimator failed: '+q.stderr[-1200:])
        rows=memory_rows(q.stdout)
        native=re.search(r'n_ctx_train\s*=\s*(\d+)',q.stderr)
        if not native:
            raise ValueError('Native context limit was not reported; refusing to guess')
        measurements.append({'ctx':ctx,'cache_mib':cache,'memory':rows})
        return rows,int(native[1])
    initial,int_native=estimate(c['ctx'],c['cache_mib'])
    n=a.input_tokens
    if a.prompt_file:
        q=subprocess.run([str(binary.with_name('llama-tokenize')),'-m',str(model),'-f',str(a.prompt_file.resolve()),'--ids','--no-escape'],env=env,capture_output=True,text=True,errors="replace",timeout=120,check=True)
        ids=json.loads(q.stdout)
        if not isinstance(ids,list) or any(type(x)!=int for x in ids):raise ValueError('Invalid native tokenizer output')
        n=len(ids)
    if n is not None and n<0:p.error('Input token count cannot be negative')
    required=None if n is None else n+a.max_output+(a.template_headroom if a.prompt_file else 0)
    ctx=context_for(required,int_native,c['ctx']) if a.ctx=='auto' else int(a.ctx)
    if ctx<1 or ctx>int_native or (required is not None and required>ctx):
        p.error('Context is outside native bounds or smaller than the request; input will not be truncated')
    available=min(free_before,free_mib())
    def used(cache):
        rows,_=estimate(ctx,cache)
        return sum(rows['CUDA0'].values())
    cache=choose_cache(c['cache_mib'],available,a.reserve_mib,used)
    final=used(cache)
    if final+a.reserve_mib>free_mib():
        raise RuntimeError('Free VRAM changed during planning; retry when the GPU is idle')
    cmd=[str(binary),'-m',str(model),'--host','127.0.0.1','--port',str(a.port),
      '--alias',c['profile'],'-np','1','-c',str(ctx),'--fit','off','-ngl',str(c['ngl']),
      '-b',str(c['batch']),'-ub',str(c['ubatch']),'-t',str(c['threads']),'--cpu-mask',c['cpu_mask'],
      '--cpu-strict','1','--cache-type-k','f16','--cache-type-v','f16','--no-warmup',
      '--no-context-shift','--offline','--spec-type','none','--cpu-moe','--moe-cache-mib',str(cache)]
    plan={'scope':'STARTUP_ONLY_NOT_LIVE_KV_RESIZING','native_context':int_native,'ctx':ctx,'cache_mib':cache,
      'input_tokens':n,'required_tokens':required,'auto_context_basis':'input plus output/headroom' if required is not None else 'existing profile default',
      'available_vram_mib':available,'reserve_mib':a.reserve_mib,'estimated_gpu_mib':final,
      'cache_cap_mib':c['cache_mib'],'measurements':measurements,'command':cmd,'environment':c['environment'],
      'precision_changed':False,'input_truncated':False,'device_scope':'single CUDA GPU; profile-specific CPU settings'}
    if a.out:
        with a.out.open('x') as stream:json.dump(plan,stream,indent=2);stream.write('\n')
    print(json.dumps(plan,indent=2),flush=True)
    if a.run:
        resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_CORE,(0,0))
        scope=['systemd-run','--user','--scope','--quiet','--unit=llama-auto-'+str(os.getpid()),
          '--property=MemoryHigh=106G','--property=MemoryMax=108G','--property=MemorySwapMax=4G',
          '--property=TasksMax=256','--']
        os.execvpe(scope[0],scope+cmd,env)

if __name__=='__main__':main()

===== SOURCE FILE tools/build.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import argparse
import datetime
import hashlib
import json
import subprocess
import shutil
from pathlib import Path

p = argparse.ArgumentParser()
p.add_argument('--source', type=Path, required=True)
p.add_argument('--build', type=Path, required=True)
p.add_argument('--arch', default='120a')
p.add_argument('--cuda-compiler', type=Path)
p.add_argument('--ui-assets', type=Path)
p.add_argument('--production-tools', action='store_true')
p.add_argument('--jobs', type=int, default=8)
a = p.parse_args()
source, build = a.source.resolve(), a.build.resolve()
if build.exists():
    p.error('Build destination must not exist; preserve previous builds')
if not (source / 'CMakeLists.txt').is_file() or not 1 <= a.jobs <= 32:
    p.error('Invalid source or jobs')
build.mkdir(parents=True)
if a.ui_assets:
    assert (a.ui_assets/'index.html').is_file()
    shutil.copytree(a.ui_assets, build/'tools/ui/dist')
configure = ['cmake', '-S', str(source), '-B', str(build),
             '-DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release', '-DCMAKE_EXPORT_COMPILE_COMMANDS=ON',
             '-DCMAKE_BUILD_RPATH_USE_ORIGIN=ON', '-DLLAMA_BUILD_COMMON=ON',
             '-DLLAMA_BUILD_TOOLS=ON', '-DLLAMA_BUILD_SERVER=ON', '-DLLAMA_BUILD_TESTS=ON',
             '-DLLAMA_BUILD_EXAMPLES=OFF', '-DLLAMA_BUILD_APP=OFF', '-DLLAMA_BUILD_UI=OFF',
             '-DLLAMA_USE_PREBUILT_UI=OFF', '-DLLAMA_CONNECT=OFF', '-DLLAMA_LLGUIDANCE=OFF',
             '-DLLAMA_USE_SYSTEM_GGML=OFF', '-DGGML_CPU=ON', '-DGGML_METAL=OFF',
             '-DGGML_CCACHE=OFF', '-DGGML_CPU_KLEIDIAI=OFF', '-DGGML_CUDA=ON',
             '-DGGML_OPENMP_FETCH=OFF', '-DGGML_CUDA_CCCL_VERSION=', '-DGGML_CUDA_NCCL=OFF',
             '-DLLAMA_BUILD_MTMD=OFF', '-DCMAKE_C_COMPILER_LAUNCHER=',
             '-DCMAKE_CXX_COMPILER_LAUNCHER=', '-DCMAKE_CUDA_COMPILER_LAUNCHER=',
             '-DGGML_NATIVE=ON', '-DGGML_OPENMP=ON', '-DCMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES=' + a.arch]
if a.production_tools:
    configure += ['-DLLAMA_BUILD_EXAMPLES=ON', '-DLLAMA_BUILD_APP=ON', '-DLLAMA_BUILD_TESTS=OFF']
if a.cuda_compiler:
    configure.append('-DCMAKE_CUDA_COMPILER=' + str(a.cuda_compiler.resolve()))
compile = ['cmake', '--build', str(build), '-j', str(a.jobs), '--target',
           'llama-server', 'llama-perplexity', 'test-backend-ops']
if a.production_tools:
    compile = ['cmake', '--build', str(build), '-j', str(a.jobs)]
record = {'status': 'BUILDING', 'source': str(source), 'commands': [configure, compile],
          'started': datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat()}
receipt = source / '.llama-overlay-receipt.json'
if receipt.is_file():
    record['overlay_receipt'] = json.loads(receipt.read_text())
(build / 'BUILD.json').write_text(json.dumps(record, indent=2))
try:
    with (build / 'build.log').open('w') as log:
        for cmd in record['commands']:
            subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
    artifacts = {x.resolve() for x in (build / 'bin').iterdir() if x.is_file()}
    record['binary_sha256'] = {x.name: hashlib.sha256(x.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for x in sorted(artifacts)}
    record['status'] = 'BUILT'
except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
    record.update(status='BUILD_FAILED', exit_code=e.returncode)
    raise
finally:
    record['finished'] = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat()
    (build / 'BUILD.json').write_text(json.dumps(record, indent=2))
print(record['status'], build)

===== SOURCE FILE tools/build_pair.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Validate and optionally compile a baseline/candidate pair; never run inference.

The default is a read-only plan (apart from an automatically removed temporary
patch reconstruction). --execute creates a NEW build root, then runs CMake
configure/build sequentially. Neither checkout is prepared or changed here.
Compiler feature probes performed by CMake are not model inference tests.

Project options were checked against llama.cpp dde879bc4f954b809892fd0d660515f42536394a:
  CMakeLists.txt, ggml/CMakeLists.txt, tools/ui/CMakeLists.txt,
  ggml/src/ggml-cuda/CMakeLists.txt. LLAMA_CURL is deprecated and is not used.
These switches disable the known dependency/UI download paths, not arbitrary
network access by an unreviewed future CMake project or compiler wrapper.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import datetime as dt
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath
import re
import shutil
import signal
import stat
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time

RECEIPT = ".llama-overlay-receipt.json"
TARGETS = ("llama-server", "llama-cli", "llama-bench")
MAX_JSON = 4 * 1024 * 1024
HASH_RE = re.compile(r"[0-9a-f]{64}\Z")
COMMIT_RE = re.compile(r"[0-9a-f]{40}\Z")

# Only explicit, reviewed options. No caller-supplied -D, -P, install target,
# toolchain/preset, arbitrary build target, shell fragment or download command.
PROJECT_OPTIONS = {
    "LLAMA_BUILD_COMMON": "ON", "LLAMA_BUILD_TOOLS": "ON",
    "LLAMA_BUILD_SERVER": "ON", "LLAMA_BUILD_TESTS": "OFF",
    "LLAMA_BUILD_EXAMPLES": "OFF", "LLAMA_BUILD_APP": "OFF",
    "LLAMA_BUILD_UI": "OFF", "LLAMA_USE_PREBUILT_UI": "OFF",
    "LLAMA_CONNECT": "OFF", "LLAMA_LLGUIDANCE": "OFF",
    "LLAMA_USE_SYSTEM_GGML": "OFF",
    "GGML_CPU": "ON", "GGML_METAL": "OFF", "GGML_CCACHE": "OFF",
    "GGML_CPU_KLEIDIAI": "OFF", "GGML_OPENMP_FETCH": "OFF",
    "GGML_CUDA_CCCL_VERSION": "", "GGML_CUDA_NCCL": "OFF",
}
DOWNLOAD_OPTIONS = {k: PROJECT_OPTIONS[k] for k in (
    "LLAMA_BUILD_UI", "LLAMA_USE_PREBUILT_UI", "LLAMA_CONNECT",
    "LLAMA_LLGUIDANCE", "GGML_CPU_KLEIDIAI", "GGML_OPENMP_FETCH",
    "GGML_CUDA_CCCL_VERSION")}


class BuildPairError(RuntimeError):
    pass


def require(condition, message):
    if not condition:
        raise BuildPairError(message)


def sha256_file(path):
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path, "rb") as handle:
        for part in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(part)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def regular_file(path):
    require(not path.is_symlink(), f"Symlink file refused: {path}")
    try:
        st = path.stat()
    except OSError as exc:
        raise BuildPairError(f"Required file is unavailable: {path}") from exc
    require(stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode), f"Regular file required: {path}")
    return path


def read_json(path):
    regular_file(path)
    require(path.stat().st_size <= MAX_JSON, f"JSON size limit exceeded: {path}")
    try:
        result = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (ValueError, UnicodeError) as exc:
        raise BuildPairError(f"Invalid JSON: {path}") from exc
    require(isinstance(result, dict), f"JSON object required: {path}")
    return result


def relpath(value):
    require(isinstance(value, str) and value and "\\" not in value and
            not any(ord(c) < 32 for c in value), "Unsafe manifest path")
    p = PurePosixPath(value)
    require(not p.is_absolute() and str(p) == value and
            all(x not in (".", "..", ".git") for x in p.parts),
            f"Unsafe manifest path: {value}")
    return value


def contained_file(root, value):
    value = relpath(value)
    p = root
    for component in PurePosixPath(value).parts:
        p = p / component
        require(not p.is_symlink(), f"Symlink in source path: {value}")
    regular_file(p)
    require(p.resolve().is_relative_to(root.resolve()), "File escapes its root")
    return p


def git_env():
    env = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if not k.startswith("GIT_")}
    env.update({"GIT_OPTIONAL_LOCKS": "0", "GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT": "0",
                "GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM": "1", "GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL": os.devnull,
                "GIT_CONFIG_SYSTEM": os.devnull, "GIT_PROTOCOL_FROM_USER": "0",
                "GIT_NO_REPLACE_OBJECTS": "1", "LC_ALL": "C"})
    return env


def git(root, args, *, allowed=(0,)):
    exe = shutil.which("git")
    require(exe is not None, "git is required for validation")
    argv = [exe, "--no-optional-locks", "-c", "core.fsmonitor=false",
            "-c", "core.untrackedCache=false", "-c", "core.hooksPath=" + os.devnull,
            "-c", "core.attributesFile=" + os.devnull,
            "-C", str(root), *args]
    try:
        proc = subprocess.run(argv, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                              stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
                              shell=False, env=git_env(), timeout=45, check=False)
    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as exc:
        raise BuildPairError("Git validation could not complete") from exc
    require(proc.returncode in allowed, "Git validation failed: " + " ".join(args[:2]))
    require(len(proc.stdout) <= 64 * 1024 * 1024, "Git validation output limit exceeded")
    return proc


def patchset_hash(manifest):
    records = [{k: item[k] for k in ("id", "file", "sha256", "targets")}
               for item in manifest["patches"]]
    return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(records, sort_keys=True,
                                    separators=(",", ":")).encode()).hexdigest()


def load_manifest(path, patches_dir):
    manifest = read_json(path)
    require(manifest.get("schema_version") == 1, "Unsupported manifest schema")
    require(COMMIT_RE.fullmatch(manifest.get("upstream", {}).get("base_commit", ""))
            is not None, "Manifest requires a full upstream.base_commit")
    require(isinstance(manifest.get("release_id"), str) and manifest["release_id"],
            "Manifest release_id is missing")
    patches = manifest.get("patches")
    require(isinstance(patches, list) and patches, "Manifest has no patches")
    before, after, ids = {}, {}, set()
    for patch in patches:
        require(isinstance(patch, dict), "Invalid patch entry")
        require(isinstance(patch.get("id"), str) and patch["id"] not in ids,
                "Missing or duplicate patch id")
        ids.add(patch["id"])
        require(HASH_RE.fullmatch(patch.get("sha256", "")) is not None,
                "Patch SHA256 is missing")
        source = contained_file(patches_dir, patch.get("file"))
        require(sha256_file(source) == patch["sha256"], "Patch content differs from manifest")
        targets = patch.get("targets")
        require(isinstance(targets, list) and targets, "Patch target list is empty")
        seen = set()
        for target in targets:
            require(isinstance(target, dict), "Patch target must be an object")
            name = relpath(target.get("path"))
            require(name not in seen, "Duplicate target in patch")
            seen.add(name)
            a, b = target.get("before_sha256", ""), target.get("after_sha256", "")
            require(HASH_RE.fullmatch(a) is not None and HASH_RE.fullmatch(b) is not None,
                    "Target before/after SHA256 is missing")
            if name in after:
                require(after[name] == a, "Patch hash chain is inconsistent")
            before.setdefault(name, a)
            after[name] = b
    extra = manifest.get("additional_files", [])
    require(isinstance(extra, list) and len(extra) <= 20, "Additional file list is malformed")
    seen_sources, seen_destinations = set(), set()
    for item in extra:
        require(isinstance(item, dict) and set(item) == {"source", "destination", "sha256"},
                "Malformed vendor source record")
        src, dst = relpath(item["source"]), relpath(item["destination"])
        vendor_ok = (src in ("modules/engram/row_cache.hpp", "modules/engram/mapped_rows.hpp",
                             "modules/engram/model_cache.hpp", "modules/engram/graph_stage.hpp") and
                     dst == "src/llama-experimental/" + src.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]) or (
                         src in ("modules/moe/upload_pipeline.hpp", "modules/moe/upload_types.hpp", "modules/moe/upload_worker.hpp", "modules/moe/upload_plan.hpp", "modules/moe/upload_scheduler.hpp", "modules/moe/upload_completion.hpp") and
                         dst == "src/llama-experimental/" + src.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]) or (
                         src in ("modules/cuda/hc_post_reuse.cuh", "modules/cuda/mmq_tile_cache.hpp", "modules/cuda/mmq_select_probe.hpp") and
                         dst == "ggml/src/ggml-cuda/llama-experimental/" + src.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]) or (
                         src == "modules/speculative/mtp_verify_window.hpp" and
                         dst == "common/llama-experimental/mtp_verify_window.hpp") or (
                         src == "modules/speculative/mtp_rewind_guard.hpp" and
                         dst == "common/llama-experimental/mtp_rewind_guard.hpp")
        require(vendor_ok, "Vendor file is outside the reviewed vendor namespace")
        require(src not in seen_sources and dst not in seen_destinations and dst not in before,
                "Vendor file path conflict")
        require(HASH_RE.fullmatch(item["sha256"]) is not None, "Vendor sha256 missing")
        require(sha256_file(contained_file(path.parent, src)) == item["sha256"],
                "Vendor file changed since manifest")
        seen_sources.add(src)
        seen_destinations.add(dst)
    return manifest, before, after


def source_identity(root, allowed_untracked=None):
    require(root.is_dir() and (root / ".git").exists(), "A checkout with .git is required")
    top = git(root, ["rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"]).stdout.decode().strip()
    require(Path(top).resolve() == root, "Source must be the checkout root")
    head = git(root, ["rev-parse", "HEAD"]).stdout.decode().strip()
    require(COMMIT_RE.fullmatch(head) is not None, "A full SHA1 HEAD is required")
    # status/diff can invoke a local clean/process filter even with hooks and
    # external diff disabled. Refuse configured helpers before inspecting the
    # worktree, without displaying a potentially sensitive command value.
    filters = git(root, ["config", "--null", "--get-regexp",
                         r"^filter\..*\.(clean|process|smudge)$"], allowed=(0, 1)).stdout
    require(all(not row.partition(b"\n")[2].strip() for row in filters.split(b"\0") if row),
            "Local Git filters are not allowed for read-only source validation")
    # A recursive submodule diff can invoke a child repository's clean/process
    # filter, which the root config check cannot see. This kit has no gitlinks;
    # reject them before any diff instead of running nested worktree inspection.
    entries = git(root, ["ls-files", "--stage", "-z"]).stdout.split(b"\0")
    require(not any(row.startswith(b"160000 ") for row in entries),
            "Gitlinks/submodules are unsupported for read-only source validation")
    # Assume-unchanged / skip-worktree flags can conceal modified tracked files.
    flags = git(root, ["ls-files", "-v", "-z"]).stdout.split(b"\0")
    require(all(not row or (not chr(row[0]).islower() and row[:1] != b"S")
                for row in flags), "Hidden tracked-file flags are not allowed")
    require(not git(root, ["diff", "--cached", "--name-only", "-z", "HEAD", "--"]).stdout,
            "Staged changes are not allowed")
    require(not git(root, ["diff", "--no-ext-diff", "--no-textconv", "--summary", "HEAD", "--"]).stdout,
            "Add/delete/rename/mode changes are not allowed")
    raw = git(root, ["diff", "--no-ext-diff", "--no-textconv", "--name-only", "-z",
                     "--ignore-submodules=none", "HEAD", "--"]).stdout
    changed = sorted(x.decode("utf-8") for x in raw.split(b"\0") if x)
    untracked = git(root, ["ls-files", "--others", "-z"]).stdout
    names = {x.decode("utf-8") for x in untracked.split(b"\0") if x}
    require(names <= {RECEIPT} | set(allowed_untracked or ()),
            "Unexpected untracked files in prepared checkout")
    return head, changed


def target_hashes(root, names):
    return {name: sha256_file(contained_file(root, name)) for name in sorted(names)}


def diff_bytes(root, names):
    return git(root, ["-c", "core.quotePath=false", "-c", "color.ui=false",
                      "-c", "diff.noprefix=false", "-c", "diff.mnemonicPrefix=false",
                      "diff", "--binary", "--no-ext-diff", "--no-textconv",
                      "HEAD", "--", *sorted(names)]).stdout


def validate_receipt(root, role, manifest, manifest_path, expected_commit, expected_files):
    receipt_path = root / RECEIPT
    receipt = read_json(receipt_path)
    require(receipt.get("schema_version") == 1 and receipt.get("role") == role,
            f"Wrong {role} receipt role/schema")
    require(receipt.get("base_commit") == expected_commit,
            f"{role} receipt commit differs from HEAD")
    require(receipt.get("release_id") == manifest["release_id"], "Receipt release differs")
    require(receipt.get("manifest_sha256") == sha256_file(manifest_path),
            "Receipt manifest SHA256 differs")
    require(receipt.get("patchset_sha256") == patchset_hash(manifest),
            "Receipt patchset SHA256 differs")
    assets = manifest.get("additional_files", [])
    if assets:
        encoded = json.dumps(assets, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")).encode()
        require(receipt.get("assetset_sha256") == hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest(),
                "Receipt vendor-set checksum differs")
        expected_assets = [] if role == "baseline" else [
            {"path": x["destination"], "sha256": x["sha256"]} for x in assets]
        require(receipt.get("additional_files") == expected_assets,
                "Receipt vendor files differ")
        for item in expected_assets:
            require(sha256_file(contained_file(root, item["path"])) == item["sha256"],
                    "Vendor source changed after preparation")
        if role == "baseline":
            require(not any(os.path.lexists(root / x["destination"]) for x in assets),
                    "Baseline must not contain experimental vendor headers")
    entries = receipt.get("files")
    require(isinstance(entries, list), "Receipt files missing")
    files = {}
    for entry in entries:
        require(isinstance(entry, dict), "Invalid receipt file")
        name = relpath(entry.get("path"))
        require(name not in files and HASH_RE.fullmatch(entry.get("sha256", "")) is not None,
                "Invalid or duplicate receipt hash")
        files[name] = entry["sha256"]
    require(files == expected_files, f"{role} receipt target hashes differ from independent expectation")
    require(target_hashes(root, files) == files, f"{role} source target content changed")
    allowed_status = ({"BASELINE_READY", "UNVALIDATED_NEW_BASE"} if role == "baseline" else
                      {"PATCHED_BASELINE", "UNVALIDATED_NEW_BASE"})
    require(receipt.get("status") in allowed_status, f"Unexpected {role} receipt status")
    actual_diff = hashlib.sha256(diff_bytes(root, files)).hexdigest()
    require(receipt.get("diff_sha256") == actual_diff, "Receipt diff SHA256 differs")
    return {"path": str(receipt_path), "sha256": sha256_file(receipt_path),
            "status": receipt["status"], "diff_sha256": actual_diff,
            "application_state": receipt.get("application_state"),
            "validation_status": receipt.get("validation_status")}


def reconstruct_new_base(baseline, commit, manifest, patches_dir, target_names):
    """Only the owned temp tree is written; no source index/worktree mutations."""
    original, expected, applications = {}, {}, []
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="llama-pair-validate-") as tmp:
        temporary = Path(tmp)
        git(temporary, ["-c", "init.templateDir=", "init", "--quiet"])
        for name in sorted(target_names):
            item = git(baseline, ["ls-tree", "-z", commit, "--", name]).stdout
            require(item.startswith((b"100644 blob ", b"100755 blob ")),
                    "Only existing regular tracked patch targets are supported")
            data = git(baseline, ["show", commit + ":" + name]).stdout
            dest = temporary / name
            dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            dest.write_bytes(data)
            if item.startswith(b"100755"):
                dest.chmod(0o755)
            original[name] = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
        for patch in manifest["patches"]:
            source = contained_file(patches_dir, patch["file"])
            check = git(temporary, ["apply", "--check", "--whitespace=error-all", "--", str(source)], allowed=(0, 1))
            if check.returncode == 0:
                git(temporary, ["apply", "--whitespace=error-all", "--", str(source)])
                state = "APPLIED"
            else:
                reverse = git(temporary, ["apply", "--reverse", "--check", "--whitespace=error-all", "--", str(source)], allowed=(0, 1))
                require(reverse.returncode == 0, "Patch does not apply exactly to selected new base")
                state = "ALREADY_PRESENT"
            applications.append({"id": patch["id"], "state": state})
        actual_paths = {p.relative_to(temporary).as_posix()
                        for p in temporary.rglob("*") if p.is_file() and
                        not p.relative_to(temporary).parts[0] == ".git"}
        require(actual_paths == set(target_names), "Patch changed undeclared file set")
        expected = target_hashes(temporary, target_names)
    return original, expected, applications


def check_project_options(root, cuda):
    inputs = [root / "CMakeLists.txt", root / "ggml/CMakeLists.txt"]
    body = "\n".join(regular_file(p).read_text(encoding="utf-8") for p in inputs)
    for option in [*PROJECT_OPTIONS, "GGML_CUDA"]:
        require(re.search(r"\b(?:option|set)\s*\(\s*" + re.escape(option) + r"\b", body, re.I),
                f"Required CMake option no longer declared: {option}")
    if cuda:
        regular_file(root / "ggml/src/ggml-cuda/CMakeLists.txt")
    return {str(p.relative_to(root)): sha256_file(p) for p in inputs}


def validate_pair(baseline, candidate, manifest_path, patches_dir, *, allow_new_base=False):
    require(baseline != candidate and not baseline.is_relative_to(candidate) and
            not candidate.is_relative_to(baseline), "Source checkouts must be separate and nonnested")
    manifest, before, after = load_manifest(manifest_path, patches_dir)
    assets = manifest.get("additional_files", [])
    base_head, base_changes = source_identity(baseline)
    cand_head, cand_changes = source_identity(candidate, {x["destination"] for x in assets})
    require(base_head == cand_head, "Baseline and candidate HEAD differ")
    require(not base_changes, "Baseline has tracked changes")
    for extra in assets:
        # Native upstream contents are never overwritten by this kit.
        require(not git(baseline, ["ls-tree", "HEAD", "--", extra["destination"]]).stdout,
                "Selected upstream already contains a declared experimental vendor file")
    known = base_head == manifest["upstream"]["base_commit"]
    applications = []
    if not known:
        require(allow_new_base, "New base requires --allow-unvalidated-base, including for a plan")
        before, after, applications = reconstruct_new_base(
            baseline, base_head, manifest, patches_dir, before)
    expected_changes = sorted(name for name in before if before[name] != after[name])
    require(cand_changes == expected_changes, "Candidate tracked diff paths differ from trusted patchset")
    records = {
        "baseline": validate_receipt(baseline, "baseline", manifest, manifest_path, base_head, before),
        "candidate": validate_receipt(candidate, "candidate", manifest, manifest_path, base_head, after),
    }
    return {"base_commit": base_head, "release_id": manifest["release_id"],
            "manifest_sha256": sha256_file(manifest_path), "patchset_sha256": patchset_hash(manifest),
            "known_base": known, "status": "BUILD_ONLY_KNOWN_BASE" if known else "BUILD_ONLY_NEW_BASE",
            "semantic_validation": "NOT_RUN", "target_files": sorted(before),
            "changed_target_files": expected_changes, "receipts": records,
            "independent_new_base_patch_reconstruction": applications}


def available_jobs():
    try:
        return max(1, len(os.sched_getaffinity(0)))
    except (AttributeError, OSError):
        return max(1, os.cpu_count() or 1)


def make_plan(args):
    baseline, candidate = args.baseline.expanduser().resolve(), args.candidate.expanduser().resolve()
    build_root = args.build_root.expanduser().absolute()
    require(not build_root.exists() and not build_root.is_symlink(), "Build root must not exist")
    require(build_root.parent.is_dir(), "Build root parent must already exist")
    build_root = build_root.parent.resolve() / build_root.name
    require(not build_root.exists(), "Resolved build root already exists")
    for source in (baseline, candidate):
        require(not build_root.is_relative_to(source) and not source.is_relative_to(build_root),
                "Build root must be outside both source checkouts")
    require(1 <= args.jobs <= available_jobs(), "--jobs must be within available logical CPUs")
    require(args.cuda or args.cuda_arch is None, "--cuda-arch requires --cuda")
    if args.cuda_arch is not None:
        require(re.fullmatch(r"[0-9]{2,3}(?:a|f)?(?:-real|-virtual)?", args.cuda_arch) is not None,
                "One explicit CUDA architecture is required (for example 120)")
    require(args.step_timeout > 0, "--step-timeout must be positive")
    manifest_path = args.manifest.expanduser().resolve()
    patches_dir = (args.patches_dir.expanduser().resolve() if args.patches_dir else
                   manifest_path.parent / "patches")
    verification = validate_pair(baseline, candidate, manifest_path, patches_dir,
                                 allow_new_base=args.allow_unvalidated_base)
    cmake = shutil.which(args.cmake)
    if args.execute:
        require(cmake is not None, "cmake was not found; no build directory created")
    cmake = str(Path(cmake).resolve()) if cmake else args.cmake
    options = {**PROJECT_OPTIONS, "GGML_CUDA": "ON" if args.cuda else "OFF"}
    common = ["-DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release", "-DCMAKE_EXPORT_COMPILE_COMMANDS=ON",
              "-DCMAKE_C_COMPILER_LAUNCHER=", "-DCMAKE_CXX_COMPILER_LAUNCHER=",
              "-DCMAKE_CUDA_COMPILER_LAUNCHER="]
    common.extend(f"-D{k}={v}" for k, v in options.items())
    if args.cuda_arch is not None:
        common.append("-DCMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES=" + args.cuda_arch)
    steps, sources = [], {}
    for role, source in (("baseline", baseline), ("candidate", candidate)):
        sources[role] = {"path": str(source), "cmake_input_sha256": check_project_options(source, args.cuda)}
        destination = build_root / role
        steps.append({"role": role, "phase": "configure",
                      "command": [cmake, "-S", str(source), "-B", str(destination), *common]})
        steps.append({"role": role, "phase": "build",
                      "command": [cmake, "--build", str(destination), "--config", "Release",
                                  "--parallel", str(args.jobs), "--target", *TARGETS]})
    return {"schema_version": 1, "mode": "execute" if args.execute else "dry-run",
            "build_root": str(build_root), "sources": sources, "verification": verification,
            "cuda_requested": args.cuda, "cuda_arch_requested": args.cuda_arch,
            "jobs": args.jobs, "cmake": cmake, "download_features_disabled": DOWNLOAD_OPTIONS,
            "network_isolation_guaranteed": False,
            "new_base_cmake_semantics_reviewed": verification["known_base"],
            "inference_executed": False, "tests_executed": False,
            "temporary_patch_reconstruction_only": not verification["known_base"], "steps": steps}


def atomic_json(path, obj):
    temporary = path.with_name(path.name + ".tmp")
    with open(temporary, "x", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        json.dump(obj, handle, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        handle.write("\n")
    temporary.replace(path)


def stop_child(proc):
    if proc.poll() is not None:
        return
    if os.name == "posix":
        os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
    else:
        proc.terminate()
    try:
        proc.wait(timeout=5)
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        if os.name == "posix":
            os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
        else:
            proc.kill()
        proc.wait()


def run_cmake_step(step, build_root, timeout):
    log_path = build_root / (step["role"] + "-" + step["phase"] + ".log")
    record = {**step, "log": log_path.name, "started_at_utc": dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat()}
    started = time.monotonic()
    with open(log_path, "xb") as log:
        try:
            proc = subprocess.Popen(step["command"], cwd=build_root, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                                    stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, shell=False,
                                    start_new_session=(os.name == "posix"), env=git_env())
        except OSError as exc:
            record.update(returncode=127, error=type(exc).__name__)
        else:
            try:
                record["returncode"] = proc.wait(timeout=timeout)
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                stop_child(proc)
                record.update(returncode=124, error="step_timeout")
            except KeyboardInterrupt:
                stop_child(proc)
                record.update(returncode=130, error="interrupted")
    record["elapsed_seconds"] = time.monotonic() - started
    record["log_sha256"] = sha256_file(log_path)
    return record


def compiler_record(build):
    keys = {"CMAKE_C_COMPILER", "CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER", "CMAKE_CUDA_COMPILER",
            "CMAKE_MAKE_PROGRAM", "CMAKE_GENERATOR", "CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES",
            "CMAKE_CACHE_MAJOR_VERSION", "CMAKE_CACHE_MINOR_VERSION", "CMAKE_CACHE_PATCH_VERSION"}
    result = {}
    cache = build / "CMakeCache.txt"
    if cache.is_file() and not cache.is_symlink():
        for line in cache.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").splitlines():
            m = re.match(r"([^:#=]+):[^=]*=(.*)$", line)
            if m and m[1] in keys:
                result[m[1]] = m[2]
    files = build / "CMakeFiles"
    if files.is_dir():
        for path in sorted(files.glob("*/CMake*Compiler.cmake")):
            if path.is_symlink() or not path.resolve().is_relative_to(build):
                continue
            for line in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").splitlines():
                m = re.match(r'set\((CMAKE_(?:C|CXX|CUDA)_COMPILER(?:_ID|_VERSION)?)\s+"([^"\r\n]*)"\)', line)
                if m:
                    result[m[1]] = m[2]
    return result


def build_artifacts(build):
    result = []
    if not build.is_dir():
        return result
    for path in sorted(build.rglob("*")):
        if not (path.name in TARGETS or path.name in {x + ".exe" for x in TARGETS} or
                re.search(r"\.(?:a|lib|dll|dylib|so(?:\.[0-9.]+)?)$", path.name)):
            continue
        resolved = path.resolve()
        if not resolved.is_relative_to(build) or not resolved.is_file():
            continue
        st = resolved.stat()
        if not stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode):
            continue
        record = {"path": path.relative_to(build).as_posix(), "bytes": st.st_size,
                  "sha256": sha256_file(resolved)}
        if path.is_symlink():
            record["symlink_target"] = resolved.relative_to(build).as_posix()
        result.append(record)
    return result


def execute_plan(plan, args):
    build_root = Path(plan["build_root"])
    # Exclusive creation is also the final no-overwrite check after validation.
    build_root.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=False, exist_ok=False)
    record = {"schema_version": 1, "plan": plan, "status": "BUILDING",
              "inference_executed": False, "tests_executed": False, "commands": [], "builds": {}}
    record_path = build_root / "build_record.json"
    atomic_json(record_path, record)
    result = 0
    try:
        for step in plan["steps"]:
            log_path = build_root / (step["role"] + "-" + step["phase"] + ".log")
            print(f"{step['role']} {step['phase']}  →  {log_path}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
            command = run_cmake_step(step, build_root, args.step_timeout)
            record["commands"].append(command)
            atomic_json(record_path, record)
            if command["returncode"]:
                result = command["returncode"]
                print(f"Failed ({result}). See {build_root / command['log']}", file=sys.stderr)
                record["status"] = "BUILD_FAILED"
                break
        if result == 0:
            # No model or newly built executable is run for this validation.
            after = validate_pair(Path(plan["sources"]["baseline"]["path"]),
                                  Path(plan["sources"]["candidate"]["path"]),
                                  args.manifest.expanduser().resolve(),
                                  args.patches_dir.expanduser().resolve() if args.patches_dir else
                                  args.manifest.expanduser().resolve().parent / "patches",
                                  allow_new_base=args.allow_unvalidated_base)
            require(after == plan["verification"], "Source provenance changed during compilation")
            record["status"] = plan["verification"]["status"]
    except (BuildPairError, OSError) as exc:
        result = 2
        record.update(status="BUILD_FAILED", error=str(exc))
    finally:
        for role in ("baseline", "candidate"):
            build = build_root / role
            record["builds"][role] = {"compiler": compiler_record(build), "artifacts": build_artifacts(build)}
        if result == 0:
            for role in ("baseline", "candidate"):
                found = {Path(x["path"]).name.removesuffix(".exe") for x in record["builds"][role]["artifacts"]}
                if not set(TARGETS) <= found:
                    result = 2
                    record.update(status="BUILD_FAILED", error="Expected compiled tools are missing: " + role)
        record["finished_at_utc"] = dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat()
        record["exit_code"] = result
        atomic_json(record_path, record)
    print(json.dumps({"status": record["status"], "record": str(record_path),
                      "inference_executed": False, "exit_code": result}, indent=2))
    return result


def parser():
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    p.add_argument("--baseline", type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument("--candidate", type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument("--build-root", type=Path, required=True, help="NEW directory outside both checkouts")
    p.add_argument("--manifest", type=Path, default=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "manifest.json")
    p.add_argument("--patches-dir", type=Path)
    p.add_argument("--jobs", type=int, default=min(4, available_jobs()))
    p.add_argument("--cuda", action="store_true", help="Explicitly enable CUDA compilation (default CPU)")
    p.add_argument("--cuda-arch", help="One architecture, e.g. 120; requires --cuda")
    p.add_argument("--cmake", default="cmake", help="CMake executable name/path")
    p.add_argument("--step-timeout", type=int, default=7200, help="Seconds per configure/build step")
    p.add_argument("--allow-unvalidated-base", action="store_true",
                   help="Reconstruct trusted patches independently on a new explicit base; build only, no semantic PASS")
    run = p.add_mutually_exclusive_group()
    run.add_argument("--execute", action="store_true", help="Actually configure/build; no inference or tests")
    run.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Default: validate and print plan without a build root")
    return p


def main(argv=None):
    args = parser().parse_args(argv)
    try:
        plan = make_plan(args)
        if not args.execute:
            print(json.dumps(plan, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
            return 0
        return execute_plan(plan, args)
    except (BuildPairError, OSError, ValueError) as exc:
        print("build_pair: " + str(exc), file=sys.stderr)
        return 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/build_r21.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import argparse
import datetime
import hashlib
import json
import subprocess
import shutil
from pathlib import Path

p = argparse.ArgumentParser()
p.add_argument('--source', type=Path, required=True)
p.add_argument('--build', type=Path, required=True)
p.add_argument('--arch', default='120a')
p.add_argument('--cuda-compiler', type=Path)
p.add_argument('--ui-assets', type=Path)
p.add_argument('--production-tools', action='store_true')
p.add_argument('--jobs', type=int, default=8)
a = p.parse_args()
source, build = a.source.resolve(), a.build.resolve()
if build.exists():
    p.error('Build destination must not exist; preserve previous builds')
if not (source / 'CMakeLists.txt').is_file() or not 1 <= a.jobs <= 32:
    p.error('Invalid source or jobs')
build.mkdir(parents=True)
if a.ui_assets:
    assert (a.ui_assets/'index.html').is_file()
    shutil.copytree(a.ui_assets, build/'tools/ui/dist')
configure = ['cmake', '-S', str(source), '-B', str(build),
             '-DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release', '-DCMAKE_EXPORT_COMPILE_COMMANDS=ON',
             '-DCMAKE_BUILD_RPATH_USE_ORIGIN=ON', '-DLLAMA_BUILD_COMMON=ON',
             '-DLLAMA_BUILD_TOOLS=ON', '-DLLAMA_BUILD_SERVER=ON', '-DLLAMA_BUILD_TESTS=ON',
             '-DLLAMA_BUILD_EXAMPLES=OFF', '-DLLAMA_BUILD_APP=OFF', '-DLLAMA_BUILD_UI=OFF',
             '-DLLAMA_USE_PREBUILT_UI=OFF', '-DLLAMA_CONNECT=OFF', '-DLLAMA_LLGUIDANCE=OFF',
             '-DLLAMA_USE_SYSTEM_GGML=OFF', '-DGGML_CPU=ON', '-DGGML_METAL=OFF',
             '-DGGML_CCACHE=OFF', '-DGGML_CPU_KLEIDIAI=OFF', '-DGGML_CUDA=ON',
             '-DGGML_OPENMP_FETCH=OFF', '-DGGML_CUDA_CCCL_VERSION=', '-DGGML_CUDA_NCCL=OFF',
             '-DLLAMA_BUILD_MTMD=OFF', '-DCMAKE_C_COMPILER_LAUNCHER=',
             '-DCMAKE_CXX_COMPILER_LAUNCHER=', '-DCMAKE_CUDA_COMPILER_LAUNCHER=',
             '-DGGML_NATIVE=ON', '-DGGML_OPENMP=ON', '-DCMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES=' + a.arch]
if a.production_tools:
    configure += ['-DLLAMA_BUILD_EXAMPLES=ON', '-DLLAMA_BUILD_APP=ON', '-DLLAMA_BUILD_TESTS=OFF']
if a.cuda_compiler:
    configure.append('-DCMAKE_CUDA_COMPILER=' + str(a.cuda_compiler.resolve()))
compile = ['cmake', '--build', str(build), '-j', str(a.jobs), '--target',
           'llama-server', 'llama-perplexity', 'test-backend-ops']
if a.production_tools:
    compile = ['cmake', '--build', str(build), '-j', str(a.jobs)]
record = {'status': 'BUILDING', 'source': str(source), 'commands': [configure, compile],
          'started': datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat()}
receipt = source / '.llama-overlay-receipt.json'
if receipt.is_file():
    record['overlay_receipt'] = json.loads(receipt.read_text())
(build / 'BUILD.json').write_text(json.dumps(record, indent=2))
try:
    with (build / 'build.log').open('w') as log:
        for cmd in record['commands']:
            subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
    artifacts = {x.resolve() for x in (build / 'bin').iterdir() if x.is_file()}
    record['binary_sha256'] = {x.name: hashlib.sha256(x.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for x in sorted(artifacts)}
    record['status'] = 'BUILT'
except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
    record.update(status='BUILD_FAILED', exit_code=e.returncode)
    raise
finally:
    record['finished'] = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat()
    (build / 'BUILD.json').write_text(json.dumps(record, indent=2))
print(record['status'], build)

===== SOURCE FILE tools/overlay.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Prepare an isolated, auditable patch checkout; never build or alter the source.

All CLI results are JSON.  A successful textual application is not a statement
of API, numerical, performance, or test compatibility.  Forward ports require
an explicit full commit ID and remain UNVALIDATED_NEW_BASE.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath
import re
import stat
import subprocess
import sys
from typing import Any


KIT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
RECEIPT_NAME = ".llama-overlay-receipt.json"
SHA256_RE = re.compile(r"[0-9a-f]{64}\Z")
COMMIT_RE = re.compile(r"(?:[0-9a-f]{40}|[0-9a-f]{64})\Z")
ADVISORY = (
    "Textual patch application is not API, semantic, numerical, test, or "
    "performance validation. Build and review the isolated candidate separately."
)


class OverlayError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, *, incompatible: bool = False,
                 details: dict[str, Any] | None = None):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.message = message
        self.incompatible = incompatible
        self.details = details or {}

    def payload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "status": "INCOMPATIBLE" if self.incompatible else "ERROR",
            "code": self.code,
            "message": self.message,
            **self.details,
        }


def safe_git_env() -> dict[str, str]:
    """Avoid ambient Git routing/configuration and optional index refreshes."""
    env = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if not k.startswith("GIT_")}
    env.update({
        "GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM": "1",
        "GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL": os.devnull,
        "GIT_CONFIG_SYSTEM": os.devnull,
        "GIT_OPTIONAL_LOCKS": "0",
        "GIT_NO_REPLACE_OBJECTS": "1",
        "GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT": "0",
        "GIT_PROTOCOL_FROM_USER": "0",
        "LC_ALL": "C",
    })
    return env


def git(cwd: Path | None, args: list[str], *, data: bytes | None = None,
        check: bool = True) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[bytes]:
    command = [
        "git", "-c", f"core.hooksPath={os.devnull}",
        "-c", "core.fsmonitor=false", "-c", "core.autocrlf=false",
        "-c", "core.quotePath=false", "-c", "color.ui=false",
        "-c", "diff.noprefix=false", "-c", "diff.mnemonicPrefix=false",
        "-c", "submodule.recurse=false", "-c", "protocol.allow=never",
        "-c", "protocol.file.allow=always", *args,
    ]
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(command, cwd=cwd, env=safe_git_env(), input=data,
                                stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
                                check=False)
    except OSError as exc:
        raise OverlayError("GIT_UNAVAILABLE", str(exc)) from exc
    if check and result.returncode:
        raise OverlayError("GIT_FAILED", result.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace").strip(),
                           details={"git_operation": args[0], "returncode": result.returncode})
    return result


def sha256_file(path: Path | str) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open("rb") as stream:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: stream.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def _relative_path(value: Any, label: str) -> str:
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value or "\\" in value or ":" in value:
        raise OverlayError("INVALID_PATH", f"{label} must be a relative POSIX path")
    path = PurePosixPath(value)
    if (path.is_absolute() or str(path) != value or
            any(p in ("", ".", "..", ".git") for p in path.parts) or
            any(ord(c) < 32 or ord(c) == 127 for c in value)):
        raise OverlayError("INVALID_PATH", f"Unsafe {label}: {value!r}")
    if value == RECEIPT_NAME:
        raise OverlayError("INVALID_PATH", "The receipt cannot be a patch target")
    return value


def _no_duplicate_keys(pairs: list[tuple[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    result: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for key, value in pairs:
        if key in result:
            raise OverlayError("INVALID_MANIFEST", f"Duplicate JSON key: {key}")
        result[key] = value
    return result


def _manifest_from_bytes(raw: bytes) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        value = json.loads(raw, object_pairs_hook=_no_duplicate_keys)
    except (ValueError, UnicodeError) as exc:
        raise OverlayError("INVALID_MANIFEST", f"Invalid JSON manifest: {exc}") from exc
    if not isinstance(value, dict) or type(value.get("schema_version")) is not int or value["schema_version"] != 1:
        raise OverlayError("INVALID_MANIFEST", "schema_version must be integer 1")
    if not isinstance(value.get("release_id"), str) or not value["release_id"].strip():
        raise OverlayError("INVALID_MANIFEST", "release_id must be a nonempty string")
    upstream = value.get("upstream")
    if (not isinstance(upstream, dict) or not isinstance(upstream.get("repository"), str)
            or not upstream["repository"] or not isinstance(upstream.get("base_commit"), str)
            or not COMMIT_RE.fullmatch(upstream["base_commit"])):
        raise OverlayError("INVALID_MANIFEST", "upstream requires repository and a full lowercase base_commit")
    patches = value.get("patches")
    if not isinstance(patches, list) or not patches:
        raise OverlayError("INVALID_MANIFEST", "patches must be a nonempty list")
    ids: set[str] = set()
    names: set[str] = set()
    previous_after: dict[str, str] = {}
    for patch in patches:
        if not isinstance(patch, dict) or not isinstance(patch.get("id"), str) or not patch["id"]:
            raise OverlayError("INVALID_MANIFEST", "Each patch needs an id")
        if patch["id"] in ids:
            raise OverlayError("INVALID_MANIFEST", f"Duplicate patch id: {patch['id']}")
        ids.add(patch["id"])
        filename = _relative_path(patch.get("file"), "patch file")
        if filename in names:
            raise OverlayError("INVALID_MANIFEST", f"Duplicate patch file: {filename}")
        names.add(filename)
        if not isinstance(patch.get("sha256"), str) or not SHA256_RE.fullmatch(patch["sha256"]):
            raise OverlayError("INVALID_MANIFEST", f"Invalid patch sha256: {filename}")
        targets = patch.get("targets")
        if not isinstance(targets, list) or not targets:
            raise OverlayError("INVALID_MANIFEST", f"No targets for {filename}")
        paths: set[str] = set()
        for target in targets:
            if not isinstance(target, dict):
                raise OverlayError("INVALID_MANIFEST", "A target must be an object")
            path = _relative_path(target.get("path"), "target path")
            if path in paths:
                raise OverlayError("INVALID_MANIFEST", f"Duplicate target in patch: {path}")
            paths.add(path)
            for key in ("before_sha256", "after_sha256"):
                if not isinstance(target.get(key), str) or not SHA256_RE.fullmatch(target[key]):
                    raise OverlayError("INVALID_MANIFEST", f"{path}: {key} must be a SHA-256 of an existing regular file")
            if target["before_sha256"] == target["after_sha256"]:
                raise OverlayError("INVALID_MANIFEST", f"No content change declared for {path}")
            if path in previous_after and previous_after[path] != target["before_sha256"]:
                raise OverlayError("INVALID_MANIFEST", f"Broken sequential target hash chain: {path}")
            previous_after[path] = target["after_sha256"]
    extra = value.get("additional_files", [])
    if not isinstance(extra, list) or len(extra) > 20:
        raise OverlayError("INVALID_MANIFEST", "additional_files must be a bounded list")
    destinations: set[str] = set()
    sources: set[str] = set()
    patch_targets = set(previous_after)
    for item in extra:
        if not isinstance(item, dict) or set(item) != {"source", "destination", "sha256"}:
            raise OverlayError("INVALID_MANIFEST", "additional_files needs source/destination/sha256")
        source = _relative_path(item["source"], "extra source")
        destination = _relative_path(item["destination"], "extra destination")
        # Explicit narrow vendor lane; never overwrite arbitrary upstream code.
        vendor_ok = (source in ("modules/engram/row_cache.hpp", "modules/engram/mapped_rows.hpp",
                             "modules/engram/model_cache.hpp", "modules/engram/graph_stage.hpp") and
                     destination == "src/llama-experimental/" + source.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]) or (
                         source in ("modules/moe/upload_pipeline.hpp", "modules/moe/upload_types.hpp", "modules/moe/upload_worker.hpp", "modules/moe/upload_plan.hpp", "modules/moe/upload_scheduler.hpp", "modules/moe/upload_completion.hpp") and
                         destination == "src/llama-experimental/" + source.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]) or (
                         source in ("modules/cuda/hc_post_reuse.cuh", "modules/cuda/mmq_tile_cache.hpp", "modules/cuda/mmq_select_probe.hpp") and
                         destination == "ggml/src/ggml-cuda/llama-experimental/" + source.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]) or (
                         source == "modules/speculative/mtp_verify_window.hpp" and
                         destination == "common/llama-experimental/mtp_verify_window.hpp") or (
                         source == "modules/speculative/mtp_rewind_guard.hpp" and
                         destination == "common/llama-experimental/mtp_rewind_guard.hpp") or (
                         source == "third_party_licenses/LICENSE-project-maya" and
                         destination == "licenses/LICENSE-project-maya") or (
                         source == "modules/cpu/cpu_gate_up.hpp" and
                         destination == "src/llama-experimental/cpu_gate_up.hpp")
        if not vendor_ok:
            raise OverlayError("INVALID_MANIFEST", "extra files are restricted to reviewed vendor paths")
        if source in sources or destination in destinations or destination in patch_targets:
            raise OverlayError("INVALID_MANIFEST", "duplicate/conflicting extra file path")
        if not isinstance(item["sha256"], str) or not SHA256_RE.fullmatch(item["sha256"]):
            raise OverlayError("INVALID_MANIFEST", "extra file needs a SHA-256")
        sources.add(source)
        destinations.add(destination)
    return value


def load_manifest(path: Path | str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return _manifest_from_bytes(Path(path).read_bytes())


def patchset_sha256(manifest: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    ordered = [{k: patch[k] for k in ("id", "file", "sha256", "targets")}
               for patch in manifest["patches"]]
    encoded = json.dumps(ordered, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"),
                         ensure_ascii=True).encode("utf-8")
    return hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest()


def assetset_sha256(manifest: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    encoded = json.dumps(manifest.get("additional_files", []), sort_keys=True,
                         separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=True).encode("utf-8")
    return hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest()


def expected_target_hashes(manifest: dict[str, Any], after: bool) -> dict[str, str]:
    result: dict[str, str] = {}
    for patch in manifest["patches"]:
        for target in patch["targets"]:
            if after or target["path"] not in result:
                result[target["path"]] = target["after_sha256" if after else "before_sha256"]
    return result


def _regular_file(root: Path, relative: str) -> Path:
    current = root
    for part in PurePosixPath(relative).parts:
        current = current / part
        if current.is_symlink():
            raise OverlayError("UNSAFE_FILE", f"Symlink not allowed: {current}")
    try:
        mode = current.stat().st_mode
    except FileNotFoundError as exc:
        raise OverlayError("MISSING_FILE", f"File missing: {current}", incompatible=True) from exc
    if not stat.S_ISREG(mode):
        raise OverlayError("UNSAFE_FILE", f"Not a regular file: {current}")
    return current


def _patch_paths(raw: bytes, cwd: Path) -> set[str]:
    # Keep the kit's trust boundary deliberately narrow: existing text files.
