    forbidden = rb"(?m)^(?:new file mode |deleted file mode |old mode |new mode |rename from |rename to |copy from |copy to |GIT binary patch|Binary files )"
    if re.search(forbidden, raw):
        raise OverlayError("UNSUPPORTED_PATCH", "Creation, deletion, mode changes, renames, copies and binary patches are not supported")
    result = git(cwd, ["apply", "--numstat", "-z"], data=raw, check=False)
    if result.returncode:
        raise OverlayError("INVALID_PATCH", result.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace").strip())
    paths: set[str] = set()
    for record in result.stdout.split(b"\0"):
        if not record:
            continue
        fields = record.split(b"\t", 2)
        if len(fields) != 3 or not fields[0].isdigit() or not fields[1].isdigit():
            raise OverlayError("UNSUPPORTED_PATCH", "Only ordinary text-file changes are supported")
        try:
            path = _relative_path(fields[2].decode("utf-8"), "patch target")
        except UnicodeError as exc:
            raise OverlayError("INVALID_PATCH", "Patch paths must be UTF-8") from exc
        paths.add(path)
    if not paths:
        raise OverlayError("INVALID_PATCH", "Patch has no file changes")
    return paths


def _bundle(manifest_path: Path, patches_dir: Path) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], str, list[bytes]]:
    raw_manifest = manifest_path.read_bytes()
    manifest = _manifest_from_bytes(raw_manifest)
    patches_dir = patches_dir.resolve(strict=True)
    patches: list[bytes] = []
    for patch in manifest["patches"]:
        raw = _regular_file(patches_dir, patch["file"]).read_bytes()
        if hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest() != patch["sha256"]:
            raise OverlayError("PATCH_HASH_MISMATCH", f"Patch integrity check failed: {patch['file']}")
        declared = {target["path"] for target in patch["targets"]}
        actual = _patch_paths(raw, patches_dir)
        if actual != declared:
            raise OverlayError("PATCH_TARGET_MISMATCH", f"Patch touches undeclared or missing targets: {patch['file']}",
                               details={"declared": sorted(declared), "actual": sorted(actual)})
        patches.append(raw)
    for extra in manifest.get("additional_files", []):
        entry = _regular_file(manifest_path.parent, extra["source"])
        if entry.stat().st_size > 4*1024*1024 or sha256_file(entry) != extra["sha256"]:
            raise OverlayError("ASSET_HASH_MISMATCH", "Vendor header differs from manifest")
    return manifest, hashlib.sha256(raw_manifest).hexdigest(), patches


def _source_root(source: Path) -> Path:
    if not source.is_dir():
        raise OverlayError("INVALID_SOURCE", "--source must be an existing local Git working tree")
    root = git(source.resolve(), ["rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"]).stdout.decode().strip()
    return Path(root).resolve()


def _reject_executable_filters(source: Path) -> None:
    # Git status/diff can execute a clean/process filter merely to compare a
    # worktree file with its index entry. Hooks/fsmonitor controls do not stop it.
    result = git(source, ["config", "--null", "--get-regexp",
                          r"^filter\..*\.(clean|process|smudge)$"], check=False)
    if result.returncode not in (0, 1):
        raise OverlayError("GIT_CONFIG_FAILED", "Could not inspect effective Git filter configuration")
    commands = []
    for record in result.stdout.split(b"\0"):
        if not record:
            continue
        key, separator, value = record.partition(b"\n")
        if not separator:
            raise OverlayError("GIT_CONFIG_FAILED", "Unexpected Git filter configuration record")
        if value:
            commands.append(key.decode("utf-8", "replace"))
    if commands:
        raise OverlayError("UNSAFE_GIT_FILTER", "Executable Git filters are not supported by the read-only source inspection; use a separate clean upstream clone",
                           details={"filter_keys": commands})


def _source_snapshot(source: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    _reject_executable_filters(source)
    # A root config guard cannot see commands in an initialized submodule's
    # local config. Refuse gitlinks before status can inspect them recursively.
    entries = git(source, ["ls-files", "--stage", "-z"]).stdout.split(b"\0")
    if any(entry.startswith(b"160000 ") for entry in entries):
        raise OverlayError("UNSUPPORTED_GITLINK", "P01 does not support source checkouts with Git submodules; no recursive inspection was performed")
    head = git(source, ["rev-parse", "--verify", "HEAD^{commit}"]).stdout.decode().strip()
    index_path = Path(git(source, ["rev-parse", "--git-path", "index"]).stdout.decode().strip())
    if not index_path.is_absolute():
        index_path = source / index_path
    tracked = git(source, ["status", "--porcelain=v1", "--untracked-files=no",
                           "--ignore-submodules=none"]).stdout.decode("utf-8", "replace")
    return {"head": head, "index_sha256": sha256_file(index_path) if index_path.is_file() else None,
            "tracked_status": tracked, "tracked_clean": not bool(tracked)}


def _hashes(root: Path, targets: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, str]:
    return {path: sha256_file(_regular_file(root, path)) for path in sorted(targets)}


def _installed_assets(root: Path, manifest: dict[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    return [{"path": x["destination"], "sha256": sha256_file(_regular_file(root, x["destination"]))}
            for x in manifest.get("additional_files", [])]


def _assets_match(root: Path, manifest: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    for x in manifest.get("additional_files", []):
        try:
            if sha256_file(_regular_file(root, x["destination"])) != x["sha256"]:
                return False
        except OverlayError:
            return False
    return True


def _install_assets(destination: Path, manifest: dict[str, Any], manifest_path: Path) -> None:
    for x in manifest.get("additional_files", []):
        source = _regular_file(manifest_path.parent, x["source"])
        target = destination / x["destination"]
        # Never replace existing files, directories or symlinks, including
        # an upstream addition that now occupies the private vendor namespace.
        if os.path.lexists(target):
            raise OverlayError("ASSET_DESTINATION_EXISTS", f"Vendor target already exists: {x['destination']}", incompatible=True)
        for part in list(target.parents)[-2::-1]:
            if part == destination.parent:
                continue
            if part.is_relative_to(destination):
                if os.path.islink(part):
                    raise OverlayError("UNSAFE_ASSET_PARENT", f"Symlink directory: {part}")
                if part.exists() and not part.is_dir():
                    raise OverlayError("UNSAFE_ASSET_PARENT", f"Non-directory: {part}")
                part.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        with target.open("xb") as stream:
            stream.write(source.read_bytes())
        if sha256_file(target) != x["sha256"]:
            raise OverlayError("ASSET_COPY_MISMATCH", f"Vendor asset differs after copying: {x['destination']}")


def _apply_check(root: Path, raw: bytes, patch_id: str) -> None:
    result = git(root, ["apply", "--check", "--whitespace=error-all"], data=raw, check=False)
    if result.returncode:
        raise OverlayError("CONTEXT_CONFLICT", f"Patch {patch_id} cannot be applied exactly; no fuzzy or three-way fallback is permitted",
                           incompatible=True,
                           details={"patch_id": patch_id, "git_error": result.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace").strip()})


def diff_sha256(root: Path, target_paths: list[str]) -> str:
    raw = git(root, ["diff", "--binary", "--no-ext-diff", "--no-textconv", "HEAD", "--", *sorted(target_paths)]).stdout
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()


def inspect(source: Path, manifest_path: Path, patches_dir: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    manifest, manifest_hash, patches = _bundle(manifest_path, patches_dir)
    source = _source_root(source)
    snapshot = _source_snapshot(source)
    before = expected_target_hashes(manifest, False)
    after = expected_target_hashes(manifest, True)
    actual = _hashes(source, before)
    state = ("PATCHED" if actual == after and _assets_match(source, manifest)
             else "BASELINE" if actual == before else "DIFFERENT")
    checks = []
    previously_touched: set[str] = set()
    if state != "PATCHED":
        for patch, raw in zip(manifest["patches"], patches):
            targets = {entry["path"] for entry in patch["targets"]}
            if targets & previously_touched:
                checks.append({"id": patch["id"], "status": "DEFERRED_SEQUENTIAL_PATCH",
                               "reason": "An earlier patch changes this file; prepare checks the sequence in the isolated checkout"})
            else:
                result = git(source, ["apply", "--check", "--whitespace=error-all"], data=raw, check=False)
                checks.append({"id": patch["id"], "status": "CHECK_PASSED" if not result.returncode else "INCOMPATIBLE",
                               "git_error": result.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace").strip() or None})
            previously_touched.update(targets)
    if _source_snapshot(source) != snapshot:
        raise OverlayError("SOURCE_CHANGED", "Source changed during inspection; no reliable inspection result is available")
    return {"operation": "inspect", "status": "INSPECTED", "source": str(source),
            "source_head": snapshot["head"], "tracked_clean": snapshot["tracked_clean"],
            "tree_state": state, "manifest_base": manifest["upstream"]["base_commit"],
            "matches_manifest_base": snapshot["head"] == manifest["upstream"]["base_commit"],
            "requires_explicit_ref": snapshot["head"] != manifest["upstream"]["base_commit"],
            "release_id": manifest["release_id"], "manifest_sha256": manifest_hash,
            "patchset_sha256": patchset_sha256(manifest),
            "assetset_sha256": assetset_sha256(manifest),
            "vendor_files_present": _assets_match(source, manifest),
            "files": [{"path": path, "sha256": digest} for path, digest in actual.items()],
            "patch_checks": checks, "source_preserved": True, "advisory": ADVISORY}


def _receipt(root: Path, manifest: dict[str, Any], manifest_hash: str, commit: str,
             role: str, status: str, application: str, applied_ids: list[str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    actual = _hashes(root, expected_target_hashes(manifest, False))
    return {"schema_version": 1, "role": role, "status": status, "base_commit": commit,
            "release_id": manifest["release_id"], "manifest_sha256": manifest_hash,
            "patchset_sha256": patchset_sha256(manifest),
            "assetset_sha256": assetset_sha256(manifest),
            "additional_files": _installed_assets(root, manifest) if role != "baseline" else [],
            "files": [{"path": path, "sha256": digest} for path, digest in actual.items()],
            "diff_sha256": diff_sha256(root, list(actual)),
            "application_state": application, "applied_patch_ids": applied_ids,
            "validation_status": "UNVALIDATED", "advisory": ADVISORY}


def _write_receipt(root: Path, receipt: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    # Exclusive creation: even an unexpected receipt in the upstream tree is not overwritten.
    with (root / RECEIPT_NAME).open("x", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
        json.dump(receipt, stream, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True)
        stream.write("\n")


def prepare(source: Path, destination: Path, manifest_path: Path, patches_dir: Path,
            ref: str | None = None, baseline_only: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
    manifest, manifest_hash, patches = _bundle(manifest_path, patches_dir)
    source = _source_root(source)
    snapshot = _source_snapshot(source)
    if not snapshot["tracked_clean"]:
        raise OverlayError("SOURCE_DIRTY", "Source tracked files/index must be clean; source was not modified")
    if os.path.lexists(destination):
        raise OverlayError("DESTINATION_EXISTS", "Destination must not exist; nothing will be overwritten")
    if not destination.parent.is_dir():
        raise OverlayError("DESTINATION_PARENT_MISSING", "Destination parent must already exist")
    destination = destination.resolve()
    common = Path(git(source, ["rev-parse", "--git-common-dir"]).stdout.decode().strip())
    common = (source / common).resolve() if not common.is_absolute() else common.resolve()
    if destination.is_relative_to(source) or destination.is_relative_to(common):
        raise OverlayError("UNSAFE_DESTINATION", "Destination must be outside the source working tree and Git directory")
    base = manifest["upstream"]["base_commit"]
    if ref is not None and not COMMIT_RE.fullmatch(ref):
        raise OverlayError("REF_NOT_FULL_SHA", "--ref must be a full lowercase commit SHA, not a branch or tag")
    commit = git(source, ["rev-parse", "--verify", "--end-of-options", f"{ref or 'HEAD'}^{{commit}}"] ).stdout.decode().strip()
    if ref is None and commit != base:
        raise OverlayError("EXPLICIT_REF_REQUIRED", "HEAD differs from the manifest base; review the new base and supply its full SHA explicitly",
                           incompatible=True, details={"source_head": commit, "manifest_base": base})
    role = "baseline" if baseline_only else "candidate"
    applied_ids: list[str] = []
    cloned = False
    try:
        # Local path only. --no-hardlinks and --dissociate avoid sharing the source's object storage.
        git(None, ["clone", "--no-hardlinks", "--no-checkout", "--dissociate",
                   "--no-recurse-submodules", "--template=", "--", str(source), str(destination)])
        cloned = True
        git(destination, ["checkout", "--detach", commit, "--"])
        if os.path.lexists(destination / RECEIPT_NAME):
            raise OverlayError("UNEXPECTED_RECEIPT", "Selected upstream already contains a receipt path; refusing to overwrite it")
        before = expected_target_hashes(manifest, False)
        after = expected_target_hashes(manifest, True)
        actual = _hashes(destination, before)
        if baseline_only:
            if commit == base and actual != before:
                raise OverlayError("BASELINE_HASH_MISMATCH", "Manifest baseline target bytes do not match the selected commit", incompatible=True)
            status = "BASELINE_READY" if commit == base else "UNVALIDATED_NEW_BASE"
            application = "SKIPPED_BASELINE"
        elif actual == after and _assets_match(destination, manifest):
            status = "PATCHED_BASELINE" if commit == base else "UNVALIDATED_NEW_BASE"
            application = "ALREADY_PRESENT"
        else:
            if any(actual[path] == after[path] for path in after):
                raise OverlayError("PARTIALLY_PATCHED", "Only part of the declared final overlay is present; automatic mixed-state repair is prohibited", incompatible=True)
            for patch, raw in zip(manifest["patches"], patches):
                target_before = {t["path"]: t["before_sha256"] for t in patch["targets"]}
                target_after = {t["path"]: t["after_sha256"] for t in patch["targets"]}
                if commit == base and _hashes(destination, target_before) != target_before:
                    raise OverlayError("BEFORE_HASH_MISMATCH", f"Baseline preimage mismatch for {patch['id']}", incompatible=True)
                _apply_check(destination, raw, patch["id"])
                git(destination, ["apply", "--whitespace=error-all"], data=raw)
                applied_ids.append(patch["id"])
                if commit == base and _hashes(destination, target_after) != target_after:
                    raise OverlayError("AFTER_HASH_MISMATCH", f"Patch result disagrees with the trusted manifest: {patch['id']}", incompatible=True)
            if manifest.get("additional_files"):
                _install_assets(destination, manifest, manifest_path)
            changed = set(filter(None, git(destination, ["diff", "--name-only", "-z", "--no-ext-diff", "--no-textconv", "HEAD"]).stdout.decode("utf-8").split("\0")))
            if not changed.issubset(before):
                raise OverlayError("UNDECLARED_CHANGE", "Isolated checkout contains a change outside the manifest targets", incompatible=True)
            if not _assets_match(destination, manifest):
                raise OverlayError("ASSET_INSTALL_FAILED", "Missing or mismatched vendor sources", incompatible=True)
            status = "PATCHED_BASELINE" if commit == base else "UNVALIDATED_NEW_BASE"
            application = "APPLIED"
        receipt = _receipt(destination, manifest, manifest_hash, commit, role, status, application, applied_ids)
        if _source_snapshot(source) != snapshot:
            raise OverlayError("SOURCE_CHANGED", "Source changed during preparation; candidate must not be accepted")
        _write_receipt(destination, receipt)
        return {"operation": "prepare", **receipt, "destination": str(destination),
                "receipt": str(destination / RECEIPT_NAME), "source_preserved": True}
    except OverlayError as exc:
        if cloned and not os.path.lexists(destination / RECEIPT_NAME):
            try:
                failure = _receipt(destination, manifest, manifest_hash, commit, role,
                                   "INCOMPATIBLE", "INCOMPLETE", applied_ids)
                failure["failure"] = exc.payload()
                _write_receipt(destination, failure)
            except (OSError, OverlayError):
                pass  # Preserve the primary error; never repair or delete a failed checkout automatically.
        if cloned:
            exc.details["isolated_destination"] = str(destination)
            exc.details["source_preserved"] = _source_snapshot(source) == snapshot
        raise


class JsonParser(argparse.ArgumentParser):
    def error(self, message: str) -> None:
        raise OverlayError("ARGUMENT_ERROR", message)


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = JsonParser(description=__doc__)
    commands = parser.add_subparsers(dest="operation", required=True, parser_class=JsonParser)
    for name in ("inspect", "prepare"):
        command = commands.add_parser(name)
        command.add_argument("--source", type=Path, required=True)
        command.add_argument("--manifest", type=Path, default=KIT_ROOT / "manifest.json")
        command.add_argument("--patches-dir", type=Path, default=None,
                             help="Patch filenames are relative to this directory (default: manifest parent / patches)")
        if name == "prepare":
            command.add_argument("--destination", type=Path, required=True)
            command.add_argument("--ref", help="Explicit full commit SHA; required when HEAD is not the manifest base")
            command.add_argument("--baseline-only", action="store_true")
    try:
        args = parser.parse_args(argv)
        patches_dir = args.patches_dir or args.manifest.resolve().parent / "patches"
        if args.operation == "inspect":
            payload = inspect(args.source, args.manifest, patches_dir)
        else:
            payload = prepare(args.source, args.destination, args.manifest, patches_dir,
                              args.ref, args.baseline_only)
        print(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True))
        return 0
    except OverlayError as exc:
        print(json.dumps(exc.payload(), ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True))
        return 2
    except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
        print(json.dumps({"status": "ERROR", "code": "IO_OR_DATA_ERROR", "message": str(exc)},
                         ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True))
        return 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/p17_smoke.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Compare greedy token IDs on two pinned servers, sequentially, over loopback."""
import argparse
from contextlib import contextmanager
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import secrets
import socket
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request

KIT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
PROMPT = "Москва — столица России. Берлин — столица"
FILL_TEXT = " The brown fox crosses a field and follows a quiet river."
API = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))


def request(port, route, body=None, timeout=10):
    payload = json.dumps(body).encode() if body is not None else None
    req = urllib.request.Request(f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/{route}", payload,
                                 headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    with API.open(req, timeout=timeout) as response:
        return json.load(response)


def new_output(parser, path):
    try:
        path.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    except FileExistsError:
        parser.error(f"Output already exists: {path}. Previous logs were preserved. "
                     "Choose a fresh --out directory, e.g. out-$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)-$$.")


def gpu_snapshot():
    """Driver-reported availability for each physical device at sampling time."""
    exe = shutil.which("nvidia-smi")
    if not exe:
        return {"available": False, "reason": "nvidia-smi not on PATH"}
    try:
        result = {}
        for name, switch, fields in (
                ("gpu", "gpu", "index,uuid,pci.bus_id,name,memory.total,memory.free,memory.used,driver_version"),
                ("process", "compute-apps", "gpu_uuid,pid,used_memory")):
            cmd = [exe, f"--query-{switch}={fields}", "--format=csv,noheader,nounits"]
            proc = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
            result[name] = {"fields": fields.split(","), "rows": proc.stdout.strip().splitlines(),
                            "error": proc.stderr.strip(), "exit_code": proc.returncode}
        return {"available": result["gpu"]["exit_code"] == 0, "memory_unit": "MiB", **result}
    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as exc:
        return {"available": False, "reason": str(exc)}


def log_diagnostic(logfile):
    # Retain the whole log on disk, but bound console and report error text.
    with logfile.open("rb") as src:
        size = src.seek(0, 2)
        src.seek(max(0, size - 65536))
        tail = src.read().decode(errors="replace").splitlines()
    clues = [line for line in tail if re.search(r"out of memory|cudaMalloc|failed to allocate|free.*MiB", line, re.I)]
    kind = "CUDA OOM" if any(re.search("out of memory", x, re.I) for x in clues) else "server failure"
    return kind + " | " + " | ".join((clues or tail)[-5:])[-1800:]


@contextmanager
def server(binary, label, env_flags, args, dspark_gguf=None):
    env = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items()
           if not k.startswith(("LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_", "LLAMA_ARG_"))}
    env.update(env_flags, LD_LIBRARY_PATH=str(binary.parent) +
               (os.pathsep + env["LD_LIBRARY_PATH"] if env.get("LD_LIBRARY_PATH") else ""))
    alias = "p17-" + secrets.token_hex(12)
    with socket.socket() as sock:
        sock.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
        port = sock.getsockname()[1]
    cmd = [str(binary), "-m", str(args.model), "--host", "127.0.0.1", "--port", str(port),
           "--alias", alias, "-lv", "4", "-np", "1", "-c", str(args.ctx), "--fit", "off",
           "-ngl", str(args.ngl), "-b", "128", "-ub", "64", "-t", str(args.threads),
           "--cache-type-k", "f16", "--cache-type-v", "f16", "--no-warmup", "--no-ui",
           "--no-context-shift", "--offline"]
    if dspark_gguf is not None:
        if args.mtp:
            raise ValueError("Choose one trained draft mechanism per comparison")
        cmd.extend(["--spec-type", "draft-dspark", "--spec-draft-model", str(dspark_gguf),
                    "--spec-draft-ngl", "all", "--spec-draft-n-max", "4", "--spec-draft-p-min", "0",
                    "--spec-draft-threads", str(args.threads)])
    elif args.mtp:
        cmd.extend(["--spec-type", "draft-mtp", "--spec-draft-n-max", str(args.draft_max)])
        if args.draft_model:
            cmd.extend(["--spec-draft-model", str(args.draft_model)])
    else:
        cmd.extend(["--spec-type", "none"])
    if args.moe_cache_mib:
        cmd.extend(["--cpu-moe", "--moe-cache-mib", str(args.moe_cache_mib)])
    logfile = args.out / (label + ".log")
    print(f"{label} — {logfile}", flush=True)
    record = {"command": cmd, "experiment_environment": env_flags, "gpu_before_launch": gpu_snapshot(),
              "cuda_visible_devices": env.get("CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES")}
    diagnostic_path = args.out / (label + ".launch.json")
    def save_record():
        diagnostic_path.write_text(json.dumps(record, indent=2) + "\n")
    save_record()
    with logfile.open("xb") as log:
        started = time.perf_counter()
        process = subprocess.Popen(cmd, env=env, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=log, stderr=log)
        try:
            record["pid"] = process.pid
            save_record()
            deadline = time.monotonic() + args.timeout
            while time.monotonic() < deadline:
                if process.poll() is not None:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"{label} exited {process.returncode}: {log_diagnostic(logfile)}. See {logfile} and {diagnostic_path}")
                try:
                    if request(port, "health")["status"] == "ok":
                        break
                except (OSError, urllib.error.URLError):
                    pass
                time.sleep(0.5)
            else:
                raise RuntimeError(f"{label} load timed out. See {logfile}")
            record["startup_to_health_s"] = time.perf_counter() - started
            props = request(port, "props")
            if (props.get("model_alias") != alias or props.get("total_slots") != 1 or
                    Path(props["model_path"]).resolve() != args.model or
                    props["default_generation_settings"]["n_ctx"] != args.ctx):
                raise ValueError(f"Unexpected server model, alias, slot count or effective context; "
                                 f"reported model {props.get('model_path')}, slots {props.get('total_slots')}, "
                                 f"n_ctx {props.get('default_generation_settings', {}).get('n_ctx')}, expected {args.ctx}")
            record["effective_slot_ctx"] = props["default_generation_settings"]["n_ctx"]
            record["model_path"] = props["model_path"]
            record["model_ftype"] = props.get("model_ftype")
            with logfile.open("rb") as handle:
                offload = next((m for line in handle if (m := re.search(
                    rb'offloaded (\d+)/(\d+) layers to GPU', line))), None)
            if args.ngl and (offload is None or int(offload[1]) != min(args.ngl, int(offload[2]))):
                raise ValueError(f"GPU layer offload was not confirmed in {logfile}")
            record["offload_layers"] = list(map(int, offload.groups())) if offload else None
            record["gpu_after_load"] = gpu_snapshot()
            save_record()
            yield port, record
        except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, RuntimeError) as exc:
            record["failure"] = str(exc)
            record["gpu_on_error"] = gpu_snapshot()
            clue = log_diagnostic(logfile)
            if "CUDA OOM" in clue and "CUDA OOM" not in str(exc):
                raise RuntimeError(f"{label}: {clue}. See {logfile} and {diagnostic_path}") from exc
            raise
        finally:
            if process.poll() is None:
                process.terminate()
            try:
                process.wait(timeout=60)
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                process.kill()
                process.wait()
            record["exit_code"] = process.returncode
            record["gpu_after_exit"] = gpu_snapshot()
            save_record()
        if process.returncode:
            raise RuntimeError(f"{label} failed on shutdown (exit {process.returncode}). See {logfile}")


def smoke(binary, label, env_flags, args):
    with server(binary, label, env_flags, args) as (port, launch):
        prompt = request(port, "tokenize", {"content": PROMPT, "add_special": True})["tokens"]
        if args.fill_tokens:
            filler = request(port, "tokenize", {"content": FILL_TEXT, "add_special": False})["tokens"]
            if not filler or args.fill_tokens < len(prompt):
                raise ValueError("Requested prompt length too small, or tokenizer returned no filler")
            n = args.fill_tokens - len(prompt)
            prompt += (filler * ((n + len(filler) - 1) // len(filler)))[:n]
        res = request(port, "completion", {"prompt": prompt, "temperature": 0, "seed": 42,
                      "n_predict": args.predict, "return_tokens": True, "ignore_eos": True,
                      "cache_prompt": False, "stream": False}, args.timeout)
        ids = res["tokens"]
        if (not isinstance(ids, list) or not all(type(t) is int and t >= 0 for t in ids)
                or len(ids) != args.predict or res["tokens_predicted"] != len(ids)
                or res["tokens_evaluated"] != len(prompt) or res["truncated"]):
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid or truncated token result from {label}")
        if args.mtp and res["timings"].get("draft_n", 0) <= 0:
            raise ValueError(f"No MTP draft tokens were verified for {label}")
        result = {"label": label, "command": launch["command"], "experiment_environment": env_flags,
                "context": args.ctx, "prompt_tokens": len(prompt),
                "prompt_ids_sha256": hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(prompt).encode()).hexdigest(),
                "tokens": ids, "content": res["content"], "timings": res["timings"]}
    return result


def validate_inputs(parser, args):
    args.model = args.model.expanduser().resolve()
    args.builds = args.builds.expanduser().resolve()
    if not args.model.is_file():
        parser.error("Model GGUF not found: " + str(args.model))
    shard = re.search(r"-(\d{5})-of-(\d{5})\.gguf$", args.model.name)
    if shard:
        count = int(shard[2])
        prefix = args.model.name[:shard.start()]
        if shard[1] != "00001" or count < 1:
            parser.error("Specify the first shard")
        for i in range(1, count + 1):
            path = args.model.with_name(f"{prefix}-{i:05}-of-{count:05}.gguf")
            if not path.is_file():
                parser.error("Missing shard: " + str(path))
    if (min(args.ctx, args.threads, args.predict, args.timeout) < 1 or
            min(args.ngl, args.fill_tokens, args.moe_cache_mib) < 0 or
            args.fill_tokens + args.predict >= args.ctx):
        parser.error("Invalid sizes or prompt/output exceed context capacity")
    bins = {r: args.builds / r / "bin/llama-server" for r in ("baseline", "candidate")}
    if not all(p.is_file() and os.access(p, os.X_OK) for p in bins.values()):
        parser.error("Missing executable in baseline/bin or candidate/bin under --builds")
    return bins


def main():
    profiles = {p["name"]: p["environment"] for p in
                json.loads((KIT / "profiles/experiments.json").read_text())["profiles"]}
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--builds", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--model", type=Path, required=True, help="GGUF or first shard")
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True, help="New report directory")
    parser.add_argument("--ctx", type=int, default=131072)
    parser.add_argument("--ngl", type=int, default=18)
    parser.add_argument("--threads", type=int, default=8)
    parser.add_argument("--moe-cache-mib", type=int, default=12288)
    parser.add_argument("--predict", type=int, default=64)
    parser.add_argument("--fill-tokens", type=int, default=0)
    parser.add_argument("--timeout", type=int, default=1800, help="Seconds per model load or completion")
    parser.add_argument("--mtp", action="store_true", help="Use single MTP on baseline and candidate")
    parser.add_argument("--draft-model", type=Path, help="Matching MTP GGUF for a separate drafter")
    parser.add_argument("--draft-max", type=int, default=3)
    allowed = sorted(set(profiles) - {"candidate_all_off"})
    parser.add_argument("--profile", choices=allowed, help="Also test this candidate profile after all-off")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if not args.mtp and (args.draft_model or (args.profile or "").startswith("p16")):
        parser.error("This draft model/profile requires --mtp")
    if args.draft_max < 1:
        parser.error("Baseline MTP comparison requires --draft-max >= 1")
    if args.draft_model:
        args.draft_model = args.draft_model.expanduser().resolve()
        if not args.draft_model.is_file():
            parser.error("Draft GGUF not found: " + str(args.draft_model))
    bins = validate_inputs(parser, args)
    new_output(parser, args.out)
    report = {"status": "RUNNING", "model": str(args.model), "runs": []}
    record = args.out / "smoke.json"
    schedule = [("baseline", "candidate_all_off"), ("candidate", "candidate_all_off")]
    if args.profile:
        schedule.append(("candidate", args.profile))
    try:
        for role, profile in schedule:
            run = smoke(bins[role], role if role == "baseline" else profile, profiles[profile], args)
            report["runs"].append(run)
            base = report["runs"][0]
            if run["prompt_ids_sha256"] != base["prompt_ids_sha256"]:
                raise ValueError(f"Tokenized prompts differ for {profile}. Compare {record}")
            if run["tokens"] != base["tokens"]:
                pos = next(i for i, (a, b) in enumerate(zip(base["tokens"], run["tokens"])) if a != b)
                raise ValueError(f"{profile}: output ID at position {pos} differs "
                                 f"({base['tokens'][pos]} vs {run['tokens'][pos]}). See {record}")
            timings = run["timings"]
            print(f"{run['label']}  prompt {run['prompt_tokens']} tokens / "
                  f"{timings['prompt_per_second']:.2f} t/s, generation {len(run['tokens'])} tokens / "
                  f"{timings['predicted_per_second']:.2f} t/s", flush=True)
            record.write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n")
        report["status"] = "PASS_TOKEN_SMOKE"
    except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError, KeyError, KeyboardInterrupt) as exc:
        report.update(status="FAIL", error=str(exc) or type(exc).__name__)
        print(report["error"], file=sys.stderr)
    finally:
        record.write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n")
    print(f"{report['status']}  {record}")
    return int(report["status"] != "PASS_TOKEN_SMOKE")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/r20_bench.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Within-host crossover benchmark. Use a fresh directory for each prompt/configuration."""
import argparse
from collections import defaultdict
import hashlib
import json
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import platform
import random
import re
import statistics
import sys
import time
import urllib.request

import p17_smoke as smoke


def file_hash(path):
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as f:
        for part in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b""):
            h.update(part)
    return h.hexdigest()


HISTORICAL_R19_MANIFEST = "79c9db6cab118b5fc6931b4d4b431c15fdc7ac27a7ebc206b51fd89f01bd4387"


def provenance(args, builds=None, expected_manifest=None):
    builds = builds if builds is not None else args.builds
    path = builds / "build_record.json"
    rec = json.loads(path.read_text())
    manifest = smoke.KIT / "manifest.json"
    ver = rec["plan"]["verification"]
    if (rec.get("status") != "BUILD_ONLY_KNOWN_BASE" or rec.get("exit_code") != 0
            or ver["base_commit"] != json.loads(manifest.read_text())["upstream"]["base_commit"]
            or ver["manifest_sha256"] != (expected_manifest or file_hash(manifest))):
        raise ValueError("Wrong or incomplete build receipt. Rebuild from this kit.")
    if args.ngl and (rec["plan"].get("cuda_requested") is not True
                     or rec["plan"].get("cuda_arch_requested") != "120"):
        raise ValueError("Expected sm_120 CUDA pair in build receipt")
    hashes = {}
    for role in ("baseline", "candidate"):
        build = builds / role
        expected = {a["path"]: a["sha256"] for a in rec["builds"][role]["artifacts"]}
        files = {p for p in (build / "bin").glob("*.so*") if p.is_file()}
        files.add(build / "bin/llama-server")
        for p in sorted(files):
            digest = hashes.get(str(p.resolve()))
            if digest is None:
                digest = hashes[str(p.resolve())] = file_hash(p)
            if digest != expected.get(p.relative_to(build).as_posix()):
                raise ValueError(f"Unrecorded or changed executable/library: {p}")
    return {"build_record_sha256": file_hash(path), "base_commit": ver["base_commit"],
            "manifest_sha256": ver["manifest_sha256"], "checked_artifacts": hashes}


def model_inventory(model):
    """Fast identity check of every shard; full hashing hundreds of GiB warms the disk cache."""
    paths = [model]
    shard = re.search(r"-(\d{5})-of-(\d{5})\.gguf$", model.name)
    if shard:
        paths = [model.with_name(f"{model.name[:shard.start()]}-{i:05}-of-{shard[2]}.gguf")
                 for i in range(1, int(shard[2]) + 1)]
    inventory = {}
    for path in paths:
        st = path.stat()
        inventory[str(path)] = [st.st_dev, st.st_ino, st.st_size, st.st_mtime_ns]
    return inventory


def bundle_flags(profiles, include_r20=False):
    """Explicit multi-flag DS4.1 hypothesis. Timing flags never enable trace or a drafter."""
    env = profiles["candidate_all_off"].copy()
    prefix = "LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_"
    flags = ["MOE_PLAN_REUSE", "DSV4_HC_POST_LAYERS", "HASH_SCRATCH", "PREFETCH_UNIQUE",
             "MOE_PIPELINE", "MOE_PERSISTENT", "MOE_SHARED_PLAN", "ENGRAM_STAGE",
             "ENGRAM_MODEL_CACHE", "ENGRAM_ASYNC", "MOE_SCHEDULER", "MOE_EVENT_FENCE",
             "DSV4_HC_POST_REUSE", "CUDA_MMQ_TILE_CACHE"]
    if include_r20:
        flags.extend(["CPU_IQ3_SIGNMASK", "MOE_BYPASS_NO_PACK", "MOE_DEMAND_READAHEAD"])
    if not all(prefix + f in env for f in flags):
        raise ValueError("Full bundle flags are missing from this kit")
    env.update({prefix + f: "1" for f in flags})
    env[prefix + "MOE_STAGING_MIB"] = "32"
    return env


def historical_flags(env):
    r20_only = {"LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_" + name for name in
                ("CPU_IQ3_SIGNMASK", "MOE_BYPASS_NO_PACK", "MOE_DEMAND_READAHEAD", "ENGRAM_TRACE")}
    return {k: v for k, v in env.items() if k not in r20_only}


def crossover(labels, repeats, seed):
    """Randomized complete blocks; adjacent pairs have exactly reversed orders."""
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    order = labels.copy()
    blocks = []
    for i in range(repeats):
        if i % 2 == 0:
            rng.shuffle(order)
        else:
            order.reverse()
        blocks.append(order.copy())
    return blocks


def tokenized(port, text, timeout):
    result = smoke.request(port, "tokenize", {"content": text, "add_special": True}, timeout)["tokens"]
    if not isinstance(result, list) or not result or any(type(t) is not int or t < 0 for t in result):
        raise ValueError("Invalid tokenizer response")
    return result


def prompt_ids(port, args):
    n = args.fill_tokens
    text = args.prompt_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8") if args.prompt_file else smoke.PROMPT
    if n and not args.prompt_file:
        # Tokenize a complete text; truncation is applied to actual tokenizer IDs.
        text += "".join(f"\nRecord {i}. Copper and water. Запись {i}: ветер у реки."
                        for i in range(n // 6 + 16))
    ids = tokenized(port, text, args.timeout)
    if n and len(ids) < n:
        raise ValueError(f"Input produced {len(ids)} tokens, need {n}. Supply a longer --prompt-file")
    ids = ids[:n] if n else ids
    if len(ids) + args.predict >= args.ctx:
        raise ValueError(f"Tokenized prompt ({len(ids)}) + output ({args.predict}) must be < {args.ctx}")
    return ids


def payload(ids, predict, stream=False):
    return {"prompt": ids, "temperature": 0, "seed": 42, "n_predict": predict, "return_tokens": True,
            "ignore_eos": True, "cache_prompt": False, "stream": stream}


def validate_completion(response, n_input, n_output):
    timings = response["timings"]
    if (response["truncated"] is not False or response["tokens_evaluated"] != n_input
            or timings.get("cache_n", 0) != 0 or timings["prompt_n"] != n_input or response["tokens_predicted"] != n_output
            or timings["predicted_n"] != n_output):
        raise ValueError("Cached, truncated, or incomplete request: "
                         f"input={n_input}, output={n_output}, timings={timings}, "
                         f"truncated={response['truncated']}")
    for key in ("prompt_per_second", "predicted_per_second", "prompt_ms", "predicted_ms"):
        if not math.isfinite(timings[key]) or timings[key] <= 0:
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid server timing {key}: {timings[key]}")


def exact_completion(port, prompt, predict, timeout):
    start = time.perf_counter()
    res = smoke.request(port, "completion", payload(prompt, predict), timeout)
    duration = time.perf_counter() - start
    validate_completion(res, len(prompt), predict)
    ids = res["tokens"]
    if not isinstance(ids, list) or len(ids) != predict or any(type(t) is not int or t < 0 for t in ids):
        raise ValueError("Server did not provide all generated token IDs")
    return {"tokens": ids, "timings": res["timings"], "request_s": duration}


def completion_stream(port, prompt, predict, timeout, on_first=None):
    """Measure first client-observed token event; SSE can omit incomplete UTF-8 token IDs."""
    req = urllib.request.Request(f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/completion",
                                 json.dumps(payload(prompt, predict, True)).encode(),
                                 headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    start = time.perf_counter()
    first = None
    count = 0
    final = None
    with smoke.API.open(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:
        if "text/event-stream" not in resp.headers.get("Content-Type", ""):
            raise ValueError("Expected a streaming SSE response")
        for line in resp:
            arrived = time.perf_counter() - start
            if arrived > timeout:
                raise TimeoutError("Streaming completion deadline exceeded")
            if not line.startswith(b"data:"):
                continue
            data = line[5:].strip()
            if data == b"[DONE]":
                break
            row = json.loads(data)
            if "error" in row:
                raise ValueError(str(row["error"]))
            if row.get("stop") is True:
                final = row
                break
            if not row.get("prompt_progress") and row.get("tokens"):
                if first is None:
                    first = arrived
                    if on_first is not None:
                        on_first()
                count += len(row["tokens"])
    if first is None or final is None:
        raise ValueError("No client-observed token event or final SSE result")
    validate_completion(final, len(prompt), predict)
    return {"ttft_s": first, "emitted_ids_count": count, "tokens_predicted": predict,
            "timings": final["timings"]}


def warmup(port, args):
    prompt = tokenized(port, "Warm up. Count from one to sixteen.", args.timeout)
    return exact_completion(port, prompt, 16, args.timeout)


def summarize(values):
    return {"n": len(values), "median": statistics.median(values),
            "p95": sorted(values)[math.ceil(.95 * len(values)) - 1],
            "variance": statistics.variance(values) if len(values) > 1 else None}


def summarize_runs(runs):
    groups = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(list))
    for r in runs:
        group = groups[r["variant"]]
        for k in ("startup_to_health_s", "ttft_s", "request_s"):
            group[k].append(r[k])
        for k in ("prompt_per_second", "predicted_per_second", "prompt_ms", "predicted_ms"):
            group[k].append(r["timings"][k])
    return {label: {k: summarize(vals) for k, vals in g.items()} for label, g in groups.items()}


def paired_gains(runs, pairs, repeats):
    blocks = {(r["block"], r["variant"]): r for r in runs}
    result = []
    for control, experiment in pairs:
        entry = {"control": control, "experiment": experiment}
        for key in ("prompt_per_second", "predicted_per_second"):
            delta = []
            for i in range(repeats):
                c = blocks[i, control]["timings"][key]
                e = blocks[i, experiment]["timings"][key]
                delta.append((e / c - 1) * 100)
            entry[key] = {"paired_pct_by_block": delta, **summarize(delta)}
        result.append(entry)
    return result


def atomic_write(path, data):
    temp = path.with_suffix(".partial")
    temp.write_text(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, allow_nan=False) + "\n")
    temp.replace(path)


def compare_ids(reference, row):
    if row["prompt_ids_sha256"] != reference["prompt_ids_sha256"]:
        raise ValueError("Tokenized input differs between launches")
    if row["offload_layers"] != reference["offload_layers"]:
        raise ValueError("Effective number of offloaded layers changed")
    if row["tokens"] != reference["tokens"]:
        mismatch = next(i for i, (a, b) in enumerate(zip(row["tokens"], reference["tokens"])) if a != b)
        raise ValueError(f"Greedy ID differs from {reference['label']} at index {mismatch}: "
                         f"{row['tokens'][mismatch]} vs {reference['tokens'][mismatch]}")


def high_water_kib(pid):
    try:
        if os.readlink("/proc/self") != str(os.getpid()):
            return None
        for line in Path(f"/proc/{pid}/status").read_text().splitlines():
            if line.startswith("VmHWM:"):
                return int(line.split()[1])
    except OSError:
        pass
    return None


def main(argv=None):
    profiles = {p["name"]: p["environment"] for p in
                json.loads((smoke.KIT / "profiles/experiments.json").read_text())["profiles"]}
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument("--model", type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument("--builds", type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument("--profile", choices=sorted(profiles), action="append",
                   help="One flag relative to --control. May repeat. Default: planner_only.")
    p.add_argument("--bundle", choices=["r19-compatible"],
                   help="Predefined full compatible DeepSeek configuration; reports flag deltas")
    p.add_argument("--r19-builds", type=Path,
                   help="Historical R19 builds directory with the original build_pair receipt (bundle only)")
    p.add_argument("--control", choices=sorted(profiles), default="candidate_all_off")
    p.add_argument("--repeats", type=int, default=6)
    p.add_argument("--predict", type=int, default=256)
    p.add_argument("--ctx", type=int, default=131072)
    p.add_argument("--threads", type=int, default=8)
    p.add_argument("--ngl", type=int, default=18)
    p.add_argument("--moe-cache-mib", type=int, default=12288)
    p.add_argument("--order-seed", type=int, default=20261103)
    p.add_argument("--timeout", type=int, default=3600, help="Seconds per model load or request")
    p.add_argument("--prompt-file", type=Path, help="UTF-8 text; tokenize once per launch")
    length = p.add_mutually_exclusive_group()
    length.add_argument("--fill-tokens", type=int, default=0, help="Use exactly N real tokenizer IDs")
    length.add_argument("--near-full", action="store_true", help="Use ctx - predict - 1 input tokens")
    args = p.parse_args(argv)
    args.mtp = False
    if args.near_full:
        args.fill_tokens = args.ctx - args.predict - 1
    if args.repeats < 5 or not 256 <= args.predict <= 512 or args.ctx < 131072:
        p.error("Acceptance requires >=5 repeats, 256–512 generated tokens and context >=131072")
    bins = smoke.validate_inputs(p, args)
    if args.prompt_file and not args.prompt_file.is_file():
        p.error("Missing --prompt-file")
    if args.r19_builds and not args.bundle:
        p.error("--r19-builds requires --bundle r19-compatible")
    if args.r19_builds:
        args.r19_builds = args.r19_builds.expanduser().resolve()
    treatments = args.profile or ([] if args.bundle else ["planner_only"])
    labels = list(dict.fromkeys(["baseline", "candidate_all_off", args.control] + treatments))
    if args.control.startswith("p16") or any(t.startswith("p16") for t in treatments):
        p.error("This benchmark fixes --spec-type none. Use p17_smoke --mtp for compatible drafters.")
    for t in treatments:
        diff = {k for k in profiles[t].keys() | profiles[args.control].keys()
                if profiles[t].get(k) != profiles[args.control].get(k)}
        if len(diff) != 1:
            p.error(f"{args.control} vs {t} differs in {len(diff)} flags. Compare adjacent profiles.")
    arms = {label: (bins["baseline" if label == "baseline" else "candidate"],
                    profiles["candidate_all_off" if label == "baseline" else label])
            for label in labels}
    if args.bundle:
        full19 = bundle_flags(profiles)
        full20 = bundle_flags(profiles, include_r20=True)
        if args.r19_builds:
            historical_bin = args.r19_builds / "candidate/bin/llama-server"
            arms["r19_all_off"] = (historical_bin, historical_flags(profiles["candidate_all_off"]))
            arms["r19_full"] = (historical_bin, historical_flags(full19))
        arms["r20_r19_full"] = (bins["candidate"], full19)
        arms["r20_full"] = (bins["candidate"], full20)
    labels = list(arms)
    smoke.new_output(p, args.out)
    path = args.out / "BENCHMARKS.json"
    order = crossover(labels, args.repeats, args.order_seed)
    report = {"schema_version": 1, "status": "RUNNING", "order": order,
              "selected_profiles": {k: env for k, (_, env) in arms.items()},
              "flag_deltas_from_all_off": {k: {key: v for key, v in env.items()
                                                if v != profiles["candidate_all_off"].get(key)}
                                           for k, (_, env) in arms.items()},
              "settings": {k: str(v) if isinstance(v, Path) else v for k, v in vars(args).items()},
              "host": platform.uname()._asdict(), "runs": []}
    atomic_write(path, report)
    try:
        report["provenance"] = provenance(args)
        if args.r19_builds:
            report["r19_provenance"] = provenance(args, args.r19_builds, HISTORICAL_R19_MANIFEST)
        report["shards_dev_ino_bytes_mtime_ns"] = model_inventory(args.model)
        # Separate excluded conditioning pass for every variant before timed blocks.
        report["conditioning"] = []
        reference = None
        for block, variants in enumerate([order[0], *order], start=-1):
            for position, variant in enumerate(variants):
                stage = "condition" if block < 0 else f"b{block + 1:02}"
                label = f"{stage}-{position + 1:02}-{variant}"
                if model_inventory(args.model) != report["shards_dev_ino_bytes_mtime_ns"]:
                    raise ValueError("Model shard identity changed during the benchmark")
                report["active"] = label
                atomic_write(path, report)
                binary, env = arms[variant]
                with smoke.server(binary, label, env, args) as (port, launch):
                    warm = warmup(port, args)
                    if block < 0:
                        report["conditioning"].append({"variant": variant, "warmup": warm})
                        continue
                    ids = prompt_ids(port, args)
                    row = {"block": block, "position": position, "label": label, "variant": variant,
                           "startup_to_health_s": launch["startup_to_health_s"],
                           "offload_layers": launch["offload_layers"],
                           "prompt_ids_sha256": hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(ids).encode()).hexdigest(),
                           "prompt_tokens": len(ids), "warmup": warm,
                           **exact_completion(port, ids, args.predict, args.timeout)}
                    report["runs"].append(row)
                    atomic_write(path, report)
                    compare_ids(reference or row, row)
                    reference = reference or row
                    row["rss_peak_kib_through_decode"] = high_water_kib(launch["pid"])
                    row["gpu_after_decode"] = smoke.gpu_snapshot()
                    row["ttft_probe"] = completion_stream(port, ids, 8, args.timeout)
                    row["ttft_s"] = row["ttft_probe"]["ttft_s"]
                print(f"{label}   PP {row['timings']['prompt_per_second']:.3f}   "
                      f"TG {row['timings']['predicted_per_second']:.3f}   "
                      f"TTFT {row['ttft_s']:.3f} s", flush=True)
                atomic_write(path, report)
        report["summary"] = summarize_runs(report["runs"])
        pairs = [("baseline", "candidate_all_off")]
        for t in treatments:
            pairs.extend([(args.control, t), ("candidate_all_off", t), ("baseline", t)])
        if args.bundle:
            for treatment in ("r20_r19_full", "r20_full"):
                pairs.extend([("baseline", treatment), ("candidate_all_off", treatment)])
            pairs.append(("r20_r19_full", "r20_full"))
            if args.r19_builds:
                pairs.extend([("r19_all_off", "r19_full"), ("baseline", "r19_full"),
                              ("r19_all_off", "candidate_all_off"),
                              ("r19_full", "r20_r19_full"), ("r19_full", "r20_full")])
        report["paired_gains"] = paired_gains(report["runs"], list(dict.fromkeys(pairs)), args.repeats)
        report.update(status="COMPLETE_EQUAL_GREEDY_IDS")
        del report["active"]
        with (args.out / "SUMMARY.tsv").open("x") as table:
            table.write("variant\tmetric\tn\tmedian\tp95\tsample_variance\n")
            for variant, group in report["summary"].items():
                for metric, stats in group.items():
                    table.write(f"{variant}\t{metric}\t{stats['n']}\t{stats['median']:.8g}\t"
                                f"{stats['p95']:.8g}\t{stats['variance']:.8g}\n")
        for label, group in report["summary"].items():
            print(f"{label}: median PP {group['prompt_per_second']['median']:.3f}, "
                  f"median TG {group['predicted_per_second']['median']:.3f} tok/s")
    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError, RuntimeError, KeyboardInterrupt) as exc:
        report.update(status="FAIL", error=str(exc) or type(exc).__name__)
        print(report["error"], file=sys.stderr)
    finally:
        atomic_write(path, report)
    print(report["status"], path, flush=True)
    return int(report["status"] != "COMPLETE_EQUAL_GREEDY_IDS")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/r20_cancel.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Disconnect a streamed request on an isolated candidate, verify early cancel and reuse."""
import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import sys
import time
import urllib.request

import p17_smoke as smoke
import r20_bench as bench


def disconnect(port, ids, predict, timeout):
    req = urllib.request.Request(f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/completion",
                                 json.dumps(bench.payload(ids, predict, True)).encode(),
                                 headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    with smoke.API.open(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:
        for line in resp:
            if line.startswith(b"data:"):
                row = json.loads(line[5:])
                if row.get("tokens") and not row.get("stop"):
                    return row["tokens_predicted"]  # closes this response on return
    raise ValueError("Stream ended without a token to interrupt")


def cancel_and_reuse(port, launch, args, logfile):
    prompt = bench.prompt_ids(port, args)
    before = bench.exact_completion(port, prompt, args.predict, args.timeout)
    stream_tokens = min(4096, args.ctx - len(prompt) - 1)
    if stream_tokens <= args.predict:
        raise ValueError("Not enough remaining context for cancellation probe")
    first = disconnect(port, prompt, stream_tokens, args.timeout)
    started = time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic() - started < args.cancel_timeout:
        slots = smoke.request(port, "slots", timeout=min(10, args.cancel_timeout))
        if len(slots) != 1:
            raise ValueError("Unexpected slot count")
        slot = slots[0]
        if not slot["is_processing"]:
            break
        time.sleep(.05)
    else:
        raise TimeoutError("Slot failed to return to idle after disconnect")
    idle = time.monotonic() - started
    # The pinned server logs the cancel task and preserves the previous task ID on an idle slot.
    marker = re.compile(rb"cancel task, id_task = " + str(slot["id_task"]).encode() + rb"\b")
    with logfile.open("rb") as f:
        cancelled = any(marker.search(line) for line in f)
    n_remaining = slot["n_prompt_tokens"]
    if not cancelled or n_remaining >= len(prompt) + stream_tokens - 1:
        raise ValueError(f"Early cancel not confirmed: cancel_log={cancelled}, cached_tokens={n_remaining}")
    after = [bench.exact_completion(port, prompt, args.predict, args.timeout) for _ in range(2)]
    result = {"context": args.ctx, "pid": launch["pid"], "prompt_tokens": len(prompt),
              "requested_stream_tokens": stream_tokens, "client_tokens_at_close": first,
              "slot_idle_after_close_s": idle, "cancelled_task_id": slot["id_task"],
              "idle_slot_prompt_tokens": n_remaining, "before": before, "after": after}
    result["same_ids"] = all(r["tokens"] == before["tokens"] for r in after)
    return result


def main():
    profiles = {p["name"]: p["environment"] for p in
                json.loads((smoke.KIT / "profiles/experiments.json").read_text())["profiles"]}
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument("--builds", type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument("--model", type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument("--ctx", type=int, default=131072)
    p.add_argument("--ngl", type=int, default=18)
    p.add_argument("--threads", type=int, default=8)
    p.add_argument("--moe-cache-mib", type=int, default=12288)
    p.add_argument("--predict", type=int, default=64)
    p.add_argument("--timeout", type=int, default=3600)
    p.add_argument("--cancel-timeout", type=int, default=120)
    p.add_argument("--profile", choices=[k for k in profiles if not k.startswith("p16")], default="candidate_all_off")
    args = p.parse_args()
    args.fill_tokens, args.prompt_file, args.mtp = 0, None, False
    bins = smoke.validate_inputs(p, args)
    smoke.new_output(p, args.out)
    report = {"status": "RUNNING", "profile": args.profile}
    path = args.out / "cancel.json"
    try:
        report["provenance"] = bench.provenance(args)
        with smoke.server(bins["candidate"], "cancel", profiles[args.profile], args) as (port, launch):
            report["result"] = cancel_and_reuse(port, launch, args, args.out / "cancel.log")
            if not report["result"]["same_ids"]:
                raise ValueError("Post-cancel request produced different IDs")
        report["status"] = "PASS_CANCEL_REUSE_SHUTDOWN"
    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, RuntimeError, KeyboardInterrupt) as exc:
        report.update(status="FAIL", error=str(exc) or type(exc).__name__)
    finally:
        bench.atomic_write(path, report)
    print(report["status"], report.get("error", ""), path)
    return int(report["status"] != "PASS_CANCEL_REUSE_SHUTDOWN")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/r20_engram_ab.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Measure exact staged raw rows in memory. Requires an unpacked, original R19 kit."""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import platform
import shutil
import statistics
import subprocess
import tempfile


def run():
    here = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument("--r19-kit", type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True, help="new result JSON outside package")
    p.add_argument("--cpu", type=int, help="pin test process to one available CPU")
    args = p.parse_args()
    if args.out.exists():
        p.error("--out must be new")
    source = here / "tests_cpp/r20_engram_measure.cpp"
    compiler = shutil.which("g++")
    if not compiler:
        p.error("g++ required")
    env = dict(os.environ, LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_ENGRAM_TRACE="0")
    if args.cpu is not None:
        os.sched_setaffinity(0, {args.cpu})
    flags = ["-std=c++17", "-O3", "-DNDEBUG", "-Wall", "-Wextra", "-Werror", "-pthread"]
    record = dict(scope="CPU-only, resident byte array; no mmap faults, CUDA or model inference measured",
                  compiler=subprocess.check_output([compiler, "--version"], text=True).splitlines()[0],
                  host=platform.platform(), cpu_affinity=sorted(os.sched_getaffinity(0)), flags=flags,
                  cpp_sha256=hashlib.sha256(source.read_bytes()).hexdigest(), inputs={}, runs=[], summaries={})
    model = Path("/proc/cpuinfo")
    if model.is_file():
        record["cpu_model"] = next((line.split(":", 1)[1].strip() for line in model.read_text().splitlines()
                                   if line.startswith("model name")), None)
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
        exe = {}
        for label, kit in [("R19", args.r19_kit), ("R20", here)]:
            headers = kit / "modules" / "engram"
            record["inputs"][label] = {f.name: hashlib.sha256(f.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
                                       for f in sorted(headers.glob("*.hpp"))}
            exe[label] = str(Path(temp) / label)
            subprocess.run([compiler, *flags, "-I", str(kit / "modules"), str(source), "-o", exe[label]], check=True)
        for case in ["duplicate", "warm", "cold"]:
            def sample(label, warmup, block):
                proc = subprocess.run([exe[label], case], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30,
                                      env=env, check=True)
                data = json.loads(proc.stdout)
                record["runs"].append(dict(case=case, label=label, warmup=warmup, block=block,
                                           stdout=proc.stdout.strip(), stderr=proc.stderr, **data))
                if case == "duplicate":
                    expected = data["rows"] * (2 if label == "R19" else 1)
                    if data["reader_calls"] != expected:
                        raise RuntimeError("duplicate-read regression")
            for label in exe:
                sample(label, True, None)
            for block in range(6):
                order = ["R19", "R20", "R20", "R19"] if block % 2 == 0 else ["R20", "R19", "R19", "R20"]
                for label in order:
                    sample(label, False, block)
            results = [x for x in record["runs"] if x["case"] == case and not x["warmup"]]
            if case == "duplicate":
                continue
            if len({(x["digest"], x["source_reads"], x["hits"]) for x in results}) != 1:
                raise RuntimeError("inconsistent row bytes or hits/misses")
            for label in exe:
                series = [r["wall_ns"] / r["rows"] for r in results if r["label"] == label]
                record["summaries"][case + "_" + label] = dict(
                    n=len(series), median_ns_per_row=statistics.median(series),
                    p95_nearest_rank_ns_per_row=sorted(series)[math.ceil(len(series) * .95) - 1],
                    sample_variance_ns2_per_row2=statistics.variance(series))
    with args.out.open("x", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(record, f, indent=2)
        f.write("\n")
    print(json.dumps(record["summaries"], indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run()

===== SOURCE FILE tools/r20_engram_native.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Linux: native CPU graph parity on a small deterministic, untrained DS4.1 GGUF."""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import platform
import random
import resource
import time
import statistics
import math
import subprocess


def sha(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    for key in ("source", "control-bin", "candidate-bin", "out"):
        parser.add_argument("--" + key, type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--bench", action="store_true", help="include six balanced 3-arm native PP/TG blocks")
    parser.add_argument("--tg", type=int, default=256, help="single-token decode steps per request")
    parser.add_argument("--order-seed", type=int, help="shuffle the six complete within-block orders")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if not 1 <= args.tg <= 400:
        parser.error("--tg must be 1..400")
    src, control, candidate, out = (x.resolve() for x in
                                    (args.source, args.control_bin, args.candidate_bin, args.out))
    fixtures = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "tests_cpp"
    try:
        out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    except FileExistsError:
        parser.error("--out must be new")
    env = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if not k.startswith(("LLAMA_", "GGML_"))}
    report = dict(scope="synthetic untrained CPU DeepSeek 4.1; mmap/lazy Q6_K Engram, 6 layers, "
                        "4 IQ3_XXS experts per layer (2 active); 128 vocabulary items, 512-token KV context",
                  os=platform.platform(), affinity=sorted(os.sched_getaffinity(0)), order_seed=args.order_seed,
                  tg_single_steps_per_request=args.tg, source_fixture_sha256=sha(src / "tests/test-llama-archs.cpp"),
                  compiler=subprocess.check_output(["g++", "--version"], text=True).splitlines()[0],
                  libs={k: sha(lib / "libllama.so") for k, lib in [("control", control), ("candidate", candidate)]},
                  commands=[], tests=[])
    def execute(name, cmd, lib, flags=None):
        env_run = dict(env, LD_LIBRARY_PATH=str(lib), **(flags or {}))
        cpu0 = resource.getrusage(resource.RUSAGE_CHILDREN)
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        with (out / (name + ".stdout")).open("w") as a, (out / (name + ".stderr")).open("w") as b:
            proc = subprocess.run(cmd, env=env_run, stdout=a, stderr=b, timeout=180)
        wall = time.monotonic() - t0
        cpu1 = resource.getrusage(resource.RUSAGE_CHILDREN)
        report["commands"].append(dict(whole_process_wall_s=wall,
                                       whole_process_cpu_s=(cpu1.ru_utime - cpu0.ru_utime + cpu1.ru_stime - cpu0.ru_stime),
                                       name=name, cmd=list(map(str, cmd)), library_path=str(lib),
                                       flags=flags, status=proc.returncode))
        if proc.returncode:
            raise RuntimeError(f"{name} failed; see {(out / (name + '.stderr'))}")
    try:
        for label in ["make", "decode"]:
            exe = out / label
            cmd = ["g++", "-std=c++17", "-O1", "-ffunction-sections", "-fdata-sections", "-Wl,--gc-sections"]
            cmd.extend("-I" + str(src / x) for x in ["tests", "include", "common", "src", "ggml/include", "vendor"])
            execute("compile-" + label, [*cmd, fixtures / f"r20_ds41_fixture_{label}.cpp", "-L" + str(control),
                    "-Wl,-rpath-link," + str(control), "-lllama", "-lggml", "-lggml-base", "-o", exe], control)
        model = out / "synthetic.gguf"
        execute("make-model", [out / "make", model], control)
        report["model"] = {"sha256": sha(model), "file_bytes": model.stat().st_size}
        reference = None
        modes = [
                ("upstream", control, (0, 0, 0)), ("off", candidate, (0, 0, 0)),
                ("local", candidate, (1, 0, 0)), ("shared", candidate, (1, 1, 0)),
                ("async", candidate, (1, 1, 1)), ("signmask", candidate, (0, 0, 0))]
        schedule = [(None, *mode) for mode in modes]
        if args.bench:
            # Each of three variants occupies each phase of a block twice.
            orders = ["012", "210", "120", "021", "201", "102"]
            if args.order_seed is not None:
                random.Random(args.order_seed).shuffle(orders)
            for block, order in enumerate(orders):
                for index in order:
                    schedule.append((block, *modes[[0, 1, 5][int(index)]]))
        for block, role, lib, switches in schedule:
            label = role if block is None else f"b{block}_{role}"
            flags = {"LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_ENGRAM_" + k: str(v)
                     for k, v in zip(("STAGE", "MODEL_CACHE", "ASYNC"), switches)}
            flags["LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_ENGRAM_TRACE"] = "1" if block is None else "0"
            flags["LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_CPU_IQ3_SIGNMASK"] = "1" if role == "signmask" else "0"
            logits = out / (label + ".f32")
            execute(label, [out / "decode", model, logits, str(args.tg)], lib, flags)
            stderr = (out / (label + ".stderr")).read_text()
            signmask_entered = "experimental_cpu_iq3_signmask: entered" in stderr
            if (role == "signmask") != signmask_entered:
                raise RuntimeError(f"{label} dispatched signmask kernel = {signmask_entered}")
            trace = [json.loads(line.partition("R20_ENGRAM_JSON ")[2]) for line in
                     (out / (label + ".stderr")).read_text().splitlines() if "R20_ENGRAM_JSON " in line]
            ids = [[int(x) for x in line.split()] for line in (out / (label + ".stdout")).read_text().splitlines()]
            if len(ids) != 3 or any(len(x) != args.tg + 1 for x in ids) or ids[0] != ids[2]:
                raise RuntimeError(f"{label} repetition/count mismatch")
            if logits.stat().st_size != (41 + 13 + 41 + args.tg * 3) * 128 * 4:
                raise RuntimeError(f"{label} logit count mismatch")
            current = (sha(logits), ids)
            if reference is None:
                reference = current
            if current != reference:
                raise RuntimeError(f"{label} disagrees with control logit bits or greedy IDs")
            count = sum(x["rows_completed"] for x in trace)
            if switches[0] and (not count or any(x["read_failures"] for x in trace)):
                raise RuntimeError(f"{label} Engram staging missing or failed")
            timings = next(json.loads(line.partition("R20_SYNTHETIC_TIMINGS ")[2])
                           for line in (out / (label + ".stderr")).read_text().splitlines()
                           if line.startswith("R20_SYNTHETIC_TIMINGS "))
            report["tests"].append(dict(label=label, role=role, block=block, timings=timings, signmask_entered=signmask_entered, logits_sha256=current[0], logits_f32=logits.stat().st_size // 4,
                                        greedy_ids=ids, row_counters=trace))
        if args.bench:
            report["summary"] = {}
            for role in ("upstream", "off", "signmask"):
                runs = [r["timings"] for r in report["tests"] if r["block"] is not None and r["role"] == role]
                stats = {}
                for kind in ("pp", "tg"):
                    samples = [1e9 * r[kind + "_tokens"] / r[kind + "_ns"] for r in runs]
                    stats[kind] = dict(n=len(samples), median_tok_s=statistics.median(samples),
                                       p95_tok_s=sorted(samples)[math.ceil(len(samples)*.95)-1],
                                       sample_variance=statistics.variance(samples))
                report["summary"][role] = stats
        report["status"] = "PASS"
    finally:
        (out / "result.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2) + "\n")
    print("PASS: " + str(out / "result.json"))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== SOURCE FILE tools/r20_gguf_inventory.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Read the tensor directory of each GGUF shard. Reads only headers of the specified local set.
Reader adapted from Project Maya v1.1.46 tools/ds4/pack_gguf.py, under the accompanying MIT license.
The numeric type codes are interpreted by the exact pinned GGML constants of the build.
"""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import math
from pathlib import Path
import re
import runpy
import struct

class Gguf:
    """One GGUF shard's metadata and tensor infos (offsets absolute in the file)."""

    def __init__(self, path: Path):
        self.path = path
        with path.open("rb") as f:
            self.f = f
            magic = f.read(4)
            if magic != b"GGUF":
                raise SystemExit("%s: not a GGUF file" % path)
            self.version, nt, nkv = struct.unpack("<IQQ", f.read(20))
            self.kv = {}
            for _ in range(nkv):
                k = self._str()
                self.kv[k] = self._val(struct.unpack("<I", f.read(4))[0])
            self.tensors = []
            for _ in range(nt):
                name = self._str()
                nd = struct.unpack("<I", f.read(4))[0]
                dims = list(struct.unpack("<%dQ" % nd, f.read(8 * nd)))
                typ, off = struct.unpack("<IQ", f.read(12))
                self.tensors.append((name, typ, dims, off))
            align = int(self.kv.get("general.alignment", 32))
            pos = f.tell()
            self.data_start = (pos + align - 1) // align * align
        del self.f


    def _str(self):
        n = struct.unpack("<Q", self.f.read(8))[0]
        return self.f.read(n).decode("utf-8", "replace")

    def _val(self, t):
        fmt = {0: "B", 1: "b", 2: "H", 3: "h", 4: "I", 5: "i", 6: "f", 7: "?", 10: "Q", 11: "q", 12: "d"}
        if t in fmt:
            n = struct.calcsize(fmt[t])
            return struct.unpack("<" + fmt[t], self.f.read(n))[0]
        if t == 8:
            return self._str()
        if t == 9:
            at, n = struct.unpack("<IQ", self.f.read(12))
            if at in fmt:
                n_b = struct.calcsize(fmt[at])
                return list(struct.unpack("<%d%s" % (n, fmt[at]), self.f.read(n * n_b)))
            return [self._val(at) for _ in range(n)]
        raise SystemExit("%s: metadata type %d" % (self.path, t))



def inventory(paths, source):
    c=runpy.run_path(str(source/'gguf-py/gguf/constants.py'))
    types, blocks = c['GGMLQuantizationType'], c['GGML_QUANT_SIZES']
    result={'shards':[], 'layers':{}, 'scope':'logical routed tensor bytes; excludes shared/dense, caching, paging, transport'}
    count=None
    for path in paths:
        g=Gguf(path)
        with path.open('rb') as f:
            header=f.read(g.data_start)
        fingerprint=hashlib.sha256(header).hexdigest()
        result['shards'].append({'name':path.name,'header_bytes':len(header),'header_sha256':fingerprint})
        if 'general.architecture' in g.kv:
            arch=g.kv['general.architecture']
            result['architecture']=arch
            count=g.kv.get(arch+'.expert_used_count',count)
            for key in ('block_count','expert_count','ple.layers','ple.ngram_size','ple.heads_per_ngram'):
                if arch+'.'+key in g.kv: result[key]=g.kv[arch+'.'+key]
        for name,tid,dim,off in g.tensors:
            if name=='per_layer_token_embd.weight':
                result['engram_table']={'shape':dim,'type':types(tid).name,'shard':path.name}
            m=re.fullmatch(r'blk\.(\d+)\.ffn_(gate_up|gate|up|down)_exps\.weight',name)
            if not m:continue
            if len(dim)!=3:raise ValueError(f'unexpected expert geometry {name}: {dim}')
            tp=types(tid); group,sz=blocks[tp]
            if dim[0]%group:raise ValueError('non-integral row blocks')
            single=math.prod(dim[:2])//group*sz
            layer=result['layers'].setdefault(m[1],{})
            if m[2] in layer:raise ValueError('duplicate tensor '+name)
            layer[m[2]]={'type':tp.name,'shape':dim,'single_expert_bytes':single,'shard':path.name,
                         'file_offset':g.data_start+off}
    result['experts_selected_per_layer']=count
    if isinstance(count,int) and count>0:
        result['logical_routed_weight_bytes_per_token']=count*sum(x['single_expert_bytes'] for l in result['layers'].values() for x in l.values())
    return result

def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('--model',type=Path,required=True);p.add_argument('--source',type=Path,required=True)
    p.add_argument('--out',type=Path,required=True)
    a=p.parse_args(); m=re.search(r'-(\d{5})-of-(\d{5})\.gguf$',a.model.name)
    if m and m[1]!='00001':p.error('Specify the first shard')
    paths=([a.model.with_name(a.model.name[:m.start()]+f'-{i:05}-of-{m[2]}.gguf') for i in range(1,int(m[2])+1)] if m else [a.model])
    result=inventory(paths,a.source)
    with a.out.open('x') as f:json.dump(result,f,indent=2);f.write('\n')

if __name__=='__main__':main()

===== SOURCE FILE tools/r20_history_ab.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Three-way mirrored CPU-only comparison of the unchanged R19 and R20 stacks."""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import statistics
import subprocess
import zipfile

KIT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
NAMES = ("r19_all_off", "r19_cpu_stack", "r20_cpu_stack")
ALL_NAMES = ("upstream", *NAMES, "r19_unstaged_prefetch")
STACK = ("HASH_SCRATCH", "PREFETCH_UNIQUE", "ENGRAM_STAGE", "ENGRAM_MODEL_CACHE", "ENGRAM_ASYNC")


def sha(file):
    return hashlib.sha256(file.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def main():
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    for arg in ("fixture", "upstream-bin", "historical-bin", "candidate-bin", "out"):
        p.add_argument("--"+arg, type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument("--core", type=int, required=True)
    args = p.parse_args()
    if args.core not in os.sched_getaffinity(0):
        p.error("CPU not in this process's permitted affinity")
    args.out = args.out.resolve()
    try:
        args.out.mkdir(mode=0o700)
    except FileExistsError:
        p.error("--out must be new")
    bins = [x.resolve() for x in (args.upstream_bin, args.historical_bin, args.historical_bin, args.candidate_bin, args.historical_bin)]
    common = None
    libraries = {}
    for directory in dict.fromkeys(bins):
        cache = {}
        for line in (directory.parent/"CMakeCache.txt").read_text().splitlines():
            if re.match(r"(CMAKE_(BUILD_TYPE|C_COMPILER|CXX_COMPILER|CXX_FLAGS_RELEASE|C_FLAGS_RELEASE)|GGML_(NATIVE|CUDA|AVX.*|FMA|F16C|BMI2)):", line):
                k,v = line.split("=",1);cache[k]=v
        if common is not None and cache != common:
            raise ValueError("unequal native build options")
        common = cache
        libraries[directory.parent.name] = {x:sha(directory/x) for x in ("libllama.so","libggml-cpu.so","libggml.so","libggml-base.so")}
