    driver = (args.fixture/"decode").resolve()
    model = (args.fixture/"synthetic.gguf").resolve()
    off = next(x["environment"] for x in json.loads((KIT/"profiles/experiments.json").read_text())["profiles"] if x["name"]=="candidate_all_off")
    flags = {**off, **{"LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_"+k:"1" for k in STACK}}
    env_modes = [off, off, flags, {**flags, "LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_CPU_IQ3_SIGNMASK":"1"},
                 {**off,"LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_HASH_SCRATCH":"1","LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_PREFETCH_UNIQUE":"1"}]
    report = dict(scope="synthetic CPU-only untrained 6-layer DS4.1, 512 context, 3 requests/run, n_gpu_layers=0; no target inference",
                  status="RUNNING", core=args.core, model_sha256=sha(model), model_bytes=model.stat().st_size,
                  driver_sha256=sha(driver), cmake=common, libraries=libraries, modes=dict(zip(ALL_NAMES,env_modes)),
                  warmups="one full unmeasured run of each role; fresh processes per run; OS cache warm",
                  tests=[], summary={})
    ref = None
    base_env = {k:v for k,v in os.environ.items() if not k.startswith(("GGML_","LLAMA_"))}
    def run(arm, block):
        nonlocal ref
        name = ALL_NAMES[arm]
        key = ("warm" if block is None else "b"+str(block))+"_"+name
        out,err,logits = (args.out/(key+"."+suf) for suf in ("stdout","stderr","f32"))
        command = ["taskset", "-c", str(args.core), str(driver), str(model), str(logits), "256"]
        with out.open("xb") as a, err.open("xb") as b:
            proc = subprocess.run(command, env=dict(base_env, LD_LIBRARY_PATH=str(bins[arm]), **env_modes[arm]),
                                  stdout=a, stderr=b, timeout=240)
        stderr=err.read_text()
        ids=[[int(x) for x in line.split()] for line in out.read_text().splitlines()]
        row=dict(role=name,block=block,exit_code=proc.returncode,stdout=out.name,stderr=err.name)
        report["tests"].append(row)
        if proc.returncode or len(ids)!=3 or any(len(v)!=257 for v in ids) or ids[0]!=ids[2] or logits.stat().st_size!=(95+3*256)*128*4:
            raise ValueError(f"{key} invalid output or process failure")
        row.update(timings=json.loads(stderr.split("R20_SYNTHETIC_TIMINGS ")[1].splitlines()[0]),
                   ids_sha256=sha(out),logits_sha256=sha(logits),logit_f32_count=logits.stat().st_size//4,
                   stage_graph_seen="engram_stage-" in stderr, iq3_entered="experimental_cpu_iq3_signmask: entered" in stderr)
        if row["stage_graph_seen"] != (arm in (2,3)) or row["iq3_entered"] != (arm==3):
            raise ValueError(f"{key} unexpected runtime branch: {row}")
        parity=(row["ids_sha256"],row["logits_sha256"])
        if ref is None: ref=parity
        if parity!=ref:
            raise ValueError(f"{key} bit mismatch against pinned upstream")
    try:
        for arm in range(len(ALL_NAMES)):
            run(arm,None)
        for block,order in enumerate(("123","321","231","132","312","213")):
            for arm in order:
                run(int(arm),block)
        for name in NAMES:
            rs=[r["timings"] for r in report["tests"] if r["role"]==name and r["block"] is not None]
            metrics=report["summary"][name]={}
            for mode in ("pp","tg"):
                samples=sorted(1e9*r[mode+"_tokens"]/r[mode+"_ns"] for r in rs)
                metrics[mode]=dict(n=len(samples),median_tok_s=statistics.median(samples),p95_tok_s=samples[math.ceil(.95*len(samples))-1],
                                   sample_variance=statistics.variance(samples))
        report["status"]="PASS"
    finally:
        with (args.out/"result.json").open("w") as f: json.dump(report,f,indent=2)
        with zipfile.ZipFile(args.out/"stdout-stderr.zip","w",zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as logs:
            for path in sorted(args.out.iterdir()):
                if path.suffix in (".stdout",".stderr"): logs.write(path,path.name)
    print(json.dumps(report["summary"]))


if __name__=="__main__":
    main()

===== SOURCE FILE tools/r20_iq3_ab.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Test the built GGML public CPU kernel with opt-in signmask off/on. No model dependency."""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import platform
import statistics
import subprocess
import tempfile

def main():
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('--source', required=True, type=Path, help='pinned llama.cpp tree for GGML headers')
    p.add_argument('--bin', required=True, type=Path, help='candidate build/bin with libggml-cpu.so')
    p.add_argument('--out', required=True, type=Path)
    group = p.add_mutually_exclusive_group()
    group.add_argument('--expert-slice', type=Path, help='isolated bytes of the blk.15 gate expert 0')
    group.add_argument('--target-shard', type=Path, help='shard 3 of the referenced seven-file set')
    a = p.parse_args()
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    lib = a.bin.resolve()
    if a.out.exists(): p.error('Output already exists')
    report = {'scope':'single CPU core, GGML registered IQ3_XXS x Q8_K dot, deterministic packed input; ns/row',
              'cpu':next((s.split(':',1)[1].strip() for s in Path('/proc/cpuinfo').read_text().splitlines() if s.startswith('model name')),platform.machine()),
              'library_sha256':hashlib.sha256((lib/'libggml-cpu.so').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),'cases':[]}
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        prog = Path(tmp)/'dot'
        subprocess.run(['c++','-O2','-std=c++17','-I'+str(a.source.resolve()/'ggml/include'),
                        str(root/'tests_cpp/r20_iq3_dot.cpp'),'-L'+str(lib),'-lggml-cpu','-lggml-base',
                        '-Wl,-rpath,'+str(lib),'-o',str(prog)],check=True)
        if hasattr(os,'sched_getaffinity'):
            cpu=min(os.sched_getaffinity(0)); os.sched_setaffinity(0,{cpu}); report['logical_cpu']=cpu
        extra = []
        cases = [(256,8192,16),(4096,4096,12),(4096,49152,2)]
        if a.expert_slice or a.target_shard:
            ref=json.loads((root/'reports/EXPERT_SLICE.json').read_text())
            with (a.expert_slice or a.target_shard).open('rb') as f:
                if a.target_shard: f.seek(ref['offset'])
                data=f.read(ref['bytes'])
            if hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()!=ref['sha256']: p.error('Target expert fingerprint mismatch')
            source_weights=Path(tmp)/'target.weights'
            source_weights.write_bytes(data);extra=[str(source_weights)]
            cases=[(5120,2304,24)]
            report.update(weight_source=ref, activations='deterministic synthetic Q8_K')
        for width, rows, passes in cases:
            result={'width':width,'rows':rows,'passes':passes,'packed_weights_MiB':rows*width/256*98/2**20,'runs':[]}
            report['cases'].append(result)
            for i in range(16):
                on = (0,1,1,0,1,0,0,1)[i%8]
                out=Path(tmp)/'outputs.f32'
                proc=subprocess.run([str(prog),str(width),str(rows),str(passes),str(out),*extra],capture_output=True,
                                    text=True,check=True,env={**os.environ,'LD_LIBRARY_PATH':str(lib),
                                    'LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_CPU_IQ3_SIGNMASK':str(on)})
                if on and 'entered x86 IQ3_XXS' not in proc.stderr: p.exit(3,'CPU backend lacks the required ISA, no comparison\n')
                digest=hashlib.sha256(out.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
                if digest != result.setdefault('all_float_outputs_sha256',digest): raise RuntimeError('Bitwise parity failed')
                result['runs'].append({'on':on,'ns_per_row':float(proc.stdout),'stderr':proc.stderr.strip()})
            def summary(flag):
                v=[r['ns_per_row'] for r in result['runs'] if r['on']==flag]
                return {'n':len(v),'median':statistics.median(v),'p95_nearest_rank':sorted(v)[math.ceil(.95*len(v))-1],
                        'sample_variance':statistics.variance(v)}
            result['off']=summary(0);result['on']=summary(1)
            result['latency_reduction_pct']=100*(1-result['on']['median']/result['off']['median'])
    report['status']='BITWISE_EQUAL_REGISTERED_KERNEL'
    with a.out.open('x') as f:json.dump(report,f,indent=2);f.write('\n')

if __name__=='__main__': main()

===== SOURCE FILE tools/r20_maya_ab.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Cross-engine, same-shard benchmark of an isolated Project Maya and R20; Linux only."""
import argparse
from collections import defaultdict
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import struct
import subprocess
import sys
import time

import p17_smoke as smoke
import r20_bench as bench
from r20_gguf_inventory import Gguf

MAYA_PIN = "3589d6f12a3f77e813b184b51a586f8fc3de2185"
MAYA_ENV = {"DS4_YOCO": "0", "STRATA_DS4_DISK_SKIP_W": "0", "STRATA_DS4_RAM_SKIP_W": "0",
            "DS4_BENCH_REPEAT": "2", "STRATA_DS4_LOAD_DROP_CACHE": "0", "DS4_COPY_DROP_CACHE": "0"}


def checked(cmd, path, timeout, env=None, data=None):
    with path.open("xb") as f:
        proc = subprocess.run(cmd, input=data, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL if data is None else None,
                              stdout=f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, env=env, timeout=timeout)
    if proc.returncode:
        raise ValueError(f"Command exited {proc.returncode}; see {path}")
    return path.read_text(errors="replace")


def validate_pack(pack, alias, shards, drafter):
    geo = (pack / "ds4.txt").read_text()
    if "format gguf" not in geo.splitlines() or f"model_dir {alias}" not in geo.splitlines():
        raise ValueError("Packer changed model directory or format")
    allowed = set(shards)
    external = drafter.with_suffix(".bin").resolve() if drafter else None
    files = {}
    with (pack / "tensors.txt").open() as f:
        for line in f:
            cols = line.split()
            if cols[0] == "file":
                fi = int(cols[1])
                path = (alias / " ".join(cols[2:])).resolve()
                if fi in files or (path not in allowed and path != external):
                    raise ValueError(f"Duplicate or unexpected source in private pack: {path}")
                files[fi] = path
            elif cols[0] == "t":
                path = files[int(cols[3])]
                if path == external and not cols[1].startswith("mtp."):
                    raise ValueError("Trunk tensor would be sourced from the drafter")
    expected = allowed | ({external} if external else set())
    if set(files.values()) != expected:
        raise ValueError("Private pack did not reference exactly the requested GGUF shards and drafter")
    return {"file_table": {i: str(v) for i, v in files.items()}, "geometry_sha256": bench.file_hash(pack/"ds4.txt"),
            "tensor_index_sha256": bench.file_hash(pack/"tensors.txt")}


def prepare_pack(args, inventory):
    # Auto-discovery in the donor packer can silently substitute neighboring Engram files.
    alias = args.out / "model-view"
    alias.mkdir()
    shards = [Path(name) for name in inventory]
    for path in shards:
        (alias/path.name).symlink_to(path)
    pack = args.out / "maya-pack"
    tool = args.maya_repo / "tools/ds4/pack_gguf.py"
    cmd = [sys.executable, "-I", "-B", str(tool), "--model", str(alias), "--out", str(pack),
           "--shards", *[p.name for p in shards]]
    if args.drafter:
        cmd.extend(["--drafter", str(args.drafter)])
    output = checked(cmd, args.out/"pack.log", args.timeout)
    if "Engram tables from" in output or re.search(r"Engram .*over the quant", output):
        raise ValueError("External Engram table/metadata rejected")
    return pack, {"command": cmd, **validate_pack(pack, alias, shards, args.drafter)}


def maya_flags(args, pack, spec):
    return [str(args.maya_bin), "--pack", str(pack), "--gpus", "0", "--tp", "1",
            "--max-context", str(args.ctx), "--cpu-threads", str(args.threads), "--parallel", "1",
            "--reserve-mib", str(args.reserve_mib), "--mmap-experts", "auto", "--threads", "on",
            "--ssd-cache", args.maya_ssd_cache, "--expert-profile", "none", "--profile-readonly",
            "--suffix-draft", "0", "--dspark", "on" if spec else "off", "--vision", "off",
            "--temperature", "0", "--seed", "42", "--prefill-chunk", "64", "--window", "64", "--verbose"]


def parse_maya(stdout, stderr, n_input, predict, spec):
    lines = [line.strip() for line in stdout.splitlines() if line.strip()]
    if len(lines) != 2 or any(not re.fullmatch(r"(?:\d+,)+", line) for line in lines):
        raise ValueError("Expected exactly two complete numeric ID sequences on Maya stdout")
    logs = stderr.split("pass 2 of 2\n")
    if len(logs) != 2:
        raise ValueError("Maya did not mark a second pass after reset")
    passes = []
    for text, log in zip(lines, logs):
        ids = [int(x) for x in text.rstrip(",").split(",")]
        pp = re.search(r"^prompt: (\d+) tokens in ([\d.]+) ms \(([\d.]+) tok/s,", log, re.M)
        tg = re.search(r"^decode: (\d+) tokens in ([\d.]+) ms \(([\d.]+) tok/s\); CPU lane: (\d+) of (\d+) experts, streamed to the GPU: (\d+)", log, re.M)
        if (pp is None or tg is None or int(pp[1]) != n_input or int(tg[1]) != len(ids)
                or not 1 <= len(ids) <= predict or (len(ids) < predict and ids[-1] != 1)
                or float(pp[3]) <= 0 or (len(ids) > 1 and float(tg[3]) <= 0)):
            raise ValueError("Maya input/output count or timing protocol mismatch")
        drafts = re.search(r"^dspark: (\d+) rounds, (\d+) drafts offered, (\d+) accepted", log, re.M)
        offered, accepted = (int(drafts[2]), int(drafts[3])) if drafts else (0, 0)
        if accepted > offered or (not spec and offered > 0):
            raise ValueError("Unexpected DSpark counters")
        passes.append({"tokens": ids, "native_eog": ids[-1] == 1, "timings": {
            "prompt_n": int(pp[1]), "predicted_n": int(tg[1]), "prompt_ms": float(pp[2]),
            "predicted_ms": float(tg[2]), "prompt_per_second": float(pp[3]), "predicted_per_second": float(tg[3])},
            "cpu_experts": int(tg[4]), "total_experts": int(tg[5]), "streamed_experts": int(tg[6]),
            "drafts_offered": offered, "drafts_accepted": accepted})
    return passes


def maya_run(args, pack, label, ids, env, spec):
    cmd = maya_flags(args, pack, spec) + ["--ids", "@" + str(args.out/"prompt.ids"), "--n", str(args.predict)]
    out, err = args.out/(label+".stdout"), args.out/(label+".stderr")
    before = smoke.gpu_snapshot()
    start = time.monotonic()
    with out.open("xb") as stdout, err.open("xb") as stderr:
        proc = subprocess.run(cmd, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=stdout, stderr=stderr, env=env, timeout=args.timeout)
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
    if proc.returncode:
        raise ValueError(f"Maya exited {proc.returncode}; see {err}")
    log = err.read_text(errors="replace")
    passes = parse_maya(out.read_text(), log, len(ids), args.predict, spec)
    if "re-quantized" in log or "decoding with prompt lookup only" in log:
        raise ValueError(f"Maya reports altered weights or missing requested DSpark. See {err}")
    return {"command": cmd, "gpu_before": before, "process_total_s": elapsed, "warmup": passes[0], **passes[1],
            "placement": [line for line in log.splitlines() if line.startswith("ds4: CUDA") or
                          ("routed experts on the CPU lane" in line and line.startswith("ds4:"))]}


def trained_draft_identity(path):
    # Same DFLASH + Markov-head discriminator as the pinned common/speculative.cpp.
    try:
        g = Gguf(path)
    except (SystemExit, struct.error) as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"Invalid trained DSpark GGUF: {path}") from exc
    if (g.kv.get("general.architecture") != "dflash" or g.kv.get("split.count", 1) != 1
            or not any(name == "markov_w1.weight" for name, *_ in g.tensors)):
        raise ValueError("Expected a single trained DSpark GGUF (dflash architecture with markov_w1.weight)")
    return {"path":str(path), "sha256":bench.file_hash(path), "format":"DFLASH GGUF / DSpark Markov head",
            "model_name":g.kv.get("general.name"), "block_size":g.kv.get("dflash.block_size")}


def draft_counts(timings, requested):
    offered = timings.get("draft_n", 0)
    accepted = timings.get("draft_n_accepted", 0)
    if (type(offered) is not int or type(accepted) is not int or
            not 0 <= accepted <= offered or (not requested and offered > 0)):
        raise ValueError("Invalid llama draft counters or active draft on a plain arm")
    return {"drafts_offered":offered, "drafts_accepted":accepted}


def llama_run(args, binary, label, ids, env, draft=None):
    def native(port):
        req = bench.payload(ids, args.predict)
        req["ignore_eos"] = False
        r = smoke.request(port, "completion", req, args.timeout)
        tokens = r["tokens"]
        n = len(tokens)
        if not 1 <= n <= args.predict or any(type(t) is not int or t < 0 for t in tokens):
            raise ValueError("Invalid native token sequence")
        if n < args.predict and r["stop_type"] != "eos":
            raise ValueError("Unexpected short native generation")
        # At native EOG on token 1, TG has zero decode steps.
        if n > 1:
            bench.validate_completion(r, len(ids), n)
        elif (r["truncated"] or r["tokens_evaluated"] != len(ids) or r["timings"]["prompt_n"] != len(ids)
              or r["timings"]["cache_n"] != 0 or r["tokens_predicted"] != 1 or r["timings"]["predicted_n"] != 1):
            raise ValueError("Invalid one-token completion")
        return {"tokens": tokens, "native_eog": r["stop_type"] == "eos", "timings": r["timings"],
                **draft_counts(r["timings"], draft is not None)}
    with smoke.server(binary, label, env, args, dspark_gguf=draft) as (port, launch):
        warm, result = native(port), native(port)
        return {"command": launch["command"], "warmup": warm, **result, "startup_s": launch["startup_to_health_s"],
                "offload_layers": launch["offload_layers"], "rss_peak_kib": bench.high_water_kib(launch["pid"])}


def first_difference(a, b):
    for i, (x, y) in enumerate(zip(a, b)):
        if x != y:
            return i
    return min(len(a), len(b)) if len(a) != len(b) else None


def main():
    profiles = {x["name"]: x["environment"] for x in json.loads((smoke.KIT/"profiles/experiments.json").read_text())["profiles"]}
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    for key in ("model", "builds", "out", "maya-repo", "maya-bin"):
        p.add_argument("--"+key, type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument("--r19-builds", type=Path)
    p.add_argument("--maya-ssd-cache", choices=("off","auto"), default="off", help="Optional second run with Maya RAM expert store; adaptive VRAM cache stays enabled")
    p.add_argument("--llama-dspark-gguf", type=Path, help="Single compatible trained DFLASH Markov-head GGUF for llama-side DSpark arms")
    p.add_argument("--drafter", type=Path, help="Compatible trained dspark.json with matching .bin")
    p.add_argument("--drafter-bin-sha256", help="Expected hash from trusted pack provenance")
    p.add_argument("--profile", choices=["candidate_all_off", "r20_full", "planner_only", "cpu_iq3_signmask"],
                   default="r20_full")
    p.add_argument("--prompt-file", type=Path)
    p.add_argument("--fill-tokens", type=int, default=0)
    p.add_argument("--order-seed", type=int, default=20261103)
    p.add_argument("--ctx", type=int, default=131072)
    p.add_argument("--threads", type=int, default=8)
    p.add_argument("--predict", type=int, default=256)
    p.add_argument("--repeats", type=int, default=6)
    p.add_argument("--reserve-mib", type=int, default=3072)
    p.add_argument("--timeout", type=int, default=7200)
    args = p.parse_args()
    args.ngl, args.moe_cache_mib, args.mtp = 18, 12288, False
    if args.repeats < 5 or args.ctx < 131072 or not 256 <= args.predict <= 512 or args.reserve_mib < 0:
        p.error("Need >=5 repeats, >=131072 context and 256–512 predicted tokens")
    if bool(args.drafter) != bool(args.drafter_bin_sha256):
        p.error("--drafter and --drafter-bin-sha256 must be set together")
    bins = smoke.validate_inputs(p, args)
    args.out, args.maya_bin, args.maya_repo = (x.expanduser().resolve() for x in (args.out, args.maya_bin, args.maya_repo))
    smoke.new_output(p, args.out)
    path = args.out/"MAYA_AB.json"
    report = {"status": "RUNNING", "settings": {k: str(v) if isinstance(v, Path) else v for k, v in vars(args).items()},
              "maya_env_explicit": MAYA_ENV, "runs": [], "gguf": str(args.model),
              "comparison": "same input/shards, engine-native KV, kernels and expert placement; native EOG"}
    # Maya explicitly uses its CUDA0; expose the same single device to both child families.
    visible = os.environ.get("CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES","0")
    if not visible or "," in visible or visible == "-1":
        p.error("Cross-engine protocol needs one CUDA device. Set CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES to its index or GPU UUID.")
    os.environ["CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES"] = visible
    report["cuda_visible_devices"] = visible
    env = {k:v for k,v in os.environ.items() if not k.startswith(("STRATA_", "DS4_", "MAYA_"))}
    env.update(MAYA_ENV)
    try:
        report["r20_build"] = bench.provenance(args)
        git = ["git", "-C", str(args.maya_repo)]
        head = subprocess.check_output([*git, "rev-parse", "HEAD"], text=True).strip()
        if head != MAYA_PIN or subprocess.run([*git, "diff", "--quiet", "HEAD", "--"]).returncode:
            raise ValueError("Maya checkout must be a clean tracked tree at pinned 3589d6f12a3f")
        version = checked([str(args.maya_bin), "--version"], args.out/"maya-version.log", 30, env).strip()
        if not re.fullmatch(r"maya-ds4 1\.1\.46 \(build (?:3589d6f12a3f|"+MAYA_PIN+r")\)", version):
            raise ValueError("Wrong Maya reported build: "+version)
        report.update(maya_pin=head, maya_version=version, maya_binary_sha256=bench.file_hash(args.maya_bin))
        inventory = bench.model_inventory(args.model)
        report["shards_dev_ino_bytes_mtime_ns"] = inventory
        if args.llama_dspark_gguf:
            args.llama_dspark_gguf = args.llama_dspark_gguf.expanduser().resolve(strict=True)
            if str(args.llama_dspark_gguf) in inventory:
                raise ValueError("Trunk and trained GGUF drafter must be separate files")
            report["llama_drafter"] = trained_draft_identity(args.llama_dspark_gguf)
        if args.drafter:
            args.drafter = args.drafter.expanduser().resolve()
            digest = bench.file_hash(args.drafter.with_suffix(".bin"))
            if digest != args.drafter_bin_sha256:
                raise ValueError("Drafter binary SHA256 mismatch")
            report["drafter"] = {"json": str(args.drafter), "json_sha256": bench.file_hash(args.drafter), "bin_sha256": digest}
        pack, report["private_pack"] = prepare_pack(args, inventory)
        checked([str(args.maya_bin), "--pack", str(pack), "--pack-info"], args.out/"pack-info.log", args.timeout, env)
        ready = checked(maya_flags(args, pack, False)+["--serve"], args.out/"maya-context.log",
                        args.timeout, env, b"QUIT\n")
        if not re.search(rf"^READY {args.ctx} stop$", ready, re.M):
            raise ValueError("Effective Maya context not confirmed in maya-context.log")
        report["maya_info"] = [line for line in ready.splitlines() if line.startswith("INFO ")]
        with smoke.server(bins["baseline"], "tokenizer", profiles["candidate_all_off"], args) as (port, _):
            ids = bench.prompt_ids(port, args)
        (args.out/"prompt.ids").write_text(",".join(map(str, ids)))
        report["prompt_sha256"] = bench.file_hash(args.out/"prompt.ids")
        arms = {"baseline": (bins["baseline"], profiles["candidate_all_off"]),
                "r20": (bins["candidate"], bench.bundle_flags(profiles, True) if args.profile=="r20_full" else profiles[args.profile])}
        if args.r19_builds:
            report["r19_build"] = bench.provenance(args, args.r19_builds, bench.HISTORICAL_R19_MANIFEST)
            arms["r19_full"] = (args.r19_builds/"candidate/bin/llama-server", bench.historical_flags(bench.bundle_flags(profiles)))
        if args.llama_dspark_gguf:
            arms["r20_dspark"] = arms["r20"]
            if args.r19_builds:
                arms["r19_full_dspark"] = arms["r19_full"]
        labels = list(arms) + ["maya_plain"] + (["maya_dspark"] if args.drafter else [])
        report["order"] = bench.crossover(labels, args.repeats, args.order_seed)
        for i, block in enumerate(report["order"]):
            for j, variant in enumerate(block):
                if bench.model_inventory(args.model) != inventory:
                    raise ValueError("Model changed during benchmark")
                label = f"b{i+1:02}-{j+1:02}-{variant}"
                print(label, flush=True)
                report["active"] = label
                bench.atomic_write(path, report)
                if variant in arms:
                    binary, flags = arms[variant]
                    row = llama_run(args, binary, label, ids, flags,
                                    args.llama_dspark_gguf if variant.endswith("_dspark") else None)
                else:
                    row = maya_run(args, pack, label, ids, env, variant == "maya_dspark")
                row.update(block=i, variant=variant, label=label,
                           warmup_first_difference=first_difference(row["warmup"]["tokens"], row["tokens"]))
                report["runs"].append(row)
                bench.atomic_write(path, report)
        controls = {r["block"]: r for r in report["runs"] if r["variant"]=="baseline"}
        by_arm = defaultdict(list)
        for r in report["runs"]:
            r["first_difference_vs_baseline"] = first_difference(r["tokens"], controls[r["block"]]["tokens"])
            by_arm[r["variant"]].append(r)
        incomplete = False
        for variant, rows in by_arm.items():
            for r in rows:
                r["first_difference_vs_first_in_arm"] = first_difference(rows[0]["tokens"], r["tokens"])
            usable = [r for r in rows if len(r["tokens"]) == args.predict and
                      (not variant.endswith("_dspark") or r["drafts_offered"] > 0)]
            report.setdefault("summary", {})[variant] = {"valid_length_and_draft_n": len(usable),
                "native_eog_count": sum(r["native_eog"] for r in rows),
                "first_difference_by_block": [r["first_difference_vs_baseline"] for r in rows],
                "descriptive_full_length_only": {k: bench.summarize([r["timings"][k] for r in usable])
                   for k in ("prompt_per_second","predicted_per_second")} if usable else None}
            incomplete |= len(usable) != len(rows)
        same = all(r["first_difference_vs_baseline"] is None and r["warmup_first_difference"] is None and
                   r["first_difference_vs_first_in_arm"] is None for r in report["runs"])
        report["all_ids_equal"] = same
        pairs = [("baseline", "r20"), ("r20","maya_plain")]
        if args.llama_dspark_gguf:
            pairs.append(("r20","r20_dspark"))
            if args.r19_builds:
                pairs.extend([("r19_full","r19_full_dspark"), ("r19_full_dspark","r20_dspark")])
            if args.drafter:
                pairs.append(("r20_dspark","maya_dspark"))
        if args.drafter:
            pairs += [("maya_plain","maya_dspark"),("r20","maya_dspark")]
        if args.r19_builds:
            pairs.append(("r19_full","maya_plain"))
        valid_pairs = [pair for pair in pairs if all(report["summary"][v]["valid_length_and_draft_n"] == args.repeats
                                                       for v in pair)]
        report["paired_gains"] = bench.paired_gains(report["runs"], valid_pairs, args.repeats)
        report["pairs_skipped_for_short_output_or_zero_drafts"] = [pair for pair in pairs if pair not in valid_pairs]
        report["status"] = ("INCONCLUSIVE_LENGTH_OR_NO_DRAFT" if incomplete else
                            "COMPLETE_EQUAL_IDS" if same else "COMPLETE_DIFFERENT_IDS")
        del report["active"]
    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, IndexError, RuntimeError, subprocess.SubprocessError, KeyboardInterrupt) as exc:
        report.update(status="FAIL", error=str(exc) or type(exc).__name__)
    finally:
        bench.atomic_write(path, report)
    print(report["status"], report.get("error", ""), path)
    return int(report["status"] != "COMPLETE_EQUAL_IDS")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/r20_moe_bench.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Time the candidate's exact upload block on a deferred host memory backend."""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import platform
import statistics
import subprocess
import tempfile

from test_moe_scheduler_bridge import ROOT, extract


def build(candidate, directory):
    source = (candidate / "src/llama-moe-cache.cpp").read_text()
    bridge = (ROOT / "tests_cpp/moe_scheduler_bridge_fixture.cpp.in").read_text()
    code = "#include <chrono>\n" + bridge[:bridge.index("static void run_case(")]
    for marker, start, stop in (
        ("@@P04_HELPER@@", "struct moe_cache_upload_stats {", "// gate, up, down or gate_up, down"),
        ("@@P09B_COPY_BLOCK@@", "        std::optional<llama_exp::moe_fill_plan> shared_plan;", "        stats & st = ids.size()"),
        ("@@RECOVERY@@", "        if (!g.upload_complete) {", "        size_t n_hit = 0;"),
        ("@@COMMIT@@", "        g.upload_complete = true;", "        return true;"),
        ("@@P14B_RECORD_BLOCK@@", "        // P14B: event is queued AFTER the final slot-map write", "        if (trace.enabled) {"),
    ):
        code = code.replace(marker, extract(source, start, stop))
    code += (ROOT / "tests_cpp/r20_moe_bench_main.cpp.in").read_text()
    cpp = directory / "bench.cpp"
    exe = directory / "moe-bench"
    cpp.write_text(code)
    subprocess.run(["c++", "-std=c++17", "-O2", "-pthread", "-Wall", "-Wextra", "-Werror",
                    "-I" + str(candidate / "src"), str(cpp), "-o", str(exe)], check=True)
    return exe, hashlib.sha256(source.encode()).hexdigest(), hashlib.sha256(code.encode()).hexdigest()


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--candidate", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--updates", type=int, default=1024)
    parser.add_argument("--r19-control", action="store_true",
                        help="Initial diagnosis on unchanged R19: compare pipeline vs serial P04")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.output.exists():
        parser.error("output already exists")
    if args.updates < 100:
        parser.error("at least 100 updates required")
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        exe, source_hash, code_hash = build(args.candidate.resolve(), Path(tmp))
        report = dict(scope="CPU mock backend, warm memory, actual extracted submit block; no model or CUDA",
                      phase="R19 control" if args.r19_control else "R20 no-pack admission toggle",
                      candidate_source_sha256=source_hash, generated_source_sha256=code_hash,
                      cpu=next((s.partition(":")[2].strip() for s in Path("/proc/cpuinfo").read_text().splitlines()
                                if s.startswith("model name")), platform.machine()),
                      affinity=sorted(os.sched_getaffinity(0)), host=platform.platform(),
                      compiler=subprocess.check_output(["c++", "--version"], text=True).splitlines()[0],
                      warmup_updates=64, expert_width_bytes=4096, banks=3, runs=[], summary={})
        # Three crossed blocks. Different styles share workload shape/data and measured updates.
        for shape in ("one", "scattered", "direct", "packable"):
            for arm in "ABBABAABABBA":
                env = {k:v for k,v in os.environ.items() if not k.startswith("LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_")}
                env["LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_PIPELINE"] = str(int(not args.r19_control or arm == "A"))
                env["LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_BYPASS_NO_PACK"] = str(int(not args.r19_control and arm == "B"))
                record = json.loads(subprocess.check_output([str(exe), shape, str(args.updates)], text=True, env=env))
                expect_pipeline = not args.r19_control or arm == "A"
                expect_inline = not args.r19_control and arm == "B" and shape != "packable"
                if (record["pipeline_updates"] != (args.updates if expect_pipeline else 0) or
                        record["inline_updates"] != (args.updates if expect_inline else 0)):
                    raise ValueError(f"Unexpected admission for {arm} {shape}")
                report["runs"].append(dict(arm=arm, **record))
            summary = report["summary"][shape] = {}
            for arm in "AB":
                values = sorted(r["us_per_update"] for r in report["runs"] if r["shape"] == shape and r["arm"] == arm)
                summary[arm] = dict(n=len(values), median_us=statistics.median(values),
                                    sample_variance_us2=statistics.variance(values), p95_us=values[math.ceil(.95*len(values))-1])
            summary["B_minus_A_us"] = summary["B"]["median_us"] - summary["A"]["median_us"]
        with args.output.open("x") as out:
            json.dump(report,out,indent=2)
            out.write("\n")
        print(json.dumps(report["summary"], indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== SOURCE FILE tools/r20_profile.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Single diagnostic server. Target CPU/fault/IO evidence is sampled separately from primary A/B."""
import argparse
from contextlib import ExitStack
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import platform
import re
import shutil
import signal
import subprocess
import time

import p17_smoke as smoke
import r20_bench as bench

def read_kv(path):
    return {k: int(v.split()[0]) for line in path.read_text().splitlines()
            if ':' in line for k,v in [line.split(':',1)] if v.split() and v.split()[0].isdigit()}

def snap(pid, expected_alias=None):
    root = Path('/proc')/str(pid)
    result = {k: None for k in ('userspace_ticks','kernel_ticks','minor_faults','major_faults',
                                 'read_bytes','write_bytes','rss_kib','peak_rss_kib')}
    errors = {}
    result.update(monotonic_s=time.monotonic(), unavailable=errors)
    if expected_alias is not None:
        try:
            argv = (root/'cmdline').read_bytes().split(b'\0')
            if not any(a == b'--alias' and b == expected_alias.encode() for a, b in zip(argv, argv[1:])):
                raise ValueError('PID does not identify the isolated server/alias in this procfs namespace')
        except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
            errors['pid_identity'] = str(exc)
            return result
    try:
        # comm may contain spaces or parentheses. After its final ')' comes field #3.
        stat = (root/'stat').read_text().rsplit(')',1)[1].split()
        result.update(userspace_ticks=int(stat[11]), kernel_ticks=int(stat[12]),
                      minor_faults=int(stat[7]), major_faults=int(stat[9]))
    except (OSError, IndexError, ValueError) as exc:
        errors['stat'] = str(exc)
    for name, fields in (('io', {'read_bytes':'read_bytes','write_bytes':'write_bytes'}),
                         ('status', {'rss_kib':'VmRSS','peak_rss_kib':'VmHWM'})):
        try:
            values = read_kv(root/name)
            result.update({k:values.get(field) for k,field in fields.items()})
        except OSError as exc:
            errors[name] = str(exc)
    return result

def change(before, after):
    keys=['monotonic_s','userspace_ticks','kernel_ticks','minor_faults','major_faults','read_bytes','write_bytes']
    d={k:after[k]-before[k] if None not in (after[k],before[k]) else None for k in keys}
    d['cpu_s_all_threads']=(None if None in (d['userspace_ticks'],d['kernel_ticks']) else
                            (d['userspace_ticks']+d['kernel_ticks'])/os.sysconf('SC_CLK_TCK'))
    return d

def traces(path):
    data=[]
    for line in path.read_text(errors='replace').splitlines():
        marker = next((m for m in ('R20_ENGRAM_JSON', 'P15C_MMQ_SELECT_JSON') if m in line), None)
        if marker:
            fields = line.split(marker, 1)[1].strip().lstrip(':').strip()
            try:
                data.append({'message':line, 'marker':marker, 'fields':json.loads(fields)})
            except ValueError:
                data.append({'message':line, 'marker':marker, 'parse_error':True})
            continue
        if 'experimental_' not in line:continue
        fields=dict(re.findall(r'([a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z_0-9]*)=([^\s,]+)',line))
        data.append({'message':line,'fields':fields})
    return data

def attach(stack, argv, logfile):
    if not shutil.which(argv[0]):return {'cmd':argv,'available':False}
    log=stack.enter_context(logfile.open('xb'))
    proc=subprocess.Popen(argv,stdout=log,stderr=log,stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,start_new_session=True)
    result={'cmd':argv, 'available':True}
    def stop():
        if proc.poll() is None: proc.send_signal(signal.SIGINT)
        try:proc.wait(10)
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            proc.kill();proc.wait()
        result['exit']=proc.returncode
    stack.callback(stop)
    return result

def main():
    profiles={p['name']:p['environment'] for p in json.loads((smoke.KIT/'profiles/experiments.json').read_text())['profiles']}
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('--model',type=Path,required=True);p.add_argument('--builds',type=Path,required=True)
    p.add_argument('--out',type=Path,required=True);p.add_argument('--profile',choices=list(profiles)+['r19-full','r20-full'],default='planner_only')
    p.add_argument('--ctx',type=int,default=131072);p.add_argument('--predict',type=int,default=256)
    p.add_argument('--ngl',type=int,default=18);p.add_argument('--moe-cache-mib',type=int,default=12288)
    p.add_argument('--threads',type=int,default=8);p.add_argument('--timeout',type=int,default=3600)
    p.add_argument('--perf',action='store_true',help='sample on-CPU stacks of only the new server after first token')
    p.add_argument('--devices',action='store_true',help='collect whole-host iostat and all-GPU dmon for this diagnostic')
    a=p.parse_args();a.mtp=False;a.fill_tokens=0;a.prompt_file=None
    bins=smoke.validate_inputs(p,a)
    smoke.new_output(p,a.out)
    result={'status':'RUNNING','scope':'one diagnostic request, no warmup; separate from throughput results',
            'host':platform.uname()._asdict(),'tools':{x:shutil.which(x) for x in ('perf','pidstat','iostat','nvidia-smi','nsys','ncu','numactl')},
            'cpuinfo':next((s.split(':',1)[1].strip() for s in Path('/proc/cpuinfo').read_text().splitlines() if s.startswith('model name')),None),
            'cpu_affinity':sorted(os.sched_getaffinity(0)),'meminfo_kib':read_kv(Path('/proc/meminfo'))}
    out=a.out/'profile.json'
    try:
        result['build']=bench.provenance(a)
        env=(bench.bundle_flags(profiles,include_r20=a.profile=='r20-full') if a.profile in ('r19-full','r20-full') else profiles[a.profile].copy())
        env.update(LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_TRACE='1',LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_ENGRAM_TRACE='1')
        if a.profile.endswith('-full'):env['LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_CUDA_MMQ_PROFILE']='1'
        with smoke.server(bins['candidate'],'diag',env,a) as (port,launch):
            off=launch.get('offload_layers')
            if a.ngl and (not off or off[0]!=min(a.ngl,off[1])): raise ValueError(f'Unexpected offload {off} for requested {a.ngl}')
            pid=launch['pid']; ids=bench.prompt_ids(port,a)
            cmd=launch['command']; alias=cmd[cmd.index('--alias')+1]
            with ExitStack() as stack:
                if a.devices:
                    result['iostat']=attach(stack,['iostat','-dxm','-y','1'],a.out/'iostat.log')
                    result['dmon']=attach(stack,['nvidia-smi','dmon','-s','putm','-d','1'],a.out/'gpu-dmon.log')
                result['before']=snap(pid,alias)
                def first():
                    result['first']=snap(pid,alias)
                    if a.perf and 'pid_identity' in result['first']['unavailable']:
                        result['perf']={'attached':False,'reason':'Could not verify procfs PID/alias'}
                    elif a.perf: result['perf']=attach(stack,['perf','record','-F','97','-g','-p',str(pid),'-o',str(a.out/'perf.data')],a.out/'perf.log')
                result['response']=bench.completion_stream(port,ids,a.predict,a.timeout,on_first=first)
                result['end']=snap(pid,alias)
                result['request_delta']=change(result['before'],result['end'])
                result['after_first_observed_delta']=change(result['first'],result['end'])
        if a.perf and (a.out/'perf.data').exists():
            with (a.out/'perf-report.txt').open('w') as f:
                cmd=['perf','report','--stdio','--no-children','--sort','dso,symbol','-i',str(a.out/'perf.data')]
                result['perf_report_exit']=subprocess.run(cmd,stdout=f,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT).returncode
        result['status']='DIAGNOSTIC_COMPLETE'
    except (OSError,ValueError,KeyError,RuntimeError,KeyboardInterrupt) as e:
        result.update(status='FAIL',error=str(e) or type(e).__name__)
    finally:
        if (a.out/'diag.log').exists():result['trace_messages']=traces(a.out/'diag.log')
        bench.atomic_write(out,result)
    print(result['status'],result.get('error',''),out,flush=True)
    return int(result['status']!='DIAGNOSTIC_COMPLETE')

if __name__=='__main__':raise SystemExit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/r20_strata_read_bench.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Linux cold/warm A/B of exact pinned llama_prefetch on private GGUF-shaped expert ranges."""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import platform
import statistics
import subprocess
import tempfile

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--source", required=True, type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--out", required=True, type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--tmp-parent", type=Path, default=Path.cwd(), help="Filesystem under test; creates one private 192-MiB file")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.out.exists():
        parser.error("output already exists")
    source = (args.source / "src/llama-mmap.cpp").read_text()
    kernel = source[source.index("void llama_prefetch(llama_memory_ranges mr) {"):source.index("size_t llama_path_max()")]
    route_source = (args.source / "src/llama-moe-cache.cpp").read_text()
    route = route_source[route_source.index("        if (l.readahead && !batched && !fills.empty()) {"):
                         route_source.index("        // The EXISTING readback synchronize above")]
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(dir=args.tmp_parent) as tmp:
        tmp = Path(tmp).resolve()
        cpp, exe = tmp/"bench.cpp", tmp/"probe"
        cpp.write_text((ROOT/"tests_cpp/r20_strata_read.cpp.in").read_text().replace("@@PREFETCH@@", kernel).replace("@@ROUTE@@", route))
        subprocess.run(["c++", "-O2", "-std=c++17", "-Wall", "-Wextra", "-Werror", "-I"+str((args.source/"src").resolve()), str(cpp), "-o", str(exe)], check=True)
        file = tmp/"private.weights"
        block = os.urandom(1048576)
        with file.open("xb") as f:
            for i in range(192):
                f.write(i.to_bytes(4, "little")+block[4:])
            f.flush()
            os.fsync(f.fileno())
        report = dict(scope="local Linux mapped file, no inference or GPU", platform=platform.platform(),
                      source_sha256=hashlib.sha256(cpp.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
                      route_source_sha256=hashlib.sha256(route_source.encode()).hexdigest(), sample_file_bytes=file.stat().st_size,
                      fs=subprocess.check_output(["stat", "-f", "-c", "%T", str(tmp)],text=True).strip(),
                      expert_width_bytes=1048576, experts_per_bank=64, banks=3,
                      routed_experts=[2,11,22,37,45,59], schedule="ABBA BAAB ABBA BAAB", rejected_preconditions=[], runs=[], summary={})
        # Wide-separated source experts map to the same requested bytes in both arms.
        for policy in ("normal", "random"):
            for state in ("cold","warm"):
                key = policy+"_"+state
                output_hash = None
                for arm in "ABBABAABABBABAAB":
                    for attempt in range(12):
                        row = json.loads(subprocess.check_output([str(exe),str(file),arm,state,policy],text=True))
                        if row["resident_before"] == (row["pages"] if state == "warm" else 0):
                            break
                        report["rejected_preconditions"].append(dict(policy=policy, state=state, arm=arm, **row))
                    else:
                        raise RuntimeError(f"Could not set initial cache state: {policy} {state} {row}")
                    if output_hash is not None and row["checksum"] != output_hash:
                        raise ValueError("byte parity failure")
                    output_hash = row["checksum"]
                    if row["resident_before"] != (row["pages"] if state == "warm" else 0):
                        raise ValueError(f"cache precondition not established: {row}")
                    report["runs"].append(dict(policy=policy, state=state, arm=arm,**row))
                summary = report["summary"][key] = {}
                for arm in "AB":
                    sub = [r for r in report["runs"] if r["policy"]==policy and r["state"]==state and r["arm"]==arm]
                    t = sorted(r["total_ms"] for r in sub)
                    summary[arm] = dict(n=len(t),median_ms=statistics.median(t),p95_ms=t[math.ceil(.95*len(t))-1],
                                        variance_ms2=statistics.variance(t),median_major_faults=statistics.median(r["major"] for r in sub),
                                        median_read_bytes=statistics.median(r["read_bytes"] for r in sub))
        with args.out.open("x") as out:
            json.dump(report,out,indent=2)
            out.write("\n")
        print(json.dumps(report["summary"]))


if __name__=="__main__":
    main()

===== SOURCE FILE tools/r21_screen.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Portable replay of the local R21 screening protocol; not a quality acceptance test."""
import argparse
import json
import resource
import time
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace

import r21_server as server
import r20_bench as bench
from overlay import sha256_file


def main(argv=None):
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('--binary', type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument('--model', type=Path, required=True, help='First GGUF shard')
    p.add_argument('--profile', type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument('--out', type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument('--prompt-file', type=Path, default=server.KIT/'benchmarks/prompts/moe-russian.txt')
    p.add_argument('--repeats', type=int, default=3)
    p.add_argument('--predict', type=int, default=256)
    p.add_argument('--timeout', type=int, default=7200)
    v = p.parse_args(argv)
    if not 1 <= v.repeats <= 100 or not 1 <= v.predict <= 4096 or v.timeout < 1:
        p.error('Invalid repeats, predict or timeout')
    cfg = json.loads(v.profile.read_text())
    if cfg['parallel'] != 1 or cfg['spec_type'] != 'none':
        p.error('This replay implements only single-slot, non-speculative decoding')
    a = SimpleNamespace(model=v.model.resolve(), out=v.out.resolve(),
        ctx=cfg['ctx'], predict=v.predict, ngl=cfg['ngl'], moe_cache_mib=cfg['cache_mib'],
        threads=cfg['threads'], batch=cfg['batch'], ubatch=cfg['ubatch'],
        cache_type_k=cfg['kv'], cache_type_v=cfg['kv'], cpu_mask=cfg.get('cpu_mask'),
        cpu_strict=True, timeout=v.timeout, mtp=False, draft_model=None, draft_max=4)
    binary = v.binary.resolve()
    if not binary.is_file() or not a.model.is_file():
        p.error('Binary or model missing')
    # Reuse the existing shard validation without its build-pair requirements.
    import re
    shard = re.search(r'-(\d{5})-of-(\d{5})\.gguf$', a.model.name)
    models = [a.model]
    if shard:
        if shard[1] != '00001' or int(shard[2]) < 1:
            p.error('Specify the first GGUF shard')
        models = [a.model.with_name(a.model.name[:shard.start()] + f'-{i:05d}-of-{int(shard[2]):05d}.gguf')
                  for i in range(1, int(shard[2])+1)]
        if any(not x.is_file() for x in models):
            p.error('Incomplete GGUF shard set')
    prompt = v.prompt_file.read_text()
    resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_CORE, (0, 0))
    server.new_output(p, a.out)
    report = {'status': 'RUNNING', 'scope': 'SCREENING_NOT_ACCEPTANCE', 'profile': cfg, 'runs': [],
              'binary_sha256': sha256_file(binary),
              'library_sha256': {x.name: sha256_file(x) for x in sorted(binary.parent.glob('*.so*')) if x.is_file()},
              'gguf_inventory': [{'name': x.name, 'bytes': x.stat().st_size, 'mtime_ns': x.stat().st_mtime_ns} for x in models],
              'inventory_is_not_a_weight_content_hash': True,
              'prompt_file_sha256': sha256_file(v.prompt_file)}
    code = 1
    try:
        with server.server(binary, 'screen', cfg['environment'], a) as (port, launch):
            report['launch'] = launch
            formatted = server.request(port, 'apply-template', {'messages': [{'role':'user', 'content':prompt}],
                'chat_template_kwargs': {'enable_thinking': False}}, a.timeout)['prompt']
            ids = server.request(port, 'tokenize', {'content':formatted, 'add_special':False, 'parse_special':True}, a.timeout)['tokens']
            if len(ids) + a.predict >= a.ctx:
                raise ValueError('Prompt and generation exceed the configured context')
            report['prompt_ids'] = ids
            report['warmup'] = bench.warmup(port, a)
            for index in range(v.repeats):
                body = bench.payload(ids, a.predict)
                body['ignore_eos'] = False
                start = time.perf_counter()
                response = server.request(port, 'completion', body, a.timeout)
                elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start
                tokens = response['tokens']
                if not tokens or len(tokens) > a.predict or any(type(x) is not int or x < 0 for x in tokens):
                    raise ValueError('Invalid generated token IDs')
                bench.validate_completion(response, len(ids), len(tokens))
                if len(tokens) < a.predict and response['stop_type'] != 'eos':
                    raise ValueError('Unexpected short completion')
                report['runs'].append({'tokens':tokens, 'timings':response['timings'],
                    'native_eog':response['stop_type']=='eos', 'request_s':elapsed})
                bench.atomic_write(a.out/'SPEED.json', report)
                print(index, response['timings']['predicted_per_second'], flush=True)
        report['status'] = 'SCREENING_COMPLETE'
        code = 0
    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, RuntimeError, KeyboardInterrupt) as exc:
        report.update(status='FAIL', error=str(exc) or type(exc).__name__)
    finally:
        bench.atomic_write(a.out/'SPEED.json', report)
    return code


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/r21_server.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Compare greedy token IDs on two pinned servers, sequentially, over loopback."""
import argparse
from contextlib import contextmanager
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import secrets
import socket
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request

KIT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
PROMPT = "Москва — столица России. Берлин — столица"
FILL_TEXT = " The brown fox crosses a field and follows a quiet river."
API = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))


def request(port, route, body=None, timeout=10):
    payload = json.dumps(body).encode() if body is not None else None
    req = urllib.request.Request(f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/{route}", payload,
                                 headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    with API.open(req, timeout=timeout) as response:
        return json.load(response)


def new_output(parser, path):
    try:
        path.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    except FileExistsError:
        parser.error(f"Output already exists: {path}. Previous logs were preserved. "
                     "Choose a fresh --out directory, e.g. out-$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)-$$.")


def gpu_snapshot():
    """Driver-reported availability for each physical device at sampling time."""
    exe = shutil.which("nvidia-smi")
    if not exe:
        return {"available": False, "reason": "nvidia-smi not on PATH"}
    try:
        result = {}
        for name, switch, fields in (
                ("gpu", "gpu", "index,uuid,pci.bus_id,name,memory.total,memory.free,memory.used,driver_version"),
                ("process", "compute-apps", "gpu_uuid,pid,used_memory")):
            cmd = [exe, f"--query-{switch}={fields}", "--format=csv,noheader,nounits"]
            proc = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
            result[name] = {"fields": fields.split(","), "rows": proc.stdout.strip().splitlines(),
                            "error": proc.stderr.strip(), "exit_code": proc.returncode}
        return {"available": result["gpu"]["exit_code"] == 0, "memory_unit": "MiB", **result}
    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as exc:
        return {"available": False, "reason": str(exc)}


def log_diagnostic(logfile):
    # Retain the whole log on disk, but bound console and report error text.
    with logfile.open("rb") as src:
        size = src.seek(0, 2)
        src.seek(max(0, size - 65536))
        tail = src.read().decode(errors="replace").splitlines()
    clues = [line for line in tail if re.search(r"out of memory|cudaMalloc|failed to allocate|free.*MiB", line, re.I)]
    kind = "CUDA OOM" if any(re.search("out of memory", x, re.I) for x in clues) else "server failure"
    return kind + " | " + " | ".join((clues or tail)[-5:])[-1800:]


@contextmanager
def server(binary, label, env_flags, args, dspark_gguf=None):
    env = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items()
           if not k.startswith(("LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_", "LLAMA_ARG_", "GGML_"))}
    env.update(env_flags, LD_LIBRARY_PATH=str(binary.parent) +
               (os.pathsep + env["LD_LIBRARY_PATH"] if env.get("LD_LIBRARY_PATH") else ""))
    alias = "p17-" + secrets.token_hex(12)
    with socket.socket() as sock:
        sock.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
        port = sock.getsockname()[1]
    cmd = [str(binary), "-m", str(args.model), "--host", "127.0.0.1", "--port", str(port),
           "--alias", alias, "-lv", "4", "-np", "1", "-c", str(args.ctx), "--fit", "off",
           "-ngl", str(args.ngl), "-b", str(getattr(args, "batch", 128)), "-ub", str(getattr(args, "ubatch", 64)), "-t", str(args.threads),
           "--cache-type-k", getattr(args, "cache_type_k", "f16"),
           "--cache-type-v", getattr(args, "cache_type_v", "f16"), "--no-warmup", "--no-ui",
           "--no-context-shift", "--offline"]
    mask = getattr(args, "cpu_mask", None)
    if mask is not None:
        if not re.fullmatch(r"(?:0x)?[0-9a-fA-F]+", mask) or int(mask, 16) <= 0:
            raise ValueError("CPU mask must be a nonzero hexadecimal mask")
        cmd.extend(["--cpu-mask", mask, "--cpu-strict", "1" if getattr(args, "cpu_strict", False) else "0"])
    if getattr(args, "flash_attn", None) is not None:
        if args.flash_attn not in ("on", "off", "auto"):
            raise ValueError("Unknown flash attention mode")
        cmd.extend(["--flash-attn", args.flash_attn])
    if dspark_gguf is not None:
        if args.mtp:
            raise ValueError("Choose one trained draft mechanism per comparison")
        cmd.extend(["--spec-type", "draft-dspark", "--spec-draft-model", str(dspark_gguf),
                    "--spec-draft-ngl", "all", "--spec-draft-n-max", str(args.draft_max), "--spec-draft-p-min", "0",
                    "--spec-draft-threads", str(args.threads)])
        if getattr(args, "draft_cpu_moe", False):
            cmd.append("--spec-draft-cpu-moe")
        if mask is not None:
            cmd.extend(["--spec-draft-cpu-mask", mask, "--spec-draft-cpu-strict", "1" if getattr(args, "cpu_strict", False) else "0"])
    elif args.mtp:
        cmd.extend(["--spec-type", "draft-mtp", "--spec-draft-n-max", str(args.draft_max)])
        if args.draft_model:
            cmd.extend(["--spec-draft-model", str(args.draft_model)])
    else:
        cmd.extend(["--spec-type", "none"])
    if args.moe_cache_mib:
        cmd.extend(["--cpu-moe", "--moe-cache-mib", str(args.moe_cache_mib)])
    logfile = args.out / (label + ".log")
    print(f"{label} — {logfile}", flush=True)
    record = {"command": cmd, "experiment_environment": env_flags, "gpu_before_launch": gpu_snapshot(),
              "cuda_visible_devices": env.get("CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES")}
    diagnostic_path = args.out / (label + ".launch.json")
    def save_record():
        diagnostic_path.write_text(json.dumps(record, indent=2) + "\n")
    save_record()
    with logfile.open("xb") as log:
        started = time.perf_counter()
        process = subprocess.Popen(cmd, env=env, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=log, stderr=log)
        try:
            record["pid"] = process.pid
            save_record()
            deadline = time.monotonic() + args.timeout
            while time.monotonic() < deadline:
                if process.poll() is not None:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"{label} exited {process.returncode}: {log_diagnostic(logfile)}. See {logfile} and {diagnostic_path}")
                try:
                    if request(port, "health")["status"] == "ok":
                        break
                except (OSError, urllib.error.URLError):
                    pass
                time.sleep(0.5)
            else:
                raise RuntimeError(f"{label} load timed out. See {logfile}")
            record["startup_to_health_s"] = time.perf_counter() - started
            props = request(port, "props")
            if (props.get("model_alias") != alias or props.get("total_slots") != 1 or
                    Path(props["model_path"]).resolve() != args.model or
                    props["default_generation_settings"]["n_ctx"] != args.ctx):
                raise ValueError(f"Unexpected server model, alias, slot count or effective context; "
                                 f"reported model {props.get('model_path')}, slots {props.get('total_slots')}, "
                                 f"n_ctx {props.get('default_generation_settings', {}).get('n_ctx')}, expected {args.ctx}")
            record["effective_slot_ctx"] = props["default_generation_settings"]["n_ctx"]
            record["model_path"] = props["model_path"]
            record["model_ftype"] = props.get("model_ftype")
            with logfile.open("rb") as handle:
                offload = next((m for line in handle if (m := re.search(
                    rb'offloaded (\d+)/(\d+) layers to GPU', line))), None)
            if args.ngl and (offload is None or int(offload[1]) != min(args.ngl, int(offload[2]))):
                raise ValueError(f"GPU layer offload was not confirmed in {logfile}")
            record["offload_layers"] = list(map(int, offload.groups())) if offload else None
            record["gpu_after_load"] = gpu_snapshot()
            save_record()
            yield port, record
        except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, RuntimeError) as exc:
            record["failure"] = str(exc)
            record["gpu_on_error"] = gpu_snapshot()
            clue = log_diagnostic(logfile)
            if "CUDA OOM" in clue and "CUDA OOM" not in str(exc):
                raise RuntimeError(f"{label}: {clue}. See {logfile} and {diagnostic_path}") from exc
            raise
        finally:
            if process.poll() is None:
                process.terminate()
            try:
                process.wait(timeout=60)
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                process.kill()
                process.wait()
            record["exit_code"] = process.returncode
            record["gpu_after_exit"] = gpu_snapshot()
            save_record()
        if process.returncode:
            raise RuntimeError(f"{label} failed on shutdown (exit {process.returncode}). See {logfile}")


def smoke(binary, label, env_flags, args):
    with server(binary, label, env_flags, args) as (port, launch):
        prompt = request(port, "tokenize", {"content": PROMPT, "add_special": True})["tokens"]
        if args.fill_tokens:
            filler = request(port, "tokenize", {"content": FILL_TEXT, "add_special": False})["tokens"]
            if not filler or args.fill_tokens < len(prompt):
                raise ValueError("Requested prompt length too small, or tokenizer returned no filler")
            n = args.fill_tokens - len(prompt)
            prompt += (filler * ((n + len(filler) - 1) // len(filler)))[:n]
        res = request(port, "completion", {"prompt": prompt, "temperature": 0, "seed": 42,
                      "n_predict": args.predict, "return_tokens": True, "ignore_eos": True,
                      "cache_prompt": False, "stream": False}, args.timeout)
        ids = res["tokens"]
        if (not isinstance(ids, list) or not all(type(t) is int and t >= 0 for t in ids)
                or len(ids) != args.predict or res["tokens_predicted"] != len(ids)
                or res["tokens_evaluated"] != len(prompt) or res["truncated"]):
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid or truncated token result from {label}")
        if args.mtp and res["timings"].get("draft_n", 0) <= 0:
            raise ValueError(f"No MTP draft tokens were verified for {label}")
        result = {"label": label, "command": launch["command"], "experiment_environment": env_flags,
                "context": args.ctx, "prompt_tokens": len(prompt),
                "prompt_ids_sha256": hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(prompt).encode()).hexdigest(),
                "tokens": ids, "content": res["content"], "timings": res["timings"]}
    return result


def validate_inputs(parser, args):
    args.model = args.model.expanduser().resolve()
    args.builds = args.builds.expanduser().resolve()
    if not args.model.is_file():
        parser.error("Model GGUF not found: " + str(args.model))
    shard = re.search(r"-(\d{5})-of-(\d{5})\.gguf$", args.model.name)
    if shard:
        count = int(shard[2])
        prefix = args.model.name[:shard.start()]
        if shard[1] != "00001" or count < 1:
            parser.error("Specify the first shard")
        for i in range(1, count + 1):
            path = args.model.with_name(f"{prefix}-{i:05}-of-{count:05}.gguf")
            if not path.is_file():
                parser.error("Missing shard: " + str(path))
    if (min(args.ctx, args.threads, args.predict, args.timeout) < 1 or
            min(args.ngl, args.fill_tokens, args.moe_cache_mib) < 0 or
            args.fill_tokens + args.predict >= args.ctx):
        parser.error("Invalid sizes or prompt/output exceed context capacity")
    bins = {r: args.builds / r / "bin/llama-server" for r in ("baseline", "candidate")}
    if not all(p.is_file() and os.access(p, os.X_OK) for p in bins.values()):
        parser.error("Missing executable in baseline/bin or candidate/bin under --builds")
    return bins


def main():
    profiles = {p["name"]: p["environment"] for p in
                json.loads((KIT / "profiles/experiments.json").read_text())["profiles"]}
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--builds", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--model", type=Path, required=True, help="GGUF or first shard")
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True, help="New report directory")
    parser.add_argument("--ctx", type=int, default=131072)
    parser.add_argument("--ngl", type=int, default=18)
    parser.add_argument("--threads", type=int, default=8)
    parser.add_argument("--moe-cache-mib", type=int, default=12288)
    parser.add_argument("--predict", type=int, default=64)
    parser.add_argument("--fill-tokens", type=int, default=0)
    parser.add_argument("--timeout", type=int, default=1800, help="Seconds per model load or completion")
    parser.add_argument("--mtp", action="store_true", help="Use single MTP on baseline and candidate")
    parser.add_argument("--draft-model", type=Path, help="Matching MTP GGUF for a separate drafter")
    parser.add_argument("--draft-max", type=int, default=3)
    allowed = sorted(set(profiles) - {"candidate_all_off"})
    parser.add_argument("--profile", choices=allowed, help="Also test this candidate profile after all-off")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if not args.mtp and (args.draft_model or (args.profile or "").startswith("p16")):
        parser.error("This draft model/profile requires --mtp")
    if args.draft_max < 1:
        parser.error("Baseline MTP comparison requires --draft-max >= 1")
    if args.draft_model:
        args.draft_model = args.draft_model.expanduser().resolve()
        if not args.draft_model.is_file():
            parser.error("Draft GGUF not found: " + str(args.draft_model))
    bins = validate_inputs(parser, args)
    new_output(parser, args.out)
    report = {"status": "RUNNING", "model": str(args.model), "runs": []}
    record = args.out / "smoke.json"
    schedule = [("baseline", "candidate_all_off"), ("candidate", "candidate_all_off")]
    if args.profile:
        schedule.append(("candidate", args.profile))
    try:
        for role, profile in schedule:
            run = smoke(bins[role], role if role == "baseline" else profile, profiles[profile], args)
            report["runs"].append(run)
            base = report["runs"][0]
            if run["prompt_ids_sha256"] != base["prompt_ids_sha256"]:
                raise ValueError(f"Tokenized prompts differ for {profile}. Compare {record}")
            if run["tokens"] != base["tokens"]:
                pos = next(i for i, (a, b) in enumerate(zip(base["tokens"], run["tokens"])) if a != b)
                raise ValueError(f"{profile}: output ID at position {pos} differs "
                                 f"({base['tokens'][pos]} vs {run['tokens'][pos]}). See {record}")
            timings = run["timings"]
            print(f"{run['label']}  prompt {run['prompt_tokens']} tokens / "
                  f"{timings['prompt_per_second']:.2f} t/s, generation {len(run['tokens'])} tokens / "
                  f"{timings['predicted_per_second']:.2f} t/s", flush=True)
            record.write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n")
        report["status"] = "PASS_TOKEN_SMOKE"
    except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError, KeyError, KeyboardInterrupt) as exc:
        report.update(status="FAIL", error=str(exc) or type(exc).__name__)
        print(report["error"], file=sys.stderr)
    finally:
        record.write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n")
    print(f"{report['status']}  {record}")
    return int(report["status"] != "PASS_TOKEN_SMOKE")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/screen.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Portable replay of the local R21 screening protocol; not a quality acceptance test."""
import argparse
import json
import resource
import time
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace

import r21_server as server
import r20_bench as bench
from overlay import sha256_file


def main(argv=None):
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('--binary', type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument('--model', type=Path, required=True, help='First GGUF shard')
    p.add_argument('--profile', type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument('--out', type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument('--prompt-file', type=Path, default=server.KIT/'benchmarks/prompts/moe-russian.txt')
    p.add_argument('--repeats', type=int, default=3)
    p.add_argument('--predict', type=int, default=256)
    p.add_argument('--timeout', type=int, default=7200)
    v = p.parse_args(argv)
    if not 1 <= v.repeats <= 100 or not 1 <= v.predict <= 4096 or v.timeout < 1:
        p.error('Invalid repeats, predict or timeout')
    cfg = json.loads(v.profile.read_text())
    if cfg['parallel'] != 1 or cfg['spec_type'] != 'none':
        p.error('This replay implements only single-slot, non-speculative decoding')
    a = SimpleNamespace(model=v.model.resolve(), out=v.out.resolve(),
        ctx=cfg['ctx'], predict=v.predict, ngl=cfg['ngl'], moe_cache_mib=cfg['cache_mib'],
        threads=cfg['threads'], batch=cfg['batch'], ubatch=cfg['ubatch'],
        cache_type_k=cfg['kv'], cache_type_v=cfg['kv'], cpu_mask=cfg.get('cpu_mask'),
        cpu_strict=True, timeout=v.timeout, mtp=False, draft_model=None, draft_max=4)
    binary = v.binary.resolve()
    if not binary.is_file() or not a.model.is_file():
        p.error('Binary or model missing')
    # Reuse the existing shard validation without its build-pair requirements.
    import re
    shard = re.search(r'-(\d{5})-of-(\d{5})\.gguf$', a.model.name)
    models = [a.model]
    if shard:
        if shard[1] != '00001' or int(shard[2]) < 1:
            p.error('Specify the first GGUF shard')
        models = [a.model.with_name(a.model.name[:shard.start()] + f'-{i:05d}-of-{int(shard[2]):05d}.gguf')
                  for i in range(1, int(shard[2])+1)]
        if any(not x.is_file() for x in models):
            p.error('Incomplete GGUF shard set')
    prompt = v.prompt_file.read_text()
    resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_CORE, (0, 0))
    server.new_output(p, a.out)
    report = {'status': 'RUNNING', 'scope': 'SCREENING_NOT_ACCEPTANCE', 'profile': cfg, 'runs': [],
              'binary_sha256': sha256_file(binary),
              'library_sha256': {x.name: sha256_file(x) for x in sorted(binary.parent.glob('*.so*')) if x.is_file()},
              'gguf_inventory': [{'name': x.name, 'bytes': x.stat().st_size, 'mtime_ns': x.stat().st_mtime_ns} for x in models],
              'inventory_is_not_a_weight_content_hash': True,
              'prompt_file_sha256': sha256_file(v.prompt_file)}
    code = 1
    try:
        with server.server(binary, 'screen', cfg['environment'], a) as (port, launch):
            report['launch'] = launch
            formatted = server.request(port, 'apply-template', {'messages': [{'role':'user', 'content':prompt}],
                'chat_template_kwargs': {'enable_thinking': False}}, a.timeout)['prompt']
            ids = server.request(port, 'tokenize', {'content':formatted, 'add_special':False, 'parse_special':True}, a.timeout)['tokens']
            if len(ids) + a.predict >= a.ctx:
                raise ValueError('Prompt and generation exceed the configured context')
            report['prompt_ids'] = ids
            report['warmup'] = bench.warmup(port, a)
            for index in range(v.repeats):
                body = bench.payload(ids, a.predict)
                body['ignore_eos'] = False
                start = time.perf_counter()
                response = server.request(port, 'completion', body, a.timeout)
                elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start
                tokens = response['tokens']
                if not tokens or len(tokens) > a.predict or any(type(x) is not int or x < 0 for x in tokens):
                    raise ValueError('Invalid generated token IDs')
                bench.validate_completion(response, len(ids), len(tokens))
                if len(tokens) < a.predict and response['stop_type'] != 'eos':
                    raise ValueError('Unexpected short completion')
                report['runs'].append({'tokens':tokens, 'timings':response['timings'],
                    'native_eog':response['stop_type']=='eos', 'request_s':elapsed})
                bench.atomic_write(a.out/'SPEED.json', report)
                print(index, response['timings']['predicted_per_second'], flush=True)
        report['status'] = 'SCREENING_COMPLETE'
        code = 0
    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, RuntimeError, KeyboardInterrupt) as exc:
        report.update(status='FAIL', error=str(exc) or type(exc).__name__)
    finally:
        bench.atomic_write(a.out/'SPEED.json', report)
    return code


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Launch an isolated llama.cpp server with an explicit measured MoE profile."""
import argparse,json,os,resource
from pathlib import Path
p=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
p.add_argument('--binary',type=Path,required=True);p.add_argument('--model',type=Path,required=True)
p.add_argument('--profile',type=Path,required=True);p.add_argument('--port',type=int,default=8081)
p.add_argument('--host',default='127.0.0.1');p.add_argument('--print',action='store_true',dest='print_only')
a=p.parse_args();c=json.loads(a.profile.read_text())
if not 1<=a.port<=65535 or not a.binary.is_file() or not a.model.is_file():p.error('Invalid port, binary or model')
if c['parallel']!=1 or c['spec_type']!='none' or c['kv']!='f16':p.error('Selected profiles require one slot, no speculative decoding and F16 KV')
cmd=[str(a.binary.resolve()),'-m',str(a.model.resolve()),'--host',a.host,'--port',str(a.port),
 '--alias',c['profile'],'-np','1','-c',str(c['ctx']),'--fit','off','-ngl',str(c['ngl']),
 '-b',str(c['batch']),'-ub',str(c['ubatch']),'-t',str(c['threads']),'--cpu-mask',c['cpu_mask'],
 '--cpu-strict','1','--cache-type-k','f16','--cache-type-v','f16','--no-warmup','--no-context-shift',
 '--offline','--spec-type','none','--cpu-moe','--moe-cache-mib',str(c['cache_mib'])]
env={k:v for k,v in os.environ.items() if not k.startswith(('LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_','LLAMA_ARG_','GGML_'))}
env.update(c['environment'])
if a.print_only:print(json.dumps({'command':cmd,'environment':c['environment']},indent=2))
else:
 resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_CORE,(0,0));os.execvpe(cmd[0],cmd,env)

===== SOURCE FILE tools/test_engram_cache.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Compile and exercise P07 host Engram row-cache component.

No GGUF/model/GPU/CUDA workload is executed. The module is standalone and NOT
part of a patched llama.cpp executable until the graph adapter is integrated.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import time


def digest(path: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def run(cmd: list[str], timeout: int = 120) -> dict:
    start = time.monotonic()
    proc = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout, check=False)
    return {"exit_code": proc.returncode, "stdout": proc.stdout,
            "stderr": proc.stderr, "elapsed_seconds": round(time.monotonic()-start, 3),
            "command": cmd}


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--source-root", type=Path, default=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1])
    parser.add_argument("--output", required=True, type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--compiler", default="c++")
    parser.add_argument("--sanitizers", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    root = args.source_root.resolve(strict=True)
    out = args.output.resolve()
    compiler = shutil.which(args.compiler)
    if out.exists() or args.output.is_symlink() or out.is_relative_to(root) or not out.parent.exists():
        parser.error("--output must be a new directory outside the source root")
    if not compiler:
        parser.error("C++ compiler unavailable")
    files = [root / "modules/engram/row_cache.hpp", root / "modules/engram/mapped_rows.hpp",
             root / "tests_cpp/engram_row_cache_fixture.cpp"]
    try:
        for f in files:
            if not f.is_file() or f.is_symlink():
                raise RuntimeError("missing/symlink source file: " + str(f))
        checksums = {str(f.relative_to(root)):digest(f) for f in files}
        out.mkdir(mode=0o700)
        flags = ["-std=c++17", "-O2", "-Wall", "-Wextra", "-Werror", "-pthread"]
        if args.sanitizers:
            flags = ["-std=c++17", "-O1", "-g", "-Wall", "-Wextra", "-Werror", "-pthread",
                     "-fsanitize=address,undefined", "-fno-omit-frame-pointer", "-fno-sanitize-recover=all"]
        executable = out / "engram_row_cache_fixture"
        build = run([compiler,*flags,str(files[2]),"-o",str(executable)])
        execution = run([str(executable)], timeout=120) if build["exit_code"] == 0 else None
        success = execution is not None and execution["exit_code"] == 0
        if success:
            try:
                payload = json.loads(execution["stdout"].strip())
                success = payload == {"status":"PASS", "tests":6, "gpu_executed":False,"model_executed":False}
            except (ValueError, TypeError):
                success = False
        status = "PASS" if success else "FAIL"
        report = {"schema_version":1,"status":status,"component":"P07 Engram raw-row cache prototype",
                  "integration_status":"STANDALONE_NOT_ATTACHED_TO_LLAMA_GRAPH",
                  "no_model_or_gpu_tests":True,"sanitizers":args.sanitizers,
                  "input_sha256":checksums,"compiler":run([compiler,"--version"])["stdout"].splitlines()[0],
                  "compile":build,"execute":execution,
                  "output_binary_sha256":digest(executable) if executable.is_file() else None}
        (out / "result.json").write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+"\n")
        if checksums != {str(f.relative_to(root)):digest(f) for f in files}:
            raise RuntimeError("source mutated during validation")
        print(json.dumps({"status":status, "record":str(out / "result.json"),"gpu_validated":False}))
        return 0 if success else 1
    except (OSError,RuntimeError,subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as error:
        if out.is_dir(): (out / "error.json").write_text(json.dumps({"status":"ERROR","message":str(error)})+"\n")
        print(json.dumps({"status":"ERROR","message":str(error)}))
        return 2

if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/test_engram_model_cache.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""P13 deterministic C++17 host parity/unload tests; never a GPU benchmark."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import time

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
FILES = [
    'tests_cpp/engram_model_cache_fixture.cpp',
    'modules/engram/graph_stage.hpp',
    'modules/engram/model_cache.hpp',
    'modules/engram/row_cache.hpp',
    'modules/engram/mapped_rows.hpp',
]


def sha(path: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def run(argv: list[str], timeout: int, env: dict[str,str] | None = None) -> dict:
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
