        p = subprocess.run(argv, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
                           text=True, timeout=timeout, env=env, check=False)
        return {'code': p.returncode, 'stdout': p.stdout[-4000:],
                'stderr': p.stderr[-4000:], 'seconds': round(time.monotonic()-start, 3)}
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        return {'code': -1, 'error': 'timeout', 'seconds': timeout}


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    ap.add_argument('--source-root', type=Path, default=ROOT)
    ap.add_argument('--compiler', default='c++')
    ap.add_argument('--output', type=Path, required=True)
    ap.add_argument('--sanitizers', action='store_true')
    args = ap.parse_args()
    source = args.source_root.resolve(strict=True)
    out = args.output.resolve()
    cc = shutil.which(args.compiler)
    if (not cc or out.exists() or args.output.is_symlink() or out.is_relative_to(source)
            or not out.parent.is_dir()
            or any(not (source/p).is_file() or (source/p).is_symlink() for p in FILES)):
        ap.error('compiler and regular input files are required; output must be new, outside source')
    before = {p: sha(source/p) for p in FILES}
    out.mkdir(mode=0o700)
    flags = ['-std=c++17', '-Wall', '-Wextra', '-Werror', '-pthread']
    if args.sanitizers:
        flags += ['-O1', '-g', '-fno-omit-frame-pointer', '-fsanitize=address,undefined',
                  '-fno-sanitize-recover=all']
    else:
        flags += ['-O2']
    binary = out/'p13_host'
    compile_result = run([cc, *flags, str(source/FILES[0]), '-o', str(binary)], 100)
    execute_result: dict = {'code': -1, 'error': 'compile failed'}
    if compile_result['code'] == 0:
        env=os.environ.copy()
        if args.sanitizers:
            env['ASAN_OPTIONS']='detect_leaks=0:halt_on_error=1'
            env['UBSAN_OPTIONS']='halt_on_error=1'
        execute_result = run([str(binary)], 100, env)
    try: result = json.loads(execute_result.get('stdout', ''))
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError): result = None
    passed = (compile_result['code'] == 0 and execute_result['code'] == 0 and
              result == {'status':'PASS','scenarios':4,'gpu':False,'model':False} and
              before == {p: sha(source/p) for p in FILES})
    data = {
        'schema': 'llama.experimental.p13.host.v1', 'status': 'PASS' if passed else 'FAIL',
        'sanitizers': args.sanitizers, 'source_sha256': before,
        'compile': compile_result, 'execute': execute_result,
        'model_or_cuda_runtime_executed': False,
        'full_llama_cpp_translation_unit_compiled': False,
    }
    (out/'result.json').write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2)+'\n')
    print(json.dumps({'status': data['status'], 'result': str(out/'result.json')}))
    return 0 if passed else 1

if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/test_engram_stage.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Compile and run opt-in Engram GGML staging's source-independent host core."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import time

def sha(path): return hashlib.sha256(Path(path).read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def main():
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--source-root',type=Path,default=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1])
    parser.add_argument('--output',type=Path,required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--compiler',default='c++')
    parser.add_argument('--sanitizers',action='store_true')
    args=parser.parse_args()
    root=args.source_root.resolve(strict=True)
    out=args.output.resolve()
    source=root/'tests_cpp/engram_graph_stage_fixture.cpp'
    dependencies=[source,*(root/'modules/engram'/p for p in ['graph_stage.hpp','mapped_rows.hpp','row_cache.hpp'])]
    if out.exists() or args.output.is_symlink() or out.is_relative_to(root) or not out.parent.is_dir():
        parser.error('output must be new and outside source root')
    if not shutil.which(args.compiler): parser.error('compiler unavailable')
    if any(not p.is_file() or p.is_symlink() for p in dependencies): parser.error('invalid source file')
    before={str(p.relative_to(root)):sha(p) for p in dependencies}
    out.mkdir(mode=0o700)
    flags=['-std=c++17','-Wall','-Wextra','-Werror','-pthread','-O2']
    if args.sanitizers:
        flags=['-std=c++17','-Wall','-Wextra','-Werror','-pthread','-O1','-g',
               '-fsanitize=address,undefined','-fno-omit-frame-pointer','-fno-sanitize-recover=all']
    binary=out/'engram_stage_fixture'
    command=[shutil.which(args.compiler),*flags,str(source),'-o',str(binary)]
    results={}
    for name,cmd,timeout in [('compile',command,120),('execute',[str(binary)],100)]:
        if name=='execute' and results['compile']['exit_code']!=0:break
        tick=time.monotonic()
        try:
            p=subprocess.run(cmd,capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=timeout,check=False)
            results[name]={'command':cmd,'exit_code':p.returncode,'stdout':p.stdout,
                           'stderr':p.stderr,'seconds':round(time.monotonic()-tick,3)}
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as e:
            results[name]={'command':cmd,'exit_code':-1,'error':'timeout','seconds':timeout}
    try: js=json.loads(results['execute']['stdout'])
    except (KeyError,ValueError):js={}
    status=('PASS' if all(x['exit_code']==0 for x in results.values()) and
            js=={'status':'PASS','scenarios':4,'gpu':False,'model':False} and
            before=={str(p.relative_to(root)):sha(p) for p in dependencies} else 'FAIL')
    report={'status':status,'sanitizers':args.sanitizers,'tests':results,'inputs_sha256':before,
            'model_or_cuda_runtime_executed':False,'graph_path_compiled':False}
    (out/'result.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
    print(json.dumps({'status':status,'result':str(out/'result.json')}))
    return 0 if status=='PASS' else 1

if __name__=='__main__':raise SystemExit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/test_frequency.py =====
from pathlib import Path
import argparse
p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('--source',type=Path,required=True);p.add_argument('--output',type=Path,required=True);a=p.parse_args()
s=(a.source/'src/llama-moe-cache.cpp').read_text();body=s[s.index('struct moe_cache_slru {'):s.index('// An opt-in limit')]
header='''#include <algorithm>\n#include <cassert>\n#include <cstdint>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <vector>\n#include <random>\n#include <iostream>\n#include <set>\n#define GGML_ASSERT assert\n#define GGML_ABORT(...) std::abort()\n'''
test=r'''
int main() {
  for (bool use_frequency : {false,true}) for (bool reuse : {false,true}) {
    setenv("LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_FREQUENCY",use_frequency?"1":"0",1);
    moe_cache_slru c; c.init(5,31,23);
    std::vector<std::vector<int32_t>> maps(5,std::vector<int32_t>(31,-1));
    for(int l=0;l<5;++l)c.slot_map[l]=maps[l].data();
    std::vector<int32_t> banks(23,-1);std::mt19937 rng(2971);
    for(int step=0;step<50000;++step) {
      if(step%811==0) {c.invalidate_banks();std::fill(banks.begin(),banks.end(),-1);}
      int l=rng()%5, n=rng()%25;std::vector<int32_t> ids;std::set<int32_t> want;
      for(int j=0;j<n;++j){int e=rng()%31;ids.push_back(e);want.insert(e);}
      size_t expected_hits=0;for(int e:want)expected_hits+=maps[l][e]>=0;
      std::vector<moe_cache_slru::fill> fills;size_t hits;
      bool ok=reuse?c.plan_reuse(l,ids.data(),ids.size(),fills,hits):c.plan(l,ids.data(),ids.size(),fills,hits);
      assert(ok==(want.size()<=23));if(!ok)continue;
      assert(hits==expected_hits && fills.size()+hits==want.size());
      for(auto f:fills){assert(want.count(f.expert));banks[f.slot]=l*31+f.expert;}
      for(int e:want){assert(maps[l][e]>=0);assert(banks[maps[l][e]]==l*31+e);}
      for(int k=0;k<23;++k)if(c.key_of[k]>=0){auto key=c.key_of[k];assert(maps[key/31][key%31]==k);assert(banks[k]==key);}
      for(int a=0;a<5;++a)for(int e=0;e<31;++e)if(maps[a][e]>=0)assert(c.key_of[maps[a][e]]==a*31+e);
      if(use_frequency){assert(c.heap.size()==23);for(size_t j=0;j<23;++j){assert(c.heap_pos[c.heap[j]]==int(j));if(j>0)assert(!c.colder(c.heap[j],c.heap[(j-1)/2]));}}
    }
  }
  setenv("LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_FREQUENCY","1",1);
  moe_cache_slru c;c.init(1,5,2);std::vector<int32_t> m(5,-1);c.slot_map[0]=m.data();
  std::vector<moe_cache_slru::fill> f;size_t hits;int32_t hot=0;
  for(int i=0;i<100;++i)c.plan(0,&hot,1,f,hits);
  int32_t cold=1;c.plan(0,&cold,1,f,hits);cold=2;c.plan(0,&cold,1,f,hits);
  assert(m[0]>=0 && m[1]<0 && m[2]>=0);
  int32_t next=3;for(int i=0;i<1024;++i)c.plan(0,&next,1,f,hits);
  cold=4;c.plan(0,&cold,1,f,hits);assert(m[0]<0 && m[3]>=0);
  int32_t both[]={3,4,3};c.plan(0,both,3,f,hits);assert(hits==2);
  int32_t too_many[]={0,1,2};auto old=m;assert(!c.plan(0,too_many,3,f,hits));assert(m==old);
  std::cout<<"PASS 200000 randomized multi-layer requests, map/bank identity, heap invariants, decay drift, duplicates, overflow, invalidation"<<std::endl;
}
'''
a.output.write_text(header+body+test)

===== SOURCE FILE tools/test_hash_scratch.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Compile and compare actual graph-input methods using host boundary fixtures.

This is not a complete llama build, GPU test, Engram storage benchmark, or
numerical test of model logits. Full production translation units are checked
separately; here backend writes and prefetch calls are captured as exact bytes.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time


def function(text: str, start: str) -> str:
    if text.count(start) != 1:
        raise ValueError("Source anchor is absent or ambiguous: " + start)
    a = text.index(start)
    opening = text.index("{", a)
    depth = 0
    for i in range(opening, len(text)):
        if text[i] == "{":
            depth += 1
        elif text[i] == "}":
            depth -= 1
            if depth == 0:
                return text[a:i+1]
    raise ValueError("Unbalanced source function: " + start)


def graph_class(text: str, name: str) -> str:
    return function(text, "class " + name + " :") + ";"


def section(text: str, kind: str, optimized: bool) -> str:
    if kind == "qwen":
        name = "llm_graph_input_qwen4exp_ple"
        helpers = ([function(text, "static bool qwen4exp_hash_scratch_enabled()"),
                    function(text, "static void qwen4exp_hash_rows_reuse(")]
                   if optimized else [])
        methods = [function(text, "void " + name + "::set_input(")]
    else:
        name = "llm_graph_input_dsv41"
        helpers = [function(text, "static bool dsv41_hash_scratch_enabled()")] if optimized else []
        methods = [function(text, "void " + name + "::set_input("),
                   function(text, "bool " + name + "::can_reuse(")]
    return "\n\n".join([*helpers, graph_class(text, name), *methods])


def run(args, output: Path) -> int:
    template = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "tests_cpp/hash_scratch_fixture.cpp"
    body = template.read_text()
    sources = {}
    for role, tree in (("baseline", args.baseline), ("candidate", args.candidate)):
        for kind, filename in (("qwen", "qwen4exp.cpp"), ("deepseek", "deepseek41.cpp")):
            p = tree / "src/models" / filename
            text = p.read_text()
            sources[role + ":" + filename] = hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
            body = body.replace("// @" + role.upper() + "_" + kind.upper() + "@",
                                section(text, kind, role == "candidate"))
    base_hash = (args.baseline / "src/models/deepseek41.cpp").read_text()
    body = body.replace("// @ACTUAL_ENGRAM_HASH@",
                        function(base_hash, "void llama_model_deepseek41::engram_hash("))
    source = output / "hash_scratch_generated.cpp"
    source.write_text(body)
    executable = output / "hash_scratch_test"
    command = [args.compiler, "-std=c++17", "-O2", "-Wall", "-Wextra", "-Werror",
               "-Wno-mismatched-new-delete", "-pthread", "-I" + str(args.candidate / "src"), str(source), "-o", str(executable)]
    if args.sanitize:
        command[1:1] = ["-fsanitize=address,undefined", "-fno-sanitize-recover=all", "-fno-omit-frame-pointer"]
    began = time.monotonic()
    compile_run = subprocess.run(command, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120)
    result = {"scope": "Actual extracted graph-input methods; fixture tensor backend and KV history; no GPU/inference",
              "source_sha256": sources, "command": command,
              "compile_exit_code": compile_run.returncode, "compile_stderr": compile_run.stderr}
    if compile_run.returncode == 0:
        env = os.environ.copy()
        if args.sanitize:
            # The execution sandbox does not permit LeakSanitizer /proc task scans.
            env["ASAN_OPTIONS"] = "detect_leaks=0"
            result["sanitizers"] = {"address": True, "undefined": True, "leaks": False}
        test = subprocess.run([str(executable)], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120, env=env)
        result.update(test_exit_code=test.returncode, stdout=test.stdout, stderr=test.stderr)
        status = test.returncode
    else:
        status = compile_run.returncode
    result["elapsed_seconds"] = round(time.monotonic() - began, 3)
    (output / "result.json").write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2) + "\n")
    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))
    return status


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--baseline", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--candidate", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, help="New local directory for generated test and result")
    parser.add_argument("--compiler", default="c++")
    parser.add_argument("--sanitize", action="store_true", help="ASan/UBSan; leak scanning disabled for sandbox")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    args.baseline = args.baseline.resolve(strict=True)
    args.candidate = args.candidate.resolve(strict=True)
    if not args.baseline.is_dir() or not args.candidate.is_dir():
        parser.error("Both inputs must be source directories")
    if args.output:
        args.output = args.output.resolve()
        if any(args.output.is_relative_to(p) for p in (args.baseline, args.candidate)):
            parser.error("--output must be outside both source trees")
        args.output.mkdir(parents=False, exist_ok=False)
        return run(args, args.output)
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="llama-hash-scratch-") as temporary:
        return run(args, Path(temporary))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/test_hc_post.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Compile actual P03 HC_POST policy snippets against instrumented host mocks.

Requires a patched llama.cpp source tree, its real headers, and a C++17 compiler.
No CMake, CUDA, model loading, inference, or network operation is performed.
This is a control-flow contract test, not numerical or paired GPU validation.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time


def sha256(data: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()


def between(text: str, start: str, end: str) -> str:
    """Fail clearly when a forward port no longer has the reviewed boundaries."""
    if text.count(start) != 1 or text.count(end) != 1:
        raise ValueError("Source anchor is absent or ambiguous: " + start)
    first, last = text.index(start), text.index(end)
    if last <= first:
        raise ValueError("Source anchors are out of order: " + start)
    return text[first:last]


def run(args: argparse.Namespace, output: Path) -> int:
    candidate = args.candidate
    template = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "tests_cpp/hc_post_policy_fixture.cpp.in"
    paths = (
        "src/models/deepseek4.cpp", "src/llama-context.cpp", "src/llama-graph.h",
        "src/llama-cparams.h", "include/llama.h", "ggml/include/ggml.h",
        "ggml/include/ggml-backend.h", "ggml/include/ggml-alloc.h",
        "ggml/include/ggml-cpu.h", "ggml/include/ggml-opt.h", "ggml/include/gguf.h",
    )
    source_bytes = {path: (candidate / path).read_bytes() for path in paths}
    model = source_bytes["src/models/deepseek4.cpp"].decode("utf-8")
    context = source_bytes["src/llama-context.cpp"].decode("utf-8")
    graph = source_bytes["src/llama-graph.h"].decode("utf-8")
    parts = {
        "LAYOUT_AND_BUILDER": between(model, "static bool dsv4_hc_post_layer_layout(",
                                       "ggml_tensor * llama_model_deepseek4::graph::build_hc_head("),
        "CONTEXT_POLICY": between(context, "        const char * hc_post_layers_env = getenv(",
                                  "        if (cparams.moe_cache_size > 0) {"),
        "REUSE_POLICY": between(graph, "        if (cparams.dsv4_hc_post_backends !=",
                                "        return\n            cparams.embeddings"),
    }
    template_bytes = template.read_bytes()
    body = template_bytes.decode("utf-8")
    for name, part in parts.items():
        marker = "@" + name + "@"
        if body.count(marker) != 1:
            raise ValueError("Fixture marker is absent or ambiguous: " + name)
        body = body.replace(marker, part)
    generated = output / "hc_post_policy_generated.cpp"
    generated.write_text(body, encoding="utf-8")
    binary = output / "hc_post_policy_test"
    command = [args.compiler, "-std=c++17", "-O1", "-Wall", "-Wextra", "-Werror"]
    if args.sanitize:
        command += ["-fsanitize=address,undefined", "-fno-sanitize-recover=all", "-fno-omit-frame-pointer"]
    command += ["-I" + str(candidate / folder) for folder in ("include", "src", "ggml/include")]
    command += [str(generated), "-o", str(binary)]
    env = os.environ.copy()
    if args.sanitize:
        # LeakSanitizer cannot enumerate /proc tasks in the development sandbox.
        # Address/UB checks remain enabled; the report excludes leak coverage.
        previous = env.get("ASAN_OPTIONS", "")
        env["ASAN_OPTIONS"] = (previous + ":" if previous else "") + "detect_leaks=0"
    records = []
    status = 0
    for kind, cmd in (("host_fixture_compile", command), ("host_control_flow_test", [str(binary)])):
        start = time.monotonic()
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120, env=env)
            record = dict(kind=kind, command=cmd, exit_code=result.returncode,
                          stdout=result.stdout, stderr=result.stderr)
            status = result.returncode
        except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as error:
            record = dict(kind=kind, command=cmd, exit_code=None, error=str(error))
            status = 1
        record["elapsed_seconds"] = round(time.monotonic() - start, 3)
        records.append(record)
        if status:
            break
    unchanged = all((candidate / path).read_bytes() == data for path, data in source_bytes.items())
    if not unchanged:
        status = 1
    report = dict(
        schema_version=1,
        status="PASS_HOST_CONTROL_FLOW_ONLY" if status == 0 else "FAIL",
        scope="Actual candidate policy/builder/reuse source; real headers; instrumented GGML/backend/scheduler mocks",
        candidate=str(candidate),
        source_header_sha256={path: sha256(data) for path, data in source_bytes.items()},
        source_snippet_sha256={name: sha256(part.encode("utf-8")) for name, part in parts.items()},
        template_sha256=sha256(template_bytes),
        generated_source_sha256=sha256(generated.read_bytes()),
        inspected_source_files_unchanged=unchanged,
        sanitizer="AddressSanitizer + UndefinedBehaviorSanitizer; leak detection disabled" if args.sanitize else "none",
        records=records,
        not_validated=["full build or linking of llama.cpp", "real scheduler placement", "CUDA support or execution",
                       "floating-point equivalence", "leak detection", "model inference", "speedup"],
    )
    (output / "result.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
    return 0 if status == 0 else 1


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--candidate", type=Path, required=True, help="Patched source checkout with real upstream headers")
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, help="New directory for generated host fixture, binary and result.json")
    parser.add_argument("--compiler", default="c++", help="C++ compiler executable, without shell arguments")
    parser.add_argument("--sanitize", action="store_true", help="Enable ASan and UBSan; leak checks remain disabled")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    try:
        args.candidate = args.candidate.resolve(strict=True)
        if not args.candidate.is_dir():
            raise ValueError("--candidate must be a source directory")
        if args.output:
            output = args.output.resolve()
            if output == args.candidate or args.candidate in output.parents:
                raise ValueError("--output must be outside the candidate source tree")
            output.mkdir(parents=False, exist_ok=False)
            return run(args, output)
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="llama-hc-post-") as temporary:
            return run(args, Path(temporary))
    except (OSError, ValueError) as error:
        parser.error(str(error))
        return 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/test_moe_fill_plan.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Compile P12 shared fill plan with exact P04 patch oracle; host-only."""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

def sha(data): return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()

def original_helper():
    p=(ROOT/'patches/0004-moe-upload-staging.patch').read_text()
    start=p.index('+struct moe_cache_upload_stats {')
    end=p.index(' // gate, up, down or gate_up, down',start)
    lines=p[start:end].splitlines(keepends=True)
    assert all(l.startswith('+') for l in lines), 'P04 helper has unexpected diff content'
    return ''.join(l[1:] for l in lines)

def invoke(args,env=None):
    start=time.monotonic()
    r=subprocess.run(args,capture_output=True,text=True,env=env,timeout=90)
    return {'status':'PASS' if r.returncode==0 else 'FAIL','code':r.returncode,
            'elapsed_s':round(time.monotonic()-start,3),
            'stdout':r.stdout[-2500:],'stderr':r.stderr[-5000:]}

def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('--compiler',default='c++')
    p.add_argument('--sanitizers',action='store_true')
    p.add_argument('--output',type=Path)
    a=p.parse_args()
    c=shutil.which(a.compiler)
    if not c: p.error('compiler not found')
    if a.output and (a.output.exists() or a.output.resolve().is_relative_to(ROOT)):
        p.error('output must not exist and must be outside source')
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='llama-p12-') as tmp:
        out=Path(tmp) if a.output is None else a.output.resolve()
        out.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        template=(ROOT/'tests_cpp/moe_fill_plan_fixture.cpp.in').read_text()
        assert template.count('@@P04_HELPER@@')==1
        content=template.replace('@@P04_HELPER@@',original_helper())
        source=out/'moe_fill_plan_fixture.cpp';source.write_text(content)
        steps=[]
        for mode in ('plain', 'sanitized'):
            if mode=='sanitized' and not a.sanitizers: continue
            exe=out/f'moe_fill_{mode}'
            command=[c,'-std=c++17','-pthread','-Wall','-Wextra','-Werror','-I'+str(ROOT/'modules'),
                     '-O2' if mode=='plain' else '-O1',str(source),'-o',str(exe)]
            if mode=='sanitized':
                command += ['-g','-fno-omit-frame-pointer','-fsanitize=address,undefined','-fno-sanitize-recover=all']
            step={'mode':mode,'compile':invoke(command)}
            if step['compile']['code']==0:
                for flag in ('enabled','disabled'):
                    env=os.environ.copy()
                    if flag=='enabled':env['LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_SHARED_PLAN']='1'
                    else:env.pop('LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_SHARED_PLAN',None)
                    if mode=='sanitized':
                        env['ASAN_OPTIONS']='detect_leaks=0:halt_on_error=1'
                        env['UBSAN_OPTIONS']='halt_on_error=1'
                    step[flag]=invoke([str(exe),flag],env)
            steps.append(step)
            if step['compile']['code'] or any(step.get(flag,{}).get('code',1) for flag in ('enabled','disabled')):break
        passed=len(steps)==(2 if a.sanitizers else 1) and all(
            x['compile']['code']==0 and x['enabled']['code']==0 and x['disabled']['code']==0 for x in steps)
        report={'schema':'llama.experimental.p12.host.v1','status':'PASS' if passed else 'FAIL',
                'scope':'host-only P04 exact semantics, thread sharing and ASan/UBSan; no CUDA or full llama.cpp build',
                'p04_patch_sha256':sha((ROOT/'patches/0004-moe-upload-staging.patch').read_bytes()),
                'p12_header_sha256':sha((ROOT/'modules/moe/upload_plan.hpp').read_bytes()),
                'generated_sha256':sha(source.read_bytes()),'steps':steps}
        if a.output:(out/'result.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
        print(json.dumps(report,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False))
        return 0 if passed else 1

if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/test_moe_metadata.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""P11 strict host-only worker metadata regression. No model/GPU/network."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
FILES = ('modules/moe/upload_worker.hpp', 'modules/moe/upload_pipeline.hpp',
         'modules/moe/upload_types.hpp', 'tests_cpp/moe_worker_metadata_fixture.cpp')

def sha(path): return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def main():
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--source-root',type=Path,default=ROOT)
    parser.add_argument('--output',type=Path)
    parser.add_argument('--compiler',default='c++')
    parser.add_argument('--sanitizers',action='store_true')
    args=parser.parse_args()
    try:
        source=args.source_root.resolve(strict=True)
        compiler=shutil.which(args.compiler)
        if not compiler: raise ValueError('C++ compiler unavailable')
        if args.output is not None:
            output=args.output.resolve()
            if output.exists() or args.output.is_symlink() or output.is_relative_to(source) or not output.parent.is_dir():
                raise ValueError('output must be new outside source')
            output.mkdir(mode=0o700,parents=False)
            return run(source,output,compiler,args.sanitizers)
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='llama-p11-host-') as d:
            return run(source,Path(d),compiler,args.sanitizers)
    except (OSError,ValueError,subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as e:
        print(json.dumps({'status':'ERROR','reason':str(e)}))
        return 2

def run(root,output,compiler,sanitizers):
    before={n:sha(root/n) for n in FILES}
    checks=[]
    for san in ([False,True] if sanitizers else [False]):
        executable=output/('p11_sanitized' if san else 'p11_plain')
        cmd=[compiler,'-std=c++17','-O1' if san else '-O2','-Wall','-Wextra','-Werror',
             '-pedantic','-pthread','-I'+str(root),str(root/'tests_cpp/moe_worker_metadata_fixture.cpp'),
             '-o',str(executable)]
        if san:
            cmd+=['-DP11_SKIP_ALLOC_ASSERT','-g','-fno-omit-frame-pointer',
                  '-fsanitize=address,undefined','-fno-sanitize-recover=all']
        proc=subprocess.run(cmd,capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=50)
        checks.append({'kind':'compile','sanitizers':san,'returncode':proc.returncode,
                       'stderr':proc.stderr[:1200]})
        if proc.returncode:break
        env=dict(os.environ)
        if san:
            env['ASAN_OPTIONS']='detect_leaks=0:halt_on_error=1'
            env['UBSAN_OPTIONS']='halt_on_error=1'
        proc=subprocess.run([str(executable)],capture_output=True,text=True,env=env,timeout=30)
        checks.append({'kind':'run','sanitizers':san,'returncode':proc.returncode,
                       'stdout':proc.stdout[:1200],'stderr':proc.stderr[:1200]})
        if proc.returncode:break
    after={n:sha(root/n) for n in FILES}
    ok=len(checks)==(4 if sanitizers else 2) and all(x['returncode']==0 for x in checks) and before==after
    report={'schema':'llama.experimental.p11.metadata.v1','status':'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL',
            'scope':'P11 C++17 host control metadata only; CUDA/model/token rates not tested',
            'checks':checks,'sources_unchanged':before==after,'file_sha256':before}
    (output/'result.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2)+'\n')
    print(json.dumps({'status':report['status'],'checks':len(checks)}))
    return 0 if ok else 1

if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/test_moe_pipeline.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Compile and exercise the P09 host-only producer/consumer scheduling module.

Does not link a llama.cpp binary, invoke a CUDA GPU, or measure PCIe overlap.
The fixture emulates deferred async backend reads to check lifetime and order.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time


def sha(path: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def execute(command: list[str], *, env=None, timeout=120) -> dict:
    start = time.monotonic()
    proc = subprocess.run(command, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                          stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
                          text=True, env=env, timeout=timeout, check=False)
    return {"exit_code": proc.returncode, "command": command,
            "elapsed_seconds": round(time.monotonic() - start, 3),
            "stdout": proc.stdout, "stderr": proc.stderr}


def run(source: Path, output: Path, compiler: str, sanitizers: bool) -> int:
    hdr = source / 'modules/moe/upload_pipeline.hpp'
    fixture = source / 'tests_cpp/moe_upload_pipeline_fixture.cpp'
    if not hdr.is_file() or not fixture.is_file():
        raise ValueError('P09 source files are missing')
    # Host-only, independent of the source tree, never edits the release.
    common = [compiler, '-std=c++17', '-Wall', '-Wextra', '-Werror', '-pthread',
              '-I'+str(source / 'modules'), str(fixture)]
    standard_binary = output / 'moe_upload_pipeline_host'
    steps = [execute([*common, '-O2', '-o', str(standard_binary)])]
    if steps[-1]['exit_code'] == 0:
        steps.append(execute([str(standard_binary)]))
    if sanitizers and all(s['exit_code'] == 0 for s in steps):
        sanitized_binary = output / 'moe_upload_pipeline_sanitized'
        steps.append(execute([*common, '-O1', '-g', '-fsanitize=address,undefined',
                              '-fno-sanitize-recover=all', '-fno-omit-frame-pointer',
                              '-o', str(sanitized_binary)]))
        if steps[-1]['exit_code'] == 0:
            env = dict(os.environ)
            env['ASAN_OPTIONS'] = 'detect_leaks=0:halt_on_error=1'
            env['UBSAN_OPTIONS'] = 'halt_on_error=1'
            steps.append(execute([str(sanitized_binary)], env=env))
    passed = all(s['exit_code'] == 0 for s in steps)
    if len(steps) != (4 if sanitizers else 2):
        passed = False
    result = {
        'schema': 'llama.experimental.p09.host.v1',
        'status': 'PASS' if passed else 'FAIL',
        'source_module': 'P09 host upload producer, NOT integrated into llama-moe-cache.cpp',
        'header_sha256': sha(hdr), 'fixture_sha256': sha(fixture),
        'compiler': compiler, 'sanitizers': sanitizers,
        'asan_ubsan': 'PASS' if sanitizers and passed else ('NOT_RUN' if not sanitizers else 'FAIL'),
        'leaks_checked': False,
        'steps': steps,
        'not_tested': ['full llama.cpp build/link', 'CUDA', 'GPU stream concurrency',
                       'real pinned-memory status', 'PCIe transfer bandwidth',
                       'model inference', 'performance or numerical parity'],
    }
    (output / 'result.json').write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + '\n')
    print(json.dumps({'status': result['status'],
                      'report': str(output / 'result.json'),
                      'full_gpu_validated': False}))
    return 0 if passed else 1


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--source-root', type=Path, default=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1])
    parser.add_argument('--output', type=Path, help='nonexistent directory outside source root')
    parser.add_argument('--compiler', default='c++')
    parser.add_argument('--sanitizers', action='store_true')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    try:
        source = args.source_root.resolve(strict=True)
        compiler = shutil.which(args.compiler)
        if not compiler:
            raise ValueError('C++17 compiler is unavailable')
        if args.output:
            dest = args.output.resolve()
            if dest.exists() or dest.is_symlink() or dest.is_relative_to(source):
                raise ValueError('output must not exist and must be outside source tree')
            if not dest.parent.is_dir():
                raise ValueError('output parent does not exist')
            dest.mkdir(mode=0o700)
            return run(source, dest, compiler, args.sanitizers)
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='llama-experimental-p09-') as temp:
            return run(source, Path(temp), compiler, args.sanitizers)
    except (OSError, ValueError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as error:
        print(json.dumps({'status': 'ERROR', 'reason': str(error)}))
        return 2


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/test_moe_pipeline_bridge.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Validate EXACT candidate P04 helper + P09B backend submission block.

Host-only: the fake backend defers device reads until synchronize; no CUDA,
no full llama.cpp compilation, and no model/speed/parity claims.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

def sha(p): return hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def run(argv, env=None):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    p=subprocess.run(argv,stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,capture_output=True,text=True,
                     timeout=75,check=False,env=env)
    return {"argv": argv,"exit_code":p.returncode,"seconds":round(time.monotonic()-t0,3),
            "stdout":p.stdout,"stderr":p.stderr}

def extract(src: str, start: str, stop: str) -> str:
    assert src.count(start) == 1, f"missing or repeated source marker: {start}"
    a=src.index(start)
    b=src.find(stop,a+len(start))
    assert b>a, f"missing end marker: {stop}"
    return src[a:b]

def build(candidate: Path, output: Path, compiler: str, sanitizers:bool) -> int:
    target=candidate/'src/llama-moe-cache.cpp'
    expected=ROOT/'modules/moe/upload_pipeline.hpp'
    vendored=candidate/'src/llama-experimental/upload_pipeline.hpp'
    if not target.is_file() or not vendored.is_file():
        raise ValueError("candidate missing patched source or vendored upload_pipeline.hpp")
    if sha(vendored)!=sha(expected):
        raise ValueError("candidate header differs from release header")
    plan_expected=ROOT/'modules/moe/upload_plan.hpp'
    plan_vendor=candidate/'src/llama-experimental/upload_plan.hpp'
    if not plan_vendor.is_file() or sha(plan_vendor)!=sha(plan_expected):
        raise ValueError("candidate P12 shared fill plan header missing or differs from release header")
    manifest=json.loads((ROOT/'manifest.json').read_text())
    p12=next(x for x in manifest['patches'] if x['id']=='P12-moe-shared-fill-plan')
    if sha(target)!=p12['targets'][0]['after_sha256']:
        raise ValueError("Expected the P12 historical target. For the current candidate use test_moe_scheduler_bridge.py.")
    src=target.read_text()
    helper=extract(src, 'struct moe_cache_upload_stats {','// gate, up, down or gate_up, down')
    snippet=extract(src,'        std::optional<llama_exp::moe_fill_plan> shared_plan;',
                         '        stats & st = ids.size()')
    assert snippet.count('moe_upload_pipeline::execute(')==1
    assert snippet.count('llama_moe_cache_upload_experts_planned(')==2
    assert snippet.count('ggml_backend_tensor_set_async')>=2
    return run_fixture(candidate, output, compiler, sanitizers, helper, snippet, sha(target),
                       "P04, P09B and P12 literal source with a deferred backend")

def run_fixture(candidate, output, compiler, sanitizers, helper, snippet, source_digest, scope):
    tmpl=(ROOT/'tests_cpp/moe_upload_bridge_fixture.cpp.in').read_text()
    code=tmpl.replace('@@P04_HELPER@@',helper).replace('@@P09B_COPY_BLOCK@@',snippet)
    assert '@@' not in code
    generated=output/'moe_bridge_generated.cpp'
    generated.write_text(code)
    command=[compiler,'-std=c++17','-Wall','-Wextra','-Werror','-pthread',
             '-I'+str(candidate/'src'),str(generated)]
    steps=[]
    obj=output/'moe_bridge_host'
    steps.append(run([*command,'-O2','-o',str(obj)]))
    for mode in ('enabled','disabled'):
        for shared in ('shared','baseline'):
            if steps[-1]['exit_code']: break
            env=dict(os.environ)
            if mode=='enabled':env['LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_PIPELINE']='1'
            else:env.pop('LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_PIPELINE',None)
            if shared=='shared':env['LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_SHARED_PLAN']='1'
            else:env.pop('LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_SHARED_PLAN',None)
            steps.append(run([str(obj),mode,shared],env=env))
    if sanitizers and all(p['exit_code']==0 for p in steps):
        bin=output/'moe_bridge_sanitized'
        steps.append(run([*command,'-O1','-g','-fno-omit-frame-pointer',
                    '-fsanitize=address,undefined','-fno-sanitize-recover=all','-o',str(bin)]))
        for mode in ('enabled','disabled'):
            for shared in ('shared','baseline'):
                if steps[-1]['exit_code']: break
                env=dict(os.environ)
                env['ASAN_OPTIONS']='detect_leaks=0:halt_on_error=1'
                env['UBSAN_OPTIONS']='halt_on_error=1'
                if mode=='enabled':env['LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_PIPELINE']='1'
                else:env.pop('LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_PIPELINE',None)
                if shared=='shared':env['LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_SHARED_PLAN']='1'
                else:env.pop('LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_SHARED_PLAN',None)
                steps.append(run([str(bin),mode,shared],env=env))
    passed=len(steps)==(10 if sanitizers else 5) and all(x['exit_code']==0 for x in steps)
    report={'schema':'llama.experimental.p12.mock_backend.v1',
            'status':'PASS' if passed else 'FAIL',
            'checks':scope,
            'original_patched_source_sha256':source_digest,
            'generated_fixture_sha256':sha(generated),
            'sanitizers':sanitizers,
            'scope':'host-only: no actual ggml backend / GPU / full CMake / CUDA / GGUF inference',
            'steps':steps}
    (output/'result.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
    print(json.dumps({'status':report['status'],'report':str(output/'result.json')}))
    return 0 if passed else 1

def main():
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--candidate',type=Path,required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--output',type=Path)
    parser.add_argument('--compiler',default='c++')
    parser.add_argument('--sanitizers',action='store_true')
    a=parser.parse_args()
    try:
        candidate=a.candidate.resolve(strict=True)
        compiler=shutil.which(a.compiler)
        if not compiler:raise ValueError('C++ compiler not found')
        if a.output:
            o=a.output.resolve()
            if o.exists() or o.is_relative_to(candidate) or o.is_relative_to(ROOT):
                raise ValueError('output must not exist and cannot be inside source or candidate')
            if not o.parent.is_dir():raise ValueError('output parent missing')
            o.mkdir(mode=0o700)
            return build(candidate,o,compiler,a.sanitizers)
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='llama-exp-p09b-') as td:
            return build(candidate,Path(td),compiler,a.sanitizers)
    except (OSError,ValueError,AssertionError,subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as exc:
        print(json.dumps({'status':'ERROR','reason':str(exc)}))
        return 2

if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/test_moe_plan.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Test the real legacy/optimized SLRU planners extracted from --candidate.

The input may be a complete isolated llama.cpp checkout or a partial source
tree containing src/llama-moe-cache.cpp. A C++17 compiler is sufficient. This
does not build or run llama, CUDA, models, or inference. The optional benchmark
measures this CPU-local planner only; it does not measure token throughput.
The candidate source is read only. All generated files live in a new output
directory, or a newly created temporary directory when --output is omitted.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import platform
import shutil
import statistics
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile


def sha(data):
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()


def planner(text):
    start = text.index("struct moe_cache_slru {")
    end = text.index("\n};", start) + len("\n};")
    return text[start:end] + "\n"


def method(text, name):
    start = text.index("    bool " + name + "(")
    end = text.index("\n    }\n", start) + len("\n    }\n")
    return text[start:end]


def command(argv, output, *, affinity=None, env=None):
    before_exec = None
    if affinity is not None:
        before_exec = lambda: os.sched_setaffinity(0, {affinity})
    proc = subprocess.run(argv, cwd=output, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, capture_output=True,
                          text=True, timeout=300, check=False, shell=False,
                          preexec_fn=before_exec, env=env)
    if proc.returncode:
        raise RuntimeError("Command failed: " + repr(argv) + "\n" + proc.stdout + proc.stderr)
    return proc.stdout


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    parser.add_argument("--candidate", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, help="New directory outside the source tree (default: new temporary directory)")
    parser.add_argument("--compiler", default="c++")
    parser.add_argument("--bench", action="store_true", help="Also run the optional CPU-only microbenchmark")
    parser.add_argument("--iterations", type=int, default=100000)
    parser.add_argument("--repeats", type=int, default=7)
    parser.add_argument("--case", action="append", default=[], help="Benchmark case name; repeat to select multiple cases")
    parser.add_argument("--pin-cpu", type=int, help="Pin only the owned test process to one permitted CPU")
    parser.add_argument("--sanitizers", action="store_true", help="Run AddressSanitizer and UndefinedBehaviorSanitizer")
    parser.add_argument("--disable-leak-check", action="store_true", help="Explicitly disable only LeakSanitizer when sandbox /proc access prevents it")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    candidate = args.candidate.expanduser().resolve()
    source = candidate / "src/llama-moe-cache.cpp"
    if not candidate.is_dir() or not source.is_file() or source.is_symlink():
        parser.error("candidate must contain a regular src/llama-moe-cache.cpp")
    if args.iterations <= 0 or args.repeats <= 0:
        parser.error("iterations/repeats must be positive")
    if args.case and not args.bench:
        parser.error("--case requires --bench")
    if args.disable_leak_check and not args.sanitizers:
        parser.error("--disable-leak-check requires --sanitizers")
    if args.pin_cpu is not None and (not hasattr(os, "sched_getaffinity") or args.pin_cpu not in os.sched_getaffinity(0)):
        parser.error("requested CPU is unavailable in the current affinity mask")
    compiler = shutil.which(args.compiler)
    if compiler is None:
        parser.error("compiler is unavailable")
    compiler = str(Path(compiler).resolve())
    source_bytes = source.read_bytes()
    text = source_bytes.decode("utf-8")
    extracted = planner(text)
    fallback = method(text, "plan")
    optimized_method = method(text, "plan_reuse")
    harness = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "tests_cpp/test_moe_plan.cpp"
    harness_bytes = harness.read_bytes()
    if args.output is None:
        temporary_parent = Path(tempfile.gettempdir()).resolve()
        if temporary_parent.is_relative_to(candidate):
            parser.error("default temporary parent is inside candidate; choose --output outside it")
        output = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="llama-moe-plan-tests-", dir=temporary_parent)).resolve()
    else:
        requested = args.output.expanduser().absolute()
        if requested.exists() or requested.is_symlink() or not requested.parent.is_dir():
            parser.error("output must be new, with an existing parent")
        output = requested.parent.resolve() / requested.name
        if output.is_relative_to(candidate) or candidate.is_relative_to(output):
            parser.error("output must be outside the candidate source tree")
        output.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=False, exist_ok=False)
    if output.is_relative_to(candidate):
        raise RuntimeError("temporary directory is inside candidate; choose --output outside it")
    header = output / "planner_under_test.h"
    header.write_text(extracted)
    test_source = output / "test_moe_plan.cpp"
    test_source.write_bytes(harness_bytes)
    report = {"status": "RUNNING", "candidate": str(candidate), "source": str(source),
              "source_sha256": sha(source_bytes), "extracted_planner_sha256": sha(header.read_bytes()),
              "fallback_plan_sha256": sha(fallback.encode()), "optimized_plan_sha256": sha(optimized_method.encode()),
              "test_harness_sha256": sha(harness_bytes), "inference_executed": False, "gpu_executed": False,
              "performance_scope": "CPU-local planner only; not inference, token throughput, or target-PC evidence",
              "commands": []}
    try:
        report["compiler"] = command([compiler, "--version"], output).splitlines()[0]
        binary = output / "test_moe_plan"
        compile_argv = [compiler, "-std=c++17", "-Wall", "-Wextra", "-Werror", "-O3",
                        "-I", str(output), str(test_source), "-o", str(binary)]
        report["commands"].append(compile_argv)
        command(compile_argv, output)
        run_argv = [str(binary)]
        if args.bench:
            run_argv += ["--bench", str(args.iterations), str(args.repeats)]
            if args.case:
                run_argv.append(",".join(args.case))
        report["commands"].append(run_argv)
        raw = command(run_argv, output, affinity=args.pin_cpu)
        (output / "optimized_stdout.jsonl").write_text(raw)
        samples = [json.loads(line) for line in raw.splitlines() if line]
        report["raw_samples"] = samples
        report["differential"] = [s for s in samples if s["type"] == "differential"]
        if not report["differential"] or any(s["status"] != "PASS" for s in report["differential"]):
            raise RuntimeError("differential test result missing or failed")
        measured_names = {s["case"] for s in samples if s["type"] == "benchmark"}
        if not set(args.case) <= measured_names:
            raise RuntimeError("one or more requested benchmark cases were not measured")
        report["benchmark_summaries"] = []
        for name in sorted(measured_names):
            rows = [s for s in samples if s.get("case") == name]
            ratios = [s["speedup"] for s in rows]
            report["benchmark_summaries"].append({
                "case": name, "samples": len(rows), "iterations_per_sample": rows[0]["iterations"],
                "baseline_median_ns_per_plan": statistics.median(s["baseline_ns_per_plan"] for s in rows),
                "reuse_median_ns_per_plan": statistics.median(s["reuse_ns_per_plan"] for s in rows),
                "paired_speedup_median": statistics.median(ratios), "paired_speedup_min": min(ratios),
                "paired_speedup_max": max(ratios),
                "hit_rate_unique_requests": rows[0]["hits"]/(rows[0]["hits"] + rows[0]["misses"]),
            })
        report["sanitizers"] = {"status": "NOT_RUN"}
        if args.sanitizers:
            binary = output / "test_moe_plan_sanitized"
            sanitize_argv = [compiler, "-std=c++17", "-Wall", "-Wextra", "-Werror", "-O1", "-g",
                             "-fno-omit-frame-pointer", "-fsanitize=address,undefined", "-fno-sanitize-recover=all", "-I", str(output),
                             str(test_source), "-o", str(binary)]
            report["commands"].append(sanitize_argv)
            command(sanitize_argv, output)
            sanitizer_env = None
            if args.disable_leak_check:
                sanitizer_env = dict(os.environ)
                sanitizer_env["ASAN_OPTIONS"] = "detect_leaks=0"
            raw = command([str(binary)], output, affinity=args.pin_cpu, env=sanitizer_env)
            (output / "sanitized_stdout.jsonl").write_text(raw)
            report["sanitizers"] = {"status": "PASS", "results": [json.loads(line) for line in raw.splitlines() if line],
                                    "leak_detection": "DISABLED_EXPLICITLY" if args.disable_leak_check else "ENABLED"}
        report["source_unchanged"] = source.read_bytes() == source_bytes
        if not report["source_unchanged"]:
            raise RuntimeError("candidate source changed during validation")
        report["status"] = "PASS"
    except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as error:
        report["status"] = "FAIL"
        report["error"] = str(error)
    cpuinfo = Path("/proc/cpuinfo")
    try:
        models = sorted({line.split(":", 1)[1].strip() for line in cpuinfo.read_text().splitlines()
                         if line.startswith("model name")}) if cpuinfo.exists() else []
    except OSError:
        models = []
    report["hardware"] = {"machine": platform.machine(), "cpu_models": models, "pinned_cpu": args.pin_cpu}
    result = output / "results.json"
    result.write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n")
    print(json.dumps({"status": report["status"], "results": str(result), "source_sha256": report["source_sha256"],
                      "differential": report.get("differential"), "sanitizers": report.get("sanitizers", {}).get("status"),
                      "benchmark_summaries": report.get("benchmark_summaries", []), "error": report.get("error")}, indent=2))
    return 0 if report["status"] == "PASS" else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        raise SystemExit(main())
    except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as error:
        print(json.dumps({"status": "FAIL", "error": str(error)}), file=sys.stderr)
        raise SystemExit(1)

===== SOURCE FILE tools/test_moe_scheduler.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""P14 exact P04 scheduling oracle and deferred mock GPU upload, host-only."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse, hashlib, json, os, shutil, subprocess, sys, tempfile, time
from pathlib import Path

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

def sha(p:Path):return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def oracle():
    # The ORIGINAL P04 source is immutable; extract its implementation directly.
    patch=(ROOT/'patches/0004-moe-upload-staging.patch').read_text()
    a=patch.index('+struct moe_cache_upload_stats {')
    b=patch.index(' // gate, up, down or gate_up, down',a)
    lines=patch[a:b].splitlines(keepends=True)
    if not all(x.startswith('+') for x in lines):raise RuntimeError('unexpected P04 oracle diff layout')
    return ''.join(x[1:] for x in lines)

def execute(argv,env=None):
    start=time.monotonic()
    try:
        p=subprocess.run(argv,text=True,capture_output=True,env=env,timeout=95)
        return {'returncode':p.returncode,'seconds':round(time.monotonic()-start,3),
                'stdout':p.stdout[-1500:],'stderr':p.stderr[-3000:]}
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        return {'returncode':124,'seconds':round(time.monotonic()-start,3),'stderr':'TIMEOUT'}

def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    ap.add_argument('--output',type=Path)
    ap.add_argument('--sanitizers',action='store_true')
    ap.add_argument('--compiler',default='c++')
    args=ap.parse_args()
    compiler=shutil.which(args.compiler)
    if compiler is None:ap.error('compiler missing')
    if args.output and (args.output.exists() or args.output.resolve().is_relative_to(ROOT)):
        ap.error('output must be new and outside source root')
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='p14-host-') as td:
        out=args.output.resolve() if args.output else Path(td)
        if args.output:out.mkdir(mode=0o700)
        tpl=(ROOT/'tests_cpp/moe_scheduler_fixture.cpp.in').read_text()
        if tpl.count('@@P04_HELPER@@')!=1:raise RuntimeError('missing single oracle template')
        src=out/'moe_scheduler_fixture.cpp'
        src.write_text(tpl.replace('@@P04_HELPER@@',oracle()))
        modes=['plain','sanitizers'] if args.sanitizers else ['plain']
        results=[]
        for mode in modes:
            bin=out/f'moe_scheduler_{mode}'
            cmd=[compiler,'-std=c++17','-Wall','-Wextra','-Werror','-pthread',
                 '-I'+str(ROOT/'modules'),'-O1' if mode=='sanitizers' else '-O2',str(src),'-o',str(bin)]
            if mode=='sanitizers':cmd+=['-g','-fno-omit-frame-pointer','-fsanitize=address,undefined','-fno-sanitize-recover=all']
            step={'mode':mode,'compile':execute(cmd),'runs':[]}
            if step['compile']['returncode']==0:
                for sch in (False,True):
                    for persistent in (False,True):
                        env=dict(os.environ)
                        env['LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_SCHEDULER']='1' if sch else '0'
                        env['LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_PERSISTENT']='1' if persistent else '0'
                        if mode=='sanitizers':
                            env['ASAN_OPTIONS']='detect_leaks=0:halt_on_error=1'
                            env['UBSAN_OPTIONS']='halt_on_error=1'
                        step['runs'].append({'scheduler':sch,'persistent':persistent,
                                             **execute([str(bin),'enabled' if sch else 'disabled'],env)})
            results.append(step)
            if step['compile']['returncode']!=0 or any(x['returncode']!=0 for x in step['runs']):break
        passed=len(results)==len(modes) and all(x['compile']['returncode']==0 and
                     len(x['runs'])==4 and all(y['returncode']==0 for y in x['runs']) for x in results)
        report={'schema':'llama.experimental.p14.host.v1','status':'PASS' if passed else 'FAIL',
                'p04_oracle_sha256':sha(ROOT/'patches/0004-moe-upload-staging.patch'),
                'scheduler_sha256':sha(ROOT/'modules/moe/upload_scheduler.hpp'),
                'fixture_sha256':sha(src),'cases_per_run':2400,
                'scope':'host-only P04 exact byte/staging estimates, mock deferred backend, no CUDA/device events',
                'steps':results}
        if args.output:(out/'result.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
        print(json.dumps({'status':report['status'],'scope':report['scope'],
                          'steps':[{ 'mode':x['mode'],'compiled':x['compile']['returncode']==0,
                                     'runs':[(r['scheduler'],r['persistent'],r['returncode']) for r in x['runs']]}
                                      for x in results]},ensure_ascii=False))
        return 0 if passed else 1

if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/test_moe_scheduler_bridge.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Compile exact P04/P09B/P12/P14A/P14B source snippet against mock events."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse, hashlib, json, os, shutil, subprocess, sys, tempfile, time
from pathlib import Path

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
def sha(p):return hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def run(argv,env=None):
    t=time.monotonic()
    try:
        p=subprocess.run(argv,stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=90,env=env)
        return {'returncode':p.returncode,'seconds':round(time.monotonic()-t,3),
                'stdout':p.stdout[-1000:],'stderr':p.stderr[-3500:]}
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        return {'returncode':124,'seconds':round(time.monotonic()-t,3),'stderr':'TIMEOUT'}

def extract(src,start,stop):
    if src.count(start)!=1:raise ValueError('missing/duplicate exact source anchor: '+start[:65])
    a=src.index(start);b=src.find(stop,a+len(start))
    if b<=a:raise ValueError('end anchor missing: '+stop[:65])
    return src[a:b]

def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('--candidate',type=Path,required=True)
    p.add_argument('--output',type=Path)
    p.add_argument('--sanitizers',action='store_true')
    p.add_argument('--compiler',default='c++')
    args=p.parse_args()
    cand=args.candidate.resolve()
    compiler=shutil.which(args.compiler)
    if not cand.is_dir() or not compiler:p.error('candidate/host compiler missing')
    if args.output and (args.output.exists() or args.output.resolve().is_relative_to(cand) or
                       args.output.resolve().is_relative_to(ROOT)):
        p.error('output must be new and outside source/candidate')
    m=json.loads((ROOT/'manifest.json').read_text())
    target_path='src/llama-moe-cache.cpp'
    hashes=[entry['after_sha256'] for patch in m['patches'] for entry in patch['targets']
            if entry['path']==target_path]
    target=cand/target_path
    if not hashes or not target.is_file() or sha(target)!=hashes[-1]:
        p.error('target source not the exact final MoE postimage')
    for vendor in ('upload_pipeline.hpp','upload_worker.hpp','upload_types.hpp',
                   'upload_plan.hpp','upload_scheduler.hpp','upload_completion.hpp'):
        local=cand/'src/llama-experimental'/vendor
        original=ROOT/'modules/moe'/vendor
        if not local.is_file() or sha(local)!=sha(original):p.error('vendor header missing/tampered: '+vendor)
    src=target.read_text()
    helper=extract(src,'struct moe_cache_upload_stats {','// gate, up, down or gate_up, down')
    snippet=extract(src,'        std::optional<llama_exp::moe_fill_plan> shared_plan;',
                         '        stats & st = ids.size()')
    if snippet.count('moe_upload_scheduler::assess(')!=1 or snippet.count('moe_upload_pipeline::execute(')!=1 or \
            snippet.count('moe_upload_abort_scope<')!=1:
        p.error('P14 or P09B integration anchor missing')
    record=extract(src,'        // P14B: event is queued AFTER the final slot-map write',
                        '        if (trace.enabled) {')
    if record.count('completion->record_after_slot_map()')!=1 or record.count('abort_completion.commit()')!=1:
        p.error('P14B event record/commit pair missing')
    recovery=extract(src,'        if (!g.upload_complete) {','        size_t n_hit = 0;')
    commit=extract(src,'        g.upload_complete = true;', '        return true;')
    tpl=(ROOT/'tests_cpp/moe_scheduler_bridge_fixture.cpp.in').read_text()
    if (tpl.count('@@P04_HELPER@@')!=1 or tpl.count('@@P09B_COPY_BLOCK@@')!=1 or \
            tpl.count('@@P14B_RECORD_BLOCK@@')!=1):
        p.error('invalid P14B mock fixture markers')
    tpl=tpl.replace('@@RECOVERY@@',recovery).replace('@@COMMIT@@',commit)
    code=tpl.replace('@@P04_HELPER@@',helper).replace('@@P09B_COPY_BLOCK@@',snippet).replace('@@P14B_RECORD_BLOCK@@',record)
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='llama-p14-bridge-') as td:
        out=args.output.resolve() if args.output else Path(td)
        if args.output:out.mkdir(mode=0o700)
        cpp=out/'moe_scheduler_bridge.cpp';cpp.write_text(code)
        steps=[]
        for mode in ('plain','sanitizers') if args.sanitizers else ('plain',):
            exe=out/f'moe_bridge_{mode}'
            argv=[compiler,'-std=c++17','-Wall','-Wextra','-Werror','-pthread',
                  '-I'+str(cand/'src'),'-O0' if mode=='sanitizers' else '-O2',str(cpp),'-o',str(exe)]
            if mode=='sanitizers':argv+=['-g','-fno-omit-frame-pointer',
               '-fsanitize=address,undefined','-fno-sanitize-recover=all']
            item={'mode':mode,'compile':run(argv),'runs':[]}
            if item['compile']['returncode']==0:
                for pipeline in (False,True):
                    for shared in (False,True):
                        for scheduler in (False,True):
                            for persistent in (False,True):
                                for events in (False,True):
                                    env=dict(os.environ)
                                    env['LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_PIPELINE']='1' if pipeline else '0'
                                    env['LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_SHARED_PLAN']='1' if shared else '0'
                                    env['LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_SCHEDULER']='1' if scheduler else '0'
                                    env['LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_PERSISTENT']='1' if persistent else '0'
                                    env['LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_EVENT_FENCE']='1' if events else '0'
                                    if mode=='sanitizers':
                                        env['ASAN_OPTIONS']='detect_leaks=0:halt_on_error=1'
                                        env['UBSAN_OPTIONS']='halt_on_error=1'
                                    item['runs'].append({'profile':{'pipeline':pipeline,'shared':shared,'scheduler':scheduler,'persistent':persistent,'events':events},
                                        **run([str(exe),'enabled' if pipeline else 'disabled',
                                                'shared' if shared else 'baseline',
                                                'scheduler' if scheduler else 'no-scheduler','event-on' if events else 'event-off'],env)})
            steps.append(item)
            if item['compile']['returncode'] or any(x['returncode'] for x in item['runs']):break
        passed=len(steps)==(2 if args.sanitizers else 1) and all(
            x['compile']['returncode']==0 and len(x['runs'])==32 and
            all(y['returncode']==0 for y in x['runs']) for x in steps)
        report={'schema':'llama.experimental.p14b.bridge.v1','status':'PASS' if passed else 'FAIL',
            'scope':'exact source-extracted P14A/P14B source + host events; not full TU, CUDA or GPU',
            'target_sha256':sha(target),'generated_sha256':sha(cpp),'steps':steps}
        if args.output:(out/'result.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
        print(json.dumps({'status':report['status'],'target_sha256':report['target_sha256'],
                          'runs':[{'mode':x['mode'],'compile':x['compile']['returncode'],
                          'passing_runs':sum(y['returncode']==0 for y in x['runs']),
                          'failed':[{'profile':y['profile'],'error':y.get('stderr','')[-350:]}
                                    for y in x['runs'] if y['returncode']!=0]} for x in steps]},ensure_ascii=False))
        return 0 if passed else 1

if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/test_moe_staging.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Test actual P04 byte packing with deferred-read and backend-capability fixtures.

The candidate checkout is read only. The generated helper test uses no model,
GPU, CUDA library or service. A helper-test pass is not inference validation.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time


def sha256(data: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()


def extract_helpers(text: str) -> str:
    begin = "struct moe_cache_slru {"
    end = "// gate, up, down or gate_up, down"
    anchors = (begin, end, "static size_t llama_moe_cache_staging_limit(",
               "static ggml_backend_buffer_ptr llama_moe_cache_alloc_staging(",
               "static moe_cache_upload_stats llama_moe_cache_upload_experts(")
    for anchor in anchors:
        if text.count(anchor) != 1:
            raise ValueError("Candidate source anchor is absent or ambiguous: " + anchor)
    first, last = text.index(begin), text.index(end)
    if last <= first:
        raise ValueError("Unexpected candidate helper ordering")
    # The later P12 wrapper follows the original P04 template.
    wrapper = text.find("// P12: prefer a single immutable group layout", first, last)
    return text[first:wrapper if wrapper >= 0 else last]


def command(argv: list[str], *, env: dict | None = None) -> dict:
    began = time.monotonic()
    result = subprocess.run(argv, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, capture_output=True,
                            text=True, timeout=120, check=False, env=env)
    return {"command": argv, "exit_code": result.returncode,
            "elapsed_seconds": round(time.monotonic() - began, 3),
            "stdout": result.stdout, "stderr": result.stderr}


def run(args, output: Path, source: bytes, helpers: str, compiler: str) -> int:
    fixture = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "tests_cpp/moe_staging_fixture.cpp"
    fixture_bytes = fixture.read_bytes()
    template = fixture_bytes.decode("utf-8")
    anchor = "// @ACTUAL_STAGING_HELPERS@"
    if template.count(anchor) != 1:
        raise ValueError("Fixture helper anchor is absent or ambiguous")
    test_source = output / "moe_staging_generated.cpp"
    test_source.write_text(template.replace(anchor, helpers))
    executable = output / "moe_staging_test"
    version = command([compiler, "--version"])
    flags = ["-std=c++17", "-O2", "-Wall", "-Wextra", "-Werror"]
    compilation = command([compiler, *flags, str(test_source), "-o", str(executable)])
    execution = command([str(executable)]) if compilation["exit_code"] == 0 else None
    normal_pass = execution is not None and execution["exit_code"] == 0
    sanitizer = {"status": "NOT_RUN"}
    if normal_pass and args.sanitizers:
        sanitized = output / "moe_staging_test_sanitized"
        sanitize_flags = ["-std=c++17", "-O1", "-g", "-Wall", "-Wextra", "-Werror",
                          "-fsanitize=address,undefined", "-fno-sanitize-recover=all", "-fno-omit-frame-pointer"]
        sanitize_compile = command([compiler, *sanitize_flags, str(test_source), "-o", str(sanitized)])
        env = dict(os.environ)
        if args.disable_leak_check:
            env["ASAN_OPTIONS"] = "detect_leaks=0"
        sanitize_run = command([str(sanitized)], env=env) if sanitize_compile["exit_code"] == 0 else None
        sanitizer = {
            "status": "PASS" if sanitize_run and sanitize_run["exit_code"] == 0 else "FAIL",
            "compile": sanitize_compile, "execution": sanitize_run,
            "leak_check_explicitly_disabled": args.disable_leak_check,
            "options": {key: env.get(key) for key in ("ASAN_OPTIONS", "LSAN_OPTIONS", "UBSAN_OPTIONS")}}
    passed = normal_pass and sanitizer["status"] != "FAIL"
    report = {
        "schema_version": 1, "status": "PASS" if passed else "FAIL",
        "scope": "Actual production helpers; CPU byte equality and deferred-read lifetime; mock backend capability/fallback. No GPU, inference, linked llama or transfer timing.",
        "candidate_source": str(args.candidate.resolve() / "src/llama-moe-cache.cpp"),
        "candidate_source_sha256": sha256(source),
        "extracted_helpers_sha256": sha256(helpers.encode()),
        "fixture_sha256": sha256(fixture_bytes),
        "test_tool_sha256": sha256(Path(__file__).read_bytes()),
        "compiler_version": version,
        "compile": compilation, "execution": execution, "sanitizers": sanitizer,
        "gpu_executed": False, "inference_executed": False,
        "performance_claim": None}
    (output / "result.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2) + "\n")
    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
    return 0 if passed else 1


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--candidate", type=Path, required=True, help="Candidate llama source checkout")
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, help="New directory; omitted uses a temporary directory")
    parser.add_argument("--compiler", default="c++")
    parser.add_argument("--sanitizers", action="store_true", help="Also run AddressSanitizer and UndefinedBehaviorSanitizer")
    parser.add_argument("--disable-leak-check", action="store_true", help="Explicitly disable LeakSanitizer if the environment prevents /proc thread inspection")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.disable_leak_check and not args.sanitizers:
        parser.error("--disable-leak-check requires --sanitizers")
    try:
        args.candidate = args.candidate.resolve(strict=True)
        if args.output:
            output = args.output.resolve()
            if output.is_relative_to(args.candidate):
                raise ValueError("--output must be outside the candidate source checkout")
            if args.output.exists() or args.output.is_symlink():
                parser.error("--output must not already exist")
        else:
            # Reject configured source-local parents before gettempdir probes
            # them for writability, as well as before TemporaryDirectory.
            for name in ("TMPDIR", "TEMP", "TMP"):
                value = os.environ.get(name)
                if value and Path(value).resolve().is_relative_to(args.candidate):
                    raise ValueError("Temporary output must be outside the candidate source checkout")
            temporary_parent = Path(tempfile.gettempdir()).resolve(strict=True)
            if temporary_parent.is_relative_to(args.candidate):
                raise ValueError("Temporary output must be outside the candidate source checkout")
        compiler = shutil.which(args.compiler)
        if compiler is None:
            parser.error("C++ compiler is unavailable")
        source = (args.candidate / "src/llama-moe-cache.cpp").read_bytes()
        helpers = extract_helpers(source.decode("utf-8"))
        if args.output:
            output.mkdir(parents=False, exist_ok=False)
            return run(args, output, source, helpers, compiler)
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="llama-moe-staging-", dir=temporary_parent) as temporary:
            return run(args, Path(temporary), source, helpers, compiler)
    except (OSError, ValueError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as error:
        print(json.dumps({"status": "ERROR", "message": str(error)}))
        return 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/test_moe_upload_completion.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""P14B host-only completion event lifetime and fail-closed tests.

No CUDA device is used: the fake backend delays each H2D source read until
backend/event synchronize; the real ggml integration requires native build.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

def sha(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def run(args, env=None):
    start=time.monotonic()
    try:
        p=subprocess.run(args, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
                         stderr=subprocess.PIPE,text=True,timeout=40,env=env)
        return {'rc':p.returncode,'seconds':round(time.monotonic()-start,3),
                'out':p.stdout[-700:],'err':p.stderr[-900:]}
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        return {'rc':124,'seconds':round(time.monotonic()-start,3),'err':'TIMEOUT'}

def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('--output',type=Path)
    p.add_argument('--compiler',default='c++')
    p.add_argument('--sanitizers',action='store_true')
    args=p.parse_args()
    compiler=shutil.which(args.compiler)
    if not compiler:p.error('C++ compiler unavailable')
    if args.output and (args.output.exists() or args.output.resolve().is_relative_to(ROOT)):
        p.error('output must be a new directory outside source')
    src=ROOT/'tests_cpp/moe_upload_completion_fixture.cpp'
    if not src.is_file() or not (ROOT/'modules/moe/upload_completion.hpp').is_file():
        p.error('P14B source files missing')
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='llama-p14b-') as td:
        output=args.output.resolve() if args.output else Path(td)
        if args.output:output.mkdir(mode=0o700)
        report={'schema':'llama.experimental.p14b.host.v1','status':'FAIL',
          'scope':'host-only fake backend events + deferred DMA; not CUDA compile/runtime/performance',
          'fixture_sha256':sha(src),'header_sha256':sha(ROOT/'modules/moe/upload_completion.hpp'),
          'runs':[]}
        for mode in (['plain','sanitizers'] if args.sanitizers else ['plain']):
            exe=output/('completion_'+mode)
            cmd=[compiler,'-std=c++17','-Wall','-Wextra','-Werror','-pthread',
                 '-O1' if mode=='sanitizers' else '-O2',str(src),'-o',str(exe)]
            if mode=='sanitizers':cmd+=['-g','-fno-omit-frame-pointer',
                         '-fsanitize=address,undefined','-fno-sanitize-recover=all']
            step={'mode':mode,'compile':run(cmd),'cases':[]}
            if step['compile']['rc']==0:
                for flag in ('', '0', '1', 'true', '11'):
                    env=dict(os.environ)
                    if flag:env['LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_EVENT_FENCE']=flag
                    else:env.pop('LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_EVENT_FENCE',None)
                    if mode=='sanitizers':
                        env['ASAN_OPTIONS']='detect_leaks=0:halt_on_error=1'
                        env['UBSAN_OPTIONS']='halt_on_error=1'
                    step['cases'].append({'flag':flag or '<unset>',**run([str(exe)],env)})
                if mode=='plain':
                    check=run([str(exe),'--unclosed'],env)
                    step['fail_closed_unreleased_event']={'rc':check['rc'],
                        'expected_nonzero':True,'passed':check['rc'] not in (0,124)}
            report['runs'].append(step)
            if step['compile']['rc'] or any(c['rc'] for c in step['cases']):break
        report['status']='PASS' if len(report['runs'])==(2 if args.sanitizers else 1) and all(
            x['compile']['rc']==0 and len(x['cases'])==5 and
            all(y['rc']==0 for y in x['cases']) and
            (x['mode']!='plain' or x['fail_closed_unreleased_event']['passed'])
            for x in report['runs']) else 'FAIL'
        if args.output:(output/'result.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2)+'\n')
        print(json.dumps({'status':report['status'],'modes':[
          {'mode':s['mode'],'compile':s['compile']['rc'],'runs':[c['rc'] for c in s['cases']]}
          for s in report['runs']]}))
        return 0 if report['status']=='PASS' else 1

if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/test_moe_worker.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""P10 host-only: compile persistent worker and original P09B caller path.

Never starts llama.cpp, CUDA, GPUs, servers, or modifies source trees.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def sha(p: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def call(cmd, env=None):
    t = time.monotonic()
    p = subprocess.run(cmd, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, capture_output=True,
                       text=True, env=env, timeout=75)
    return {'cmd':cmd,'exit_code':p.returncode,'seconds':round(time.monotonic()-t,3),
            'stdout':p.stdout,'stderr':p.stderr}


def run(source: Path, output: Path, compiler: str, sanitizers: bool) -> int:
    tracked = ['modules/moe/upload_types.hpp', 'modules/moe/upload_worker.hpp',
               'modules/moe/upload_pipeline.hpp',
               'tests_cpp/moe_upload_worker_fixture.cpp',
               'tests_cpp/moe_upload_pipeline_fixture.cpp']
    before = {k:sha(source/k) for k in tracked}
    steps = []
    for fixture in ('moe_upload_worker_fixture.cpp', 'moe_upload_pipeline_fixture.cpp'):
        for sanitized in ([False, True] if sanitizers else [False]):
            exe = output/(fixture.removesuffix('.cpp')+('_san' if sanitized else '_plain'))
            cmd=[compiler,'-std=c++17','-Wall','-Wextra','-Werror','-pedantic',
                 '-pthread','-I'+str(source),'-I'+str(source/'modules'),
                 str(source/'tests_cpp'/fixture),'-o',str(exe)]
            if sanitized:
                cmd += ['-O1','-g','-fno-omit-frame-pointer',
                        '-fsanitize=address,undefined','-fno-sanitize-recover=all']
            else: cmd += ['-O2']
            steps.append(call(cmd))
            if steps[-1]['exit_code']: break
            # Check compatibility of the same P09B call-site in both modes.
            for mode in ('old', 'persistent'):
                env=dict(os.environ)
                if mode=='persistent':env['LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_PERSISTENT']='1'
                else:env.pop('LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_PERSISTENT',None)
                if sanitized:
                    env['ASAN_OPTIONS']='detect_leaks=0:halt_on_error=1'
                    env['UBSAN_OPTIONS']='halt_on_error=1'
                steps.append(call([str(exe)],env))
                if steps[-1]['exit_code']: break
            if any(x['exit_code'] for x in steps):break
        if any(x['exit_code'] for x in steps):break
    after = {k:sha(source/k) for k in tracked}
    ok=(before==after and all(x['exit_code']==0 for x in steps) and
        len(steps)==(12 if sanitizers else 6))
    result={'schema':'llama.experimental.p10.persistent_host.v1',
            'status':'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL','sanitizers':sanitizers,
