            'source_files_unchanged':before==after,'hashes':before,'steps':steps,
            'scope':'C++17 host only; no full llama.cpp TU/CUDA, no runtime/gpu-speed claims',
            'not_run':['real PCIe DMA','long context model parity','GPU profiling']}
    (output/'result.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2)+'\n')
    print(json.dumps({'status':result['status'],'checks':len(steps)}))
    return 0 if ok else 1


def main():
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--source-root',type=Path,default=ROOT)
    parser.add_argument('--output',type=Path)
    parser.add_argument('--compiler',default='c++')
    parser.add_argument('--sanitizers',action='store_true')
    args=parser.parse_args()
    try:
        source=args.source_root.resolve(strict=True)
        compiler=shutil.which(args.compiler)
        if not compiler: raise ValueError('compiler missing')
        if args.output is not None:
            dest=args.output.resolve()
            if dest.exists() or dest.is_symlink() or dest.is_relative_to(source):
                raise ValueError('output must not exist and must be outside source root')
            if not dest.parent.is_dir(): raise ValueError('output parent does not exist')
            dest.mkdir(mode=0o700)
            return run(source,dest,compiler,args.sanitizers)
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='llama-exp-p10-') as d:
            return run(source,Path(d),compiler,args.sanitizers)
    except (OSError,ValueError,subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as exc:
        print(json.dumps({'status':'ERROR','reason':str(exc)}))
        return 2

if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/test_moe_worker_bridge.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""P10 source-regression bridge: reconstitute unchanged P04/P09B patched hunks.

This is a host-only deferred-backend test of the EXACT earlier patch text,
compiled against the P10 vendor module. Does not fetch or compile full upstream.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile

from test_moe_pipeline_bridge import run_fixture

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def after_hunks(patch: Path) -> str:
    lines = []
    in_hunk = False
    for line in patch.read_text().splitlines():
        if line.startswith('@@ '):
            in_hunk = True
        elif line.startswith('diff --git '):
            in_hunk = False
        elif in_hunk and line and line[0] in ('+', ' '):
            lines.append(line[1:])
    return '\n'.join(lines)+'\n'


def extract(data: str, first: str, last: str):
    if data.count(first)!=1 or data.count(last)!=1:
        raise ValueError('required source anchor absent or repeated')
    i=data.index(first);j=data.index(last,i+len(first))
    if j<=i:raise ValueError('required source anchor order invalid')
    return data[i:j]


def main() -> int:
    try:
        patch4=ROOT/'patches/0004-moe-upload-staging.patch'
        patch9=ROOT/'patches/0008-moe-async-upload-pipeline-integration.patch'
        manifest=json.loads((ROOT/'manifest.json').read_text())
        hashes={p['file']:p['sha256'] for p in manifest['patches']}
        for patch in (patch4,patch9):
            if hashlib.sha256(patch.read_bytes()).hexdigest()!=hashes[patch.name]:
                raise ValueError('prior patch byte integrity failed')
        p04=extract(after_hunks(patch4),'struct moe_cache_upload_stats {',
                    '// gate, up, down or gate_up, down')
        p09=extract(after_hunks(patch9),
                    '        // P09B: the producer only prepares disjoint host upload slices.',
                    '        stats & st = ids.size()')
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='llama-exp-p10-source-') as td:
            td=Path(td);candidate=td/'mock_candidate';src=candidate/'src';src.mkdir(parents=True)
            hdr=src/'llama-experimental';hdr.mkdir()
            for name in ('upload_types.hpp','upload_worker.hpp','upload_pipeline.hpp','upload_plan.hpp'):
                expected=ROOT/'modules/moe'/name
                records=[x for x in manifest['additional_files'] if x['source']=='modules/moe/'+name]
                if len(records)!=1 or hashlib.sha256(expected.read_bytes()).hexdigest()!=records[0]['sha256']:
                    raise ValueError('vendor file not manifest verified: '+name)
                shutil.copyfile(expected,hdr/name)
            output=td/'test_result'
            output.mkdir()
            os.environ['LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MOE_PERSISTENT']='1'
            rc=run_fixture(candidate,output,'c++',True,p04,p09,
                           hashlib.sha256((p04+p09).encode()).hexdigest(),
                           'P04/P09B patch hunks with persistent worker')
            if rc:
                return rc
            report=json.loads((td/'test_result/result.json').read_text())
            if report['status']!='PASS' or not report['sanitizers']:
                raise ValueError('source bridge sanitizer failure')
            print(json.dumps({'status':'PASS','scope':'P04+P09B unchanged patch hunks + P10 persistent vendor',
                'mock_backend':report['status'],'host_sanitizers':True,'steps':len(report['steps']),
                'actual_full_llama_cpp_compilation':False}))
        return 0
    except (OSError,ValueError,KeyError,subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as exc:
        print(json.dumps({'status':'ERROR','reason':str(exc)}))
        return 2

if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/test_p15_hc_post.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Compile host-executed CUDA kernel math against the pinned upstream kernel.

This is not a CUDA, nvcc, model or performance test.  Builds fixture from the
actual R14 candidate modules and an exact upstream function body fixture.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
REF=ROOT/'tests_cpp/hc_post_reference_original.hpp'
FIXTURE=ROOT/'tests_cpp/hc_post_reuse_fixture.cpp'
HEADER=ROOT/'modules/cuda/hc_post_reuse.cuh'

def execute(out: Path, compiler: str, sanitizer: bool)->int:
    binary=out/'hc_post_reuse_host'
    cmd=[compiler,'-std=c++17','-O1','-g','-Wall','-Wextra','-Werror',
         '-Wno-unknown-pragmas','-ffp-contract=off','-fno-fast-math']
    if sanitizer:
        cmd+=['-fsanitize=address,undefined','-fno-sanitize-recover=all',
              '-fno-omit-frame-pointer']
    cmd+=[str(FIXTURE),'-o',str(binary)]
    env=os.environ.copy()
    if sanitizer:
        env['ASAN_OPTIONS']=env.get('ASAN_OPTIONS','')+':detect_leaks=0'
    reports=[]
    for name,args in [('compile',cmd),('run',[str(binary)])]:
        try:
            p=subprocess.run(args,stdout=subprocess.PIPE,stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
                             text=True,timeout=90,env=env)
            reports.append({'name':name,'returncode':p.returncode,'stdout':p.stdout,'stderr':p.stderr})
            if p.returncode != 0: break
        except (OSError,subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as e:
            reports.append({'name':name,'error':str(e)})
            break
    ok=len(reports)==2 and all(x.get('returncode')==0 for x in reports)
    result={'status':'PASS_HOST_PARITY_ONLY' if ok else 'FAIL',
            'sanitizers':sanitizer,'reports':reports,
            'sha256':{str(p.relative_to(ROOT)):hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
                      for p in (REF,FIXTURE,HEADER)},
            'not_tested':['NVCC and GPU device','CUDA graph behavior','model logits',
                          'real HC_POST performance']}
    (out/'result.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2)+'\n')
    print(json.dumps(result,indent=2))
    return 0 if ok else 1

def main():
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--sanitizers',action='store_true')
    parser.add_argument('--compiler',default='c++')
    parser.add_argument('--output',type=Path)
    a=parser.parse_args()
    if a.output:
        path=a.output.resolve()
        if path.exists() or ROOT==path or ROOT in path.parents:
            parser.error('output directory must not exist and must be outside the source package')
        path.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=False)
        return execute(path,a.compiler,a.sanitizers)
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='llama-p15-') as td:
        return execute(Path(td),a.compiler,a.sanitizers)

if __name__=='__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/test_p15b_mmq_tile.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""P15B source-extracted exact tile-selection parity test (HOST ONLY).

Reconstructs candidate mmq.cu from pinned upstream via the real patch,
extracts the original MMQ J solver and P15B wrapper for a C++17 mock.
It DOES NOT compile nvcc or infer actual CUDA throughput.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
SOURCE=ROOT/'validation/p15b_upstream_mmq.cu'
PATCH=ROOT/'patches/0014-cuda-mmq-moe-tile-cache.patch'
HEADER=ROOT/'modules/cuda/mmq_tile_cache.hpp'
TEMPLATE=ROOT/'tests_cpp/mmq_tile_cache_fixture.cpp.in'
UPSTREAM_BLOB='49e0ce9050c021f4ffa03924f7bc510f4a96cc41'

def sha(p:Path)->str:return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def run(cmd, *, cwd=None,env=None,timeout=120):
    result=subprocess.run(cmd,cwd=cwd,env=env,stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
                          stderr=subprocess.PIPE,text=True,timeout=timeout)
    if result.returncode:
        raise RuntimeError('failed: '+str(cmd)+'\n'+result.stdout+'\n'+result.stderr)
    return result.stdout.strip()

def execute(path:Path,compiler:str,sanitize:bool):
    manifest=json.loads((ROOT/'manifest.json').read_text())
    entry=manifest['patches'][13]
    if entry['file']!=PATCH.name or entry['targets'][0]['before_sha256']!=sha(SOURCE):
        raise RuntimeError('Pinned source does not match manifest')
    if sha(PATCH)!=entry['sha256'] or sha(HEADER)!=manifest['additional_files'][11]['sha256']:
        raise RuntimeError('Patched sources do not match manifest')
    if run(['git','hash-object',str(SOURCE)])!=UPSTREAM_BLOB:
        raise RuntimeError('Pinned git blob differs from upstream')
    src=path/'ggml/src/ggml-cuda/mmq.cu'
    src.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
    src.write_bytes(SOURCE.read_bytes())
    run(['git','apply','--check','--whitespace=error-all',str(PATCH)],cwd=path)
    run(['git','apply','--whitespace=error-all',str(PATCH)],cwd=path)
    if sha(src)!=entry['targets'][0]['after_sha256']:
        raise RuntimeError('Candidate postimage SHA mismatch')
    original=SOURCE.read_text()
    candidate=src.read_text()
    begin='static int ggml_cuda_mmq_get_J_best('
    end='\nvoid ggml_cuda_mul_mat_q('
    assert original.count(begin)==1 and original.count(end)==1
    original_solver=original[original.index(begin):original.index(end)]
    before='static bool ggml_cuda_mmq_moe_tile_cache_type('
    assert candidate.count(before)==1
    cached_wrapper=candidate[candidate.index(before):candidate.index(end)]
    template=TEMPLATE.read_text()
    assert template.count('// ORIGINAL_MMq_SOLVER')==1
    assert template.count('// P15B_HELPERS')==1
    generated=(template.replace('// ORIGINAL_MMq_SOLVER',original_solver)
                .replace('// P15B_HELPERS',cached_wrapper))
    cpp=path/'candidate_parity.cpp';cpp.write_text(generated)
    binary=path/'candidate_parity'
    cmd=[compiler,'-std=c++17','-O1','-g','-Wall','-Wextra','-Werror','-pthread',
         '-I',str(HEADER.parent)]
    if sanitize:
        cmd+=['-fsanitize=address,undefined','-fno-sanitize-recover=all',
              '-fno-omit-frame-pointer']
    cmd += [str(cpp),'-o',str(binary)]
    run(cmd,timeout=120)
    reports={}
    for name,flag in [('off','0'),('on','1'),('invalid','1more')]:
        env=os.environ.copy();env['LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_CUDA_MMQ_TILE_CACHE']=flag
        if sanitize:
            env['ASAN_OPTIONS']='detect_leaks=0:halt_on_error=1'
            env['UBSAN_OPTIONS']='halt_on_error=1'
        reports[name]=run([str(binary)],env=env,timeout=120)
    assert 'cache_enabled=0' in reports['off']
    assert 'cache_enabled=1' in reports['on']
    assert 'cache_enabled=0' in reports['invalid']
    run(['git','apply','--reverse','--check',str(PATCH)],cwd=path)
    run(['git','apply','--reverse',str(PATCH)],cwd=path)
    if src.read_bytes()!=SOURCE.read_bytes():
        raise RuntimeError('Reverse did not restore byte-exact source')
    report={'status':'PASS_HOST_SOURCE_EXTRACTED',
      'sanitizers':sanitize,'upstream_blob':UPSTREAM_BLOB,
      'upstream_sha256':sha(SOURCE),'patch_sha256':sha(PATCH),
      'candidate_sha256':entry['targets'][0]['after_sha256'],
      'results':reports,
      'not_tested':['NVCC compilation/link','GPU kernel behavior','RTX 5090 latency',
                    'GGUF logits/model parity','actual MMQ tile-selection performance']}
    (path/'result.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2)+'\n')
    print(json.dumps(report,indent=2))

def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('--sanitizers',action='store_true')
    p.add_argument('--compiler',default='c++')
    p.add_argument('--output',type=Path)
    a=p.parse_args()
    if a.output:
        target=a.output.resolve()
        if target.exists() or target==ROOT or ROOT in target.parents or not target.parent.is_dir():
            p.error('--output must be a new directory outside the source package')
        target.mkdir(mode=0o700)
        execute(target,a.compiler,a.sanitizers)
    else:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='llama-p15b-') as tmp:
            execute(Path(tmp),a.compiler,a.sanitizers)

if __name__=='__main__':main()

===== SOURCE FILE tools/test_p15c_mmq_probe.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""P15C host-only source-extracted probe/dispatch and JSONL reporting tests.

Does NOT compile nvcc, execute GPU code, or measure MMQ kernel time.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
BASE=ROOT/'validation/p15b_upstream_mmq.cu'
PATCH_B=ROOT/'patches/0014-cuda-mmq-moe-tile-cache.patch'
PATCH_C=ROOT/'patches/0015-cuda-mmq-selection-host-probe.patch'
TEMPLATE=ROOT/'tests_cpp/mmq_tile_cache_fixture.cpp.in'
MODULE=ROOT/'modules/cuda/mmq_select_probe.hpp'
HEADER_B=ROOT/'modules/cuda/mmq_tile_cache.hpp'
FIXTURE=ROOT/'tests_cpp/mmq_select_probe_fixture.cpp'


def sha(path:Path)->str:
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def invoke(argv, *, cwd=None, env=None, timeout=180):
    p=subprocess.run(argv,cwd=cwd,env=env,stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
                     stderr=subprocess.PIPE,text=True,timeout=timeout)
    if p.returncode:
        raise RuntimeError(f"return={p.returncode} command={argv}\nstdout:{p.stdout[-2000:]}\nstderr:{p.stderr[-3000:]}")
    return p


def cxx(src:Path, out:Path, *, compiler:str, sanitize:bool):
    cmd=[compiler,'-std=c++17','-O1','-g','-Wall','-Wextra','-Werror',
         '-pthread','-I',str(MODULE.parent)]
    if sanitize:
        cmd+=['-fsanitize=address,undefined','-fno-omit-frame-pointer',
              '-fno-sanitize-recover=all']
    return invoke([*cmd,str(src),'-o',str(out)])


def run_case(binary:Path, env_flags:dict, args:list[str]=None):
    env=os.environ.copy()
    env.update(env_flags)
    env['ASAN_OPTIONS']='detect_leaks=0:halt_on_error=1'
    env['UBSAN_OPTIONS']='halt_on_error=1'
    return invoke([str(binary),*(args or [])],env=env)


def report(log:str)->dict:
    import sys
    sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT/'tools'))
    try:
        from analyze_p15c_trace import accumulate
        return accumulate(log.splitlines())
    finally:
        sys.path.remove(str(ROOT/'tools'))


def execute(work:Path, compiler:str, sanitize:bool):
    manifest=json.loads((ROOT/'manifest.json').read_text())
    assert manifest['schema_version'] == 1
    p14,p15=manifest['patches'][13:15]
    assert p14['file']==PATCH_B.name and p15['file']==PATCH_C.name
    assert sha(BASE)==p14['targets'][0]['before_sha256']
    assert sha(PATCH_B)==p14['sha256'] and sha(PATCH_C)==p15['sha256']
    assert sha(HEADER_B)==manifest['additional_files'][11]['sha256']
    assert sha(MODULE)==manifest['additional_files'][12]['sha256']
    assert invoke(['git','hash-object',str(BASE)]).stdout.strip()=='49e0ce9050c021f4ffa03924f7bc510f4a96cc41'
    source=work/'ggml/src/ggml-cuda/mmq.cu'
    source.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    source.write_bytes(BASE.read_bytes())
    for patch,entry in [(PATCH_B,p14),(PATCH_C,p15)]:
        invoke(['git','apply','--check','--whitespace=error-all',str(patch)],cwd=work)
        invoke(['git','apply','--whitespace=error-all',str(patch)],cwd=work)
        assert sha(source)==entry['targets'][0]['after_sha256'],patch.name
    full=source.read_text()
    pinned=BASE.read_text()
    begin='static int ggml_cuda_mmq_get_J_best('
    helper='static bool ggml_cuda_mmq_moe_tile_cache_type('
    end='\nvoid ggml_cuda_mul_mat_q('
    assert all(x in full and x in pinned if x==begin else x in full for x in (begin,helper,end))
    original_solver=pinned[pinned.index(begin):pinned.index(end)]
    helper_source=full[full.index(helper):full.index(end)]
    glue_start='        // P15C: CPU-side selection timing only, NOT CUDA execution timing.'
    glue_end='    }\n    GGML_ASSERT(J_best > 0);'
    assert full.count(glue_start)==1 and full.count(glue_end)==1
    glue=full[full.index(glue_start):full.index(glue_end)]
    template=TEMPLATE.read_text().replace('#include "mmq_tile_cache.hpp"',
                '#include "mmq_tile_cache.hpp"\n#include "mmq_select_probe.hpp"')
    generated=template.replace('// ORIGINAL_MMq_SOLVER',original_solver).replace('// P15B_HELPERS',helper_source)
    function='''
struct mock_tensor { ggml_type type; };
static int traced_selector(ggml_type type, bool moe, int64_t columns, int & threads) {
    const mock_tensor tensor{type};
    const mock_tensor * src0 = &tensor;
    int mock_ids = 0;
    const int * ids = moe ? &mock_ids : nullptr;
    const int64_t ncols_opt = columns;
    const bool fallback = false;
    const int cc = 90;
    const size_t smpbo = 131072;
    const ggml_prec prec_src1 = GGML_PREC_Q8;
    int J_best = 0;
    int nthreads_best = -777;
''' + glue + '''
    threads = nthreads_best;
    return J_best;
}
static void check_traced_dispatch_parity() {
    for (ggml_type type : quant_types) {
        for (int ncols : {1,2,3,4,8,16,64,65,127,256,513}) {
            for (bool moe : {false,true}) {
                int reference_nt=-777, candidate_nt=-777;
                const int reference=ggml_cuda_mmq_get_J_best(
                      type, ncols, false, 90, 131072, GGML_PREC_Q8, &reference_nt);
                const int candidate=traced_selector(type,moe,ncols,candidate_nt);
                assert(reference==candidate && reference_nt==candidate_nt);
            }
        }
    }
    for (int i=0;i<100;++i) {
        int nt=0;
        assert(traced_selector(GGML_TYPE_Q3_K,true,64,nt)>0);
    }
}
'''
    generated=generated.replace('int main() {',function+'\nint main() {\n    check_traced_dispatch_parity();')
    cpp=work/'source_extracted.cpp';cpp.write_text(generated)
    binary=work/'source_extracted'
    cxx(cpp,binary,compiler=compiler,sanitize=sanitize)
    checks={}
    for name,flags in [
        ('both_off',{'LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_CUDA_MMQ_TILE_CACHE':'0','LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_CUDA_MMQ_PROFILE':'0'}),
        ('profile_no_cache',{'LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_CUDA_MMQ_TILE_CACHE':'0','LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_CUDA_MMQ_PROFILE':'1'}),
        ('profile_cache',{'LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_CUDA_MMQ_TILE_CACHE':'1','LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_CUDA_MMQ_PROFILE':'1'}),
        ('invalid_profile',{'LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_CUDA_MMQ_TILE_CACHE':'1','LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_CUDA_MMQ_PROFILE':'1x'}),
    ]:
        case=run_case(binary,flags)
        assert 'P15B host parity OK' in case.stdout
        if name in ('both_off','invalid_profile'):
            assert 'P15C_MMQ_SELECT_JSON ' not in case.stderr
            checks[name]={'trace':0,'parity':'PASS'}
        else:
            x=report(case.stderr)
            assert x['total_calls']==12*11*2+100
            assert x['dropped_samples']==0
            if name=='profile_no_cache':
                assert x['total_cache_hits']==0
            else:
                assert x['total_cache_hits']>0
            checks[name]={'parity':'PASS','trace_calls':x['total_calls'],
                          'cache_hits':x['total_cache_hits'],'trace_records':x['records']}
    fixture_binary=work/'standalone'
    cxx(FIXTURE,fixture_binary,compiler=compiler,sanitize=sanitize)
    off=run_case(fixture_binary,{'LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_CUDA_MMQ_PROFILE':'0'})
    assert not off.stderr.strip()
    cases={}
    for name,args in [('normal',[]),('overflow',['overflow']),('saturated',['saturate'])]:
        run=run_case(fixture_binary,{'LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_CUDA_MMQ_PROFILE':'1'},args)
        x=report(run.stderr)
        cases[name]={'calls':x['total_calls'],'dropped':x['dropped_samples'],'threads':x['threads']}
        if name=='normal':
            assert x['total_calls']==4096+32+4*64 and x['threads']==5
            assert x['total_cache_hits']==4095+4*63
        elif name=='overflow':
            assert x['dropped_samples']==529-512
        else:
            assert x['total_calls']==2
            assert x['groups'][0]['select_ns']==(2**64)-1
    for patch in (PATCH_C,PATCH_B):
        invoke(['git','apply','--reverse','--check',str(patch)],cwd=work)
        invoke(['git','apply','--reverse',str(patch)],cwd=work)
    assert source.read_bytes()==BASE.read_bytes(), 'reverse patch chain not byte-identical'
    result={'status':'PASS_HOST_SOURCE_EXTRACTED','compiler':compiler,
            'sanitizers':sanitize,'source_sha256_r15':p15['targets'][0]['before_sha256'],
            'source_sha256_r16':p15['targets'][0]['after_sha256'],
            'branch_checks':checks,'probe_checks':cases,
            'not_tested':['nvcc / CUDA compilation','GPU memory or kernel speed','real GGUF parity','model tokens/s']}
    (work/'result.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2)+'\n')
    print(json.dumps(result,indent=2))


def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('--compiler',default='g++')
    p.add_argument('--sanitizers',action='store_true')
    p.add_argument('--output',type=Path)
    a=p.parse_args()
    if a.output:
        path=a.output.resolve()
        if path.exists() or ROOT==path or ROOT in path.parents or not path.parent.is_dir():
            p.error('--output must be a NEW directory outside the source package')
        path.mkdir(mode=0o700)
        execute(path,a.compiler,a.sanitizers)
    else:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='llama-p15c-host-') as t:
            execute(Path(t),a.compiler,a.sanitizers)


if __name__=='__main__':main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Host-only P16A MTP accept/capture parity gate (does not load model or CUDA)."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
CPP=ROOT/'tests_cpp/mtp_verify_window_fixture.cpp'
HEADER=ROOT/'modules/speculative/mtp_verify_window.hpp'

def sha(p:Path)->str:
    return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def main()->int:
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--output',required=True,type=Path,help='New directory outside the project')
    parser.add_argument('--compiler',default='g++')
    parser.add_argument('--sanitizers',action='store_true')
    args=parser.parse_args()
    output=args.output.resolve()
    if output.exists() or args.output.is_symlink() or output.is_relative_to(ROOT.resolve()) or not output.parent.is_dir():
        parser.error('output must be a new directory outside source, with an existing parent')
    compiler=shutil.which(args.compiler)
    if not compiler:
        parser.error('C++ compiler not available')
    output.mkdir(mode=0o700)
    report={'status':'RUNNING','scope':'HOST_CXX17_MTP_WINDOW_ONLY_NO_GPU_NO_LLAMA_LINK',
        'sanitizers':args.sanitizers,'compiler':compiler,'source_sha256':sha(CPP),'header_sha256':sha(HEADER),'modes':{}}
    result=output/'p16a_result.json'
    def save():result.write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2)+'\n')
    save()
    try:
        binary=output/'test_p16a_mtp_window'
        cmd=[compiler,'-std=c++17','-O1' if args.sanitizers else '-O2',
             '-Wall','-Wextra','-Werror','-pthread',str(CPP),'-o',str(binary)]
        if args.sanitizers:
            cmd.extend(['-g','-fno-omit-frame-pointer','-fsanitize=address,undefined'])
        build=subprocess.run(cmd,stdout=subprocess.PIPE,stderr=subprocess.PIPE,text=True,timeout=90)
        (output/'build.log').write_text(build.stdout+build.stderr)
        if build.returncode: raise RuntimeError('host C++ compilation failed')
        for mode in ('unset','0','1','1x'):
            env={**os.environ,'ASAN_OPTIONS':'detect_leaks=0'}
            if mode=='unset':env.pop('LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MTP_VERIFY_WINDOW',None)
            else:env['LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_MTP_VERIFY_WINDOW']=mode
            run=subprocess.run([str(binary)],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=60,env=env)
            (output/f'{mode}.log').write_text(run.stdout+run.stderr)
            if run.returncode!=0 or 'parity OK' not in run.stdout:
                raise RuntimeError(f'parity failed in env mode: {mode}')
            report['modes'][mode]={'rc':run.returncode,'output':run.stdout.strip()}
            save()
        report['status']='PASS_HOST_ONLY'
    except Exception as exc:
        report['status']='FAIL_HOST_ONLY';report['error']=str(exc)
        save()
        print(str(exc),file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    save();print(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False))
    return 0

if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/test_p16a_source_bridge.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Compile P16A's exact patched process()/accept() snippets against a mock ggml host."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
PARTS=ROOT/'validation/p16a_upstream_fragments.json'
TEMPLATE=ROOT/'tests_cpp/mtp_source_bridge_fixture.cpp.in'
HEADER=ROOT/'modules/speculative/mtp_verify_window.hpp'

def sha(p):return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    ap.add_argument('--output',required=True,type=Path)
    ap.add_argument('--compiler',default='g++')
    ap.add_argument('--sanitizers',action='store_true')
    opts=ap.parse_args()
    out=opts.output.resolve()
    if out.exists() or opts.output.is_symlink() or out.is_relative_to(ROOT.resolve()) or not out.parent.is_dir():
        ap.error('output must be a NEW directory outside the package')
    compiler=shutil.which(opts.compiler)
    if not compiler:ap.error('compiler not available')
    data=json.loads(PARTS.read_text())
    f=data['fragments']
    assert len(f)==5
    process=f[3]['modified'];accept=f[4]['modified']
    assert process.startswith('        for (llama_seq_id seq_id') and process.endswith('    }\n')
    assert accept.startswith('    void accept(') and accept.endswith('};\n')
    assert process.count('window.retained_rows')==3
    assert accept.count('window.accepted_row(n_accepted)')==1
    code=TEMPLATE.read_text().replace('@@P16_PROCESS@@',process.rstrip()).replace('@@P16_ACCEPT@@',accept.rsplit('};\n',1)[0].rstrip())
    out.mkdir(mode=0o700)
    result={'status':'RUNNING','scope':'SOURCE_EXTRACTED_HOST_ONLY','compiler':compiler,'sanitizers':opts.sanitizers,
            'source_fragment_sha256':sha(PARTS),'header_sha256':sha(HEADER)}
    def save():(out/'result.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2)+'\n')
    save()
    try:
        cpp=out/'source_bridge.cpp';cpp.write_text(code)
        exe=out/'source_bridge'
        cmd=[compiler,'-std=c++17','-O1' if opts.sanitizers else '-O2','-Wall','-Wextra','-Werror',
             '-I',str(HEADER.parent),str(cpp),'-o',str(exe)]
        if opts.sanitizers:cmd+=['-g','-fno-omit-frame-pointer','-fsanitize=address,undefined']
        built=subprocess.run(cmd,capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=60)
        (out/'build.log').write_text(built.stdout+built.stderr)
        if built.returncode:raise RuntimeError('compile failed: '+built.stderr[-4000:])
        env={**os.environ,'ASAN_OPTIONS':'detect_leaks=0'}
        run=subprocess.run([str(exe)],capture_output=True,text=True,env=env,timeout=30)
        (out/'run.log').write_text(run.stdout+run.stderr)
        if run.returncode or 'PASS' not in run.stdout:raise RuntimeError('host bridge mismatch: '+run.stderr)
        result.update(status='PASS_SOURCE_EXTRACTED_HOST_ONLY',output=run.stdout.strip())
    except Exception as exc:
        result.update(status='FAIL_SOURCE_EXTRACTED_HOST_ONLY',error=str(exc))
        save();print(str(exc),file=sys.stderr);return 1
    save();print(json.dumps(result,ensure_ascii=False));return 0

if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""P16B host-only checkpoint/replay state gate; NEVER loads a model or uses CUDA."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse,hashlib,json,os,shutil,subprocess,sys
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
CPP=ROOT/'tests_cpp/mtp_rewind_guard_fixture.cpp'
HEADER=ROOT/'modules/speculative/mtp_rewind_guard.hpp'

def main():

                raise RuntimeError('host fixture failed mode='+mode+' '+run.stderr[-1500:])
            result['modes'][mode]={'rc':run.returncode,'output':run.stdout.strip()}
            save()
        result['status']='PASS_HOST_ONLY'
    except Exception as exc:
        result.update(status='FAIL_HOST_ONLY',error=str(exc))
        save();print(str(exc),file=sys.stderr);return 1
    save();print(json.dumps(result,ensure_ascii=False));return 0

===== SOURCE FILE tools/test_patch_hunks.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Round-trip P08 patch against a synthetic hunk-backed source fixture.

This deliberately does NOT claim application to the full pinned upstream source.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import subprocess
import tempfile

def hash_bytes(x):return hashlib.sha256(x).hexdigest()

def exec_git(root,*args):
    r=subprocess.run(['git','-c','core.autocrlf=false',*args],cwd=root,
                     capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=40)
    if r.returncode:raise RuntimeError(f"git {' '.join(args)}: {r.stderr.strip()}")
    return r.stdout

def main():
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--source-root',type=Path,default=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1])
    parser.add_argument('--output',type=Path,required=True)
    args=parser.parse_args()
    root=args.source_root.resolve(strict=True)
    output=args.output.resolve()
    if output.exists() or output.is_relative_to(root) or not output.parent.is_dir():
        parser.error('--output must be new outside source tree')
    manifest=json.loads((root/'manifest.json').read_text())
    patch_entry=next(p for p in manifest['patches'] if p['id']=='P08-deepseek41-engram-graph-stage')
    assert patch_entry['id']=='P08-deepseek41-engram-graph-stage'
    patch_path=root/'patches'/patch_entry['file']
    raw=patch_path.read_bytes()
    assert hash_bytes(raw)==patch_entry['sha256']
    lines=raw.decode().splitlines(keepends=True)
    assert lines[0].startswith('diff --git a/src/models/deepseek41.cpp')
    hunk_re=re.compile(r'^@@ -(\d+),(\d+) \+(\d+),(\d+) @@')
    old_lines=['// untouched fixture position %04d\n'%i for i in range(1,1100)]
    hunks=0
    for j,line in enumerate(lines):
        m=hunk_re.match(line)
        if not m:continue
        hunks+=1
        old_start,old_count,new_start,new_count=map(int,m.groups())
        pos=old_start-1
        actual_old=actual_new=0
        for content in lines[j+1:]:
            if content.startswith('@@'):break
            if content.startswith('diff --git'):break
            if content.startswith(('---','+++')):break
            sign=content[:1]
            if sign in (' ','-'):
                if not old_lines[pos].startswith('// untouched') and old_lines[pos]!=content[1:]:
                    raise RuntimeError('Overlapping hunk conflict')
                old_lines[pos]=content[1:]; pos+=1;actual_old+=1
            if sign in (' ','+'):actual_new+=1
        if actual_old!=old_count or actual_new!=new_count:
            raise RuntimeError('Malformed unified hunk record')
    output.mkdir(mode=0o700)
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='llama-p08-hunks-') as tmp:
        git_root=Path(tmp)
        (git_root/'src/models').mkdir(parents=True)
        source=git_root/'src/models/deepseek41.cpp'
        source.write_text(''.join(old_lines))
        original=source.read_bytes()
        exec_git(git_root,'init','-q')
        exec_git(git_root,'config','user.email','fixture@example.invalid')
        exec_git(git_root,'config','user.name','Synthetic Fixture')
        exec_git(git_root,'add','--all')
        exec_git(git_root,'commit','-qm','synthetic')
        exec_git(git_root,'apply','--check','--whitespace=error-all',str(patch_path))
        exec_git(git_root,'apply','--whitespace=error-all',str(patch_path))
        modified=source.read_bytes()
        if original==modified:raise RuntimeError('Patch did not modify fixture')
        exec_git(git_root,'apply','--reverse','--check',str(patch_path))
        exec_git(git_root,'apply','--reverse',str(patch_path))
        if source.read_bytes()!=original:raise RuntimeError('Reverse patch is not byte-exact')
    report={'status':'PASS','test':'P08 synthetic hunk source only','hunks':hunks,
            'source_sha256':hash_bytes(original),'modified_sha256':hash_bytes(modified),
            'patch_sha256':hash_bytes(raw),'roundtrip_exact':True,
            'pinned_full_source_applied':False,'native_engine_compiled':False}
    (output/'result.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2)+'\n')
    print(json.dumps({'status':'PASS','hunks':hunks,'output':str(output/'result.json')}))

if __name__=='__main__':main()

===== SOURCE FILE tools/test_prefetch_rows.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Host-only page advice parity for real llama_prefetch_rows bodies.

No model files, inference, CUDA execution, system changes or disk preload.
Do not claim that this proves I/O throughput or latency improvements.
"""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys

KIT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def extract(source: str) -> str:
    prefix = 'void llama_prefetch_rows(const ggml_tensor * tensor, const int32_t * rows, size_t n_rows) {'
    if source.count(prefix) != 1:
        raise ValueError('Expected exactly one upstream llama_prefetch_rows()')
    start = source.index(prefix)
    depth = 0
    for pos in range(source.index('{', start), len(source)):
        if source[pos] == '{':
            depth += 1
        elif source[pos] == '}':
            depth -= 1
            if depth == 0:
                return source[start:pos + 1]
    raise ValueError('Unterminated prefetch function')


def sha(path: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def invoke(cmd: list[str], env: dict, path: Path) -> dict:
    p = subprocess.run(cmd, env=env, stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
                       stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True, timeout=180)
    path.write_text(p.stdout + p.stderr)
    if p.returncode:
        raise RuntimeError(f'{cmd[0]} failed exit={p.returncode}: {p.stderr[-1500:]}')
    return json.loads(p.stdout.splitlines()[-1])


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--baseline', required=True, type=Path)
    parser.add_argument('--candidate', required=True, type=Path)
    parser.add_argument('--output', required=True, type=Path)
    parser.add_argument('--compiler', default='c++')
    parser.add_argument('--sanitize', action='store_true')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    baseline, candidate = args.baseline.resolve(strict=True), args.candidate.resolve(strict=True)
    out = args.output.resolve()
    if baseline == candidate or any(out.is_relative_to(x) for x in (baseline, candidate)):
        parser.error('sources and output must be separate')
    if out.exists() or not out.parent.is_dir():
        parser.error('output must be a new directory')
    compiler = shutil.which(args.compiler)
    if compiler is None:
        parser.error('C++ compiler unavailable')
    template = (KIT/'tests_cpp/prefetch_rows_fixture.cpp.in').read_text()
    flags = ['-std=c++17', '-O2', '-Wall', '-Wextra', '-Werror']
    if args.sanitize:
        flags = ['-std=c++17', '-O1', '-g', '-Wall', '-Wextra', '-Werror',
                 '-fsanitize=address,undefined', '-fno-sanitize-recover=all']
    report = {'schema_version':1, 'status':'RUNNING',
              'scope':'Source-extracted host page advice parity only; no actual madvise, NVMe or GPU testing',
              'cases':[], 'source_hashes':{}}
    out.mkdir(mode=0o700)
    try:
        for label, tree in [('baseline', baseline), ('candidate', candidate)]:
            source = tree/'src/llama-impl.cpp'
            report['source_hashes'][label] = sha(source)
            function = extract(source.read_text())
            fixture = out/(label+'.cpp')
            fixture.write_text(template.replace('@@PREFETCH_FUNCTION@@', function))
            binary = out/(label+'.bin')
            compiled = subprocess.run([compiler,*flags,str(fixture),'-o',str(binary)],
                                      capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=180)
            (out/(label+'-compile.log')).write_text(compiled.stdout + compiled.stderr)
            if compiled.returncode:
                raise RuntimeError(label+' compile failed: '+compiled.stderr[-1000:])
            profiles = ['unset','0','1','invalid']
            for profile in profiles:
                env = dict(os.environ)
                env.pop('LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_PREFETCH_UNIQUE', None)
                if args.sanitize:
                    env['LLAMA_PREFETCH_TEST_QUICK'] = '1'
                if profile != 'unset':
                    env['LLAMA_EXPERIMENTAL_PREFETCH_UNIQUE'] = 'invalid' if profile == 'invalid' else profile
                case = invoke([str(binary)], env, out/(label+'-'+profile+'.log'))
                report['cases'].append({'source':label,'profile':profile,**case})
        if len({x['cases'] for x in report['cases']}) != 1:
            raise RuntimeError('Mismatch in tested case counts')
        if not all(x['status']=='PASS' for x in report['cases']):
            raise RuntimeError('Fixture failed')
        orig = [x for x in report['cases'] if x['source']=='baseline']
        cand = [x for x in report['cases'] if x['source']=='candidate']
        if any(x['input_ranges']!=orig[0]['input_ranges'] for x in orig):
            raise RuntimeError('Baseline changed with env var')
        for x in cand:
            if x['profile'] != '1' and x['input_ranges'] != orig[0]['input_ranges']:
                raise RuntimeError('Candidate not disabled by '+x['profile'])
        if not next(x for x in cand if x['profile']=='1')['input_ranges'] < orig[0]['input_ranges']:
            raise RuntimeError('Unique planner did not reduce the tested input ranges')
        report['status']='PASS_HOST_PAGE_PARITY'
    except (OSError, RuntimeError, ValueError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as exc:
        report.update(status='FAIL', error=str(exc))
    (out/'result.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2)+'\n')
    print(json.dumps({'status':report['status'],'result':str(out/'result.json')}))
    return 0 if report['status']=='PASS_HOST_PAGE_PARITY' else 1

if __name__=='__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE tools/validate_source.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Run source-level acceptance for the cumulative patch package without a GPU.

Compile actual changed/dependent translation units and exercise the real source
helpers with explicit host fixtures. Does not configure CMake, link llama.cpp,
load a GGUF, start a server, install anything, or benchmark inference.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import time

UNITS = (
    "src/llama-moe-cache.cpp", "src/llama-context.cpp", "src/llama-graph.cpp", "src/llama-impl.cpp",
    "src/models/deepseek4.cpp", "src/models/deepseek41.cpp", "src/models/qwen4exp.cpp",
)


def digest(path: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def snapshot(root: Path, expected: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, str]:
    files = {root / name for name in (*UNITS, *expected)}
    for part in ("include", "src", "ggml/include"):
        for pattern in ("*.h", "*.hpp"):
            files.update((root / part).rglob(pattern))
    return {p.relative_to(root).as_posix(): digest(p) for p in sorted(files)}


def run_step(command: list[str], logfile: Path, sanitize: bool = False) -> dict:
    start = time.monotonic()
    proc = subprocess.run(command, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, capture_output=True,
                          text=True, timeout=600, shell=False,
                          env={**os.environ, "ASAN_OPTIONS": "detect_leaks=0:halt_on_error=1"}
                          if sanitize else None)
    logfile.write_text(proc.stdout + proc.stderr)
    return {"command": command, "exit_code": proc.returncode,
            "seconds": round(time.monotonic() - start, 3),
            "log": logfile.name, "log_sha256": digest(logfile)}


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--baseline", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--candidate", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True, help="New directory outside both source trees")
    parser.add_argument("--compiler", default="c++")
    parser.add_argument("--sanitize", action="store_true", help="ASan/UBSan helper runs; leak scan disabled in sandbox")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    baseline, candidate = args.baseline.resolve(strict=True), args.candidate.resolve(strict=True)
    output = args.output.resolve()
    if baseline == candidate or any(output.is_relative_to(p) for p in (baseline, candidate)):
        parser.error("Separate baseline/candidate trees and external output are required")
    if output.exists() or args.output.is_symlink() or not output.parent.is_dir():
        parser.error("--output must be a new directory with an existing parent")
    compiler = shutil.which(args.compiler)
    if not compiler:
        parser.error("C++ compiler is unavailable; no output directory created")
    kit = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    manifest = json.loads((kit / "manifest.json").read_text())
    base_expected, candidate_expected = {}, {}
    for patch in manifest["patches"]:
        for target in patch["targets"]:
            base_expected.setdefault(target["path"], target["before_sha256"])
            candidate_expected[target["path"]] = target["after_sha256"]
    for asset in manifest["additional_files"]:
        if digest(kit / asset["source"]) != asset["sha256"]:
            parser.error("Package header checksum mismatch: " + asset["source"])
        candidate_expected[asset["destination"]] = asset["sha256"]
    trees = (("baseline", baseline, base_expected), ("candidate", candidate, candidate_expected))
    try:
        before = {role: snapshot(tree, expected) for role, tree, expected in trees}
    except OSError as exc:
        parser.error(str(exc))
    for role, _, expected in trees:
        for name, checksum in expected.items():
            if before[role][name] != checksum:
                parser.error(f"{role} differs from the pinned manifest at {name}")
    output.mkdir(mode=0o700, exist_ok=False)
    report = {"schema_version": 1, "status": "RUNNING", "source_files": before,
              "release_id": manifest["release_id"], "base_commit": manifest["upstream"]["base_commit"],
              "scope": "Source/host-only acceptance; no full engine link, CUDA, model inference or speed validation",
              "compiler": subprocess.run([compiler, "--version"], capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout.splitlines()[0],
              "steps": [], "not_run": ["full CMake build and linking", "CUDA execution", "numerical model parity",
                                        "KV/long-context/cancel runtime", "images/video/tools API runtime", "tok/s measurements"]}
    record = output / "source_validation.json"
    def save():
        record.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2) + "\n")
    save()
    code = 0
    try:
        for role, tree in (("baseline", baseline), ("candidate", candidate)):
            for name in UNITS:
                label = role + "_" + Path(name).stem
                print("Compiling " + role + " " + name, file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
                cmd = [compiler, "-std=c++17", "-O0", "-Wall", "-Wextra", "-Werror", "-c",
                       *["-I" + str(tree / p) for p in ("include", "src", "src/models", "ggml/include")],
                       str(tree / name), "-o", str(output / (label + ".o"))]
                step = run_step(cmd, output / (label + ".log"))
                report["steps"].append(step); save()
                if step["exit_code"]:
                    raise RuntimeError("Translation-unit compilation failed: " + label)
                step["object_sha256"] = digest(output / (label + ".o"))
        tools = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
        cases = [
            ("moe_plan", ["--sanitizers", "--disable-leak-check"]),
            ("moe_staging", ["--sanitizers", "--disable-leak-check"]),
            ("hc_post", ["--sanitize"]),
            ("hash_scratch", ["--sanitize"]),
            ("prefetch_rows", ["--sanitize"]),
        ]
        for name, sanitizer_args in cases:
            print("Checking " + name, file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
            cmd = [sys.executable, str(tools / ("test_" + name + ".py")),
                   "--candidate", str(candidate), "--output", str(output / name), "--compiler", compiler]
            if name in ("hash_scratch", "prefetch_rows"):
                cmd += ["--baseline", str(baseline)]
            if args.sanitize:
                cmd += sanitizer_args
            step = run_step(cmd, output / (name + ".log"), args.sanitize)
            report["steps"].append(step); save()
            if step["exit_code"]:
                raise RuntimeError("Host-source check failed: " + name)
        # The final P14B bridge covers the P04/P09/P10/P12 call sites, scheduler
        # and event lifetime together against the installed candidate headers.
        # Historical bridges extract earlier layouts which are no longer present.
        for name in (
                "engram_cache", "engram_stage", "engram_model_cache",
                "moe_pipeline", "moe_worker", "moe_metadata", "moe_fill_plan",
                "moe_scheduler", "moe_upload_completion", "moe_scheduler_bridge",
                "p15_hc_post", "p15b_mmq_tile", "p15c_mmq_probe",
                "p16a_mtp_window", "p16a_source_bridge", "p16b_mtp_guard"):
            print("Checking " + name, file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
            cmd = [sys.executable, str(tools / ("test_" + name + ".py")),
                   "--output", str(output / name), "--compiler", compiler]
            if name == "moe_scheduler_bridge":
                cmd += ["--candidate", str(candidate)]
            if args.sanitize:
                cmd.append("--sanitizers")
            step = run_step(cmd, output / (name + ".log"), args.sanitize)
            report["steps"].append(step)
            save()
            if step["exit_code"]:
                raise RuntimeError("Host-source check failed: " + name)
        after = {role: snapshot(tree, expected) for role, tree, expected in trees}
        if before != after:
            raise RuntimeError("Inspected source/header files changed")
        report["source_files_unchanged"] = True
        report["status"] = "PASS_SOURCE_HOST_ONLY"
    except (OSError, RuntimeError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as error:
        code = 1; report.update(status="FAIL", error=str(error))
    finally:
        save()
    print(json.dumps({"status": report["status"], "record": str(record), "full_engine_or_gpu_validated": False}))
    return code


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

===== SOURCE FILE vendor/maya-store/include/strata/platform/commit.hpp =====
// include/strata/platform/commit.hpp - how far Windows' commit limit (the RAM plus the page files) can still grow.
//
// An integrated GPU's memory is charged to Windows' commit, and Windows sees only the RAM outside its carve-out (32 GB
// of a 128 GB Strix Halo that gives its GPU 96), so the engines keep their expert pools within what may still be
// committed (glm_fast_path.cu pool_check, ds4_model.cu commit_room).  A page file Windows manages (its default) is
// not at its full size, though: Windows grows it when more is committed, so the commit available now is not all
// there is - counting only that kept most experts off the GPU of a laptop with the default page file (decode 17-22 ->
// 10-15 tok/s, v1.1.15 -> v1.1.17).  commit_growth: the bytes the page files may still grow by.
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdlib>

#if defined(_WIN32)
#ifndef NOMINMAX
#define NOMINMAX
#endif
#include <windows.h>

#include <cwchar>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#endif

namespace strata::platform {

/// The bytes Windows' page files may still grow by: a page file Windows manages (PagingFiles "?:\pagefile.sys", or a
/// file's sizes "0 0") up to 3x the RAM Windows sees (4 GiB at least) and an eighth of its volume, a set one up to
/// its maximum - each within its volume's free space less a tenth of the volume.  0 off Windows, without a page file,
/// or when the settings cannot be read.  STRATA_COMMIT_GROWTH=0: 0 (as v1.1.17 counted).
inline uint64_t commit_growth(uint64_t ram_total) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    if (const char* v = std::getenv("STRATA_COMMIT_GROWTH"); v != nullptr && std::atoi(v) == 0) return 0;
    // the setting, through advapi32 as loaded (RegGetValueW), so no program links it for this
    using Get = LONG(WINAPI*)(HKEY, LPCWSTR, LPCWSTR, DWORD, LPDWORD, PVOID, LPDWORD);
    HMODULE m = LoadLibraryA("advapi32.dll");
    if (m == nullptr) return 0;
    const auto get = reinterpret_cast<Get>(reinterpret_cast<void*>(GetProcAddress(m, "RegGetValueW")));
    const wchar_t* key = L"SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\Session Manager\\Memory Management";
    DWORD bytes = 0;
    if (get == nullptr || get(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, key, L"PagingFiles", RRF_RT_REG_MULTI_SZ, nullptr, nullptr, &bytes) !=
                              ERROR_SUCCESS || bytes < 4) {
        FreeLibrary(m);
        return 0;
    }
    std::vector<wchar_t> buf(bytes / sizeof(wchar_t) + 2, L'\0');
    const LONG rc = get(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, key, L"PagingFiles", RRF_RT_REG_MULTI_SZ, nullptr, buf.data(), &bytes);
    FreeLibrary(m);
    if (rc != ERROR_SUCCESS) return 0;
    wchar_t sysdrive[8] = L"C:";
    GetEnvironmentVariableW(L"SystemDrive", sysdrive, 8);
    uint64_t grow = 0;
    for (const wchar_t* e = buf.data(); *e != L'\0'; e += std::wcslen(e) + 1) {
        // "<path> [<initial MB> <maximum MB>]"
        std::wstring s(e), path = s.substr(0, s.find(L' '));
        unsigned long long init_mb = 0, max_mb = 0;
        if (s.size() > path.size()) std::swscanf(s.c_str() + path.size(), L"%llu %llu", &init_mb, &max_mb);
        const bool managed = path.rfind(L"?:", 0) == 0 || (init_mb == 0 && max_mb == 0);
        if (path.rfind(L"?:", 0) == 0) path = std::wstring(sysdrive) + path.substr(2);
        if (path.size() < 3 || path[1] != L':') continue;
        const std::wstring root = path.substr(0, 2) + L"\\";
        ULARGE_INTEGER free_b{}, total_b{};
        if (!GetDiskFreeSpaceExW(root.c_str(), &free_b, &total_b, nullptr)) continue;
        // its size now, from its directory entry (the file itself is Windows' own: GetFileAttributesEx fails on it)
        uint64_t now = 0;
        WIN32_FIND_DATAW fd{};
        const HANDLE h = FindFirstFileW(path.c_str(), &fd);
        if (h != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
            now = ((uint64_t) fd.nFileSizeHigh << 32) | fd.nFileSizeLow;
            FindClose(h);
        }
        uint64_t most = managed ? (std::max)(3 * ram_total, uint64_t{4} << 30) : (uint64_t) max_mb << 20;
        if (managed) most = (std::min)(most, total_b.QuadPart / 8);
        const uint64_t keep = total_b.QuadPart / 10;
        const uint64_t room = free_b.QuadPart > keep ? free_b.QuadPart - keep : 0;
        grow += (std::min)(most > now ? most - now : 0, room);
    }
    return grow;
#else
    (void) ram_total;
    return 0;
#endif
}

}  // namespace strata::platform

===== SOURCE FILE vendor/maya-store/src/ds4/ds4_os.hpp =====
// src/ds4/ds4_os.hpp - the few OS services maya-ds4 needs, on Linux and on Windows: files read at an offset and
// mapped read-only (the experts read in place), page-cache hints, anonymous host memory on a NUMA node, thread pinning,
// each NUMA node's physical cores, the RAM this process can still get.  (On Windows as src/core/expert_source.cpp
// does it for GLM: no FILE_FLAG_RANDOM_ACCESS - the page cache must keep what it read.)
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <atomic>
#include <cerrno>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstring>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <iterator>
#include <mutex>
#include <sstream>
#include <string>
#include <thread>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <vector>

#if defined(_WIN32)
#define WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#ifndef NOMINMAX
#define NOMINMAX
#endif
#include <windows.h>
#include <psapi.h>   // (K32QueryWorkingSetEx: kernel32's, no psapi.lib)
#include "strata/platform/commit.hpp"
#else
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <pthread.h>
#include <sched.h>
#include <sys/mman.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/syscall.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#endif

namespace strata::ds4::os {

using fd_t = intptr_t;
constexpr fd_t kBadFd = -1;

#if defined(_WIN32)
inline std::wstring wide(const std::string& s) {   // UTF-8 -> UTF-16 (a path with non-ASCII characters)
    const int n = MultiByteToWideChar(CP_UTF8, 0, s.c_str(), -1, nullptr, 0);
    std::wstring w((size_t) (n > 0 ? n : 1), L'\0');
    if (n > 0) MultiByteToWideChar(CP_UTF8, 0, s.c_str(), -1, &w[0], n);
    return w;
}
#endif

/// A file opened for reading (kBadFd: it could not be).
inline fd_t open_ro(const std::string& path) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    HANDLE h = CreateFileW(wide(path).c_str(), GENERIC_READ, FILE_SHARE_READ, nullptr, OPEN_EXISTING,
                           FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL, nullptr);
    return h == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE ? kBadFd : (fd_t) h;
#else
    const int fd = ::open(path.c_str(), O_RDONLY);
    return fd < 0 ? kBadFd : (fd_t) fd;
#endif
}
/// The same file opened for unbuffered reads (Linux: O_DIRECT - no page cache, the disk straight into the buffer;
/// kBadFd where the filesystem refuses it, or on Windows, whose unbuffered reads of a mapped file take a slow path)
inline fd_t open_direct(const std::string& path) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    (void) path;
    return kBadFd;
#else
    const int fd = ::open(path.c_str(), O_RDONLY | O_DIRECT);
    return fd < 0 ? kBadFd : (fd_t) fd;
#endif
}
/// [off, off + n) through an unbuffered descriptor into dst, a kDirectAlign-aligned buffer of at least
/// direct_span(off, n) bytes: the file read from off rounded down, so the bytes land at dst + (off % kDirectAlign).
/// False if a read fails (the caller reads buffered instead).
constexpr uint64_t kDirectAlign = 4096;
inline uint64_t direct_span(uint64_t off, uint64_t n) {
    const uint64_t a0 = off & ~(kDirectAlign - 1), a1 = (off + n + kDirectAlign - 1) & ~(kDirectAlign - 1);
    return a1 - a0;
}
inline bool pread_direct(fd_t f, void* dst, uint64_t n, uint64_t off) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    (void) f, (void) dst, (void) n, (void) off;
    return false;
#else
    const uint64_t a0 = off & ~(kDirectAlign - 1), need = direct_span(off, n), want = (off - a0) + n;
    uint8_t* p = (uint8_t*) dst;
    uint64_t got = 0;
    while (got < need) {
        const ssize_t r = ::pread((int) f, p + got, (size_t) std::min<uint64_t>(need - got, 1ull << 30), (off_t) (a0 + got));
        if (r < 0) return false;
        if (r == 0) break;   // (the file's end: the last aligned read is short)
        got += (uint64_t) r;
        if ((uint64_t) r % kDirectAlign != 0) break;
    }
    return got >= want;
#endif
}
/// Read an arbitrarily aligned expert span through O_DIRECT into a reusable aligned staging buffer,
/// then copy its exact bytes to the NUMA destination. A failure leaves the caller to use buffered I/O.
inline bool copy_direct(fd_t f, uint64_t off, uint64_t n, void* dst, std::vector<uint8_t>& staging) {
    if (f == kBadFd || n == 0 || off > UINT64_MAX - n || off + n > (uint64_t) INT64_MAX - kDirectAlign) return false;
    const uint64_t span = direct_span(off, n);
    if (span > SIZE_MAX - kDirectAlign) return false;
    staging.resize((size_t) span + kDirectAlign);
    auto* aligned = reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>((reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(staging.data()) + kDirectAlign - 1) & ~(uintptr_t)(kDirectAlign - 1));
    if (!pread_direct(f, aligned, n, off)) return false;
    std::memcpy(dst, aligned + off % kDirectAlign, (size_t) n);
    return true;
}

/// Windows: the file opened for unbuffered reads (FILE_FLAG_NO_BUFFERING), for a file no view of this process maps -
/// unbuffered reads of a mapped file take a slow path there (open_direct); kBadFd elsewhere
inline fd_t open_unbuffered(const std::string& path) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    HANDLE h = CreateFileW(wide(path).c_str(), GENERIC_READ, FILE_SHARE_READ, nullptr, OPEN_EXISTING,
                           FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL | FILE_FLAG_NO_BUFFERING, nullptr);
    return h == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE ? kBadFd : (fd_t) h;
#else
    (void) path;
    return kBadFd;
#endif
}
/// [off, off + n) through open_unbuffered's handle (one thread's) into buf, kDirectAlign-aligned and at least
/// direct_span(off, n) bytes: the bytes land at buf + off % kDirectAlign
inline bool pread_unbuffered_aligned(fd_t f, void* buf, uint64_t n, uint64_t off, uint64_t alignment) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    if (alignment == 0 || off > UINT64_MAX - n) return false;
    const uint64_t a0 = off - off % alignment, delta = off - a0;
    if (delta > UINT64_MAX - n || delta + n > UINT64_MAX - (alignment - 1)) return false;
    const uint64_t span = (delta + n + alignment - 1) / alignment * alignment;
    if (span > MAXDWORD) return false;
    OVERLAPPED ov{};
    ov.Offset = (DWORD) a0;
    ov.OffsetHigh = (DWORD) (a0 >> 32);
    DWORD got = 0;
    return ReadFile((HANDLE) f, buf, (DWORD) span, &got, &ov) && got >= delta + n;
#else
    (void) f, (void) buf, (void) n, (void) off, (void) alignment;
    return false;
#endif
}
inline bool pread_unbuffered(fd_t f, void* buf, uint64_t n, uint64_t off) {
    return pread_unbuffered_aligned(f, buf, n, off, kDirectAlign);
}
/// The same file opened for reads from many threads at once (Windows runs the reads of a synchronous handle one at a
/// time, so an overlapped one; Linux: a plain descriptor, pread is concurrent anyway)
inline fd_t open_par(const std::string& path) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    HANDLE h = CreateFileW(wide(path).c_str(), GENERIC_READ, FILE_SHARE_READ, nullptr, OPEN_EXISTING,
                           FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL | FILE_FLAG_OVERLAPPED, nullptr);
    return h == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE ? kBadFd : (fd_t) h;
#else
    const int fd = ::open(path.c_str(), O_RDONLY);
    return fd < 0 ? kBadFd : (fd_t) fd;
#endif
}
/// n bytes at off through open_par's handle (any thread, concurrently)
inline bool pread_par(fd_t f, void* dst, uint64_t n, uint64_t off) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    struct Ev {   // (each thread its own, closed when it ends)
        HANDLE h = CreateEventW(nullptr, TRUE, FALSE, nullptr);
        ~Ev() {
            if (h != nullptr) CloseHandle(h);
        }
    };
    static thread_local Ev ev;
    if (ev.h == nullptr) return false;
    uint8_t* p = (uint8_t*) dst;
    while (n > 0) {
        const DWORD want = (DWORD) std::min<uint64_t>(n, 1ull << 30);
        OVERLAPPED ov{};
        ov.Offset = (DWORD) (off & 0xffffffffu);
        ov.OffsetHigh = (DWORD) (off >> 32);
        ov.hEvent = ev.h;
        ResetEvent(ev.h);
        DWORD got = 0;
        if (!ReadFile((HANDLE) f, p, want, nullptr, &ov) && GetLastError() != ERROR_IO_PENDING) return false;
        if (!GetOverlappedResult((HANDLE) f, &ov, &got, TRUE) || got == 0) return false;
        p += got, off += got, n -= got;
    }
    return true;
#else
    uint8_t* p = (uint8_t*) dst;
    while (n > 0) {
        const ssize_t r = ::pread((int) f, p, (size_t) std::min<uint64_t>(n, 1ull << 30), (off_t) off);
        if (r <= 0) return false;
        p += r, off += (uint64_t) r, n -= (uint64_t) r;
    }
    return true;
#endif
}
/// `from` renamed to `to`, replacing `to` if it exists (rename(2) does; Windows' rename fails on an existing file, so
/// MoveFileExW with MOVEFILE_REPLACE_EXISTING)
inline bool replace_file(const std::string& from, const std::string& to) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    return MoveFileExW(wide(from).c_str(), wide(to).c_str(), MOVEFILE_REPLACE_EXISTING | MOVEFILE_WRITE_THROUGH) != 0;
#else
    return std::rename(from.c_str(), to.c_str()) == 0;
#endif
}
inline void close_fd(fd_t f) {
    if (f == kBadFd) return;
#if defined(_WIN32)
    CloseHandle((HANDLE) f);
#else
    ::close((int) f);
#endif
}
inline uint64_t file_size(fd_t f) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    LARGE_INTEGER sz{};
    return GetFileSizeEx((HANDLE) f, &sz) ? (uint64_t) sz.QuadPart : 0;
#else
    struct stat sb;
    return ::fstat((int) f, &sb) == 0 && sb.st_size > 0 ? (uint64_t) sb.st_size : 0;
#endif
}
/// n bytes at offset off into dst (any thread, concurrently); false if the file ends first or a read fails.
inline bool pread_full(fd_t f, void* dst, uint64_t n, uint64_t off) {
    uint8_t* p = (uint8_t*) dst;
    while (n > 0) {
        const uint64_t want = std::min<uint64_t>(n, 1ull << 30);
#if defined(_WIN32)
        OVERLAPPED ov{};
        ov.Offset = (DWORD) (off & 0xffffffffu);
        ov.OffsetHigh = (DWORD) (off >> 32);
        DWORD got = 0;
        if (!ReadFile((HANDLE) f, p, (DWORD) want, &got, &ov) || got == 0) return false;
        const uint64_t r = got;
#else
        const ssize_t rr = ::pread((int) f, p, (size_t) want, (off_t) off);
        if (rr <= 0) return false;
        const uint64_t r = (uint64_t) rr;
#endif
        p += r, off += r, n -= r;
    }
    return true;
}
/// The whole file mapped read-only (nullptr: it could not be).
#if defined(_WIN32)
/// fd -> the view map_ro returned for it (advise's Windows branch unlocks through the view; a mapping is per fd here,
/// and a stale entry can only name a dead or reused view - VirtualUnlock on it fails harmlessly).
inline std::unordered_map<fd_t, const uint8_t*>& view_of_fd() {
    static std::unordered_map<fd_t, const uint8_t*> r;
    return r;
}
inline std::mutex& view_mu() { static std::mutex m; return m; }
#endif
inline void* map_ro(fd_t f, uint64_t bytes) {
    if (bytes == 0) return nullptr;
#if defined(_WIN32)
    HANDLE m = CreateFileMappingW((HANDLE) f, nullptr, PAGE_READONLY, 0, 0, nullptr);
    if (m == nullptr) return nullptr;
    void* v = MapViewOfFile(m, FILE_MAP_READ, 0, 0, 0);
    CloseHandle(m);   // (the view keeps the mapping)
    if (v != nullptr) {
        const std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(view_mu());
        view_of_fd()[(size_t) f] = (const uint8_t*) v;
    }
    return v;
#else
    void* p = ::mmap(nullptr, (size_t) bytes, PROT_READ, MAP_SHARED, (int) f, 0);
    return p == MAP_FAILED ? nullptr : p;
#endif
}
inline void unmap(void* p, uint64_t bytes) {
    if (p == nullptr) return;
#if defined(_WIN32)
    (void) bytes;
    {
        const std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(view_mu());
        auto& r = view_of_fd();
        for (auto it = r.begin(); it != r.end();)
            it = it->second == p ? r.erase(it) : std::next(it);
    }
    UnmapViewOfFile(p);
#else
    ::munmap(p, (size_t) bytes);
#endif
}
/// Demote [p, p + n)'s whole interior pages of this process's working set to the standby list (Windows: VirtualUnlock
/// on unlocked pages removes them from the working set even though it reports ERROR_NOT_LOCKED - the working set
/// documentation; a clean file-backed page stays resident and soft-faults back with its contents, so this only makes
/// it count as available again). Returns the bytes accepted. Interior pages only: an edge page may hold a neighbour's
/// bytes. Linux has no equivalent for a shared map - its page cache already counts as available.
inline uint64_t unlock_range(const void* p, uint64_t n) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    if (p == nullptr || n < 4096) return 0;
    SYSTEM_INFO si{};
    GetSystemInfo(&si);
    const uint64_t pg = si.dwPageSize ? si.dwPageSize : 4096;
    const uint64_t a = ((uint64_t) (uintptr_t) p + pg - 1) & ~(pg - 1);
    const uint64_t b = ((uint64_t) (uintptr_t) p + n) & ~(pg - 1);
    if (b <= a) return 0;
    SetLastError(ERROR_SUCCESS);
    const BOOL unlocked = VirtualUnlock((LPVOID) (uintptr_t) a, (SIZE_T) (b - a));
    // (NOT_LOCKED is the documented success path for pages this process never locked)
    return unlocked || GetLastError() == ERROR_NOT_LOCKED ? b - a : 0;
#else
    (void) p, (void) n;
    return 0;
#endif
}

/// A hint that [off, off + n) of the file is needed soon (need) or not again soon (!need); nothing where the OS has
/// no such hint for a file. Windows' !need, where this process maps the file, demotes the range's interior pages out
/// of its working set (Linux: the page cache is reclaimable and counts as available, so the hint stays a no-op there).
inline void advise(fd_t f, uint64_t off, uint64_t n, bool need) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    if (need || f == kBadFd) return;
    const uint8_t* base = nullptr;
    {
        const std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(view_mu());
        const auto it = view_of_fd().find((size_t) f);
        if (it == view_of_fd().end()) return;
        base = it->second;
    }
    if (off > UINT64_MAX - n) return;
    (void) unlock_range(base + off, n);
#else
    posix_fadvise((int) f, (off_t) off, (off_t) n, need ? POSIX_FADV_WILLNEED : POSIX_FADV_DONTNEED);
#endif
}

/// This process's resident working-set bytes (the trimmer's measure; 0: it could not be read).
inline uint64_t working_set_bytes() {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    PROCESS_MEMORY_COUNTERS pm{};
    pm.cb = sizeof pm;
    return GetProcessMemoryInfo(GetCurrentProcess(), &pm, pm.cb) ? (uint64_t) pm.WorkingSetSize : 0;
#else
    std::FILE* f = std::fopen("/proc/self/statm", "r");
    if (f == nullptr) return 0;
    unsigned long long size = 0, resident = 0;
    const int got = std::fscanf(f, "%llu %llu", &size, &resident);
    std::fclose(f);
    return got == 2 ? resident * 4096ull : 0;
#endif
}

/// Release only whole source-file pages after their bytes have been copied to private RAM.
/// Edge pages may contain another tensor or a concurrent reader's bytes: leave them cached.
/// Windows trims those pages from this process's working set with VirtualUnlock: clean file-backed contents stay
/// recoverable by a later fault, unlike MEM_RESET. Linux advises DONTNEED. Returns bytes accepted as a hint;
/// neither interface promises global cache eviction (another process may still map the pages).
inline uint64_t release_copied_cache(fd_t f, const void* mapped_base, uint64_t off, uint64_t n) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    SYSTEM_INFO si{};
    GetSystemInfo(&si);
    const uint64_t p = si.dwPageSize;
    if (mapped_base == nullptr) return 0;
#else
    const long page = sysconf(_SC_PAGESIZE);
    const uint64_t p = page > 0 ? (uint64_t) page : 0;
    (void) mapped_base;
#endif
    if (f == kBadFd || p == 0 || n == 0 || off > UINT64_MAX - n || off + n > (uint64_t) INT64_MAX - p) return 0;
    const uint64_t rem = off % p;
    const uint64_t skip = rem == 0 ? 0 : p - rem;
    if (skip >= n) return 0;
    const uint64_t begin = off + skip, end = (off + n) / p * p;
    if (end <= begin || end > (uint64_t) INT64_MAX) return 0;
#if defined(_WIN32)
    void* address = const_cast<uint8_t*>(static_cast<const uint8_t*>(mapped_base)) + begin;
    SetLastError(ERROR_SUCCESS);
    const BOOL unlocked = VirtualUnlock(address, (SIZE_T) (end - begin));
    const DWORD error = GetLastError();
    // Microsoft's Working Set documentation: unlocked pages are removed even though the call reports NOT_LOCKED.
    return unlocked || error == ERROR_NOT_LOCKED ? end - begin : 0;
#else
    return posix_fadvise((int) f, (off_t) begin, (off_t) (end - begin), POSIX_FADV_DONTNEED) == 0
               ? end - begin : 0;
#endif
}

/// Ranges of a file's mapping about to be read: the OS reads them in now, with large requests side by side, instead of
/// a page fault at a time later (Windows: PrefetchVirtualMemory - cold expert-sized pieces came in 5x faster on an NVMe;
/// Linux: madvise WILLNEED, the kernel's read-ahead of the whole range).  Best effort: a failure changes nothing.
inline void prefetch(const std::vector<std::pair<const void*, size_t>>& ranges) {
    if (ranges.empty()) return;
#if defined(_WIN32)
    std::vector<WIN32_MEMORY_RANGE_ENTRY> r(ranges.size());
    for (size_t i = 0; i < ranges.size(); ++i) {
        r[i].VirtualAddress = const_cast<void*>(ranges[i].first);
        r[i].NumberOfBytes = ranges[i].second;
    }
    PrefetchVirtualMemory(GetCurrentProcess(), (ULONG_PTR) r.size(), r.data(), 0);
#else
    const uintptr_t pg = 4096;
    for (const auto& x : ranges) {
        const uintptr_t a = (uintptr_t) x.first, b = a & ~(pg - 1);
        madvise((void*) b, x.second + (a - b), MADV_WILLNEED);
    }
#endif
}

/// Whether every page of [p, p + n) of a file's mapping is in the page cache (Linux mincore; elsewhere false: the
/// caller reads buffered anyway, which the cache serves)
inline bool resident(const void* p, size_t n) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    (void) p, (void) n;
    return false;
#else
    const uintptr_t pg = 4096, a = (uintptr_t) p, b = a & ~(pg - 1);
    const size_t len = n + (a - b), np = (len + pg - 1) / pg;
    thread_local std::vector<unsigned char> v;
    if (v.size() < np) v.resize(np);
    if (mincore((void*) b, len, v.data()) != 0) return false;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < np; ++i)
        if ((v[i] & 1) == 0) return false;
    return true;
#endif
}

/// Whether a range of a file's mapping looks resident, so that prefetch() would only cost CPU time (it walks every page:
/// ~50 ns a 4 KiB page on Windows - 0.34 ms for a 6.3 MB expert matrix on a Strix Halo): `samples` pages spread over
/// it all resident (Linux: mincore, the page cache; Windows: QueryWorkingSetEx, the process's working set - a page on
/// the standby list counts as not resident, and prefetch() is what brings those back cheaply).  An expert's pages are
/// read together, so they come and go together: a wrong "yes" costs page faults, a wrong "no" a prefetch.
inline bool resident_sampled(const void* p, size_t bytes, int samples = 8) {
    if (bytes == 0) return true;
    const uintptr_t pg = 4096, a = (uintptr_t) p, span = bytes - 1;
    samples = std::max(1, std::min(samples, 16));
#if defined(_WIN32)
    PSAPI_WORKING_SET_EX_INFORMATION w[16] = {};
    for (int i = 0; i < samples; ++i)
        w[i].VirtualAddress = (PVOID) ((a + (samples > 1 ? span / (samples - 1) * i : 0)) & ~(pg - 1));
    if (!K32QueryWorkingSetEx(GetCurrentProcess(), w, (DWORD) (samples * sizeof w[0]))) return false;
    for (int i = 0; i < samples; ++i)
        if (!w[i].VirtualAttributes.Valid) return false;
    return true;
#else
    for (int i = 0; i < samples; ++i) {
        unsigned char v = 0;
        const uintptr_t at = (a + (samples > 1 ? span / (samples - 1) * i : 0)) & ~(pg - 1);
        if (mincore((void*) at, pg, &v) != 0 || !(v & 1)) return false;
    }
    return true;
#endif
}
/// prefetch() of the ranges that do not look resident (resident_sampled) - on Windows, where PrefetchVirtualMemory walks
/// every page of a resident range (0.34 ms a 6.3 MB expert matrix on a Strix Halo); Linux's madvise(WILLNEED) on cached
/// pages costs ~1 us a matrix (a Core i5-12600T), less than the residency check (2.5 us): every range there
inline void prefetch_cold(const std::vector<std::pair<const void*, size_t>>& ranges) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    static const bool all = [] {   // (DS4_PF_ALL=1: every range, as before)
        const char* v = std::getenv("DS4_PF_ALL");
        return v != nullptr && std::atoi(v) != 0;
    }();
    if (all) return prefetch(ranges);
    std::vector<std::pair<const void*, size_t>> cold;
    for (const auto& x : ranges)
        if (!resident_sampled(x.first, x.second)) cold.push_back(x);
    prefetch(cold);
#else
    prefetch(ranges);
#endif
}

/// Whether [p, p + n) of a file's mapping is in RAM now: every page (Linux, one mincore), sampled pages of the working
/// set (Windows; a page on the standby list counts as not in RAM)
inline bool in_ram(const void* p, size_t n) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    return resident_sampled(p, n, 8);
#else
    return resident(p, n);
#endif
}

/// (a test of DISK_SKIP_AUTO's switch back) this process's file mappings out of RAM: Linux - its pages unmapped
/// (madvise DONTNEED) and the files' cached pages dropped (posix_fadvise DONTNEED; clean pages, no root needed);
/// Windows - the mapped views out of the working set (VirtualUnlock of unlocked pages), then the rest of it
inline void drop_cached(const std::vector<std::pair<uint8_t*, size_t>>& maps, const std::vector<fd_t>& fds) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    (void) fds;
    for (const auto& m : maps)
        if (m.first != nullptr && m.second > 0) VirtualUnlock((LPVOID) m.first, m.second);
    K32EmptyWorkingSet(GetCurrentProcess());
#else
    for (const auto& m : maps)
        if (m.first != nullptr && m.second > 0) madvise((void*) m.first, m.second, MADV_DONTNEED);
    for (fd_t f : fds)
        if (f != kBadFd) posix_fadvise((int) f, 0, 0, POSIX_FADV_DONTNEED);
#endif
}

/// Anonymous memory on NUMA node `node` of `nodes` (node < 0 or nodes < 2: wherever the first touch puts it), on huge
/// pages where the OS gives them; nullptr if it cannot be had.  Linux: the node is preferred, not required
/// (MPOL_PREFERRED) - a node that fills up (locked Engram tables, another program's memory) lends the rest from the
/// other nodes, where MPOL_BIND had the kernel end the process with RAM free on the other node (#507)
inline void* anon_alloc(size_t bytes, int node, int nodes) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    if (node >= 0 && nodes > 1)
        return VirtualAllocExNuma(GetCurrentProcess(), nullptr, bytes, MEM_RESERVE | MEM_COMMIT, PAGE_READWRITE,
                                  (DWORD) node);
    return VirtualAlloc(nullptr, bytes, MEM_RESERVE | MEM_COMMIT, PAGE_READWRITE);
#else
    void* m = ::mmap(nullptr, bytes, PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE, MAP_PRIVATE | MAP_ANONYMOUS, -1, 0);
    if (m == MAP_FAILED) return nullptr;
    madvise(m, bytes, MADV_HUGEPAGE);
    if (node >= 0 && nodes > 1) {
        unsigned long mask[4] = {0, 0, 0, 0};
        mask[node / 64] = 1ul << (node % 64);
        if (syscall(SYS_mbind, m, bytes, 1 /* MPOL_PREFERRED */, mask, 256, 0) != 0) {
            // (said once: without it the per-node copies land wherever first touch puts them and nothing shows it -
            //  Docker's default seccomp profile refuses mbind to a container without CAP_SYS_NICE)
            static std::atomic<bool> said{false};
            if (!said.exchange(true))
                std::fprintf(stderr, "ds4: mbind to NUMA node %d failed (%s): the RAM copies do not prefer their node "
                                     "(a container needs SYS_NICE)\n", node, std::strerror(errno));
        }
    }
    return m;
#endif
}
inline void anon_free(void* p, size_t bytes) {
    if (p == nullptr) return;
#if defined(_WIN32)
    (void) bytes;
    VirtualFree(p, 0, MEM_RELEASE);
#else
    ::munmap(p, bytes);
#endif
}

/// The kernel's pool of explicit 2 MB huge pages (reserved by the admin: `sysctl vm.nr_hugepages=N`): the bytes a new
/// mapping can still take from it (free less already reserved); 0 where there is none or it cannot be read, and on
/// Windows.  MemAvailable does not count this pool.
constexpr size_t kHuge2M = (size_t) 2 << 20;
inline uint64_t huge2m_free() {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    return 0;
#else
    auto num = [](const char* path) -> uint64_t {
        std::FILE* f = std::fopen(path, "r");
        if (f == nullptr) return 0;
        unsigned long long v = 0;
        const bool ok = std::fscanf(f, "%llu", &v) == 1;
        std::fclose(f);
        return ok ? (uint64_t) v : 0;
    };
    const uint64_t fr = num("/sys/kernel/mm/hugepages/hugepages-2048kB/free_hugepages"),
                   rs = num("/sys/kernel/mm/hugepages/hugepages-2048kB/resv_hugepages");
    return fr > rs ? (fr - rs) * kHuge2M : 0;
#endif
}

/// Anonymous memory on explicit 2 MB pages from that pool (MAP_HUGETLB): `bytes` rounded up to whole pages, `*got` the
/// size mapped (what anon_free takes back), NUMA node `node` of `nodes` preferred as anon_alloc does.  The pages are
/// reserved at the call, so a mapping it returns does not fault for want of them later; nullptr: the pool cannot hold
/// it (or Windows).  Unlike a transparent-huge-page arena, a pin (cudaHostRegister) of it keeps its 2 MB pages.
inline void* huge_alloc(size_t bytes, int node, int nodes, size_t* got) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    (void) bytes, (void) node, (void) nodes, (void) got;
    return nullptr;
#else
#ifndef MAP_HUGE_SHIFT
#define MAP_HUGE_SHIFT 26
#endif
    const size_t hb = (bytes + kHuge2M - 1) / kHuge2M * kHuge2M;
    void* m = ::mmap(nullptr, hb, PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE, MAP_PRIVATE | MAP_ANONYMOUS | MAP_HUGETLB | (21 << MAP_HUGE_SHIFT),
                     -1, 0);
    if (m == MAP_FAILED) return nullptr;
    if (node >= 0 && nodes > 1) {
        unsigned long mask[4] = {0, 0, 0, 0};
        mask[node / 64] = 1ul << (node % 64);
        syscall(SYS_mbind, m, hb, 1 /* MPOL_PREFERRED */, mask, 256, 0);
    }
    if (got != nullptr) *got = hb;
    return m;
#endif
}

/// The calling thread, or `t`, on logical CPU `cpu` (Windows: the first 64 CPUs, processor group 0).
inline void pin_self(int cpu) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    if (cpu >= 0 && cpu < 64) SetThreadAffinityMask(GetCurrentThread(), (DWORD_PTR) 1 << cpu);
#else
    cpu_set_t set;
    CPU_ZERO(&set);
    CPU_SET(cpu, &set);
    sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof set, &set);
#endif
}
inline void pin_thread(std::thread& t, int cpu) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    if (cpu >= 0 && cpu < 64) SetThreadAffinityMask((HANDLE) t.native_handle(), (DWORD_PTR) 1 << cpu);
#else
    cpu_set_t set;
    CPU_ZERO(&set);
    CPU_SET(cpu, &set);
    pthread_setaffinity_np(t.native_handle(), sizeof(set), &set);
#endif
}

/// A physical core: the logical CPUs of it this process may run on (the first one first), and its efficiency class
